tēvanēyam – 5





patippāciriyar
pulavar. irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ










tamiḻmaṇ patippakam




nūṟkuṟippu
nūṟpeyar 	:	tēvanēyam - 5
tokuppāciriyar 	:	pulavar. irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
patippāḷar 	:	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
mutaṟpatippu	:	2004
maṟupatippu 	:	2015
tāḷ 	:	16.0 ki. mēplittō
aḷavu 	:	1/8 temmi
pakkam 	:	8 +  312 = 320
paṭikaḷ 	:	1000
vili 	:	uru. 300/-
nūlākkam	:	pāvāṇar kaṇiṉi
		tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 17.  
aṭṭi vaṭivamippu 	:	tamiḻkkumaraṉ
accu 	:	veṅkaṭēcuvarā 
		āpceṭ piriṇṭar								irāyappēṭṭi, ceṉṉi - 14.

veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilippaṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!	iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

akattiyat tarukka cūttiram
 1.	poruḷkuṇaṅ karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumi
 	iṉmi yuṭaṉporuḷ ēḻeṉa moḻipa.
 2. 	maṇpuṉal aṉalkāl veḷipoḻu tāci
 	āṉmā maṉatō ṭoṉpatum poruḷē.
 3. 	vaṭivam cuviyiru nāṟṟam ūṟeṇ
 	aḷavu vēṟṟumi puṇarcci pirivu
 	muṉmi piṉmi tiṇmi nekiḻcci
 	cikkeṉal ōci uṇarcci yiṉpam
 	tuṉpam viruppam veṟuppu muyaṟci
 	aṟamaṟam vātaṉiyoṭu kuṇamaṟu nāṉkē.
 4. 	eḻumpal viḻutal vaḷital nimirtal
 	naṭatta luṭaṉē karumami vakittē.
 5.	potumi mēlkīḻ eṉiru vakiyē.
 6. 	maṉṉiya poruḷiṉ kaṇṇavā yavaṟṟiṉ
 	vēṟṟumi terippaṉa palavām ciṟappē.
 7. 	oṟṟumi yāppaḵ toṉṟē yeṉka.
 8. 	muṉṉiṉmi piṉṉiṉmi muṟṟu miṉmi
 	 oṉṟiṉoṉ ṟiṉmiyeṉ ṟiṉmi nāṉkē.
 9. 	maṇnīr aṉalkāl muṟiyē nāṟṟam
 	taṭpam veppam ūṟṟam āki
 	meypporuḷ aḻiporuḷ mēvum eṉka.
 10. 	aṇukkaḷ meypporuḷ kāriyam aḻiporuḷ
 	pirutivi nittiya anittiya vaṇampeṟum
 	niliyaṇup poruḷnili yillatu kāriyam.
 11. 	atuvē
 	uṭalpoṟi viṭayam mūvakip paṭumē
 	nammaṉōr yākki maṇkūṟ ṟuṭampu
 	nāṟṟaṅ kavarvatu nāciyiṉ nuṉiyē
 	maṇkal mutaliya viṭayam ākum.
 12.	nīriṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭunī ruṭampu
 	cuvit tiṟam kavarvatu nāviṉ nuṉiyē
 	kaṭalyā ṟāti viṭayam ākum.
 13.	katirōṉ varippiṟ kaṭṭaṉa luṭampē
 	uruvam kavarvatu karumaṇi viḻiyē
 	maṇviṇ vayiṟā karamnāl viṭayam.
 14.	vaḷiyiṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭukā luṭampē
 	ūṟṟam kavarvatu mīntōl eṉka
 	viṭayam maramutal acitaṟ kētuvē
 	pirāṇaṉ uṭalakat tiyaṅkum kāṟṟē.
 15.	vicumpē kālam ticiyō ṭāṉmā
 	maṉamivi yintum nittiyap poruḷē.
 16. 	ōcip paṇpiṟ ṟākā yammē.
 17. 	iṟappumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētuk kālam.
 18.	kiḻakkumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētut ticiyē.
 19.	aṟivup paṇpiṟ ṟāṉmā eṉka
 	iṟiyē īcaṉmuṟ ṟaṟivaṉōr mutalē
 	uyirtāṉ uṭaltoṟum vevvē ṟākum.
 20.	maṉamaṇu vaṭivāy varumiṉ pāti
 	aṟitaṟ kiṉṟi yamiyāk karuvi
 	yākip palavāy aḻiviṉ ṟuṟumē.
ivi tamiḻp paṇṭitar kō. vaṭivēluc ceṭṭiyāriṉ tarukkaparipāṣi patippiṟ kaṇṭavi. ēraṇam eṉpatu tarukka nūlik kuṟikkum tūya teṉcol. iccol camaṟkirutattil iṉmi kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar eṉṉum tirucciṟṟampalak 
 kōvic cāttuppāt toṭarum,
ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icikaṇak kiratañcālam
tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampamnīr nilamu lōkam
māraṇam poruḷ eṉṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇam koṇṭatantō vaḻivaḻip peyarum māḷa.
eṉṉum taṉippāṭalum, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattil tarukka nūl iruntamiyiyum, ataṉ peyar ēraṇam eṉpataṉiyum, teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa ēraṇanūl orēvakiyē. vaṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa tarukka nūl vicēṭikam, niyāyikam eṉa iruvakippaṭum. avaṟṟi muṟiyē ciṟappikam, muṟiyikam eṉalām. tamiḻ ēraṇam ciṟappika muṟippaṭṭatākum. uṟaḻum allatu tarukkam vaki potuvāyiṉum, aṭippaṭip poruṭpākupāṭṭi nōkkiṉ, ciṟappika muṟiyē ciṟantatum muntiyatumākum. ellāpporuḷkaḷiyum, muṟiyika muṟippaṭi patiṉāṟu vakiyākap pakuppatiṉum, ciṟappika muṟippaṭi eḻuvakiyākap pakuppatē, muḻuniṟivāyum iṭiveḷiyillatāyum oṉṟi yoṉṟu taḻuvātatāyumiruttal kāṇka.
ēraṇam (Logic) eṉṉum col maṭṭumiṉṟi tarukkam (Debate) eṉṉuñ collum, tamiḻāyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. ērtal = eḻutal. ēraṇam = coṟpōril oruvaṉtaṉ pakivaṉ mītu eḻucci koḷvataṟku ētuvāṉa muṟikaḷik kaṟpikkum nūl.
tuḷ - tuḷir. tuḷirttal = 1. koḻuntuviṭutal, 2. ceḻittal.
tuḷir - taḷir. taḷirttal = 1. koḻuntu viṭutal, 2. taḻittal, māriyāl vaṟṟiniṉṟa cantaṉan taḷirttatēpōl (cīvaka. 545). 3. ceḻittal tuḷ-taḷ-taḷa. taḷataḷattal = parumiyātal, koḻukoḻuttal.
taḷ - taḻu - taḻi. taḻittal = 1. ceḻittal, 2. mikutal. mit taḻiyā niṉṟa māmiṭaṟ ṟampalavaṉ (tirukkōvi, 102). 3. vaḷartl. meytaḻi kaṟpi (tiruviḷi. vaḷiya. 15).
taḻu - taḻukku. taḻukkutal = ceḻippuṟutal, taḻukkiya nāḷil tarumamuñ ceyyīr (tiruman. 254)
taḻukku - tarukku. tarukkutal = 1 mikuttal. taṉṉoṭu mavaḷoṭun tarukkiya puṇarttu (tol. akat. 50). 2. ūkkam mikuttal. vempōrt tarukkiṉār mintar. (cīvaka. 1679). 3. akaṅkarittal. taṉṉi viyantu tarukkalum tirikaṭu. 38).
tarukku - tarukkam = taṉ kūṟṟi mikukkum coṟpōr. tarukku - cerukku = akaṅkāram.
tarukku - va. tark. tarukkam - va. tarkka. tarukku eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamiṉmi kavaṉikkattakkatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya ēraṇa nūṟpākkaḷ akattiyat tarukka nūṟpākka ḷallā viṭiṉum, tarukka nūli vaḷarttaṟ kēṟṟa aṭippaṭi niṟivā yamintiruttal kāṇka. avi mikap paḻiyaṉavā yiruttal paṟṟiyē akattiyar peyaril vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
avaṟṟil varum vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikac cilavāyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
avvaṭa coṟkaṭku nēr teṉ coṟkaḷ varumāṟu :-
 vaṭacol	-	teṉcol
 āci	-	mātiram, tiki
 āṉmā	-	ātaṉ, uṟavi, pulampaṉ

iṉi, vaṉivum necavum, kammiyamum pōlap pala kalikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyamiṉṟiyē vaḻivaḻi vaḻaṅki vantiruttal vēṇṭum, eṉpatu collāmalē viḷaṅkum.
tamiḻar paraval
makkaṭ perukkam, vāṇikam, putunāṭu kāṇum viruppam, pōr koḷḷi paki pañcam kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya palakaraṇiyaṅkaḷ paṟṟi, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar palvēṟu nilikaḷiṟ palvēṟu ticiyiṟ paravic ceṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ vaḷarcciyaṭintapiṉ avar paravic ceṉṟatu vaṭakku nōkkiyē. vaṅkat talinakarkku iṉṟum kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉ ṉum tūya teṉcoṟ peyar vaḻaṅkuvatē, paṇṭik kālattē tamiḻar paṉimalivari allatu kaṅkivari paraviyiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām.
tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻiyāyum orē moḻiyāyum vaḻaṅkiyataṉāl, nīṇṭa kālamāka ataṟkuc ciṟappup peyar ēṟpaṭavilli; moḻiyeṉṉum potuppeyarē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. vaṭakkē cellac cella moḻi mellamellat tirintatu. ataṉāl vaṭa nāṭṭi moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar. piṉṉart teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikkut tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟu.
tamiḻ tirintu tamiḻarkku viḷaṅkāk kiḷimoḻiyāy māṟiya pōtu, tamiḻam eṉṉum collum tiraviṭam eṉṟu tirintatu. (tamiḻ - tamiḻam - tramiḷa - tramiṭa - traviṭa - tiraviṭam - tirāviṭam.) ataṉāl, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta moḻi tirāviṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. atu mutaṟkaṇ oṉṟāyiruntu piṉpu palavākak kiḷittatu. mutaṉmutalākat tirinta tiraviṭamoḻi teluṅkāta lāl atu vaṭaku (vaṭanāṭṭu allatu vaṭakkattu moḻi) eṉappaṭṭatu. atu piṉṉar vaṭuku eṉat tirintatu. vaṭuku eṉpatu ammīṟu peṟṟu vaṭukam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. vaṭaku eṉpatu iṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattiṟ paṭaku eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. nīlamaliyil vāḻum kaṉṉaṭat tirimoḻiyāḷar paṭakar eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
aṇmiyiṟ pirinta maliyāḷam nīṅkalākat tiraviṭa moḻika ḷellām vaṭapālē vaḻaṅkuvatum, vaṭakkē cellac cellat tirintum citintum ciṟuttum citaṟiyum ilakkaṇa vilakkiyam kuṉṟiyum iṉṟiyum pōvatum, intiyāviṟku veḷiyē vaḻaṅkāmiyum, iṉṟu tamiḻum ōraḷavu tirintuḷḷamiyum, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē iṟṟit tamiḻukkum ellāt tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkum tāyeṉpatit terivikkum.
moḻikaḷiṉ allatu coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpum tiripum nōkkātu, ciṟitum peritum vaṭivoppumi yoṉṟiyē nōkki, tamiḻariṉ allatu tiraviṭariṉ muṉṉōr vaṭamēli nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vantirukkalāmeṉṟu, varalāṟṟāciriyar maṭṭu maṉṟi moḻinūlaṟiñarum mayaṅki iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉar. coṟkaḷiṉ vērum varalāṟum aṟiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam iṭarp paṭār. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, oru teluṅkuc colliyum oru pirākuvīc colliyum eṭuttu īṇṭu viḷakkuvām.
teluṅkuc col : māṭṭāṭu, māṭlāṭu. māṟu - māṟṟam = māṟiccollum col, col. māṟṟam - te. māṭṭa. māṭṭa + āṭu = māṭṭāṭu. o. nō : collāṭu, uriyāṭu, te. māṭṭalu (paṉmi) + āṭu = māṭṭalāṭu. ta. kaḷ (paṉmiyīṟu) te. kaḷu - kalu - lu. kaḷḷutal = kalattal, kūṭutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭṭam, avi. kūṭutalik kuṟikkum col, pala poruṭ kūṭṭamākiya paṉmiyi yuṇarttuṅ kuṟiyāyiṟṟu.
te. māṭṭu - ka. māttu, māttaṉu. māttu + āṭu = māttāṭu, māttaṉu + āṭu = māttaṉāṭu.
ka. māttu - kō. māntu, mānt = col, moḻi.
ka. = kaṉṉaṭam. kō. = kōttam (kōttar moḻi)
pirākuvīc col : pāk = vāykaḷ.
ta. vāy - pi. pā. ta. kaḷ - pi. k
vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭaruḷ oru cārārē, vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟu cumēriya nākarikattit tōṟṟuvittaṉar.
ki.mu. 20-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta āpirakāmiṉ ūr, ūr eṉṟē peyar peṟṟiruntatu. ūr eṉpatu pāpilōṉiya moḻiyil nakaram eṉṉum poruḷatu. paṇṭittamiḻ nāṭṭil ūr eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntatu marutanila makkaḷ kuṭiyiruppē. nakaram tōṉṟiyatum marutanilamē.
ūr eṉṉum nakaram irunta iṭattil tōṇṭiyeṭukkap paṭṭa uttaram, ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa cēranāṭṭut tēkkeṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. āpirakām eṉṉum peyariṉ muṟpakutiyākiya āp eṉpatu, appaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc col tiripē.
āpirakāmiṉ pēraṉāṉa yākkōpu tūṅkik kāṭci kaṇṭa iṭam, pettēl eṉappaṭṭatu. pettēl eṉpatu tēvaṉ vīṭu eṉṉum poruḷatu.
ta. vīṭu - ka. pīṭu - pīṭ - kā. pet.
 ta. el = oḷi, katiravaṉ, teyvam. pet + el = pettel.
iṅṅaṉam kūṭṭuc col muṟimāṟi yamivatu cēmiya vaḻakku. kā. = kāṉāṉiyam.
āpirakām kālattil arapiyum epirēyamum tōṉṟavilli. avaṉ pēciṉatu akkēṭiyaṉ.
ātam (ātām) eṉṉum peyar, ceṅkaḷi maṇṇil uruvākkap paṭṭavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ attam eṉṉum tamiḻc col lōṭum, makkaḷiṉat tanti eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ attaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc collōṭum, (mutal) māntaṉ eṉṟuporuḷ paṭiṉ ātaṉ (āṉmā) eṉṉum tamiḻc collōṭum, toṭarpuṭiyatāyirukkalām.
attam = civappu. atti = civanta paḻamaram.
ēvā (ēvāḷ) eṉṉum peyar, makkaḷiṉattiṟku uyirit tantavaḷ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ āvi eṉṉum tamiḻc col lōṭum, makkaḷiṉat tāy eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ avvi eṉṉum tamiḻc collōṭum toṭarpuṭiyatā yirukkalām.
cumēriya nākarikatti vaḷartta makkaḷ vaḻiyiṉaruḷ oru cārār ekipatu vaḻiyākavum maṟṟoru cārār ciṉṉa āciyā vaḻiyāka vum, naṇṇilakkaṭal pukuntu airōppāvi yaṭintu mēli yāriyarāka māṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pūṉi ekipatu nāṭṭil pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarkkappaṭṭu vantapiṉ ki.pi. 4-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil airōppāviṟkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭateṉṟum, atu kirēkkattil katta eṉṟum ilattīṉil kattus eṉṟum peyar peṟṟateṉṟum, āriya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ pirintu pōkumuṉ avaṟṟiṭi ataṟkup potup peyar illiyeṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil mārkāra (Margara) viṭāla eṉṉum iru peyar ataṟkuṇṭeṉṟum, mārkkacu mullar kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
pūṉi toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻakak kāṭṭil iyaṟkiyāka vāḻntu varukiṉṟatu. anniliyil ataṟku veruku eṉṟu peyar. atu vīṭṭiṟ paḻakkappaṭṭa piṉ, aṭikkaṭi mukam pūcuva tāl (kaḻuvuvatāl) pūciyeṉṟum, piḷḷipōl vaḷarkkap paṭṭamiyāl piḷḷi yeṉṟum, pakaṟkālattil naṉṟāy kaṇ teriyāmiyāl kotti yeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu.
pūcu - pūci - pūṉi - pūñi. ma. pūcca.
piḷḷi - te. pilli. o.nō : taḷḷi - te. talli.
kotti - kuruṭu, kuruṭaṉ. kotti - ka. kotti. iccol kumarināṭṭut tamiḻilum vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. vīṭṭup pūṉiyiṉ muppeyarum mēliyāriya moḻikaḷiṟ paravi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
pūci - E puss, pus, pusse, Du. poes, L.G. puns, Sw. pus, Norw. puse, puus, Lith. puz, puiz, Ir and Gael. pus.

Cat. gat, Pr. cat, ONF. cat, F. chat, OE. cat, catt, ON kott-r, Sw. katt, Da. kat, OE.catte WGer. katta, MLG. katte, MDu. katte, kat, Du. kat, Sw. katta, OHG. chazza, (MHG. mod. G. katze), fem.

Pussy eṉṟa coṟku vēr teriyavilli yeṉṟum, cat eṉṟa coṟku vanta vaḻi teriyavilliyeṉṟum, pirittāṉiyak kalikkaḷañciyam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, pūṉiyik kuṟikkum iru vaṭacoṟkaḷum, teṉ colliṉiṉṟu piṟantaviyēyām.
mārkāra allatu mārjāla eṉṉum peyar kaḻuvuvatu allatu tuṭippatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, pūciyeṉṉum teṉ colliṉ moḻi peyarppēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. va. mruj kaḻuvu, tuṭi, tuppuravākku.
iṉi, viṭāla eṉṉum collum viṭaravaṉ eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripēyeṉpatu, āyntu pārppiṉ teḷivām.
pūṉiyiṉ pakaṟkaṇ naṭuvil tōṉṟum tiṟappuk kīṟṟu oru viṭar pōliruppatāl, pūṉikku viṭaravaṉ eṉṟum oru peyar (yāḻ. aka.) uṇṭu. viṭar piḷappu.
viṭaravaṉ - viṭarakaṉ - va. viṭāraka - viṭālaka - viṭāla - piṭāla.
pūṉikkaṇ niṟamuḷḷa oru maṇivakikkup pūṉikkaṇ (cat’s eye) eṉpatu uvamiyāku peyar. ikkaraṇiyam paṟṟiyē viṭāra eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu viṭūrya eṉṉum peyar tirin tuḷḷatu. viṭāraka - viṭāra - viṭūrya. mākkacu mullar ic coṟkuk kūṟiyuḷḷa poruṭkaraṇiyamum ituvē.
Puss eṉpatu pūṉikku viḷippeyareṉṟu, erutantuṟi (Oxford) āṅkila akaramutali kūṟukiṉṟatu. nelli māvaṭṭattil iṉṟum pūṉiyip pūcu pūcu eṉṟaḻippatu iyalpu.
kāṭṭuppūṉikkuk kāṉpuli, pākkaṉ, maṇṭali eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
mēṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, pūṉi tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu mēṉāṭu ceṉṟa teṉpatu veḷḷiṭi mali. paḻiya ekipatiya moḻiyil vēṟucila tamiḻc coṟkaḷum uḷa.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar cuṟṟukkaṭalōṭikaḷāy (Circumnavigators) iruntataṉāl, kaṭaṟ peyarum cila kalap peyarkaḷum mēli yāriya moḻikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
vaḷ - var - vār. vārtal = vaḷital, cūḻtal. vār - vāri = nilattic cūḻnta kaṭal.
vār - vāraṇam = kaṭal. vāraṇam - vāraṇaṉ = kaṭal teyvam. vāraṇaṉ - va. varuṇa. vāri - L. mare. E marina (f.It. and Sp.) = a promenade or esplanade by the sea.
paṭaku : paṭam = cīli, tiriccīli, pāyccīli. paṭam - paṭavu paṭa. paṭavu - paṭavar - paravar. cempaṭavu - cempaṭavar.

nāvāy :

nāvāy (peruṅkalam) - va. nu = paṭaku.
naṅkūram :
E. anchor, OE. ancor, L. ancora, GK. ankura, OHG. anchar LG., MHG. anker, ON. akkeri, Sw. ankare, Da. anker, OF. ancre. It. ancora. L. anchora.
nāṅkūlam = nāṅkuḷu (nāṅkūḻ), maṇṇit tuḷippatu pōl nilattit tuḷittu uḻum kalappi. te. nākēla. va. lāṅkala, ta. nāṅkūlam - nāṅkul - nāñcil = kalappi.
nāṅkūlam - nāṅkūram - naṅkūram - naṅkuram = kappali niṟuttum kalappi vaṭivāṉa iruppuk karuvi. pāra. laṅkar.
vaḷivik kuṟikkum ank eṉṉum viṉi mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu anchor eṉṉum col tirintuḷḷatāka. āṅkila akaramutalikaḷ kūṟum. aḵtuṇmi yāyiṉ, atuvun tamiḻ vaḻiyē.
vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku - aṅku. aṅkutal = vaḷital, cāytal. aṅkaṇam = vāṭṭañcāṭṭamāṉa cālakam (cāykaṭi).
iṉi, vaṭapār muṉiyil avvappōtu tōṉṟum oruvaki miṉṉoḷiyiyuṅ kaṇṭu, ataṟku vaṭavi allatu vaṭanti eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar kumarināṭṭuk kaṭalōṭikaḷ.
ūḻiyiṟuti yulakaḻivu neruppiṉāl nērumeṉṟu civaneṟiyār nampiṉatiṉāl, avvaṭaviyē anneruppiṉ mūlamāyirukku meṉṟu oru karutteḻuntatu. ataṉāl ati uttaramaṭaṅkal eṉṟaṉar.
uttaram = vaṭakku. maṭaṅkal = kūṟṟuvaṉ. uttara maṭaṅkal eṉpatu uttaram eṉṟu curuṅkiyum vaḻaṅkum.
vaṭakkiluḷa neruppu eṉṉum poruḷil, atu ilakkiyattil vaṭavik kaṉal, vaṭa aṉal eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
mēliyar vaṭaviyi Aurora borealis eṉpar. ataṟku vaṭa viṭiyal allatu vaṭaveḷiccam eṉṟu poruḷ. Borealis eṉpati vaṭa ticiyiyum vaṭakāṟṟiyum kuṟikkum. vaṭanti eṉṉum col vaṭakāṟṟiyuṅ kuṟippatum, vaṭakāṟṟiṉ peyarāṉa vāṭi eṉpatu vaṭavikkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatum, oppu nōkkattakkaṉa.
āriyar vaṭavi eṉṉum teṉcolli vaṭavā allatu paṭapā eṉṟu tirittu, mukam eṉṉum colliyuñ cērttu, peṭṭik kutiriyiṉ mukattil tōṉṟum neruppeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu, paḻaṅkarunāṭakap paṭṭimaruṭṭām.
āriyar tirumpal
vaṭatirāviṭar airōppāviṉ vaṭamēṟkiṟ kāṇṭiṉēviyam vari ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟiyapiṉ, allatu āriyattaṉmi yaṭinta piṉ, avaruḷ oru cārār teṉkiḻakku nōkkit tirumpi ittāliyāvilum kirēkka nāṭṭilum kuṭiyēṟiṉar. kirēkkattiṟku iṉamāṉa oru moḻiyip pēciṉa kūṭṭattār, teṉkiḻakku nōkkiyē toṭarntu pāracīka vaḻiyāka vantu intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar. vaṭintiyāvilirunta pirākirutap pēriṉattōṭu kalantu pōṉatiṉāl, avar moḻiyum pirākirutattōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu, ekara okarak kuṟil kaḷi yiḻantatu. akkalavi moḻiyilēyē vētam iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu.
āriya māntar nūṟṟiṟkut toṇṇūṟ ṟoṉpatiṉmar pirākirutap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷoṭu kalantu pōṉālum, āriyap pūcāriyār maṭṭum pirintu niṉṟu, vētavāyilākavum vēḷvi kaḷ mūlamākavum āriyap paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi aviyātu kāttuvantirukkiṉṟaṉar. āriyam eṉṉum moḻip peyarum āriyam eṉṉum iṉappeyarum, intiya āriyariṭiyē mutaṉmutal tōṉṟi ēṉi nāṭukaḷilum mākkacu mullar vāyilākap paraviyuḷḷaṉa.
vēta āriyar āṭumāṭu mēykkum mulli nākarikarākavē yirun tataṉāl, avar pūcāriyarum eḻuttaṟiyātavarākavē yiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, vēta mantiraṅkaḷi maṉappāṭamāka ōti vantataṉāl niṉivāṟṟalum, tam mēmpāṭṭik kāttu vantataṉāl cūḻccit tiṟaṉum, avarkku mikutiyāka iruntaṉa. ataṉāl, vaṭanāṭṭup pirākirutap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷip pōṉṟē teṉ ṉāṭṭut tamiḻp paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiyum, tām nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum tam vēta moḻi tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum ēmāṟṟi aṭimippaṭuttiviṭṭaṉar. itaṟku avar veṇṇiṟa mum vētamoḻiyiṉ eṭuppoliyum tuṇi niṉṟaṉa. irucār paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum ēmāṟṟappaṭuvataṟku, avar paḻaṅkuṭip pētimiyum matappittamum koṭi maṭamum ētuvāyiruntaṉa.
viralviṭ ṭeṇṇak kūṭiya oru cilarāṉa āriyap pūcāriyarē, teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu avar moḻiyiyum ilakkiyattiyuṅ kaṟṟu, tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟu kiranta eḻutti yamittu, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻc coṟ kaḷi vēta moḻiyoṭu kalantu camaṟkirutam eṉṉum aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya moḻiyit tōṟṟuvittu, tamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām camaṟkirutattil moḻipeyarttapiṉ mūla nūlkaḷi yaḻittu viṭṭu moḻi peyarppu nūlkaḷiyē mutaṉūlkaḷākak kāṭṭi, intiya nākarikam āriyar kaṇṭateṉa mēliyarum nampumāṟu nāṭṭi viṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻilakkiyam, kumarināṭṭu makkaḷāṉa oru pēriṉattār, kaṟ kālam mutal iruppukkālam vari paṉṉeṭuṅkālamāka vaḷarttu vanta paltuṟi nākarikap paṇpāṭṭiṉ viḷi veṉṟum; camaṟ kiruta ilakkiyam, oru cila vantēṟikaḷ, aintiliru pakuti tamiḻā yuḷḷa camaṟkirutameṉṉum ilakkiyak kalavi moḻiyil, tamiḻilakkiyatti moḻipeyarttu virivu paṭuttiyum māṟṟiyum amittateṉṟum; vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.  (ta.i.va.)



kumarināṭē nākarika māntaṉ piṟantakam cāṉṟukaḷ 
nilat toṉmi
ñālattil mikamikap paḻamiyāṉa nilappakutiyum, paravi tōṉṟu muṉpē nīṇṭa kālam nilittiruntatum, kumarik kaṇṭamē, ekipatiya nākarikattiṟku muntiyatu kumarinākarikam.

2. ñāla naṭumi
ñālanaṭuviṭam naṇṇilak kōṭṭi (Equator) yaṭutta kumarik kaṇṭamēyaṉṟi, camataṭpaveppa maṇṭalattatic cārntatum naṇṇilak kaṭal eṉṟu tavaṟākap peyar peṟṟuḷḷatumāṉa iṭattic cērntataṉṟu.
āṭiyiṉṟi nīṇṭa kālamākak kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻntirunta iyaṟki māntaṉiṉ uṭal muḻutum pōrttirunta mayir kaḻivataṟku, veppa nāṭē ēṟṟatu.

3. iyaṟki vaḷam
iyaṟki viḷiyuḷiyē perumpālum uṇṭu vanta mutaṟkāla māntaṉukkēṟṟa kāyuṅ kaṉiyum kiḻaṅkum vittum vilaṅkum, paṟaviyum, mīṉum, ērāḷamāyk kiṭittirukkak kūṭiyatu kumarik kaṇṭamē. āṭum, māṭum, ūṉum pālun tōlum oruṅkē utaviṉa.

4. uyirvāḻvataṟku ēṟpu
nāṉkām maṇṭalak kālattil airōppāviṟ paṭarnta paṉik kaṭṭip paṭalam pōṉṟa iyaṟkip pēriṭarppāṭu, kumarik kaṇṭattil nikaḻntatilli.

5. tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkap paḻimi
paṇpaṭṭa teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻariṭi yiruntu arukivarum kātu vaḷarppu, pacci kuttal mutaliyaṉa. muntiyal māntar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷākum.

6. muntiyal māntar vāḻnila aṇmi
vilaṅkāṇṭiyarum anākarikarumāṉa muntiyal māntar vaḻiyiṉar vāḻnilaṅkaḷāṉa āppirikkāvum āttirēliyāvum, kumarik kaṇṭamirunta iṭattic cūḻntē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. anākarika nilikku aṭuttatē nākarika nili.

7. ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻi vaḻaṅkiya iṭam
ulaka mutaṟṟāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya iṭam paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarināṭē.
	māntaṉ nākarika vaḷarccik kēṟṟatu kumarināṭē: cāṉṟukaḷ -
1. puḷmāp perukkam
uḻavut toḻiṟkuriya kāḷi yerumi mutaliyaṉavum, kāvaṟ ṟoḻiṟkuriya nāy pūṉi mutaliyaṉavum, ceyti viṭuttaṟ kuriya kiḷi puṟā mutaliyaṉavum, kumarināṭṭil ērāḷamāyiruntaṉa.
2. karuvip poruḷvaḷam
vīṭu kaṭṭuvataṟkuriya maramuṅ kallum cuṇṇāmpum, karuviyum kalamum aṇikalamum paṭikkalamum ceytaṟkuriya poṉṉum veḷḷiyum cempum, irumpum, maramum, āṭi neytaṟkuriya paṭṭiyum nārum, pañcum, mayirum, kūrivēytaṟkuriya taṭṭi yuntāḷum pulluṅ kōriyum, taḻiyum, ōliyum, kūṭiyum peṭṭiyum, cuḷakum, muṟamum muṭitaṟkēṟṟa mūṅkilum paṉinārum pāyuṅ kaṭṭilum piṉṉaṟ kuriya kōriyum nārum, tirumukamum cuvaṭiyum varitaṟ kēṟṟa maṭalum ōliyum, icik karuvikaḷ ceytaṟkuriya maramun tōlum narampum, tīkkaṭi kōluṅ kaṭṭiyum, iṉṉō raṉṉa piṟavum, kumari nāṭṭiṟ pōl vēṟeṅkuṅ kiṭittirukka muṭiyātu.
3. nāṉila aṇmi
kuṟiñci nākarikattiṉiṉṟu mulli nākarikattiṟkum mulli nākarikattiṉiṉṟu maruta nākarikattiṟkum kalañcelutti āṟu kaṭattaliṉiṉṟu kaṭal kaṭattaṟkum, aṭuttaṭuttuc cellumāṟu nāṉilamum aṇittaṇittākat toṭarntamintiruntatu, kumari nāṭṭilēyē. innilimi iṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭilumuṇṭu. paḵṟuḷiyāṟum kumarimalit toṭarum kaṅkiyum, paṉi maliyum pōṉṟiruntaṉavē.
kumutam 
kumutam - kumuta (a.vē.)
kummutal = kuvital. kum - kumutam = katiravaṉik kaṇṭu kuvivatākac collappaṭum nīrppū.
ku + muta eṉṟu pakuttu, eṉṉa makiḻcciyuṟuttuvatu (‘exciting what joy’) eṉṟu vaṭavar poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvatu ettuṇi viyappāṉatu!
ku eṉpatu kutas (eṅkiruntu), kutra (eṅku), kuvit, kuha eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi viṉāc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭiyākak koḷḷap paṭṭuḷḷatu. mut (va.) = makiḻcci.  (va.va.)
kuyil 
kuyil - kōkila
kū (kūvu) - (kūyil) - kuyil. ma. kuyil, ka. kukil, te. kukkil, marā. kōyīla, va. kōkila.  (va.va.)
kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāy 
oruvaṉ viḷā marattiluḷḷa kuraṅkik kallāl eṟintāl atu avaṉit tiruppi eṟitaṟku vēṟoṉṟum illāmiyāl viḷaṅkāyālēyē eṟiyum. itu ēṟamuṭiyāta viḷā maratti luḷḷa kāyi valakkāramāyp peṟum vaḻiyām. iṅṅaṉam kuraṅku eṟiyum viḷaṅkāyē kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāy. kuraṅku tāṉāka viḷaṅkāy eṟiyāviṭṭālum, kalleṟintavaṉiṭam paḻi vāṅkuvataṟku eṟivatu pōla āciriyaṉ tāṉāka oṉṟic collāviṭṭālum māṇavar valakkāramāy viṉavum viṉā viṟku viṭiyiṟukkumpōtu taṉṉiyaṟiyāmalē atic colla nērntu viṭuvatu iyalpu. itiyaṟinta māṇavar immuṟiyik kiyāḷuvar. itaṉik kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāy eṉpatu ilakkaṇa āciriyar viḷakkam. (col. 14. )
kuralē caṭcam
iyaṟṟamiḻuṇarcci pōṉṟē icictamiḻuṇarcciyum mikku varum ikkālattil, icittamiḻip palarum palavāṟṟāl tattamak kicintavāṟu ārāyvatu takkatē. āṉāl, oru paṭattit talikīḻt tiruppip pārppatupōl, mutaṉūli, vaḻinūlākavum, vaḻinūli mutaṉūlākavum piṟaḻakkoṇṭu ārāycci naṭāttuvatu oru ciṟitum takkataṉṟu. muttamiḻil et tamiḻi ārāyiṉum muṉṉatāka iṉṟimiyātu aṟiya vēṇṭiya uṇmikaḷ mūṉṟuḷa. avi tamiḻum tamiḻarum tōṉṟiyatu kumarināṭeṉpatum, āriyar varumuṉṉarē tamiḻar pala tuṟiyi lum muḻunākarika maṭintiruntaṉa reṉpatum, tamiḻar allatu tiraviṭariṭattiṉiṉṟē āriyar nākarikatti yaṭintaṉareṉ patumām. āriyar varumuṉṉarē tamiḻ muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiyatāta liṉ, oruvar ettuṇik kalipayil teḷivum ārāycci vaṉmiyum vāyntava reṉiṉum, āriya vētaṅkaḷi ici mutaṉūlākak koṇṭu ārāyiṉ, viḷakkiṉṟip puttiruḷ vaḻi pōvār pōl iṭarppaṭuvārāvar. tamiḻk kalikaḷiyum nūlkaḷiyum vaṭamoḻiyil peyarkkum pōtu kuṟiyīṭu, pāku pāṭu, tokiyīṭu mutaliyavaṟṟi iyaṉṟavari māṟṟi vaḻi nūliyē mutaṉūlākak kāṭṭuvatu āriyar vaḻakkam.
tamiḻppoḻil 17ām tuṇar 4ām malariliruntu icit tamiḻippaṟṟi vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ eḻutivanta ārāyccip pakutikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟip paṭittup pārttēṉ. avaṟṟāl, av vārāycci mutalilēyē kōṇiyatāka eṉ ciṟṟaṟiviṟ keṭ ṭiṟṟu. uṭaṉē icittamiḻt tōṟṟatti yāṉē ārāyat toṭaṅkiṉēṉ. ataṉ palaṉākak kuralē caṭcam eṉat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, iti aṟiñar ārāyntu uṇmi kāṇu māṟu variyalāṉēṉ.
	kuralē caṭcam eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
1. taṉmata niṟuttal
(1) ēḻici variciyil kural mutaliṟ kūṟappaṭutal.
itukāṟum, et tamiḻnūlāyiṉum, kural tuttam kikkiḷi uḻi iḷi viḷari tāram eṉṟa variciyilēyē ēḻu curaṅkaḷum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
2. mattaḷattiṟku valakkaṇ kuraleṉṟal
 cintāmaṇiyil, 675ām ceyyuḷuriyil,
iṭakkaṇ iḷiyāy valakkaṇ kuralāy
naṭappatu tōliyaṟ karuvi yākum
eṉpatu mēṟkōḷ. taṇṇumi, muḻavu, mattaḷam, mirutaṅkam eṉappalavāṟu collappaṭum ōriṉat tōṟkaruvikaḷil, toṉṟutoṭṭu valakkaṇṇē caṭcamā yiruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
3.kurali mutaṟṟāṉa meṉṟal
11ām puṟappāṭṭil, kural puṇarcīr eṉṉun toṭarukku mutaṟṟāṉamākiya kuralilē vantu poruntum aḷaviyuṭiya pāṭṭi eṉpatu paḻiyavuri.
4. kēḷvic curam (curuti) iyalpāyc caṭcamāyiruttal
evar pāṭiṉum iyalpāyp pāṭumpōtu mattimamaṉṟic caṭcamē curutiyāyiruttaliṉ, ataṉiyē mutaṟ curamāka nam muṉṉōruṅ koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.
5. kural eṉṉuñ col toṇṭiyiyum atiṟ piṟakkum ōciyiyuṅ 
	kuṟittal
kul > kur > kural. kulavu = vaḷi. L. curuvs, E. curve. kuravi = vaṭṭamāka niṉṟu āṭuṅ kūttu. E.H.L. chorus, Gk. choros, orig. a dance in a ring. kural = vaḷinta tuḷi, tuḷiyuḷḷa toṇṭi, toṇṭiyiṟ piṟakkum ōci. o.nō : vaḷi = kuḻi. 
toṇṭi = toḷiyuḷḷatu. (toḷ + ti).
Dan. kroc, E. craw, the throat of fowls, Dan. kroe, Ger. kragen, Scot. crig, the neck, E. crop, the craw of a bird, A.S. crop, Dut. crop, a bird’s crop.
ellā ōciyuṅ kuraleṉappaṭumēṉum, iyalpāṉa kēḷvi yiciyē (curuti), kuraleṉṉun toṇṭip peyarāṟ kūṟap paṭutaṟkēṟṟal kāṇka.
6. kuralē caṭcamāka māperum pulavar āpirakām paṇṭitar koṇṭami.
icittamiḻk kaṭali (cākaratti)t taṉippaṭak kaṭintu karuṇāmirtam eṉṉum ariya amutiyeṭuttu nām uṇṇa vitta kālañ ceṉṟa irāvcākipu āpirakām paṇṭitar avar kaḷiṉ iciyārāycci nūlil, iraṇṭoru ciṟu carukkalka ḷiruppatākat teriyiṉum, kuraliciyip paṟṟiya koḷki valiyuṟuvatākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. 
2. piṟaṉmata maṟuttal
(vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ kural mattimam eṉṟu kūṟuvati maṟuppatu.)
1. kūṟṟu
ca ri ka ma pa ta ni eṉṉum ēḻu curaṅkaḷukkum poruntiya curutikaḷ 4, 3, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2 ākum
 kuraltuttam nāṉku kiḷimūṉ ṟiraṇṭāṅ
 kuriyā uḻiiḷi nāṉku - viriyā
 viḷariyeṉiṉ mūṉṟiraṇṭu tārameṉac coṉṉār
 kaḷaricēr kaṇṇuṟ ṟavar
eṉṟamiyiṉ, kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉṉum ēḻum iḷik kiramattilē, 4, 4, 3, 2, 4, 3, 2 eṉa alaku peṟṟu niṉṟaṉa. ivitamiyē iḷi mutalāka iḷi, viḷari, tāram, kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻiyeṉa niṟuttu 
miṭattu alakukaḷ 4, 3, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2 eṉa niral peṟutal kāṇka.
maṟuppu : mutalāvatu, alakukaḷē iṉṉum ittaṉi yeṉṟu tiṭṭamāka villi. alaku mottam 24 eṉṟum, 22 eṉṟum iruvēṟu koḷkikaḷ iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭāvatu, paṇṇukkēṟṟapaṭi alakup pakuppu vēṟupaṭuvatākat teriva tāl, oru paṇṇukku vakutta alaku muṟiyip potumip paṭuttuvatu poruntātu. mūṉṟāvatu, icittamiḻ mutaṉūlkaḷ aḻintapiṉ, āriya vaḻinūlkaḷit taḻuviyum tamiḻici nūlum nūṟpāvum tōṉṟiyirukkalāmātaliṉ, tamiḻiluḷḷa nūlel lām taṉittamiḻk koḷkikaḷik kūṟupaviyē yeṉṟu koḷvataṟkum iṭaṉiṉṟu.
2. kūṟṟu
 (vaṭanūl) 		(tamiḻnūl)
nārata		caṅkīta	piṅka			tivākaramum
ciṭci 		rattiṉākaram 	lanti 			cūṭāmaṇiyum
ca	-	mayil 	mayil 	kural ma 	vaṇṭu vaṇṭu
ri 	- 	pacu 	cātakam 	tuttam pa 	kiḷi 	kiḷi
ka 	- 	āṭu 	āṭu 	kikkiḷita kutiri 									 kutiri
ma 	- 	kokku 	kireñcam	uḻini	yāṉi 
		yāṉi
pa 	- 	kuyil 	kuyil 	iḷi ca 	kuyil tavaḷi
ta 	- 	kutiri 	kutiri 	viḷari 	ri pacu 	pacu
ni 	- 	yāṉi 	yāṉi 	tāram 	ka āṭu āṭu
iruṣapam (viḷari), pacu, kāntāram (tāram), āṭu, tivatam (kikkiḷi), kutiri, niṣātam (uḻi), yāṉi eṉpaṉa irumoḻi marapukkum ottuniṉṟaṉa.
maṟuppu : mutaṉmutal yāḻiṟ pala narampukaḷik kaṭṭi mīṭṭiya pōtu, kīḻk kuralōṭotta mēṟkuraliyum avaṟṟuk kiṭiyi luḷḷa piṟa curaṅkaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭu ēḻicikaḷi yaṟintārkaḷē yoḻiya, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu mayil pacu mutaliya paṟavi vilaṅku kaḷiṉ ōcikaḷi oruṅkēyō taṉit taṉiyō kēṭṭaṟiya villi. ēḻicikaḷip puḷḷoṭum vilaṅkoṭum icittuk kūṟuvatu, puḷnūliyum kuṟinūliyum oṭṭiya kaṟpaṉik koḷkiyākavum irukkalām. ivvāṟē, piṟkālattārum, eḻutīvu, eḻumuṉivar, eḻumarapu, ēḻāṟu, eḻutēvati, eḻuniṟam mutaliyavāka ēḻeṉṉun tokipeṟṟa poruḷkaḷō ṭel lām tattam viruppiṟkum uṉṉattiṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu ēḻiciyi icittuk kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ikkūṟ ṟellām icinūlōṭu eḷḷaḷavum toṭarpuṟṟavalla.
kurali mattimamākak koṇṭu, irumoḻi nūlkaḷilum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷa puḷ vilaṅkukaḷi oppunōkkumiṭattum ri ka ta ni eṉṉum nāṟcuraṅkaṭ kuriyaviyē ottirukkiṉṟaṉa; ēṉi muccuraṅkaṭkum ovvavilli. kuralic caṭcamākak koṇṭu nōkkumiṭattum, irumoḻiyilum pañcamattiṟkuk kūṟiya paṟavi (kuyil) ottirukkiṉṟatu. iṉi,
caṅku kuyilmayil yāṉi puravi
ceṅkā laṉṉam kāṭiyivaṟ ṟōci
eṉṉuṅ kallāṭa mēṟkōḷil, vaṭanūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭāta mūṉ ṟum tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭāta nāṉkuṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl, puḷ vilaṅkuk kuṟippikkoṇṭu oru koḷkiyi niṟuva muṭiyāteṉpatu pōtarum.
3. kūṟṟu
kuralatu miṭaṟṟiṟ ṟutta nāviṉiṟ
kikkiḷi yaṇṇattiṟ ciratti ṉuḻiyē
iḷineṟ ṟiyiṉil viḷari neñciṉil
tāra nāciyivi tampiṟap peṉpa
eṉak kūṟiyiruppati vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷ kūṟum piṟappiṭaṅ kaḷōṭu oppavittu nōkkumiṭattu, kural tuttam kikkiḷi uḻi iḷi viḷari tāram eṉṉum ēḻum ma pa ta ni ca ri ka vām eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.
maṟuppu : mēṟkūṟiya nūṟpāvil, kuralukkuk kūṟiya iṭantavirap piṟavellām uṭalnūṟkum olinūṟkum ovvāveṉpatu, kaṇṭa allatu kēṭṭa maṭṭilēyē teḷḷatteḷivām.
iṉi, muṟkāla yāḻellām orutoki narampiṉavum oru tiṟattavumalla. cilavum palavumāṉa narampukaḷik koṇṭa pala yāḻkaḷil, cila kuralilum cila uḻiyilum cila iḷiyilum cila tārattilum toṭaṅkiṉa. uḻiyil (mattimattil) toṭaṅkiya kōviyic cila paṇṇukkuk kuraṉ mutalatākak koṇṭu vācittaṉar. itaṉāl, orē kōvi (mēḷam) ici vakiyāl, oṉṟum iṭavakiyāl oṉṟumāka iruvēṟupaṭṭu, kuralukkuk kūṟiyati mattimattiṟkum mattimattiṟkuk kūṟiyatik kuralukku māka mayaṅkak koṇṭaṉar.
paṇṇukkēṟṟapaṭi entac curattiyuṅ kuralākak koṇṭu vācittati, kural kuralāyatu, tuttaṅ kuralāyatu, kikkiḷi kuralāyatu mutaliya toṭarkaḷāluṇarka.
4. kūṟṟu
iḷi narampiṟkup `paṭṭaṭi eṉa oru peyaruṇṭu, vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭi yāḻmēl vittāṅku (araṅkēṟṟu kāti, 63) eṉpataṟku aṭiyārkkunallār kūṟum uriyiṉi nōkkuka. `ellāp paṇṇiṟkum aṭimaṇiyātaliṉ iḷi ip peyar peṟṟatu. `ṣaṭjam eṉṉuñ collum ēṉi āṟu cuvaramum piṟattaṟku iṭamākiyatu eṉṉum poruḷiṉit tarum.
maṟuppu : ca - pa eṉṉum curappiṭippē paṇkaḷukkellām aṭippaṭiyātaliṉ, pañcam (iḷi) paṭṭaṭi yeṉṟum. vaṇṇappaṭṭaṭi yeṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭu vatāyiṟṟu. pala paṇṇuc cakkaraṅkaḷ ca - pa muṟiyil iyaṅkuvati aṭiyārkku nallā ruriyilum karuṇāmirta cākarattiluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
tārattuṭ ṭōṉṟum uḻiuḻi yuṭṭōṉṟum
ōruṅ kuralkurali luṭṭōṉṟic... cērum iḷi...
eṉṟatum im muṟipaṟṟiyē.
iṉi, itaṉ virivi, āpirakām paṇṭitaṉār makaṉār varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa icittamiḻ viḷakkattiṟ kaṇṭu teḷika.  (ce.ce. kaṭakam. 1943)

aṭikkaruttu (kūṭal)
kul - kulam = 1. kūṭṭam. pūmaṉivāy vāḻkiṉṟa puṭkulaṅkāḷ naḷa. 37), māḷikik kulantukittu (taṇikippu. cīpari. 373).   2. peṟṟōrum piḷḷikaḷum kūṭiya kuṭumpam. 3. marapuvaḻik kuṭumpam. kulantāṅku cātikaṇāliṉum (tiv. tiruvāy. 3:7:9), maliyuṟi kaṭavuḷ kulamutal vaḻutti (aiṅ, 259). 4. uyarmarapu vaḻik kuṭumpam. kulattiṟ piṟantārvāyc col (kuṟaḷ. 959), 5. uṟaviṉar tokuti. kulattik keṭukkuṅ kōṭarik kāmpu. 6. nāṭu moḻi toḻil matam paṟṟiya vakuppu (Community) allatu kuṭumpam. 7. āriyar vakutta piṟavi vakuppu (cāti). kulam kuppiyilē, paṇam pantiyilē. 8. pāl (varuṇam). kulam nāṉkākap pakutta vakiyāl terinta antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷareṉac collappaṭṭārirukkum nāṉkāy vēṟu paṭṭa terukkaḷum (cilap. 14 : 212, aṭiyārkku nallāruri).
9. iṉam (Species). oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum (tiruman. 2104). 10. koṅku vēḷāḷar kulappirivu. tūraṉkulam. 11. kuṭumpam kuṭiyirukkum vīṭu (piṅ.). 12. aracaṉ vīṭākiya araṇmaṉi (tivā.). 13. iṟivaṉ vīṭu pōṉṟa kōyil. tēvakulamun teṟṟiyum paḷḷiyum (maṇi. 26 : 72). kulam - kulaṉ.

 kaḷ - kaḷam = 1. kūṭṭam. 2. kūṭumiṭam. 3. neṟkaḷam. kāva luḻavar kaḷattakattup pōrēṟi (muttoḷ. 65). 4. pōrkkaḷam. īrim patiṉmarum porutukaḷat toḻiya (puṟanā. 2 : 15). 5. avi. 6. iṭam (tivā.).

kaḷaku - kaḻaku = kaḻakam. kaḻaku - kaḻakam = 1. pēravi. kaḻakamēṟēl nampī (tiv. tiruvāy. 6 : 2 : 6). 2. pulavaravi (tivā.). 3. kalvi payilum iṭam. kantaṉi yaṉiyavar kali teri kaḻakam (kamparā. nāṭṭup. 48). 4. paṭikkalam maṟpōr mutaliyaṉa payilum iṭam. (tivā.) 5. kavaṟāṭum iṭam. kaḻakattuk kāli pukiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 937). ma. kaḻakam.
kaḻakam eṉṉuñ col, iyalpāka ōr aviyi allatu avikkaḷattik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟi, kavaṟāṭum araṅkik kuṟikkātu. kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamōṭamarntu (tiruviḷi. tirunāṭ. 56), nāṭṭāṇmik kaḻakam, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. tirukkuṟaḷiṟ cūtu eṉṉum atikārattilēyē, kaḻakam eṉṉum col cūtāṭaraṅkik kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu. kala - kaḷa kaḷaku - kaḻaku - kaḻakam.
kaḷ - kaṭci = 1. pōrkkaḷam. kaṭciyuṅ karantiyum pāḻpaṭa (cilap. 12. urip. 25). 2. oru koḷki paṟṟiya kūṭṭam. 3. mara maṭarnta kāṭu. kalava maññi kaṭciyiṟṟaḷariṉum (malipaṭu. 235).
kaḷ - kaṇ. kaṇṇutal = poruntutal, puṭikaṇṇiya voḷi rāḻiyiṉ (iraku. yāka. 17).
kaṇ - kaṇa, kaṇattal = kūṭutal. kaṇa - kaṇam = 1. kūṭṭam kaṇaṅ koṇṭu cuṟṟattār kalleṉ ṟalaṟa (nālaṭi. 25), māṉkaṇam maṟalā (cilap. 13 : 6). 2. paṭippakuti. kaṇam - va. kaṇa (gana).

(ciṟupañ. 31).

kaḷ - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭutal = cērttal, poruttutal, puṉital, piṇittal, pūṭṭutal, uṭuttal, toṭuttal, amittal, tiraḷtal, neṉmūṭṭi kaṭṭi araciṟiyākac celuttutal. tāli pūṭṭi maṇattal, celavoṭu varavu poruntutal, ottal, poyyākap puṉital, mūṭutal, taṭuttal, aṭakkutal.
k., bj., f., tu. kaṭṭu.
kaṭṭu = 1. piṇippu. kaṭṭaviḻtār vāṭkaliyaṉ (aṣṭap. nūṟṟeṭ. kāppu). 2. malappiṇippu. kaṭṭaṟut teṉiyāṇṭu (tiruvāca. 5 : 49). 3. mūṭṭi. 4. kaṭṭaṭam (piṅ.). 5. kaṭṭuppāṭu. 6. kaṭṭaḷi. kaṭṭuṭik kāvaliṟkāmar kaṉṉiyē (cīvaka. 98). 7. kāval. matuvaṉattik kaṭṭaḻittiṭṭatu (kamparā. tiruvaṭi. 18). 8. araṇ. kaṭṭavi mūṉṟu meritta pirāṉ (tēvā. 386:7). 9. matippu. kaṭṭōṭu pōṉāṟ kaṉattōṭu varalām 10. nellik kaṭṭik kūṟaṅ kuṟi. kaṭṭiṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉum (tol. po. 115). 11. poyyuri. karumaṅ kaṭṭaḷiyeṉṟal kaṭṭatō (kamparā. kiṭ. araci. 16).
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭam = 1. kavaṟāṭṭattiṟku variyum aṟikaḷ. (peruṅ. makata. 14 : 56). 2. kāṭu (tivā.). 3. mōvāyk kaṭṭi (piṅ.). te. kaṭṭamu (gaddamu).
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭakam = kāntakkal. nalamali kaṭṭakam (ñāṉā. 55:13).

kaṭṭu - kaṭṭaṇam = 1. pāṭi, 2. celuttum paṇam. kaṭṭaṇam ka. kaṭṭaṇa.
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭaḷi = 1. ceṅkal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ accu. kaṭṭaḷi kōṭit tiriyiṉ (aṟaneṟi. 56). 2. uruvaṅkaḷ vārkkuṅ karuvi. 3. oṉṟupōl amitta uruvam. kāṭṭi vittatōr kaṭṭaḷi pōla (peruṅ. uñcik. 33 : 113). 4. uvami (piṅ.). 5. urikal, cālpiṟkuk kaṭṭaḷi yāteṉiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 986). 6. oḻuṅku. 7. kaṭṭuppāṭu. 8. camaya neṟimuṟikaḷi yuṇarttum nūl. 9. kōyiliṟ ciṟappāka ōr aṟañceytaṟkuc ceyyappaṭum ēṟpāṭu. 10. (te. kaṭṭaṭa, ka. kaṭṭaḷē) atikāriyiṉ uttaravu allatu ēval.
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭāṭi = kuṟicolvōṉ.

kul - (kur) - kural = 1. cērkki. kuralami yoru kāḻ (kalit. 54:7). 2. kottu. kamaḻkuraṟ ṟuḻāy (patiṟṟup. 31: 8). 3. katir. varakiṉiruṅ kural (maturik. 272). 4. peṇṭir kūntal. nallār kuraṉāṟṟam (kalit. 88). 5. makaḷir kuḻal muṭikkum vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. (pu.ve. 9: 35. uri).
kur - kuru - (karu) - karuvi = tokuti. karuvi tokuti (tol. col. uri. 56). It. gruppo, F. groupe, E. group.
kur - kuṟi = avi. kuṟikūṭṭi (cōḻavami. 55). peruṅkuṟi = ūrppēravi. (S.I.I. iii, 175 : 22).
kuḷ - kuḻu = 1. T£l« (âth., piṅ.). 2. tokuti.
intaṉak kuḻuvi (ñāṉā. 63 : 11). kuḻu - kuḻūu. ma. kuḻu.

kuḻu - kuḻumpu = tiraḷ. kaḷiṟṟuk kuḻumpiṉ (maturik. 24).
kuḻumpu - kuṭumpu = 1. kāy kaṉikkuli (piṅ.). 2. paṇṭi ūravit tērtalukku vakutta ūrppakuti (ward).

kuḻu - keḻu. keḻutaki = naṭpurimi. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉoṭu keḻutaki vēṇṭi (maṇi. 25 : 14).
keḻutakimi = naṭpurimi. naṭpiṟ kuṟuppuk keḻutakimi (kuṟaḷ. 802).
keḻu - keḻi = naṭpu. oruvaṉ keḻiyiṉmi kēṭṭālaṟika (nāṉmaṇi. 63). ka. keḷe.
keḻu - keḻuvu. keḻuvutal = 1. poruntutal. maṅkiyik keḻu viṉa yōkiṉar (tēvā. 951 : 5). 2. niṟital. keḻuviya kātali yeṉṟu (taṇikippu. kaḷavu. 530). 3. paṟṟukkoḷḷutal (cūṭā.).
keḻuvu - naṭpu. kēḷir maṇaliṉ keḻuvu mituvō(paripā. 8: 63).
keḻuvu - keḻumu. keḻumutal = 1. kiṭṭutal. oṉṉātār paṭipukaḻumi (pu. ve. 3: 12, koḷu). 2. poruntutal tērō ṭat tukaḷ keḻumi (paṭṭiṉap. 47). 3. niṟital. keḻumi yeṅ kaṇumāyk kiḷaroḷic caṭiyaṉi (vaḷḷa. cattiyañāṉa. patiyiyal, 9).
kuḷ - kūḷ. kūḷutal = tiraḷutal. kūṇṭaṉa karikaḷ (iraku.mīṭci. 42).
kūḷam = palavakit tāḷum vikkōluṅ kalanta māṭṭuṇavu.
kūḷappaṭi = palavakiyiṉar kalanta kūṭṭuppaṭi, (īṭu. 6 : 6 : 1 aruka).
kūḷ - kūḷi = 1. kūṭṭam (piṅ.). 2. kuṭumpam (piṅ.).
kūḷiyaṉ = kūṭṭāḷi, naṇpaṉ (cūṭā.)
kūḷ = kūṭu. kūṭutal = oṉṟu cērtal, tiraḷutal, poruntutal, naṭpuṟutal, mikutal.
k., bj., f., tu. - kūṭu.
kūṭa = uṭaṉ. k., bj., f., tu. - kūṭa.
kūṭu - kūṭṭam = kūṭuki, tiraḷ, avi, iṉattār, naṭpiṉar.
k., T£l« bj., T£lK, f., tu. - kūṭṭa.

kūṭal = 1. kūṭutal. 2. poruntuki. 3. āṟu kaṭaloṭu kūṭumiṭam. maliyōtat tolikūṭal (paṭṭiṉap. 98). kūṭalūr (Cuddalore). mukkūṭal = mūvāṟukaḷ kūṭumiṭam. 4. talivaṉip pirinta talivi avaṉ varuṅ kuṟiyaṟiya maṇalil iḻikkum kūṭaṟcuḻi nīṭalan tuṟiyiṟ kūṭaliḻittatu (tirukkōvi. 186). 5. pulavar kaḻakam. 6. kaḻakamirunta maturi.  kūṭa ṉeṭuṅkoṭi yeḻavē (kalit. 31). 7 tōppu.  ceyalik kūṭalē (iraku. tēṉuvan. 70).

kūṭṭar = tōḻar, iṉattār, ma. kūṭṭar.
kūṭṭaravu = kūṭip paḻakutal. pōta mikkavar kūṭṭaravu (ñāṉavā. mumuṭ.25).
kūṭṭar = tōḻar, iṉattār, ma. kūṭṭāḷaṉ.
kūṭṭāḷi = naṇpaṉ, paṅkāḷi, ottavaṉ, ma. kūṭṭāḷi.
 kūṭṭuṟavu = kūṭiyuṟavāṭal, kūṭit toḻil ceytal. kūṭṭuṟavuk kaḻakam = (Co-operative Society).

koḷ - koḷḷi = 1. kūṭṭam. koḷḷiyiṟ palar kūṟalum ( kantapu. viṇkuṭi. 14). 2. mikuti. koḷḷimā matatta nālvāyk kuñcaram (pākava. 1 : 15 : 14).
koḷḷa = nirampa. uṇavu koḷḷak kiṭittatu. koḷ - kōḷ = kuli. ceḻuṅkōḷ vāḻi (puṟanā. 168 : 13). kōṭ ṭeṅkiṟ kulivāḻi (paṭṭiṉap. 16).

kōḷ - kōṭu - kōṭakam = pala teruk kūṭumiṭam (piṅ.). kōṭu - kōṭi. kōṭittal = kaṭṭutal. amittal. kaṭimaṇṭapa muṉ kōṭippa (kāñcippu. tirumaṇa. 4.).



	kiḷik karuttukkaḷ
1. oppumi
kaḷḷa, kaṭuppa eṉpavi tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa uvami yurupukaḷ. kaṇattal = kūṭutal, poruntutal, ottal. kuraṅku kaṇakka ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉpatu nelli vaṭṭāra ulakavaḻakku. kūṭṭu = oppumi. maṭaṅkal kūṭṭaṟa veḻunteri vekuḷiyāṉ (kamparā. atikāya. 1). keḻuva eṉpatu ōr uvamiyurupu (tol. po. 286, uri) kuḻu - keḻu - kēḻ = oppu. kēḻēvariyu millōṉ (tirukkōvi. 269). koḷḷutal = ottal vaṇṭiṉam yāḻ koṇṭa koḷi (paripā. 11 : 125) iṉam iṉattōṭē, veḷḷāṭu taṉṉōṭē eṉṉum muṟimi paṟṟik kūṭutaṟ karuttil oppumik karuttup piṟantatu.
2. paṉmi
paṉmi palporuṭ kūṭṭamātalāl, kūṭutalik kuṟikkum kaḷḷutal eṉṉum viṉic colliṉiṉṟu kaḷa eṉṉum paṉmiyurupu amikkappaṭṭatu.
e-ṭu : makkaḷ (maka + kaḷ), maraṅkaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ; yāṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ.
3. niṟivu
paṉmiyāl niṟivuṇṭākum. kaḻumutal = niṟital. keḻuvutal = niṟital.
4. mikuti
 aḷaviṟku miñciṉa paṉmiyē mikuti. kūṭutal = mikutal. kaḻumutal = mikutal.
 o.nō : nirampa = niṟiya, mika.
5. kōtu
oru nīrp poruṭ kalattil kacaṇṭu aṭiyil taṅkic ceṟivata ṉālum, kaḻiporuḷāṉa kuppiyiṟ palavakip poruḷ kalappatālum, ceṟital allatu kalattaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kaḻi poruṭ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. kūḷam = tippi, maṇṭi (dreg = sediment) o.nō : maṇṭu - maṇṭi, maṇṭutal = ceṟital. kūḷaṉ = payaṉaṟṟavaṉ. uḻi pēṇāk kūḷaṉ (tiruppukaḻ, 109) kūḷi = kuṟṟam (cūṭā.) kuppi kūḷam eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
6. kaṇakku
kūṭṭal kaḻittal perukkal vakuttal eṉṉum nālvakik kaṇakkuḷ, mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyatu kūṭṭaṟ kaṇakkē.
kūṭu - kūṭṭal. kaṇattal = kūṭutal. kaṇa - kaṇakku = kūṭṭu, kūṭṭuttoki, motta aḷavu, aḷavu, varampu, kūṭṭaṟ kaṇakku, kaṇakku. kaṇakku - kaṇakkaṉ, kaṇakkappiḷḷi.
7. puṇarcci
kātalaṉ kātaliyar meyyuṟak kūṭal puṇarcci. peritāṟṟip peṭpak kalattal (kuṟaḷ. 1276). kalaviyāṉ makiḻntāḷ pōl (cilap. 7: 24, kaṭṭuri). kūṭaliṟ kāṇappaṭum (kuṟaḷ. 1327). kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅkāli (tol. po. kaḷa. 1.). kaṇavaṉ = kūṭuvōṉ. kaṭuvaṉ = āṇkuraṅku.
8. poruttu
kūḷi = poli kāḷi. uṟuppukkaḷ allatu avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kūṭuvatu poruttākum. kalavu = uṭaliṉ mūṭṭuvāy. kalavukkaḻi kaṭumuṭi (akanā. 3).
kaḷ - kaḷam = uṭal muṇṭattit taliyoṭu poruttum uṟuppākiya 1. kaḻuttu. 2. toṇṭi. pāṭukaḷa makaḷirum (cilap. 6 : 157). kaḷ - (kaṇṭu) - kaṇṭam = kaḻuttu, toṇṭi. kārāruṅ kaṇṭaṉi (tēvā. 1071 : 1).
kaḷam - va. gala. L. collum. kaṇṭam va. kaṇṭa (kantha.)
kaṇ - kaṇu. kaṭṭu - kaṭṭāṇi = aṇikaḷiṉ kaṭippūṭṭāṇi. kuyil - kayil = pūṇkaṭippuṇarvu (tivā.). kayil - kayiṟu = kaṭṭuvatu. ma. kayaṟu.
9. neyavu
neṭukkiḻiyuṭaṉ ūṭiyip poruttip piṉṉuvatu neyavu. kuyilutal = 1. neytal. ūciyoṭu kuyiṉṟa tūcum paṭṭum (tol. col. 74, uri). 2. piṉṉutal. kuyil - kuyiṉar = tiyaṟ kārar (piṅ). kōṭi - kōṭikam = āṭi. kōṭikar = āṭi neyvōr (peruṅ. ilāvāṇa. 8 : 67).
10. pōr
māṟupaṭṭa iruvar allatu iru kūṭṭattār kalantē pōr ceyvar. kikalattal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. kala - kalaku = kalakam. kalakittil = kalakañceytal, kalakam - kalām. kalāyttal = ciṉattal, māṟupaṭutal. kalāvutal = ciṉattal kalāvu - kalāvam - kalāpam = kalakam, aitar kalāpam. kalāpittal - kalakañceytal. kalāpi - kalāpiṉi - te. kalāpaṉa. kūṭṭam = pōr (tivā).
11. kalaṅkal
nīrum maṇṇuṅ kalattal kalaṅkal. kala - kalaku - kalaṅku - kalaṅkal. kala - kaluḻ. kaluḻtal = kalaṅkal. kaluḻ = nīrkkalakkam. kaluḻi = kalaṅkaṉīr. kaluḻ - kaliḻi = kalaṅkaṉīr. kaluḻi - kaliḻi. kaluḻ - kaluḻam - kaluṭam - va. kaluṭi.
12. kalakkam
oru vaḻiyōṭu piṟavaḻikaḷ kalakkumpōtum, amitiyāṉa maṉattiṟ tuyar kalakkumpōtum, oru ceyti paṟṟip pala karuttukkaḷ eḻumpōtum maṉaṅ kalaṅkum. kalaṅku - kalakku kalakkam. kalakku (piṟaviṉi) - ma. kalakku, ka. kalakku, te. kalatcu. kalakku (peyar) - ka. kalakku, te. kalakkuva. kalakkam (toḻiṟ peyar) - ma. kalakkam, te. kalakka.
kaluḻtal = maṉaṅ kalaṅkutal. kalavarittal = kalaṅkutal. kalati, kalavaram, kalavari eṉpaṉa kalakkaṅ kuṟippaṉa. kalavaram - te. kalavaramu, ka. kalavaḷa. kaḻumutal = mayaṅkutal. kuḻamputal = kalaṅkutal. ma. kuḻampu. kuḻampu - kuḻappu - kuḻappam = tāṟumāṟu, amitiyiṉmi. kōḻampam = kuḻappam.
13. tiraṭci
palaporuḷkaḷ oṉṟākac cēriṉ tiraṭciyuṇṭākumātalāl, kūṭutaṟ karuttil tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟum. kuḷ - kuḷampu = vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tiraṇṭa kālukir. kuḻu - kuḻi = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi. kuḻu - kaḻu - kaḻuku = perumpaṟavi. kaḻu muraku = mikap peritu. kuḷ - kuṇṭu - kaṇṭu = kaṭṭi, nūṟṟiraḷi. kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam = periya tuṇṭu. kaṇikkāl = tiraṇṭa keṇṭikkāl. kaṇi = tiraṇṭa piṭi. kaṇiyam = tiraṇṭa eḻu. kuṟaṭu = tiṇṇi. kuṇṭi (kāḷi), kuṇṭāntaṭi, kuṇṭaṭiyaṉ (āṇciviṅki), kuṇṭukkaḻuti eṉpaṉavum tiraṭci paṟṟiyaṉavē.
14. parumi
 tiraṭciyāṟ parumiyuṇṭām.
 kuru = parumi. kurūuk kaḷiṟṟuk kuṟumpuṭittaliṉ (puṟanā. 97). kūḷi = peruṅkaḻuku. kūḷippaṉi = tāḷippaṉi.
15. kaḷam
tiraṭciyālum parumiyālum kaṉamuṇṭām. kuru = kaṉam. pacumaṭ kurūut tiraḷ (puṟanā. 32). (kul) - kal = kaṉamuḷḷatu. f., k., kal. bj., J., kallu.
16. perumi
paru - peru. parupporuṭ karuttāṉa perumi kaṉattiṉiṉṟu, nuṇporuṭ karuttāṉa perumi kaṉam tōṉṟum. kuru kurukkaḷ, kurucil - kuricil, kuravaṉ, kurumakaṉ, kaṉam poruntiya, kaṉavāṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷiyum vaḻakkukaḷiyum nōkkuka. kalanti = perumi.
ciṟukiḷik karuttukkaḷ
poruttaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu, kaṭṭutal, amittal, puṉital, celuttutal, aṇital, maṇattal mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum. tiraṭcik karutti ṉiṉṟu tiṇmi, uṟuti, tiṟami ākiya karuttukkaḷum tōṉṟum.
tiṇmi kuḻampu = tiṇṇiya cāṟu. kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi. kūḷiyar = paṭimaṟavar.
uṟuti kaṭṭu = uṟuti. kaṭṭi - keṭṭi (kaṭṭaḷi = timicu)
tiṟami keṭṭi - keṭṭikkāraṉ. kaṭṭāṇi = tiṟavōṉ. te. kaṭṭāṇi.
kuṟippu : - kum - kummal - kumiyal - kuviyal, kum - kumpu - kumpal, kumpu - kuppu - kuppal, kuppi, kumpu - kumpi mutaliya coṟkaḷ kuvitaṟ karuttōṭu kūṭaṟ karuttiyuṅ koṇṭaṉavēṉum, avi um eṉṉum uyirmutal vērc colliṉiṉṟu tirintaṉa vātaliṉ, iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭila.  (vē.ka.)
kul1 (tōṉṟaṟ karuttu vērccol)
tōṉṟaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu, iḷami, meṉmi, ciṟumi, kuṟumi mutaliya karuttukkaḷ kiḷikkum.
kul - kuṉ - kuṉṉi = ciṟupiḷḷi, ciṟṟuyiri, ciṟuporuḷ, naṉṉiyuṅ kuṉṉiyum eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu ulaka vaḻakku.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷaku = 1. iḷantaḻi. vāraṇamuṉ kuḷakarunti (kalit. 42). 2. vilaṅkuṇā. maṟikuḷa kuṇṭaṉṉa maṉṉā makiḻcci (nālaṭi. 17). ilinukar vilaṅku ṇāvē kuḷakeṉa viyampa lākum. (cūṭā. 6 : 32). 3. taḻiyuṭi. kuḷakaric cuṟṟiya kuṟamaṭantiyar (kācikaṇ. 44 : 16). 4. ilikkaṟi. (w).
kuḷaku - kuḷakaṉ = iḷiñaṉ. 
kuḷakaṉ vantuḻi (kantapu. kumāra. 1.).
kuḷa - kuḻa = iḷamiyāṉa. maḻavuṅ kuḻavum iḷamip poruḷa (tol. uri. 14). kuḻa - va. kuṭa (guda).
kuṭa eṉṉum vaṭa colliṉiṉṟu kuḻa eṉṉun teṉcol tirintuḷḷatākac ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutali talikīḻākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu.
kuḻa - kuḻavu = iḷami.
kuḻavu - kuḻavi = 1. iḷañcēy, kikkuḻanti. īṉṟa kuḻavi mukaṅ kaṇ ṭiraṅki (maṇi. 11 : 114). 2. yāṉi, āvu, erumi, kaṭami, mari, kuraṅku, mucu, ūkam mutaliya vilaṅkiṉ iḷamip peyar. (tol. marapiyal, 19 - 23). 3. ōraṟivuyiriṉ iḷamip peyar. vīḻil tāḻik kuḻavi (tol. marapiyal, 24, uri). 4. oru kuṟumip poruḷ muṉṉoṭṭu. kuḻavit tiṅkaḷ (tol. mara. 24, uri).
o.nō : E, cule, F. cule, L. culus, cula, culum. - dim suf. E. cule cle, cel etc.
kuḻa - kuḻantu - kuḻanti = 1. kippiḷḷi, kuḻantiyi yuyirtta malaṭikku (kamparā. urukkā. 65). 2. naṭi paḻakuñ ciṟupiḷḷi.3. iḷamparuvam. kuḻanti veṇmati (kamparā. ūrtēṭu. 209).
o.nō : OE. cild, E. child. c = k.
kuḻavu - kuḻaku = 1. kuḻanti. kuḻakeṉa veṭuttukantavumi (tiruppu. 106). 2. iḷamic celvi. kommik kuḻakāṭuṅ kōlavari mārpar (cīvaka. 2790). 3. aḻaku. koṉṟi cūṭik kuḻakāka.... viḷiyāṭum (tēvā. 468 : 7).
kuḻaku - kuḻakaṉ = 1. iḷiñaṉ. niṉmaṇak kuḻakaṉ (tiruviḷi. tirumaṇa. 44). 2. aḻakaṉ. koṭkap peyarkkuṅ kuḻakaṉ (tiruvāca. 3 : 12). 3. murukaṉ. (piṅ.). 4. piṟarkkiṇaṅkupavaṉ. kuravi kōtta kuḻakaṉi (tiv. tiruvāy. 3 : 6 : 3.).
kuḻamakaṉ = 1. iḷam paruvat talivaṉ. kuḻamakaṉik kali veṇpāk koṇṭu..... viḷaṅka vurit tāṅku (ilak. vi. 858). 2. peṇṭir kuḻamakaṉip pukaḻum paṉuval. (toṉ. 283). 3. marap pāvi. uttariyap paṭṭuṅ kuḻamakaṉṟaṉakku nalki (pārata. nirimī. 136).
kuḻa - kuḻi = taḷir. polaṅkuḻi yuḻiñi (puṟam. 50). ka. koḻacci.
kuḻa - kuḻali - kutali = 1. maḻalic col. tam makkaḷ maḻalic coṟ kēḷā tavar (kuṟaḷ. 66). 2. iṉiya moḻi. kutalic cev vāyk kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir (cilap. 30 : 114). 3. aṟivilāṉ, (tivā.).
o.nō : maḻa - maḻali - matali = kuḻanti, makaṉ, maḻali moḻi.
kuḻa - kuḻantu - koḻuntu = 1. iḷantaḷir. ēṟpa virīiya iliyuṅ koḻuntum (peruṅ. ilāvāṇa. 2 : 143). 2. iḷamiyāṉatu. piṟikkoḻuntu (niṭata. aṉ. tūtu. 12). 3. meṉmi. vāḻiyuṟṟa - kōtilā maṭalē pōlak koḻuntuḷa ceviya tāki (cūṭā. 12 : 150). 4. meṉmiyāṉa marukkoḻuntu. koḻuntu cevvanti (kantapu. intiraṉkayi. 42.). 5. paṭiyiṉ muṉṉaṇi. cēṉiyiṉ koḻuntupōyk koṭimatiṉ mitiliyi ṉelli kūṭiṟṟē (kamparā. eḻucci. 24). 6. kavarinuṉi. koḻuntuṭiya cāmari (kamparā. kōlaṅ. 25).
ma. koḻunnu.
koḻuntu - koḻuntāṭi = karumpiṉ nuṉittaḻi. kuḻa - kuta kata - katali - kacaḷi = keṇṭik kuñcu.
kul - kur - kuru. kuruttal = 1. tōṉṟutal. atiṉiṉṟu moru puruṭaṉ kuruttāṉ (vināyakapu. 72 : 4). 2. vērkkuru uṇṭātal.
kuru - kuruttu = 1. iḷantāḷ, iḷantōki, iḷavōli. 2. karumpunuṉi. kuruttiṟ karumputiṉṟaṟṟē (nālaṭi. 211). 3. kātuk kuruttu. 4. iḷami. 5. veṇmi. (piṅ.) 6. yāṉi maruppiṉ uṭpakuti. āḷi naṉmāṉ.... vēḻattu veṇkōṭu vāṅkik kuruttaruntum (akam. 381).
ma. kuruttu.
kuruttu - kuruntu = (piṅ.). 1. kuḻanti. 2. veṇkuruttu.
kuruntu - karuntu = marakkaṉṟu. (u.va.). kuru - kuruku. 1. paṉaṅkuruttu. kuruku paṟiyā nīḷirum paṉimici (paripā. 2 : 43). 2. iḷami (cūṭā.). 3. vilaṅkiṉ kuṭṭi, ciṅkak kuruku (tiv. tiruppā. 1, viyā.). 4. veṇmi. (tivā.).
veṇmik karuttu, paṉaṅkurutti ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu.
k.bj., tu. kuru.
kurukukkiḻaṅku = iḷaṅkiḻaṅku.
kuru - kurumpi = 1. teṉṉi paṉikaḷiṉ iḷaṅkāy. irum paṉiyiṉ kurumpinīrum (puṟam. 24 : 2). 2. iḷanīr. kuṉṟuṅ kurumpiyum veṟuttaniṉ ṉiḷamuli (kallā. 52 : 8)
ma. kurumpa. ka. kurumpe.
kuru - kuruḷ - kuruḷi = 1. kuḻanti. aruṭ kuruvāṅkuruḷi (ci.ci. parapak. pāyiram. 4). 2. nāy, paṉṟi, puli, muyal, nari mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ kuṭṭi.

3. pāmpiṉ kuṭṭi. ciṟuveḷ ḷaravi ṉavvarik kuruḷi (kuṟun. 119).
o.nō : kuṭṭi = ciṟupeṇ, E. girl, female child.
M.E. gurle, girle, gerle, perh. cogn. with LG. gor. child. - C.O.D.
kurumaṉ = oru cār vilaṅkiṉ iḷamip peyar.
kurumaṉ - kurumāṉ. kuṭṭikurumāṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
kuru - karu = 1. cūl. māṇiḻi makaḷir karuccitit tōrkkum (puṟam. 34). 2. muṭṭi. 3. muṭṭikkaru. puṟavukkaru vaṉṉa puṉpula varakiṉ (puṟam, 34). 4. piṟappu. karuvit tuṭippa (pirapuliṅ. kokki. 15). 5. uṭampu. karuvuḷ vīṟṟiruntu (tiv. tiruvāy. 5: 10: 8). 6. kuḻanti. cōrartaṅ karuvit taṅkaḷ karuveṉat tōḷi lēnti (pārata. niri. 116). 7. kuṭṭi. kācaṟik karuvum (cilap. 25: 52). 8. viṉimutaṟ karaṇiyam (nimitta kāraṇam) karuvā yulaki ṉukku (tiruvāca. 10 : 14). 9. accukkaru. tiruvuruviṉik karuviṉāṟ kaṇṭu (tiruviḷi. iraca. 9.). 10. naṭu. uḷḷūrk karuvelā muṭal (kamparā. kiṅkarar. 44). 11. uṭporuḷ. 12. aṭippaṭi. karuvō ṭivaraṭi..... kāṇāmiyiṉ (iraku. nakarap. 20). 13. nuṇ ṇaṇu. karuvaḷar vāṉattu (paripā. 2 : 5). 14. karupporuḷ. teyvamuṇāvē...... karuveṉa moḻipa (tol. akat. 18).
ma. karu. ka. karu.
karu = naṭu. o.nō: ME, E. core.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutali, karu eṉṉum tūya teṉcollik karuppam (garbha) eṉṉum vaṭacolli ṉiṉṟu tirittuḷḷatu. vaṭacollē teṉ colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
kuḷ - kuṭṭu - kuṭṭam = kuraṅkukkuṭṭi. taṅkuṭṭaṅkaḷi.... mantikaḷ kaṇvaḷarttum (tiv. periyāḻ. 3 : 5 : 7),
f., kuh¤., kuṭṭa (gudda).

ma. kuṭṭaṉ, ka. kuṭṭa (gudda).

5. karu allatu ciṉi.
e-ṭu : kuṭṭipaṭutal = ciṉippaṭutal.
ōraṟivuyirp peyariṉ iḷamipporuḷ muṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu : kuṭṭip piṭavam, kuṭṭiviḷā (piḷḷi viḷātti). 6. ciṟṟappaṉ ciṟṟaṉṉiyar peyar muṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu : kuṭṭiyappaṉ, kuṭṭiyāttāḷ.
kuḷ - kuy - kuyñcu - kuñcu = 1. paṟaviyiṉ iḷamip peyar.
e-ṭu: kōḻikkuñcu, puṟākkuñcu. kākkikkum taṉkuñcu poṉ kuñcu (paḻamoḻi). 2. mīṉ, eli, aṇil mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iḷamip peyar. 3. kuḻanti allatu ciṟuvaṉ āṇkuṟi. 4. ciṟiyatu.
ma. kuññu. ka. kūcu (kuḻanti).
kuñcu - kuñcāṉ = kuḻanti allatu ciṟuvaṉ āṇkuṟi.
kuñcu - kuñci = 1. paṟaviyiṉ iḷamippeyar. kariya kuñciyiṉ ṟāka mārpaci (ci.ci. para. māyāvātimaṟu. 1.). 2. ciṟṟappaṉ ciṟṟaṉṉiyar peyar muṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu : kuñciyappaṉ, kuñciyātti, kuñciyācci, kuñciyāy (yāḻp.) 3. kuḻanti allatu ciṟuvaṉ āṇkuṟi. 4. ciṟiyatu.
kuñcu - kuccu - koccu = ciṟiyatu.
koccup piyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ. ma. koccu.
koccu - koccaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ.

kuyam - kayam = 1. iḷami. (tivā.). 2. meṉmi (piṅ.).
kayaveṉ kiḷavi meṉmiyuñ ceyyum  (tol. uri. 24)

mukkāḻ kayantalit tāḻa (kalit. 86 : 2)

ciṟupiḷḷikaḷik kaṉṟu kayantali yeṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
ciṟumik karuttum kuṟumik karuttum:
kuñcuk kaṭakam = ciṟiya ōlippeṭṭi.
kuñcuc cippi = ciṟiya muttuc cippi. kuñcup peṭṭi = ciṟiya peṭṭi. (yāḻp.).
kuñcam = 1. kuṟaḷ. (tivā.). 2. kuṟaḷi. (tivā.). kupja eṉṉum vaṭacolli itaṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka tamiḻ akara mutaliyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟu. vaḷintatu eṉpatē vaṭacoṟ poruḷ. 3. kuṉṟimaṇi. (tivā.).
kuñcaṉ = kuṟaḷaṉ (piṅ.).
kuñci = kuṉṟimaṇi. kuñcip peṭṭi = ciṟupeṭṭi. kuñcik kūṭi = ciṟukūṭi (W.). kuñcu - koñcu - koñcam = ciṟitu. koñcantaṅ kiṉpan tantu (tiruppu. 609).
ma. koñcam, te. koñcemu, ka. koñca, tu. kontra.
koñcaṉ = pullaṉ (aṟpaṉ). vañcaṉ koñcaṉ (tiruppu. 609).
koccu = ciṟitu, ciṟiya.

kocuku - kotuku. kotukaṟāk kaṇṇi ṉōṉpikaḷ (tēvā. 381: 9).
ma. kotuku.
kuḷ - kuṇ - kuṇil = 1. kuṟuntaṭi. karumpu kuṇilā māṅkaṉi yutirkkum (aiṅkuṟu. 87). 2. paṟiyaṭikkuṅ kuccu. kuṇilpāy muraciṉiraṅku maruvi (puṟam. 143).
kutali - katali = ciṟukāy vāḻi. montaṉ vāḻikku etirāṉatu. neṭṭilip piṅkatali (tirukkāḷat. pu. tārukā. 15).
kuruvi = 1. ciṟu paṟavi vaki. kuruvi cērvari (cīvaka. 2237). 2. kuṉṟimaṇi. (mali.).
kuruvikkaṇ = 1. ciṟukaṇ. 2. ciṟutuḷi. kuruvittali = ciṟutali. kuruvit tēṅkāy = ciṟu tēṅkāy.
kuḷ - kuḷḷam = 1. kuṭṭi. 2. kuṟaḷ.

kuḷḷi - kūḻi = 1. kuṭṭi, kuṭṭiyāṉatu. nāy kūḻi vālāṟ kuḻikkiṉṟatu pōla (tiv. tiruvāy. 9 : 4 : 3). 2. matik kuṟivu . kūḻi māntartañ celkati (tēvā. 462 : 9). 3. kūḻit toṭi. īṟili kūḻi (kāriki. uṟup. 19). 4. kuḷḷap pāmpu.

kuḷ - kuḷku - kuṭku - kukku. kukkutal = uṭampik kuṟukki vittal. o.nō : veḷku - veṭku, cuḷ - cuḷku - cuṭku cukku. kukku - kukkal = ciṟunāy vaki. kukkalip piṭittu nāvik kūṇṭiṉi laṭittu vittu (vi. cin.).
te. kukka, va. kukkara.
kukkal - kukkaṉ = ciṟunāy vaki (tivā.).
kukkar = nāypō liḻintōr. kuṭimiyiṟ kaṭimip paṭṭa kukkaril (tiv. tirumāli. 39).
kuṭṭam = 1. kuṟumi. 2. ceyyuḷaṭik kuṟumi. kuṭṭamum nēraṭik kottaṉa veṉpa (tol. cey. 114).
kuṭṭāṉ = 1. ciṟu paṭappu. 2. ciṟiya ōlippeṭṭi. kuṭṭāṉ kaṭṭutal = paṟavi kottātapaṭiyum aṇil kaṭiyāta paṭiyum kāykaḷiṉmītu mūṭi kaṭṭutal.
kuṭṭāṉ - koṭṭāṉ = ciṟunārp peṭṭi allatu ōlip peṭṭi.
koṭṭāṉ + kāy + cil = koṭṭāṅ kāycci, koṭṭāṅ kacci. koṭṭāṉ peṭṭi pōṉṟa tēṅkāy ōṭṭut tuṇṭu.
kuṭṭi = ciṟiyatu. kuṭṭikkalakam, kuṭṭikkiḻaṅku, kuṭṭic cāttaṉ, kuṭṭiccuvar, kuṭṭit tiruvācakam, kuṭṭit tolkāppiyam, kuṭṭippal, kuṭṭimaṇiyam, kuṭṭiyāṇṭavaṉ, kuṭṭiviral, kuṭṭit teyvam (cāmi) eṉpaṉa ciṟumi kuṟittaṉa.
 kuṭṭu - kuṭṭi = 1. kuṟukiya uruvam. 2. kuṭṭēṟu (ciṟiya kāḷi). kuṭṭēṟṟik kōvarttaṉaṉik kaṇṭīrē (tiv. nāyc. 14 : 2). 3. ciṟu tuṇi. e-ṭu : talikkuṭṭi, kikkuṭṭi. te. kuṭṭa (gudda). 4. ciṟukuḷam. 5. kuṭṭi maram (toḻumaram). 6. kuṟuṇi. (tol. col. 400, uri).
kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi = 1. uyarak kuṟivu. e-ṭu : āḷ kaṭṭi, kaṭṭimaṇ. 2. akalak kuṟivu. e-ṭu : akalak kaṭṭi. 3. nīḷak kuṟivu. e-ṭu : kaṭṭiviral, kaṭṭit tuṭippam, tuṭippak kaṭṭi. 4. matippuk kuṟivu. e-ṭu : vilikkaṭṭi, kaṭṭip poṉ. māṟṟuk kaṭṭi. 5. ōcik kuṟivu. e-ṭu : kaṭṭik kural (cārīram). 6. oruvaki icik kuṟṟam.
taṉipaṭu kaṭṭi yeṭṭiṉ ṟakutiyiṟ ṟikaḻum pollā
viṉimiyil curattik kaṇṭāṅ kiruntavar viyantā rilli	  (tiruvarala. 57 : 26)
7. tiṟakkuṟivu. e-ṭu : kaṭṭimati (putti).
itaṉ mūlatti aṟiyātu, marakkaṭṭi, viṟakukkaṭṭi, ceppukka ṭṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ taṭippattik kuṟikkum kaṭṭi yeṉṉum collōṭu iṇittuḷḷatu, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka tamiḻakaramutali.
 kuru - kuṟu = 1. ciṟu. 2. kuṟukiya. ma. kuṟu.
 kuṟu - kuṟṟi = ciṟu kuccu. kuṟṟi - kucci - kuccu.
 kuṟu + il = kuṟṟil - kuccil. o.nō:
muṟam + il = muṟṟil - muccil.
 kuccil - kuccu. kuccuvīṭu = ciṟu kūrivīṭu.
 kuṟu - kuṟṟu - kuṟṟam = kuṟivu, tavaṟu.
 kuṟu - kuṟi - kuṟivu, kuṟiccal.
 kuṟumakaṉ - kuṟumāṉ = ciṟuvaṉ. kuṟuñcirippu - kuñcirippu.

aṭikkaruttu (vaḷital)
vaḷitaṟ karuttu, iyaṉmuṟiyil vaḷivu, curuṭci, vaṭṭam, vaḷiyam, uruṇṭi, uruḷi mutaliya paṇpuk karuttukkaḷi yum; ceyaṉ muṟiyil vaḷital, curuḷtal, tirital, cūḻtal, cuṟṟutal, cuḻalutal, uruḷutal mutaliya viṉik karuttukkaḷi yum taḻuvum.
vaḷivu eṉṉum paṇpiṉ peyarum, vaḷital eṉṉum viṉiyiṉ peyarum, appaṇpiyum viṉiyiyuṅ koṇṭa poruḷkaḷi ākupeyarākak kuṟikkum.
kōṇal, cāytal eṉpaṉa vaḷitaliṉ muntiya nilikaḷāta liṉ, aviyum ataṉuḷ aṭaṅkum.

kiḷik karuttukkaḷ
 1.	tiraṭci. kōl = tiraṇṭa kampu. kōl - kāl = marattūṇ, kaṟṟūṇ. nāṟkāṟpantal eṉpatil marattūṇum, nūṟṟuk kāl maṇṭapam eṉpatiṟ kaṟṟūṇum, kuṟikkappaṭutal kāṇka. uruṭcik karuttiṉiṉṟum tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. piṉvarupavi vaḷivu allatu tiripu eṉpati aṭimaṇi yākak koṇṭavi.
 2.	tīmi. e-ṭu : koṭuviṉi = tīviṉi.
 3. 	neṟitiṟampal. e-ṭu : koṭuṅkōl, koṭuntamiḻ.
 4. 	kaṭumi. e-ṭu : koṭumpaṉi - kaṭumpaṉi. koṭu - kaṭu - kaṭumi.
 5. 	neraṭu. koṭumaliyāḷam, kaṭunaṭi.
 6. 		koṭumi. koṭumpāṭu, koṭumpāvi. kōṭu = koṭumi, kōṭaṇi = koṭumi.
 7. 	maṉakkavali. kōṭṭaravu.
 8. 	mūḷikkēṭu. kiṟukku - kiṟukkaṉ. kōṭu (ta.vi.) - kōṭṭu (pi.vi.) - kōṭṭi = kiṟukku, kiṟukkaṉ. (aṟivu tirintavaṉ).
kiṟukku eṉpatu cuṟṟaṟ karuttiyum, kōṭṭi eṉpatu tiritaṟ karuttiyum aṭiyākak koṇṭaṉa.
ciṟukiḷik karuttukkaḷ
1.	kōl = aracu, āṭci. ceṅkōl = nērmiyāṭci. koṭuṅkōl = koṭiya āṭci.
2.	kāl = 1. tūṇpōl uṭampit tāṅkum uṟuppu. 2. uṭampiṟ kālpōl nāliloru pakuti. 3. kālpōl oṭuṅki nīṇṭu cellum ōṭunīrnili (vāykkāl, kālvāy.) 4. nīṇṭu iyaṅkum kāṟṟu. kāl - kāṟṟu. 5. eṉṟum nīṇṭu iyaṅkik koṇṭēyirukkum kālam. kāl - kāli, kālam. 6. kāl pōṉṟa uṟuppu. (mukkāli, nāṟkāli). 7. pirivu. 8. aṭinilam (koṭikkāl, nāṟṟaṅkāl). 9. nīṇṭu toṭarum kuṭimarapu. iviyum ivi pōṉṟa piṟavum uvamiyaṭip paṭiyil tōṉṟiyaṉavākum.
 3.	koṭi eṉṉum coṟku mēṟkuṟitta patiṉāṟ poruḷum, kuṟikkap peṟāta piṟavum, ciṟu kiḷikkaruttuc coṟkaḷē.
kul 3 (kuttaṟ karuttu vēr)
kul
kul - kuḷ. kuḷḷutal = kiḷḷutal (nelli nāṭṭu vaḻakku).
kuḷ - kuḷavi = koṭṭun tēṉī.
ma. kuḷavi, ka. koḷavi.
kuḷ - kaḷ = muḷ. kaḷ - kaḷḷi = muṭceṭi. o. nō :
muḷ - muḷḷi. kaḷḷiyaṅ kaṭattiṭi (aiṅ. 323).
k., ka. kaḷḷi. kaḷ + maṇi = kaṇmaṇi = muṭpōṟ kūrmuṉi yuḷḷa uruttirākkam.
muṭpōṉṟa muṉiyuṇmiyāl uruttirākkam muṇmaṇi yeṉṟum peyar peṟum.
muṇmaṇikaḷ kāykkumaram muppatuṭa ṉeṭṭē eṉṟu virut tācalap purāṇam (uruttirāk-4) kūṟutal kāṇka. palākkāyiṉ mēṟ kāṇappaṭum muṉikaḷum muḷḷeṉṟu peyar peṟ ṟiruttali nōkkuka. āriyar teṉṉāṭu varumuṉṉarē civaneṟi kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya teṉmatamā talāl, ati āriyap paṭuttiya vaṭavar kaṇmaṇi yeṉṉuṅ kūṭṭuc colliṉ poruḷip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, ataṟkēṟpa, muḷḷuḷḷa teṉṟē poruḷpaṭum akkam (akku - muḷ, muḷḷuḷḷa uruttirākkam. akku - akkam) eṉṉum teṉ colli akaṭi eṉṟu tirittu, uruttiraṉ kaṇṇīril tōṉṟiyateṉṟu muppurerippuk katiyoṉṟuṅ kaṭṭi viṭṭaṉar.
kaḷ - kaṭu = 1. muḷ (tivā.). 2. muḷḷi (mali).
ma. kaṭu. kaṭu - kaṭi = kūrmi. kaṭiyeṉ kiḷavi kāppē kūrmi (naṉ. 457).
kaḷ - kaṇṭu = muḷḷuḷḷa kaṇṭaṅkattari.
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi = 1. muḷḷuḷḷa uruttirākkam, 2. uruttirākka māli.
kaṇṭiyiṟ paṭṭa kaḻuttuṭiyīr  (tēvā. 586 : 6)
kaṇṭi - kaṇṭiki = uruttirākkamāli (piṅ.). kaṇṭiki kaṇṭikā (va).
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam = 1. kaḷḷi (mali). 2. eḻuttāṇi (piṅ.). 3. kaṇṭaṅkattari. uruttirākkamaṇi. kaṇṭaṅkattiri - kaṇṭakāri (va). kaṇṭam (muḷ) - kāṇṭā (inti).
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭal = 1. muḷḷi (cūṭā). 2. nīrmuḷḷi (mali). 3. muḷḷuḷḷa tāḻi. kaṇṭal tiri yalikkuṅ kāṉal (nālaṭi. 194).
kaṇṭal - kaṇṭalam = 1. muḷḷi, 2. nīr muḷḷi (mū.a.).
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭakam = 1. muḷ. iḷaṅkaṇṭakam viṭanākattiṉ nāvokkum (iṟi. 41 : 172). 2. nīrmuḷḷi. kaṇṭakaṅkāḷ muṇṭakaṅkāḷ (tēvā. 268 : 2). 3. uṭivāḷ. (tivā.). 4. vāḷ (cūṭā).
kaṇṭaka caṅkam = muṭcaṅku. kaṇṭakam - kaṇṭaka (va).
kaṇṭakam - kaṇṭaki = 1. tāḻi. vemmiṉatu kaṇṭa viyaṉ kaṇṭaki yeṉavum (kantapu. tēvarpula. 20). 2. ilanti. (ilak. aka.). 3. oruvaki mūṅkil (ilak. aka.) 4. mutukelumpu.
kuḷ - kiḷ, kiḷḷutal = muḷḷutal. kiḷ - kiḷḷi - kiḷi = kūriya alakiṉāṟ kottum allatu kaṭikkum paṟavi vaki.

kuṭṭu - kuttu. o.nō : muṭṭu - muttu.
kuttutal = 1. muṭṭiyāṟ kuttutal. kuttumōti (kamparā. kiṭkintā. vālivatip. 43). 2. kompiṉāl muṭṭutal. 3. ūci, āṇi mutaliyavaṟṟāl tuḷiyiṭutal. uṉṉik kātu kutta (tiv. periyāḻ. 2 : 3 : 1). 4. kattiyāṟ kuttutal. 5. puḷḷi kuttutal. 6. muttiriyaṭittal. 7. ulakkiyāl iṭittal. 8. kuttit tit tal. pūmpuṉal nuḻiyum puriyak kutti (peruṅ. vattava. 12:48). 9. kuttip paṟittal. 10. kuttik kaḷital. 11. paṟavi kottutal. 12. kottit tiṉṉutal. kāṉak kōḻi katir kutta (poruta. 222). 13. ūṉṟutal pūntalik kuntaṅ kutti (mullip. 41). 14. ceṅkalic ceṅkuttāka vittal. kallik kuttik kaṭṭu (u.va.). 15. veḷḷam allatu pērali kariyil mōtutal. viyi kotittakaṉ kari kutti (tiruviḷi. viṭi. 10). 16. uṭampu allatu uṟuppu nōveṭuttal. talikkuttut tīrvucāttaṟku (tiruvaḷ. ve. mā.) kuttaluṅ kuṭiccalum (u.va.). 17. maṉam puṇpaṭac collutal. 18. kāycciya pālil uṟi mōr ūṟṟutal. piṟi kutta vēṇṭum (u.va.). 19. nilat tiṟ paṭintiruttal. 20. muṭṭut tāṅkutal. 21. ceṅkuttātal. 22. nērātal.
k., f., tu. kuttu. te. kuttu (paraṉaṉara).

kuti - kutir. kutirtal = paṭital, kuṭiyamartal, oḻuṅkātal. bj., ka. kuturu.
kutir - katir = 1. nērākac cellum oḷiyiḻi. viri katir ñāyiṟun tiṅkaḷum viḷaṅki (cilap. 11 : 43). 2. katir vīcum iruviṇcuṭar. katir vilakic cūḻum (cētupu. muttīr = 5). 3. kuṟaṭṭiṉiṉṟu (kuṭattiṉiṉṟu) katirpōṟ cellum ārakkāl. 4. oḷikkatirum ārakkālum pōl neṭukak kiḷikkum payirkkatir. 5. nērāka nūl nūṟkuṅkaruvi. “fânu kâahf” (e‹ - 24) k., ka. katir. te. katuru (nūṟkatir). katirttal = 1. viḷaṅkutal, oḷivīcutal. katirtta naki maṉṉum (tirukkō 369). 2. veḷippaṭutal. vāymi katirppac ceṉṟa (kamparan. 1) 3. mikutal. katirtta kaṟpiṉār.
kuti - kuti = kutiṅkāl pōṉṟa villiṉ aṭi. kutivaric cilinutal (kamparā. pāla. nakara. 49). k., kuta.
kutittal = viṟkutiyil nāṇ pūṇutal. kutikkiṉṟaṉa nimir veñcili (kamparā. uyut. irāvaṇaṉ va. 46).
kuttu - kutti - katti = kutti veṭṭum allatu aṟukkuṅ karuvi. kuttu - kattu = kuttiveṭṭu (iṟantu paṭṭa vaḻakku). tattu katti. k., bj., f., J., katti.
o.nō : ME - cutte, kitte, kette, Sw. kata (kuta). E. cut. inti, kāṭ (veṭṭu).
kuttukkōṭari = kuttiveṭṭuṅ kōṭari.
katti + ari = kattari. kattarittal = 1. ciṟitu ciṟitāy (koñcaṅ koñcamāy) veṭṭiyaṟuttal. (tivā.) 2. aṟuttal. tali pattuṅ kattarikka veytāṉ (kantaralaṅ. 22), 3. veṭṭi vilakkutal, naṭpuppirittal. 4. pirittu kavarātal.
kattari = kattarikkōl. kattari - kattiriki = kattarik kōl. mayiraritaṟkoru kattariki tarukeṉa (peruṅ. vattava. 14 : 7). kattari - karttarī (va.).
kuttu - kutti - katti - kati. katittal = nērātal. katikka = nērāka. kiḻakkē katikkap pō (nelli vaḻakku). ceṅkuttu allatu naṭṭukkuttuk karuttiṉiṉṟu nērātaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
kuttu - kuntu. kuntutal = 1. puṭṭam nilattiṟkutta uṭkārtal. 2. kuttutal. 3. paṭital.

L. cand, shine, be white. L. candela, cylinder of Spernaceti enclosing with, for giving light. O.E. candel, E. candle.
L. candescere - E. candescent = glowing with white heat.
L. candidus, white - E. candide - E. candid = frank.
L. candor. - E. candour = open - mindedness.
L. candidatus - E. candidate = Roman aspirant who was white-robed when canvassing votes.
Skt. chand, shine, chandra - noon that which shines.
kāy - kāyṅku - kāṅku - kāṅki = veppam.
ka. kāṅke, te. kākka (kaka).
kāṅku - kaṅku = aḻal tuṇṭu.
kuḷ-kuṇ-kuṇpu - kumpu. o.nō: caṇpu - campu, koṇpu - kompu.
kumputal = camikkum uṇavu tīyntu pōtal.
kumpal = kumpal nāṟṟam. kumpi vīcukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
kumpu - kumpi = 1. taḻal. 2. cuṭucāmpal. te. 3. eri narakam. kumpi kummunarakarkaḷ (tiv. tiruvāy. 3 : 7 : 8).
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa kumpī eṉṉuñ col, kumpattiyaṉṟi narakattik kuṟikkātu. ātalāl, kalattiṟ camittal allatu kalattic cuṭutal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum kumpīpāka eṉṉum toṭarccolli, kumpi (narakam) eṉṉum teṉ coṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉip palkalikaḻakattil tamiḻ akaramutali kāṭṭiyiruppatu. ettuṇik kayamiyāṉa tamiḻp pakimi eṉpati nōkkuka. tīyōrkkut tīyuḻi (narakam) kalañcuṭu cuḷḷiyum aṭukalamum pōṉṟirukkiṉṟatām!
kumpiyiṭucaṭṭi - kumpiṭucaṭṭi = 1. kaṇappuc caṭṭi. 2. taṭṭār neruppuc caṭṭi.
te. kumpaṭṭi, ka. kumpaṭṭe.
kumpināṟṟam = kumpal nāṟṟam.
kuṇ - kaṇ - kaṇa - kaṇam = kaṇinōy (piṅ).
kaṇakaṇattal = 1. erital. 2. cuṭutal.
kaṇakaṇaveṉṟu erikiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
kaṇa - kaṇappu = kuḷir kāyum tī. kaṇappuccaṭṭi = kuḷir kāyum neruppuc caṭṭi.
kaṇa - kaṇi = uṭampukkāṅki nōy. kaṇic cūṭu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
kaṇ - kaṭu. kaṭuttal = 1. uṟittal. 2. nōveṭuttal. 3. virital. kāleṉak kaṭukkuṅkaviṉpeṟu tērum. (maturik. 388). 4. mikutal. neñcaṅ kaṭuttatu (kuṟaḷ. 706). 5. ciṉattal. maṅkiyik kaṭuttu (aric. pu. nakarnī. 110). 6. veṟuttal. poṉpeya ruṭiyōṉ taṉpeyar kaṭuppa (kallā. 5).
ma. kaṭu.
kaṭu - kaṭuppu = 1. tēṭkoṭṭup pōṉṟa kūr nōvu. kaṭup puṭip paṟavic cātiyaṉṉa (perumpāṇ. 229). 2. vekuḷi. kaṭunavi yaṇaṅkuṅ kaṭuppum (paripā. 4 : 49). 3. vēkam. maṇṭu kaṭuppiṉiṟ paṭarum vāmpari (cētupu. katturu. 15.) 3. kaṭumpuḷippu. tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭutēṟal (puṟam. 392). 4. uṟippu - kāntal. kaṭuppuk kaḻiccal = vayiṟṟuḷiccal (cītapēti).
kaṭu - kaṭuku = kāramuḷḷatu. ma. kaṭuku.
kaṭukutal = 1. mikutal. paci kaṭukutalum 2. virital. kālvici kaṭukak kaṭalkalak kuṟutaliṉ (maṇi. 14 : 80).
kaṭuku - kaṭukam = 1. kārppu. (tivā.). 2. cukku miḷaku tippili eṉṉum mukkaṭukaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kaṭukam - va. kaṭuka (katuka). kaṭu kāṭu = mikuti. eṅkup pārttālum veḷḷakkāṭu (u.va.).
kaṭu - kaṭṭam = 1. kaṭumi. 2. mey varuttam. kaṭṭam - va. kaṣṭa. o.nō : naṭu - naṭṭam.
kaṣṭa eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkuk kaṣ eṉṉum viṉi mutaṉiliyi aiyaṟavu mūlamākak kuṟittuḷḷār māṉiyar villiyamcu. kaṣ eṉṉum viṉic coṟkut tēy eṉpatē poruḷ.
kaṭṭam - kāṭṭam = 1. erinōvu. 2. uṟippu. 3. kuṇavēkam. cāṟāyam mikak kāṭṭamāyirukkiṟatu. (u.va.).
te. kāṭṭu (g)
kaṭu - kaṭi = 1. viḷakkam. aruṅkaṭip peruṅkāli (puṟam. 166.) 2. ciṟappu. aruṅkaṭi māmali taḻīi (maturik. 301). 3. mikuti. kaṭimuraciyampak koṭṭi (cīvaka. 440). 4. virivu. emmampu kaṭiviṭutum (puṟam. 9). 5. uṟippu. kaṭimiḷaku tiṉṟa kallā manti (tol. col. 384, uri). 6. accam. aruṅkaṭi vēlaṉ (maturik. 611). 7. pēy. kaṭivaḻaṅ kāriṭi (maṇi. 9 : 49).

kari - kāram = uṟippu, mikuti.
palakāram = pala cuvi mikka ciṟṟuṇṭi.
kāram = 1. uṟippu. 2. kāravuppu. (w.) 3. cāmpaluppu. 4. vaṇṇāṉ kāram. 5. cāyakkāram. 6. cīṉikkāram. 7. veṇkāram. 8. akkara kāram. (tilava tila, 112). 9. kōḷaka nañcu. (caṅ. aka.). 10. tirunīṟu. kārameṉṟurippar (tirukkāḷat. pu. 26 : 4). 11. ciṉam avaṉ eṉmītu kāramāyirukkiṟāṉ. (u.va.).
ma. kāram, te. kāramu, ka, tu. kāra.
kāraccīli, kāraccēvu, kārattuḷi (pūnti), kārappaci, kārap poṭi, kāramaruntu mutaliyaṉa vēkattiyum uṟippi yumuṭiya poruṭpeyarkaḷ.
kāram = mikuti, kaṭumi, valimi, urimi, poṟuppurimi, viṉiyurimi, uṟavurimi, uṭimi.
kāram - kāraṉ = valiyaṉ, uriyaṉ, uṭiyāṉ. kāri (pe.pā.) vīṭṭukkāraṉ eṉpatu urimiyum, paṇakkāraṉ eṉpatu uṭimiyum, kāvaṟkāraṉ eṉpatu viṉiyuṭimiyum (kulappeyarāyiṉ viṉiyurimiyum), aṇṇaṉkāraṉ eṉpatu uṟavurimiyum, uṇarttum peyarkaḷām. tōṭṭakkāraṉ, vaṇṭikkāraṉ, māṭṭukkāraṉ eṉpaṉa, uriyavaṉik kuṟikkā viṭattup poṟuppurimikkāraṉik kuṟikkum. uriyavaṉ - contakkāraṉ.
kāraṉ kāriyīṟṟup peyarkaḷ nūṟṟukkaṇakkiṉa.
kāraṉ - va. kāra.
vaṭa moḻiyāḷar ivvīṟṟi vaṭacollākkuvataṟku, kru (cey) eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirittuc ceypavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar. āṭṭukkāraṉ, kōḻikkāraṉ, contak kāraṉ, taṇṇīrkkāraṉ, nāḻimaṇikkāraṉ, puḷḷikkāraṉ, muṭṭikkāraṉ, veḷḷikkāraṉ mutaliya peyarkaṭkuc ceypavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ poruntāmi kāṇka. vēlik kāraṉ, kūlikkāraṉ eṉpaṉavum, viṉiyuṭimiyē kuṟikkum.
ceyvāṉik kuṟikkum vaṭamoḻi yīṟukaḷ, kāra, kāru, kāri, kāriṉ (ceypavaṉ), kāriṇī (ceypavaḷ) eṉap pala vaṭivil niṟkum.
atikarittal = mikutal. atikari - atikāram = mikuti. valimi, urimi, nūṟperum pirivu. atikāram - atikāri, atikāraṉ. atikari eṉṉum viṉiccol vaṭamoḻiyil illi. atittal = mikutal. ati - ataṉam = mikuti. ic collum vaṭamoḻiyil illi. atikari eṉpatu oru mīmicic col.

aṭikkaruttukkaḷum kiḷik karuttukkaḷum
kul - kuli. kulittal = 1. kiḷaṟik kalittal. 2. kaṭṭuk kōppip pirittal. 3. pirittal allatu oṟṟumiyik keṭuttal. 4. tāṟu māṟākkutal. 5. aḻittal.
kuli - kali. kalital = pirital. kalittal = 1. kaṭṭuk kōppip pirittal. 2. kūṭṭattip pirittal. 3. pakivaric citittal. paṟṟalarik kali... vēntar (aṣṭap. tiruvaraṅ kattan. 25). 4. eṇṇatti allatu tiṭṭattik keṭuttal. irāvaṇaṉik kalikkiṉṟāṉē (irāmanā. uyut. 31). 5. palakiyil eḻutiyati aḻittal. ma. kala.
kul - kur - kural. o. nō : ul - ur - ural.
kural = 1. toṇṭik kuḻi (kuralvaḷi). 2. toṇṭi (miṭaṟu). maṇikkura laṟuttu (kuṟun. 263). 3. toṇṭiyil eḻum oli. 4. col. yāvarun taṇkural kēṭpa (kalit. 142 : 9) 5. pāṭum ici. kāmaṉ kāmuṟap paṭuṅkuraṟarumitu (cīvaka. 1218). 6. ēḻici yuḷ mutalāvatu. kural kuralākat taṟkiḻami tirintapiṉ (cilap. 3 : 85). 7. aḵṟiṇip pēccoli. cēval mayiṟ peṭikkup pēcuñ ciṟukural kēṭṭu (naḷaveṇ. cuyam. 41). 8. paṟiyōci. tāḻkuraṟ ṟaṇṇumi (cilap. 3 : 27).

tuṇiviṉikaḷ taṟporuṭṭut tuṇiviṉi
e-ṭu : mukam vaḻittuk koṇṭāṉ, rṇṭiyiṭṭukbkāṇṭaṉar.vâ®Kiw yēva lorumit tuṇiviṉi
e-ṭu : añcātē koḷ.
añcātē koṇmiṉ eṉṟu paṉmikkum payaṉpaṭutta ilakkaṇam iṭantarum.
iṇimoḻit tuṇiviṉi
ceya vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam :
e-ṭu : varak koḷḷa, pēcak koḷḷa nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam (ulaka vaḻakku) :
e-ṭu : vantukoṇṭu, pāṭikkoṇṭu.
kuttaṟ karuttu vērccol :
kuḷ - keḷ - keḷiṟu - keṭiṟu - keḷiṟṟi - koḷutti.  (vē.ka.)
kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya vakikaḷ
āriyar (pirāmaṇar) varumuṉ tōṉṟiya kulaṅkaḷellām toḻil paṟṟiyaṉavē yām.
(1) nāṉilat toḻil
marutam - uḻavar, mulli - āyar, kuṟiñci - vēṭṭuvar, neytal - mīṉ piṭiyar (nuḷiyar).
mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya kulaṅkaḷ in nālvakit toḻil vakuppukkaḷē. in nāṉkum uṇavu paṟṟiyaṉavē.
(2) aintām (niliyillā) nilat toḻil - kavarvu
mulliyum ciṟumalik kuṟiñciyum mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭataṉāl, aṅkuḷḷa vēṭṭuvar āṟalippōrum cūṟi koḷvōrumāka māṟi, ampeyvatāl eynar (-eyiṉar) eṉṟum, maṟamikiyāl maṟavar eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar.
(3) maruta nilattoḻil nāṉku
marutanila makkaḷ toki perukiya piṉ, paṇṭamāṟṟiṟku vaṇikarum, vaḻakkut tīrppiṟkum kāvaṟkum ūrk kiḻavarum (talivarum), nōy nīkkavum maḻipeyvikkavum teyvatti vēṇṭa uvaccaṉum (pūcāriyum), uḻavariṉiṉṟu pirintaṉar.
ūrk kiḻavarē aracarkulat toṭakkam. āṭcip parappu viriyaviriya, vēḷirum maṉṉarum kōkkaḷum vēntarum muṟiyē tōṉṟiṉar.
mutaṟkālattil, nāṉilattiṟkum potuvāṉa paṇṭamāṟṟu marutanilat tilēyē nikaḻntatu. piṉṉar nīrvāṇikam tōṉṟiya piṉ neytal nilattilum nikaḻntatu.
marutanilattiṟ pōṉṟē ēṉi nilaṅkaḷilum, kuṭiyirupput talivarum tēvarāḷarum (pūcārikaḷum) tōṉṟiṉar.
uvaccar vakuppiṉiṉṟē, nāḷaṭivil pulavarum āciriyarum tuṟaviyarum muṉivarum mutaṉmutalākat tōṉṟiṉar. kalvi, ulakiyalum mataviyalum eṉa iraṇṭākap pirintatu.
 (4) marutanilap pērūrp patiṉeṇ kittoḻilkaḷ
maruta nilap pērūrkaḷil, uḻaviṟkup pakkat tuṇiyākap pati ṉeṇ kittoḻilkaḷ paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉa. piṟanilak kuṭiyiruppukkaḷilum, avvannilattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu cila kit toḻilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
(5) paṟpala kittoḻilkaḷ
tiṇimayakkam ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, nāṉila makkaḷum maruta nila nakaraṅkaḷilum neytal nilap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷilum amarntu, palvēṟu karuvip poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu nākarika vāḻkkik kēṟṟa paṟpala toḻilkaḷic ceytu vantaṉar.
(6) toḻiṟ pirivukaḷ
nākarikamum makkaḷtokiyum mikuntapiṉ, ellāt toḻil kaḷum allatu vakuppukkaḷum iraṇṭum palavumākap pirintaṉa.
(7) peyar māṟṟaṅkaḷ
makkaḷ perunilap parappiṟ paṭarntu paraviṉapiṉ, orē vakup pārkkē iṭavēṟupāṭṭāl peyar vēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu.
toḻilkaḷ nikaḻum vakikaḷ
viḷippu, vaḷarppu, piṭippu, tokuppu, puṇarppu, ceyvu, piritteṭuppu, paṇṭamāṟṟu, nilimāṟṟu, kāppu (māntaṉ kāppu, teyvak kāppu), āḷvu, ceppaṉīṭu, vilakku, vāḻvippu, iṉpuṟuttam, āyvu, aṟivippu, uyppu, uṭaluḻippu, aḻippu mutaliyaṉa.
kulappirivukaḷ tōṉṟiya vakikaḷ
(1)	niṟam - veḷḷāḷaṉ (veṇkaḷamaṉ), kārāḷaṉ (karuṅkaḷamaṉ), iruḷaṉ.
(2) 	iṭam - cōḻiya vēḷāḷaṉ, ārkkāṭṭu mutali, maliyāḷi, tici - teṉṟici veḷḷāḷar, mēlnāṭṭāṉ.
(3)	kuṭiyiruppu - kōṭṭi veḷḷāḷaṉ, teruvāṉ (cēra nāṭṭuc cāliyaṉ).
(4) 	ūṇ - civa (marakkaṟi) veḷḷāḷaṉ, puliyaṉ, tavaḷi tiṉṉi (paṟiyaṉ).
(5) 	uṭi - taṇṭappuliyaṉ, kuḷicīliyāṇṭi (kōvaṇāṇṭi).
(6) 	aṇi - pavaḷaṅkaṭṭi (koṅku veḷḷāḷar).
(7)	tāli - ciṟutāli maṟavar, aintāli (añcāli) yiṭiyar. koṇṭimuṭippu - koṇṭi kaṭṭi vēḷāḷar.
(8)	kōlam - paccikutti (kuṟavar, vēḷāḷar), nīṟupūci (veḷ ḷāḷaṉ).
(9)	mutaṟkaruvi - poṟkollar, veṇkalakkaṉṉār.
(10)	karuvi - villi, valiyaṉ.
(11)	karuviyaḷavu - ciṟupāṇar, perumpāṇar, ciṉṉa mēḷam, periyamēḷam (mēḷakārar).
(12)	paṇṭamāṟṟup poruḷ - kūla vāṇikaṉ, ili vāṇiyaṉ, eṇṇey vāṇiyaṉ.
(13)	toḻiṟpirivu - tīkkollaṉ, irumpuk kollaṉ, kaṭiccaṟ kollaṉ.
 	toḻilnuṭpam - kaṇṇuḷar, kaṇikiyar.
 	toḻil vēṟupāṭu - kōliyaṉ.
 	teyvappaṟṟu - tēvakaṇikiyar (kōviṟ kaṇikiyar).
 	tiruttoṇṭuvaki - pūvāṇṭip paṇṭāram, uppāṇṭip paṇṭāram.
paṇpu - maṟavaṉ
aluval - kaṇakkaṉ.
kalvi - pulavaṉ, paṇṭāram, ōtuvāṉ.
muṉṉōr patavi - mutaliyār (paṭimutaliyār).
muṉṉōr paṭṭam - mutali, mutaliyār, piḷḷi.
muṉṉōr veṟṟi - kaḷam veṉṟār.
muṉṉōr iṭam - cempiyaṉ nāṭṭār.
muṉṉōr toḻil - ceṅkuntar.
muṉṉōr ceyal - mutukar (mutuvar).
muṉṉōr peyar - tiruvaḷḷuvaṉ (vaḷḷuvaṉ), vāṇar.
oḻukkam - parattiyar, tirumuṭik kavuṇṭar.
caṭaṅku - paṉṉiraṇṭunāḷ (paḷḷi).
vaḻakkam - pantal muṭṭip paḷḷi, kikāṭṭi (kaṇakkaṉ), turuvāḷar.
nikaḻcci - paṉṉiraṇṭām ceṭṭi.
kati - kārkāttār.
paṭṭam - uṭiyāṉ.
pirivuppeyar - illattuppiḷḷi.
muṟippeyar - ammāppaḷḷar, āttāppaḷḷar.
iṭamāṟṟamum muṉṉōr ceyalum - paḻiyar.
iṭamāṟṟamum peyar māṟṟamum - tēṉvaṉṉiyar (teṉṉārkkāṭṭu iruḷar).
iṭavēṟupāṭum peyar vēṟupāṭum - paravar, paṭṭaṇavar, cempaṭavar.
toki - āyira vaṇikar (viciyar), aiññūṟṟāṉ (pāṇaṉ).
ciṉṉam - āṉi, ānti, kāṭi (koṅku veḷḷāḷar).
vilaṅku pēṇvu - pākar, vātuvar (kutiri).
vilaṅku piṭippu - narikkuṟavaṉ.
vilaṅkuttuṇi - nāyāṭi (vēṭṭuvaṉ), erutāṇṭi.
kalappu - mēḷakkāraṉ (icivēḷāḷaṉ), iḷamakaṉ.
kiḷi - cavaḷakkāraṉ, kāvaṟkāraṉ.
tokuti - mukkulattār, ampala(k)kāraṉ.
matam - civaṉiyar (civar), tirumāliyar (viṇavar).
 	matamāṟṟam - camaṇar, pavuttar, kiṟittavar, mukamatiyar (tulukkar).
matappirivu - paḻañcaviyar (Roman Catholic), cīrtiruttac caviyar (Protestant)
teyvam - aiyaṉār (kaḷḷar pirivu).
celvam - oṟṟiccekkāṉ (vāṇiyaṉ), iraṭṭiccekkāṉ (vāṇiyaṉ).
kaṭci - valaṅki, iṭaṅki (paṇiceyvōṉ).
iṭamāṟṟam - kuṉṉuvar, mutuvar, paccimaliyāḷiyar.
varici - kaṭiyar. aracaṉ peyar - toṇṭimāṉ.
irappu - nōkkaṉ, poṉṉampalattār, muṭavāṇṭi.

kaṉṉāṉ cempuk kālattilum iruppuk kollaṉ irumpuk kālattilumē tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
 tuṭiyaṉ pāṇaṉ paṟiyaṉ kaṭampaṉeṉ
 ṟinnāṉ kallatu kuṭiyu milli
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟuk kūṟṟu (335) oru kuṟippiṭṭa iṭam paṟṟiyatē yaṉṟip potuppaṭak kūṟiyataṉṟu.
kuṭimakkaḷ eṉṉuñ col, niliyāṉa kuṭikaḷākiya uḻavarkkup pala toḻiluñ ceytu makkaḷpōl utavupavar eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatu. kuṭimakkaḷ ellāruḷḷum vitappākak kuṭimakaṉ eṉṟu iṉṟuñ collappaṭuvaṉ muṭitiruttāḷaṉē. kuṭimakkaḷ palarum tam toḻiṟku allatu toṇṭiṟku uriya kūliyik kaḷattilum uḻavarmaṉiyilum vāṅki vantaṉar.
vayalilum vāykkālilum mīṉ piṭikkappaṭumēṉum, kaṭuṅ kōṭik kālattil āṟuṅ kuḷamum vaṟṟi viṭumātalālum, kaḷarnilattu uppiṉum kaṭaluppē ciṟantatākiyālum, cempaṭavarum umaṇarum marutatti yaṭutta neytal nilattil taṅki, muṟiyē, mīṉpiṭittum uppuviḷittum varuvārāyiṉar. avar kuṭiyiruppiṟkuk kuppam eṉṟum tuṟi yeṉṟum peyar.
paḻaṅ kaṟkālattiṟ paṭaveḻuttum (Pictograph), putukkaṟ kālattiṟ karutteḻuttum (Ideograph), tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. paṭa veḻuttāvatu, oru ceykiyip paṭamākavē varintu kāṭṭuvatu. karutteḻuttāvatu, ovvoru karuttiyum paṭaveḻuttaṭip paṭiyil oru kuṟiyāṟ kuṟippatu.
poṟkālattil aciyeḻuttut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. aci yeḻuttāvatu (Syllabary), uyirmeyyi uyirum meyyu mākap pirikkāmal taṉiyeḻuttāka eḻutuvatu.
paḻaṅ kaṟkālattil pattuvariyum, putuk kaṟkālattil nūṟuvari yum, poṟkālattil āyiramvariyum, kumarimāntar eṇṇat terintirukkalām.
kūṭṭūrāṭci niliyil, marutanilit talivaṉ ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ (makiṇaṉ) eṉṟum, mulli nilat talivaṉ aṇṇal, tōṉṟal, kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ eṉṟum, kuṟiñci nilattalivaṉ veṟpaṉ, cilampaṉ, poruppaṉ eṉṟum, neytal nilattalivaṉ tuṟivaṉ, cērppaṉ, koṇkaṉ, mellam pulampaṉ eṉṟum, patavippeyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. makiḻnaṉ eṉpatu, marutanilattu āṭalpāṭalik kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum piṟa iṉpaṅkaḷi nukarn tum makiḻvuṟṟavaṉ eṉpati yuṇarttum. kuṟunila vāṭci nili yil. aracar aṉivarum maṉṉar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
kulattalivar 
kulattalivarāvār, nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periya taṉakkāraṉ, ampalakkāraṉ, ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ūrāḷi, mūppaṉ eṉa āṅkāṅku vevvēṟu peyarāl aḻikkappeṟum kulaviyal ūrāṭciyāḷar. (ta.ti.2.)
kulappaṭṭa varalāṟu
 paṭṭam eṉṉum col varalāṟu
paṭṭutal = aṭittal, taṭṭutal, paṭṭaṭi = cuttiyalāl allatu camaṭṭiyāl taṭṭuvataṟku aṭiyāṉa kal allatu irumpu.
paṭṭaṟi = kollarum, taṭṭārum taṭṭi vēli ceyyum aṟi.
paṭṭacāli = aṅṅaṉam taṭṭi vēli ceyyum kūṭam.
paṭṭu - paṭṭam = taṭṭiyākat taṭṭi aṭikkappaṭṭa takaṭu.
paṭṭu - paṭṭi = takaṭu. taṭṭiyāṉa maravuri. atu pōṉṟa pūccu.
paṭṭam - va. paṭṭa.
paṭṭi - paṭṭiyam - paṭṭayam = āvaṇam poṟikkum takaṭu.
paṭṭam eṉṉum coṟporuḷkaḷ
paṭṭam = 1. paṭṭiyāṉa tuṇṭu. 2. caṭṭaṅkaḷi iṇikka utavum takaṭu. āṇikaḷum paṭṭaṅkaḷumākiya pariya irumpālē kaṭṭi (neṭunal. 80. uri). 3. paṭṭi vaṭivu. 4. āṭcit tali mikku aṟikuṟiyāka neṟṟiyilaṇiyum poṟṟakaṭu. paṭṭamuṅ kuḻiyu miṉṉa (cīvaka. 472). 5. peṇṭir neṟṟiyaṇi. paṭṭaṅ kaṭṭip poṟṟōṭu peytu (tiv. periyāḻ. 3:7:6). 6. maṇi kaḷil tīrum paṭṭi. 7. 
tuṇi (aka.ni). 8. peruṅkoṭi(piṅ.). 9. kāṟṟāṭi, piḷḷikaḷ paṟpala vuyarpaṭṭam viṭalpōl (tiruppō. can. piḷḷit. cappāṇi. 8). 10. urōmāṉiyak 
kurumār uccantaliyil vaṭṭamāka maḻittuk koḷḷum iṭam. 11. āṭcippatavi (piṅ.). 12. āṭci. 13. patavippeyar : paṭṭamum pacumpoṟpūṇum parantu (cīvaka. 112). 14. ciṟappuppeyar e-ṭu: teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ, uttama cōḻap pallavarāyaṉ. 15.kalvip peyar. e-ṭu: pulavaṉ, mutukali, paṇṭita maṇi. perum pērāciriyar. 16. kulappeyar. e-ṭu : piḷḷi, mutaliyār.
kaḻakak (caṅka) kālattil, ikkalattiṟ pōṟpiṟa vikkulappaṭṭap peyar vaḻaṅkavilli. kūlavāṇikaṉ, aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ, maruttuvaṉ, vaṇṇakkaṉ, kaṇiyaṉ, kiḻār eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa toḻil vakuppup peyarkaḷē aṭimoḻikaḷāka vaḻaṅkivantaṉa.
e-ṭu : kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalic cāttaṉār.
kiḻār eṉpatu uḻavar vakuppup peyarākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, aricilkiḻār, ālattūrkkiḻār, āvūrkiḻār, iṭik kuṉṟūrkiḻār, kārikiḻār, kuṟuṅkōḻiyūrkiḻār, kuṉṟūr kiḻār, kūṭalūrkiḻār, kōvūrkiḻār, tuṟiyūr ōṭikiḻār, nocci niyamaṅkiḻār, peruṅkuṉṟūrkiḻār, māṅkuṭikiḻār, vaṭamōtaṅkiḻār eṉap perumpālum iyaṟpeyariṉṟi ūrp peyaraṭutta toḻiṟpeyarākavē vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālattuc cēvūrkkiḻār - cēkkiḻār eṉpatum aṅṅaṉamē.
āvūr mūlaṅkiḻār, aiyūr mūlaṅkiḻār eṉṉuṅ kaḻakak kālap peyarkaḷil, mūlam eṉpatu nāḷik kuṟippatākak karutiṉār paṇṭārakar u.vē. cāminātiyar.
kiḻār eṉpatu kiḻavar eṉpataṉ marūu. kiḻavar eṉpatu kiḻavaṉ eṉpataṉ uyarvuppaṉmi. kiḻavaṉ = nilattiṟkuriyavaṉ, uḻavaṉ.
cellāṉ kiḻavaṉ iruppiṉ nilampulan
tillāḷiṉ ūṭi viṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 1039)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, kiḻavaṉ eṉpatu nilakkiḻavaṉāṉa uḻavaṉik kuṟittal kāṇka.
kiḻāṉ eṉṉum marūu iṉṟu intiyil kicāṉ eṉṟu tirintu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
katappiḷḷi allatu kantappiḷḷi eṉpatu, puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalāciriyaruḷ oruvar peyarākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu ikkālattiṟpōṟ piḷḷip paṭṭattōṭu kūṭiya māntaṉ peyaraṉṟu; murukaṉik kuṟikkum teyvappeyar oru pulavarkku iṭappaṭṭatē. vināyakar eṉṉum āṉimukat teyva vaṇakkam. ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉṉarē tamiḻakattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭatu. ataṟku muṉ, murukaṉukkē civaṉ makaṉ  eṉṉum karuttiṉāl piḷḷi allatu piḷḷiyār eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. piṟkālattil vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu iṟakku matiyāṉa āṉimukavaṉ, kālattāl iḷiya piḷḷiyāyiṉum āriyac cārpiṉāṟ ciṟappup peṟṟu mūtta piḷḷiyāk kappaṭṭāṉ.
āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar vakuppukaḷellām toḻil vēṟupāṭṭāl ēṟpaṭṭaṉavum, viruppappaṭi māṟakkūṭi yaṉavum, kūṭṭuṟaviṟku muṭṭukkaṭṭi yākātaṉavumā yiruntaṉa. āriyar vantapiṉ, kaḻakakkālattil, antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum nāṟperun tamiḻ vakuppukaḷ pirāmaṇar, cattiriyar, viciyar, cūttirar eṉṉum āriyap piṟavip peruṅ kulaṅkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉpiṉ, an nāṟperuṅ kulaṅkaḷum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa ciṟu kulaṅkaḷākap pirikkap paṭṭum perukkappaṭṭum pōyiṉa. tamiḻariṉ oṟṟumiyiyum valimiyiyum kuṟippataṟkup piṟavikkulap piriviṉiyē taliciṟanta vaḻiyātaliṉ kulavoḻukkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷum vēṟupāṭukaḷum varavarak kaṇṭippākkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
peyar, uṭi, uṇavu, aṇi, kuṭiyiruppu, toḻil, paḻakka vaḻakkam, caṭaṅku, teyvam, pēccu, iṭukāṭu allatu cuṭukāṭu mutaliya ellā vakikaḷilum kulaṅkaḷ vēṟupaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.
peyaraḷavil vēṟupāṭṭit teḷivākkuvataṟku, kulaṅkuṟitta coṟkaḷum īṟṟiṟ paṭṭap peyarākac cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. avi perumpālum kulattalivaṉ allatu toḻiṟṟalivaṉ allatu ūrttalivaṉ paṭṭap peyarākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tēvaṉ eṉpatu kulattalivaṉ paṭṭam; mutaliyār eṉpatu toḻiṟṟalivaṉ paṭṭam : nāṭār eṉpatu ūrttalivaṉ paṭṭam.
nāṭaṉ - nāṭāṉ - nāṭār. nāṭaṉ - nāṭṭāṉ - nāṭṭār. rakara meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ uyarvup paṉmi kuṟippaṉa.
paṭimutaliyār allatu cēṉimutaliyār eṉpataṉ kuṟukkamē mutaliyār eṉpatu. ariyanāyaka mutaliyār vicayanakarap paṭit talivaruḷ oruvar. avar patavip peyarkkuṟukkam, mutaṟkaṇ avar makkaṭkum, piṉṉar uṟaviṉarkkum, ataṉpiṉ avar kulat tārākak karutappaṭum aṉivarkkum paṭṭap peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum. iv vaḻakkam mēṉāṭu kaḷil, aracar makkaṭkum perumakkaḷ makkaṭṭum vaḻaṅkivarum matippuṟavup paṭṭam (Courtesy Title) pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ, aṅkut tanti paṭṭattiṉum tāḻntatē. aṭimoḻiyākavō kaṭi moḻiyākavō makkaṭku maṭṭum vaḻaṅki varum. iṅkō, tantiyiṉ paṭṭamē makkaṭku maṭṭumaṉṟi, uṟaviṉarkkum, kulattāreṉṟu karutappaṭum aṉivarukkum iṟutic ciṟappup peyarāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
mutaliyār eṉpatu pōṉṟavē nāyar. nāyuṭu eṉpaṉavum.
taṇṭanāyaṉ = (kēraḷa nāṭṭup) paṭittalivaṉ. nāyaṉ eṉpataṉ uyarvup paṉmi nāyar. taṇṭa nāyar - nāyar (kuṟukkam)
nāyaṉ - nāyakaṉ - nāyakkaṉ - nāykkaṉ. tirumali nāyakkaṉ, pōṭi nāyakkaṉ, petta nāyakkaṉ eṉpaṉa teluṅkap paṭittalivar peyar. taṇṭa nāyakkaṉ - nāyakkaṉ (kuṟukkam) nāyakkar, nāykkar eṉpaṉa uyarvup paṉmi.
vicayanakarap pēraraciṉ paṭit talivarākavum paṭinikarāḷi yarākavumirunta cilar. piṟkālattil tamiḻnāṭṭu aracarāyiṉar.
nāyaṉ - nāyaṭu - nāyuṭu (teluṅku), nāyar, nāykkaṉ, nāyuṭu eṉṉum paṭṭappeyarkaḷ. iṭikkālap paṭittalivarkku vaḻaṅkiṉatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, avar vaḻiyiṉarkkum vaḻivaḻi vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉamē, eṭṭi eṉṉum vaṇikar paṭṭappeyarum, eṭṭutal = uyartal, eṭṭu - eṭṭam = uyaram, uyarvu, eṭṭi = uyarntavaṉ, vaṇikaruṭ ciṟantōṉ.
eṭṭi eṉpatu, kaḻakak kālattil mūvēntarum vaṇikar talivaṉukku aḷittuvanta ciṟappup peyar. atu ikkālattu aracar, nāṇmaṅkala viḻāvil, aṟivāṟṟal potunalat toṇṭu arumaṟac ceyal, mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku aḷittuvarum ciṟappup peyarkaḷ (Birthday Honours) pōṉṟatu.
eṭṭi - ceṭṭi. o.nō : ēṇ - cēṇ.
vaṭanāṭṭup pirākiruta moḻikaḷil ekarak kuṟiliṉmiyāl, avaṟṟiṟ ceṭṭi eṉpatu cēṭṭi eṉa nīḷum, atu iṉṟu cēṭ eṉak kaṭi kuṟintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ceṭṭi eṉpatu cirēṣṭi eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripaṉṟu.
