eṉpatu piṅkala nikaṇṭu
nāykar eṉpatu vaṇikarik kuṟippiṉ, nāvikar eṉpataṉ marūu. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē, nilavāṇikat talivaṉukkuc cāttaṉ. cāttavaṉ, cāttuvaṉ, cāttuvāṉ eṉṟum; nīr vāṇikat talivaṉukku nāvikaṉ, nāykaṉ eṉṟum ciṟappup peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, kōvalaṉ tanti mācāttuvāṉ eṉṟum, kaṇṇaki tanti mānāykaṉ eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar.
cārttu - cāttu = kāḷi kōvēṟu kaḻuti, kutirikaḷiṉ mītu potiyēṟṟic cellum nilavaṇikaṉ kūṭṭam.
nāvu - nāvāy - kaṭalnīrik koḻittuc cellum kappal.
 nāvu - nāvi - nāvikaṉ = kappaṟkāraṉ, kaṭal vāṇikaṉ.
ātiri kaṇavaṉāṉa cātuvaṉum, nīrvāṇikam, ceytavaṉēṉum, peṟṟōrā liṭappaṭṭa nilavāṇikaḷ peyarit tāṅkiṉavaṉā yiruntirukkalām. cāttuvaṉ - cātuvaṉ.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil, tamiḻariṉ iyaṟpeyarōṭu kulap paṭṭap peyar iṉṟupōl iṇintu vaḻaṅkiyatilli. āriyac cārpāṉa piṟavik kulappiriviṉi akkālattē nāṟ pālaḷavil vērūṉṟiyiruntatēṉum, mūvēntarāṭciyum muṭinta 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉṉarē. ciṟukulap paṭṭap peyar kaḷellām ciṟanteḻuntatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
ap paṭṭappeyarkaḷ ellāp piṟavik kulattārkkum nilittu niṉṟaviyalla. avaravar allatu avvak kulattār āṟṟaluk kum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum viruppattiṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu. māṟiyum vantuḷḷaṉa. itik kaḷḷaṉ maṟavaṉ kaṉattā lakampaṭiyaṉ, meḷḷa meḷḷa vantupiṉ veḷḷāḷa ṉāṉāṉē eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum uṇarttum.
mutaliyār eṉṉum paṭṭam, vicayanakarap pēraraciṉ paṭit talivarāṉa ariyanāyakam pōṉṟa veḷḷāḷariṉ vaḻiyiṉarkku, mutaṟkaṇ ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ, cōḻar kālattiṟ ceṅkuntap paṭi tāṅkip poruta nilippaṭi maṟavariṉ vaḻiyiṉarāṉa necavut toḻilāḷarkku. orupuṭi yoppumi paṟṟi ap paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉṉar akampaṭiyaruḷ oru cārār ap paṭṭattit tāmākat tamakku iṭṭuk koṇ ṭaṉar. iṉṟō, mutalip peṇṇi maṇantavarum, necavut toḻil ceyvāruḷ oru cārārum, tamakku mutaliyārp paṭṭam cūṭṭikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. iṉi, orucilar tam viruppam oṉṟē karaṇiyamākat tam peyarukkuppiṉ mutaliyārp paṭṭatti iṇittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, veḷḷāṇ mutaliyār, ceṅkunta mutaliyār, akampaṭiya mutaliyār, mutalippeṇ koṇṭa mutaliyār, necavuttoḻil mutaliyār, tāṉṟōṉṟi mutaliyār eṉa aṟuvaki mutaliyār uḷar.
ittakiya vakiyīṭu ēṉic cila kulaṅkaṭkum okkum.
piḷḷip paṭṭamō, iṉṟu pirāmaṇar tavirap piṟa kulattārk kellām potuvāyviṭṭatu. āyiṉum, atu avvak kulattiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu aḷavu matippuḷḷatāyuḷḷatu.
iṉi,
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṟ paṭiyuṅ kaṇṇiyum
vāyntaṉa reṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē  (marapu. 83)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṟku ēṟpa, cōḻarkkup pōrk kālattiṟ porunarāka vantataṉāṟ paṭiyāṭciyeṉṟu paṭṭan tāṅkum, oru cār uḻutūṇ vēḷāḷarāṉa tamiḻaruḷ orucilar. teluṅkarkkēyuriya ireṭṭiyār eṉṉum paṭṭap peyarit tamakku vaḻaṅki varuvatu, oru vēṭikkiyāṉa ceytiyē.
iṅṅaṉam, uṇmikkum pakuttaṟiviṟkum poruntāta kulap paṭṭap peyarkaḷi, intiyariyellām kulappirivaṟṟa orē kumukāyamākka vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ kuṟikkōḷ koṇṭavarum kūṭa. tamakku vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, mūtintiyarkku, ciṟappākat tamiḻarkku mūvakik kēṭukaḷ nērkiṉṟaṉa.
1. kumukāyat tāḻvu
āriyac cārpāṉa piṟavik kulak kumukāya ēṇippaṭikaḷuḷ. ovvoṉṟum ovvoru kulattiṟku niliyāka otukkappaṭ ṭiruppatāl, tamiḻar allatu tiraviṭar allatu paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum ettuṇi muṉṉēṟṟa muyaṟci ceyyiṉum, av vēṇiyiṉ uccappaṭiyi allatu tamakku mēṟpaṭṭa paṭiyi aṭiya muṭiyātu. ovvoru paṭṭamum ovvoru kulattiṉ muttiriyāyiruppatāl, ap paṭṭattit tāṅkik koṇṭē mēleḻuvatu, oruvaṉ taṉ pātattiṟ pāṟāṅ kallik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu uyarak kutippatu pōṉṟatākum.
2. oṟṟumik kulivu
oru vakuppār cērntu vāḻvatē ciṟanta valimi. oṟṟumi yiṉṟēl uraṅkuṉṟip pōvatāl, iṉa muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku eṭṭuṇiyum iṭamilli. ākavē, ulakuḷḷa aḷavum aḵ ṟiṇi pōṉṟē niṉṟa niliyil niṟṟal vēṇṭum.
3. pakuttaṟivu vaḷarcciyiṉmi
oruvaṉ taṉ iyaṟpeyarōṭu oru piṟavikkulap paṭṭap peyari iṇittuk kūṟuvatu tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa toḻilukkuc caṟṟum poruntātatum, vēṟen nāṭṭilum illātatum, pakuttaṟiviṟku ovvātatum, muṉṉōr marapiṟku muṟṟum muraṇāṉatum, evvaki naṟpayaṉumaṟṟatum, nuṇṇaṟivu vaḷarc cikkut taṭiyāṉatumāy iruttalāl, ati aṟavē viṭṭu viṭuvatē uyartiṇi yoḻukkattiṟkum tamiḻaṉ talimikkum takkatām.  (teṉmoḻi mēḻam 1976)
kulaveṟi koḷḷāmi 
tamiḻarip pirittuk keṭuttaṟkeṉṟē, piṟappoṭu toṭar puṟṟa kulappiriviṉi ayalārāṟ pukuttappaṭṭa teṉṟum, atu nūlutti paṭṭaṟivukaṭku muṟṟum muraṇāṉa teṉṟum ulaka muḻumiyilum in nāvalan tēyattilēyē atu uḷḷateṉ ṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar tirumūlar mutaliya periyō rellārālum kaṇṭikkappaṭṭateṉṟum, uṇarāta tamiḻar, iṉṉum kula vuṇarcciyilēyē tiḷittu varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, ovvoru kulattārum tamakkuḷḷēyē maṇantu, tamiḻiṉattil pēraṟivuc cuṭarkaḷ peruvāriyāyp piṟavātapaṭi taṭuttu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu tamiḻarkkuḷ oṟṟumiyum illātu pōkiṉṟatu.
oṉṟu paṭṭāluṇṭu vāḻvu - iṅku
oṟṟumi yiṉṟēl aṉivarkkum tāḻvu
itit tamiḻ mākāṇa muṉṉēṟṟam paṟṟiyāvatu kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.
nīṇṭa kālamākat toṭarntuvarum kulamuṟiyit tiṭumeṉṟu nīkkuvatu iyalātēṉum, uyarttap paṭṭōrkkum tāḻttap paṭṭōrkkum itupōtu tuppuravu paṟṟi ēṟṟattāḻvu uḷḷa tēṉum, vēḷāḷarum mutaliyārum pōlvārum, maṟavarum iṭiyarum pōlvārum iraṭṭiyārum nāyuṭuvum pōl vārum, tammuḷ maṇantu koḷḷalāmē!
tamiḻariṉ palvēṟu peruṅkulaṅkaḷaṉṟi, (nāṭum ūrumākiya) iṭam, uṇporuḷ, uṭi, ciṟuteyvam, koḷki, caṭaṅku, ceyporuḷ, ciṟappu nikaḻcci, kuṇam, niṟam, kuṭumi nili, vaḻakkam, aṇi, tālivaki, moḻi, aluval, paṭṭam, kula varici, ciṉṉam, makkaḷ toki, tokuppu, kalappu, tōṟṟak kati, tiṟami, muṟippeyar mutaliya paṟpala vakipaṟṟip pirintu kiṭakkum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa poruntāk kulap pirivukaḷiṟ palavum, akamaṇa vakuppukaḷākavē yiruntu varuvatu, tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku māperun taṭiyātaliṉ, kalvi kaṟṟa tamiḻ iḷiñar kuruṭṭut taṉamum kuṟukiya nōkkumuḷḷa kulamutiyar coṟ koḷḷātu, ati virintu takartteṟiyak kaṭavar.  (ta.ti. 49, 50.)
kulikuliyāy muntirikkāy viḷiyāṭṭu
pala piḷḷikaḷ vaṭṭamāy uḷnōkki uṭkārntirukka, oru piḷḷi tiripōl muṟukkiya tuṇi yoṉṟik kiyil vittuk koṇṭu, kulikuliyāy muntirikkāy eṉṟu viṭṭu viṭṭu urakkac colli, vaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē piḷḷikaṭku arukil valamākac cuṟṟic cuṟṟivarum. ap piḷḷi kuli... kāy eṉṟu colluntoṟum, uṭkārn tirukkum, piḷḷikaḷellām oruṅkē nariyē, nariyē, ōṭivā eṉṟu kattic colvar.
iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntu koṇṭirukkumpōtē, cuṟṟi varum piḷḷi tiṭumeṉṟu tiriyi oru piḷḷiyiṉ piṉṉāl vittuviṭum; tirivikkappaṭṭa piḷḷi uṭaṉē kaṇṭu ati eṭukkāviṭiṉ, vittapiḷḷi oru cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi vantu, vikkap paṭṭa piḷḷiyiṉ mutukil at tiriyāl ōraṭi ōṅkivittu eḻuppi, ati ap piḷḷiyiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, ap piḷḷi yiṉ iṭattil tāṉ uṭkārntukoḷḷum. tiri vittavuṭaṉ vikkappaṭṭa piḷḷi kaṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭu eḻuntuviṭiṉ, vitta piḷḷi virintu oru cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi vantu veṟṟiṭattil uṭkārntu aṭikkut tappikkoḷḷum.
tiriyeṭutta allatu tirivāṅkiṉa piḷḷi, muṉpōl kuli....... kāy, eṉṟu muṉcolla, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷel lām nariyē...... vā eṉṟu piṉcolvar. piṉpu tirivippatum piṟavum nikaḻum, iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.
iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkut tirittiri pantam eṉṟu peyar.
pāṇṭināṭṭil, piḷḷikaḷ vaṭṭamāy uṭkārātu variciyāy uṭkārntu, iv viḷiyāṭṭu āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu yāṉittiri eṉṟu peyar peṟum.  (ta.nā.vi.)
kuli vakikaḷ 
kottu avari tuvari mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kuli;
kuli koṭi muntiri pōṉṟatiṉ kuli;
tāṟu vāḻikkuli;
katir kēḻvaraku cōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ katir;
alaku allatu kural nel tiṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ katir;
cīppu vāḻittāṟṟiṉ pakuti.  (col : 68.)
kuvaḷi 
kuvaḷi - kuvala
 kuvaḷi = 1. karuṅkuvaḷi.
kuvaḷi.... koṭicci kaṇpōl malartalum (aiṅkuṟu. 399).
2. ceṅkuvaḷi
viḷakkiṭ ṭaṉṉa kaṭikamaḻ kuvaḷi  (cīvaka. 256).
 3. oru pēreṇ
neytaluṅ kuvaḷiyum āmpaluñ caṅkamum  (paripā. 2: 13). (va.va: 129)
kuḻavi 
kuḻavi : kuḻa - kuḻavu = iḷami. kuḻavu - kuḻavi. kuḻa - kuḻanti, kuḻa - koḻuntu, kuḻa - kuṭa (va.). (ti.ma : 741)
kuḻavi vaḷarppoli 
kuḻavi vaḷarppuk kālattil kuḻavi vāyilum tāy vāyilum piṟak kum olikaḷ, kuḻuvi vaḷarppolikaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ, kuḻavi yolikaḷ, uḷḷiya (Voluntary) voli uḷḷā (involuntary) voli eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. pacciḷaṅkuḻavikaḷ eṇṇātu iyalpāyk kattum olikaḷ uḷḷāvoliyum, pēccuk kaṟkum piḷḷikaḷ tappolikaḷākak kūṟum tiruntāc coṟkaḷ uḷḷiya voliyum, ākum. mu.tā.
1. kuḻaviyolikaḷ
(1) uḷḷāvoli :
e.ṭu. - iṅkā = pāl.
kuḻavikaḷ iyalpāka miṭaṟṟiṟ piṟappikkum iṅku eṉṉum poruḷaṟṟa volikku, pāl eṉṉum poruḷic ceviliyar paṭittuk koṇṭaṉar.
ammā appā eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ ivvakiyiṟ piṟantaṉavāka, mēli moḻinūl vallār koḷvar. atu poruntātu.
(2) uḷḷiyavoli :
 miyyā (pūṉi)
 ḷoḷ ḷoḷ (nāy)
 pīppī (ūtukuḻal)
 ṭumṭum (mēḷam)
ittakiya volikaḷ pala. āppirikka moḻikaḷum ātti rēliya moḻikaḷum pōṉṟa tiruntā moḻikaḷil, collāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
āṅkilattiluḷḷa mew, bow - vow, pipe, tom - tom eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ ittakiyaṉavē.
tamiḻilum, kākki (kākkā), ñaḷḷi (ñaḷ), tuntumi tumtum -tuptup - tuntupi. (t.), caṭapuṭā mutaliya coṟkaḷ im muṟiyilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa.
ittakiyavolikaḷellām uṇmiyil oppolikaḷē yāyiṉum, kuḻavi vāyiṉiṉṟu varuvaṉa eṉṉum ciṟappuppaṟṟi vēṟu pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉka. ivaṟṟiṉ tokutiyik kuḻavimoḻi (child language) eṉpar. (mu. tā.)
tāyolikaḷ
kuḻaviyit toṭṭililiṭṭut tāy lālāveṉṟolit tāṭṭutal lālāṭṭu. āṅkilarum iti lullaby eṉpar.
lālā - rārā - rārāṭṭu. rārā - rōrō rōrāṭṭu. lakaram tamiḻiṟ coṉmutaleḻuttaṉmiyiṉ, lālāṭṭu eṉpatu tālāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. la-ta, pōli.
o.nō. kalampakam - katampam, calaṅki - cataṅki.
piḷḷit tamiḻp paṉuvalil, tālāṭṭup paruvam tālapparuvam eṉappaṭum. tālam eṉpatu tāl eṉak kaṭik kuṟintum varum. tālapparuvac ceyyuṭkaḷil makuṭamāka varum tālō tālē lō eṉṉun toṭar, tālāṭṭuc collākum.
tālāṭṭu - tārāṭṭu.
makkaḷ toṭakkūṭātatum kuḻantikaḷ tiṉṉak kūṭātatumāṉa iḻiporuḷi, tāymār kuḻantikaḷiṭam cukkā eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟippar.
uṇarvolikaḷum kuṟiyolikaḷum, ciṟupāṉmiyilum moḻi vaḷarccikkup peruntuṇi ceyyā vakiyilum ōraṉṉa taṉmiya. kuḻaviyolikaḷiṉum vāycceyki yolikaḷum, vāycceykiyolikaḷiṉum oppolikaḷum, ivviruvaki yilum ciṟantaṉavām.
uṇarvoli, kuṟiyoli, kuḻavioli, vāycceykioli, oppoli iṇittuk kāṇka. (mu.tā.)
kuḷam 
kuḷam 1 - kūla = ciṟukuḷam.  (va.va - 9)
kuḷam2 - kula (g) = vellam.
kuḷam = vellavuruṇṭi.  (va.va: 129-130)
kuḷavaki 
kuḷam kuḷikkum nīrnili; teppakkuḷam teppattēr ōṭum kuḷam. ūruṇi ūrāl uṇṇappaṭum allatu ūr naṭuvi luḷḷa kuḷam; ēri ērttoḻiṟku nīrppāyccum kuḷam; kaṇvāy ciṟukālvāyāl nīr nirappum kuḷam; taṭam, taṭākam akaṉṟa allatu periya kuḷam; kayam āḻamāṉa kuḷam; kuṭṭam kuḷattiṉ āḻamāṉa iṭam. kuṭṭi ciṟukuḷam; kuṇṭu vaṟṟiya kuḷattiṉ nīr niṟinta kiṭaṅku; poyki maliyaṭutta iyaṟkiyāṉa kuḷam; cuṉi nīrcurakkum malikkuṇṭu; kiṇaṟu veṭṭappaṭṭa āḻamāṉa ciṟu nīr nili; kēṇi maṇaṟkiṇaṟu; kūval ciṟu kiṇaṟu; turavu cuṟṟuk kaṭṭillāta peruṅkiṇaṟu; maṭu aruvi viḻum kiṭaṅku.  (col. 49)
kuḷiki 
kuḷiki - kulikā (g) = uruṇṭi, māttiri.
kuḷam - kuḷiki = uruṇṭi māttiri.
kuḷiyam=uruṇṭi, uruṇṭi, uruṇṭi māttiri. (va.va : 130)
kuḷir 
kuḷir - kuḷīra = naṇṭu.
kuḷḷutal = kiḷḷutal, kiṇṭutal, kīṟutal. kuḷ - kuḷir = kūriya kavikkālāṟ kaṭikkum allatu kūriya kālkaḷāl nilattik kīṟum naṇṭu. (va.va: 130)
kuṟṟamun taṇṭaṉiyum
kuṟṟa vaki : kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, aṟanūlukku māṟāṉavum, araciyalukku māṟāṉavum, teyvattiṟku māṟāṉavum, eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
kuṭi poy kaḷavu kāmam koli eṉpaṉa aṟanūlukku māṟāṉavi. ivi aimperuṅ kuṟṟam eṉappaṭum. piṟariṭam vāṅkiṉati maṟuttalum, taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa piṟarporuḷi oḷittalum, kaḷḷak kiyeḻuttum, āḷmāṟāṭṭamum, poyyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
iṟiyiṟukkāmi, uṟuporuḷ kavartal, pakiyoṟṟu, aṟi pōtal, aracaṉatu poruḷ kavartal, aracaṉatu iṉpap poruḷ nukartal, ūrukku allatu nāṭṭiṟku iraṇṭakañ ceytal, aracaṟkiraṇṭakañ ceytal, aracāṇi mīṟal, koḷḷiyaṭit tal, kalakañceytal eṉpaṉa, araciyaṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷām. putiyalum piṟaṅkaṭiyillāc cottum uṟu poruḷākum.
oppip paṇiceyyāmi, kōyiṟ poruṭkavarvu eṉpaṉa teyvattiṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ. kōyilil viḷakkerippatit taṇṭaṉiyākap peṟṟavaṉum, kōyilil viḷakkerikkap poṉ peṟṟavaṉum, aṅṅaṉam erikkāmiyum; kōyiṟ paṇ ṭārattiṟ peṟṟa kaṭaṉukku vaṭṭiyiṟukkāmiyum; oppip paṇiceyyāmiyākum. kōyiṟ kalaṅkaḷiyum aṇikalaṅ kaḷiyum kaḷavāṭalum, vaḻipāṭṭu viḻāc celavik kuṟitta lum aṭiyōṭu niṟuttalum, kōyiṟporuṭ kavarvākum.
mūvakik kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷḷum, kaṭumiyāṉavi mēṟpaṭu kuṟṟam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
taṇṭaṉi vaki : taṇṭaṉiyāṉatu, taṇṭam, māṉakkēṭu, vētaṉippāṭu, ciṟippu, vēlinīkkam, koli eṉa aṟuvakippaṭṭiruntatu.
avaṟṟuḷ, taṇṭam eṉpatu, tokiyiṟuppu, taṇṭattīrvi, kōyil viḷakkerippu eṉa mūvaki. toki yiṟuppāvatu oru kuṟitta tokiyi mottamāyc celuttal.
māṉakkēṭu eṉpatu, mukattiṟ cempuḷḷi karumpuḷḷi kuttik kaḻutimēlēṟṟi āṉmayan tōytta alakālaṭittu ūrvalam varuvittalum, kulattiṉiṉṟu vilakkalum, ākum.
vētaṉippāṭu eṉpatu, tūṇiṟ kaṭṭivittu 50 aṭi mutal 100 aṭivari aṭittal, kallēṟṟal, veyilil niṟpittal, kiṭṭi pūṭṭal, naṭiviḷak kerittal eṉap palavakippaṭum. kiṭṭi eṉpatu keṇṭik kāli nerukkum oruvakik karuvi. naṭi viḷakkerittal eṉpatu, taliyil akalviḷakkēṟṟi ūrvalam varuvittal.
ciṟippu eṉpatu, taḷiyiṭṭuc ciṟikkōṭṭattil vittal.
koli eṉpatu, veṭṭal, kaḻuvēṟṟal, vilaṅkāṟ kol vittal, cittiravati ceytal eṉa nālvaki. yāṉiyiviṭṭu mitippittalum pulikkaruttutalum pōl vaṉa vilaṅkāṟ kolvittal. erumik kāliṟ kaṭṭiyōṭṭutalum vaṇṭic cakkarattiṟ kaṭṭiyōṭṭutalum pōlvaṉa cittiravatiyākum.
kuṟṟat taṇṭaṉi : mūvakik kuṟṟavāḷiyarkkum kuṭavōli yiṭappeṟāmi potut taṇṭaṉiyākum. kaṭkuṭikku itu tavira vēṟu taṇṭaṉiyilli.
aṟanūlukku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ : poykkut taṇṭamum; kaḷaviṟkup poruḷ mīṭpoṭu māṉakkēṭum, poruḷmīṭkap peṟāviṭattut taṇṭamum, taṇṭam iṟukkappaṭāviṭattuc ciṟiyum; kāmattiṟku māṉakkēṭum; kolikkuk koliyum; taṇṭaṉiyākum.
koli ceytavaṉ ōṭippōṉaviṭattu, avaṉ cottu muḻutum paṟimutalākik kōyiṟkuc cērkkappaṭṭatu. ōṭip pōṉa koliñaṉik kolvataṟkup potumakkaṭkum urimiyirun tatu. koliñaṉ koliyuṇṭapiṉ, avaṉatu paṟimutalāṉa cottip piṟaṅkaṭiyāṉ vēṇṭip peṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyum. taṟceyalāy nērnta kolikkuk kōyil viḷakkerippē taṇṭaṉiyāka vitikkappaṭṭatu. koli yāṉatu virumpic ceyyappaṭṭaviṭattum, koliñaṉ makaṉ koliyuṇṭava ṉiṉ neruṅkiya vuṟaviṉaroṭu oppantañ ceytu koḷḷiṉ, taṇṭaṉi nīṅka iṭamiruntatu.
narapali, maṟpōr, koṭumpāvi kaṭṭiyiḻuttal ākiya mūviṭattum nikaḻuṅ kolikkut taṇṭaṉiyēyilli. taṟkoliyit taṭuppataṟkuc caṭṭamuṟiyāṉa taṭiyum akkālattilli.
araciyaṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ, iṟiyiṟukkāmikkuc cottup paṟimutalum, uṟuporuḷ kavartalukkuc ciṟiyum, piṟavaṟṟiṟkuk koliyum, potuvāka vitikkappaṭum taṇṭaṉi yākum. iṟiyiṟātārkku īrāṇṭut tavaṇiyum, ataṉmēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkup piṇippēṟum tarappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉ piṉṉum iṟātāṉ cottu paṟimutalākik kōyiṟku viṟkap paṭṭu, araciṟittoki pōka eñciya tellām kōyi loṭu cērkkappaṭṭatu. koḷḷiyaṭittavar kalakañ ceytavar poruḷkaḷum paṟimutal ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
teyvattiṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaṭkut taṇṭamum, kuṟṟavāḷi kōyil viṉiñaṉāyiṉ ciṟupāṉmi vēli nīkkamum taṇṭa ṉiyāyiruntaṉa. kōyil viṉiñaruḷ, kīḻōr kuṟṟatti 
mēlōrum; mēlōr kuṟṟatti ūraviyār, nāṭṭatikāri, 
aracaṉāl vitantuviṭukkappaṭṭa atikāri, aracaṉ ākiyavaruḷ oruvar allatu palarum; kēṭṭut tīrppatu vaḻakkam. oppip paṇiceyyātavarkkuk kaṭuntaṇṭavariyum, kōyiṟporuḷ kavarnta pūcakaṉ aṟikāraṉ (ukkirāṇattāṉ) mutaliyōrkkut taṇṭamum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. aṇikalamum taṭṭumuṭṭuṅ kaḷavāṭi yavarāl iṟukkappaṭṭa taṇṭat tokiyik koṇṭu, ap poruḷkaḷ vāṅki vikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
taṇṭamiṟukka iyalāta pūcakar tam kōyiṉ muṟi nāṭkaḷil cila pala viṭṭukkoṭuttaṉar.
perumpālum kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭē taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. aiyuṟavāṉa viṭattil makkaḷi vatittuk kēṭpatum uṇṭeṉ patu, paḻiyañciya tiruviḷiyāṭalāl aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. akkālattil naṭuvuniliyāka muṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉpatu, maṉumuṟic cōḻaṉ poṟṟip pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi mutaliyōr ceytiyāl aṟiyalām. āyiṉum, cilaviṭattu ārāyāmal taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭateṉpatu, vārttikaṉ, kōvalaṉ, paṭṭiṉattār mutaliyōr varalāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. paṭṭiṉattār tam niṟimoḻi vaṉmiyālēyē taṇṭaṉiyi ṉiṉṟu tappiṉar.
vali kuṉṟiya aracar kālattil aracarkkaṭaṅkātu vāḻnta makka ḷum uḷar. avar kūṭivāḻnta ūr aṭaṅkāppaṟṟu eṉap paṭṭatu.

kuṟṟiyalukaram uyirīṟē
eḻutteṉap paṭupa.
akaramutala ṉakara iṟuvāy
muppaḵ teṉpa;
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē  (tol. 1)
avitām
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa  (tol. 2)
 eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
iraṇṭām nūṟpāvil, kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eṉap pirittuk kūṟātu kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eṉṟu `muppāṟ puḷḷi eṉpatu mūveḻuttiyum potuppaṭat taḻuvumāṟu ummittokiyāyc cērttuk kūṟiyiruttaliṉ. kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum āytam pōlap paṇṭik kālattiṟ puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa eṉpatuṇarappaṭum.
nūṟpāvil muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉpatu, puḷḷi peṟṟa mūveḻuttu kaḷiyum muppuḷḷi vaṭivāṉa āytattiyum oruṅkē kuṟippatu āmākōṉav vaṇiyavum peṟumē eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṉ āmā eṉpatu pōṉṟa iraṭṭuṟalākum.
tolkāppiya marapiyal nūṟpā 110-l, vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal eṉṉum uttiyuriyil.
kuṟṟiyalikarattip puḷḷiyeṉṟalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉavākaliṉ aviyum iṉi vārāmiyāṉ vantuḻi vantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu, iṉip puḷḷi yeṉa mēl āḷa vārātataṉip puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟ ṟōṭu mayakkaṅ kūṟutaleṉpatu. `avitāṅ kuṟṟiya likaraṅ kuṟṟiyalukara - māyta meṉṟa - muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa (tol - eḻut. nūṉ. 2) eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram. puḷḷi yeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷa vārāmiyāṉum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum aṅṅaṉam puḷḷi yeṉṟu āḷa varuṅ kuṟ ṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉ kūṟutalāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉ kūṟākkāṟ puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramumeṉac cūttiram peṟutal vēṇṭuva tāvāṉ cellumeṉpatu eṉṟurittār pērāciriyar.
 civañāṉa muṉivar tam tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttira viruttiyil.
oru moḻiyic cārntuvaru miyalpiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅku miyalpu tamakkilaveṉṟaliṉ, avi tammiyē yeṭuttōtik kāṭṭa lākāmiyiṉ, varuñ cūttirattāṉ avaṟṟiṟku vēṟuvēṟu peyariṭṭu avitāṅ kuṟṟiyalikaraṅ kuṟṟiyalukara māytam eṉṟum, am mūṉṟum puḷḷi peṟutal paṟṟip potup peya rāka muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eṉṟum, avi taṉitteḻutappaṭā vāyiṉum moḻiyoṭu cārttiyeḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉ ṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭṭiṟ kuriyaveṉpār. `eḻuttō raṉṉa eṉṟum ōtiṉār eṉṟār.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, avitām... eḻuttō raṉṉa eṉṉum cārpeḻuttu nūṟpāvuriyiṉ iṭiyil.
āytameṉṟa ōcitāṉ aṭuppukkūṭṭuppōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytameṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷi yumeṉṟār. ataṉi ikkālattār naṭuvu vāṅkiyiṭ ṭeḻutupa. itaṟku vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. ēṉi yoṟṟukaḷpōla uyirēṟātu ōci vikāramāy niṟpatoṉṟākaliṉ, eḻuttiyal taḻā ōcikaḷ pōlak koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟaṟku eḻuttēyā meṉṟār. itaṉip puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉavē ēṉi yeḻuttukaḷellām varivaṭiviṉa vātal peṟṟām eṉṟu kūṟiṉārēṉum, toṭakkattil,
avitām - mēṟcārntu varumeṉappaṭṭavitām. kuṟṟiya likaraṅ kuṟṟiyalukaram āytamumeṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatākiya puḷḷi vaṭivumām : eḻuttōraṉṉa - aviyum muṟkūṟiya muppateḻuttōṭu orutaṉmiyāy vaḻaṅkum eṉṟa vāṟu eṉṟu mūveḻuttum puḷḷipeṟumeṉa orutaṉmip paṭavē urittār.
mayilinātar, tolli vaṭiviṉa eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvuriyil.
āṇṭu eṉṟa mikiyāṉē. tātu, ētu eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu āriya moḻikaḷum, eṭṭu koṭṭu eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup potumoḻikaḷum, kuṉṟiyātu, nāṭiyātu eṭṭiyāṇṭuḷatu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup puṇarmoḻip poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kaḷum, aṟitaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟṟukarak kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēṟ puḷḷi koṭuppārum uḷareṉak koḷka eṉṟu urittaṉar.
kuṟṟiyalikaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum : eṉṉi? kuṟṟiya likaramum kuṟṟiya lukaramu
maṟṟavi tāmē puḷḷi peṟumē
eṉpatu caṅka yāppākaliṉ eṉpatu yāpparuṅkala virutti (pa.27).
mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil puḷḷiyiṭ ṭeḻutappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum aṅṅaṉam eḻutiṉatu avaṟṟiṉ olik kuṟukkattiyum nāṭiyātu, eṭṭi yāṇṭu mutaliya puṇarmoḻip poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭiyum kāṭṭaṟku eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
nāṭiyātu. eṭṭiyāṇṭu eṉpaṉa, nāṭi yātu, eṭṭi yāṇṭu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭumātaliṉ, avi nāṭu+yātu, eṭṭu+yāṇṭu eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷ eṉṟu kāṭṭaṟkuk kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ mēṟ puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭateṉka.
kuṟṟukaratti mēṟcuṉṉamiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu iṉṟum maliyāḷa (cēra) nāṭṭu vaḻakkam. kuṟṟikara kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ puḷḷi peṟu meṉṟu naṉṉūliṟ kūṟappaṭāmiyāl, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē iv vaḻakku. cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷil oḻintatu eṉpati aṟiyalām. ekara ēkāraṅkaṭkum okara ōkāraṅkaṭkum avi cērntuḷḷa uyirmeykaṭkum vari vēṟupāṭu tol kāppiyattilē kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil av veḻuttukaḷ kuṟil neṭil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi eḻutap paṭṭāṟ pōla, kuṟṟiyalukaramum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yeḻutappaṭṭa teṉka.
oru poruḷ kuṟukiṉ ap poruḷēyaṉṟi vēṟuporu ḷākātu : ataṉ kuṟukkam aḷavu vēṟupāṭēyaṉṟip poruḷ vēṟupāṭaṉṟu. atupōl, kuṟṟiyalukaramum, muṟṟiyalukaram pōla uyirēyaṉṟi meyyākātu. itaṉālēyē,
ikara ukaraṅ kuṟukiniṉṟaṉa, vikāravakiyāṟ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭu taliṉ, ivaṟṟi iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal ellārkkum oppa muṭintatu. cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅ kōlākātu atu pōla uyiratu kuṟukkamum uyirēyām. ivaṟṟip puṇarcci vēṟṟumiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumiyumpaṟṟi vēṟōr eḻuttāka vēṇṭiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, poruḷ vēṟṟumi yeṉṟatu paṇṭik kālattil kaṭṭu koṭṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷ ēval viṉiyām pōtu muṟṟukara vīṟāyum mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarāmpōtu kuṟṟukara vīṟāyum, olik kappaṭṭavi nōkki, tarukku aṇukku eṉpaṉa viṉikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. (tol. 36. uri). kātu kaṭṭu kattu muṟukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟ pōla (tol. 68, uri). eṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka. tolkāppiyar tam kālattil kuṟṟu karamum meypōlap puḷḷi peṟṟataṉālēyē, puṇariyalil.
 meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (tol. 104)
 kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa  (tol. 105)
eṉṟu māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiṉār.
im māṭṭēṟṟik kavaṉiyātu, kuṟṟiya... moḻipa. eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟku īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaramum (puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum) attaṉmittu eṉṟu colluvar eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum, īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum uri kūṟuvatu poruntātu. aṟṟeṉa moḻipa eṉṉum māṭṭēṟu. puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal eṉ patiyē taḻuvumātaliṉ iv viruvarum iṅṅaṉam urittaṟku ivar kālattiṟku muṉpē kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟum vaḻakkam vīḻntamiyē kāraṇamākum. ivar kūṟiya uri iṅkup poruttumāyiṉ tolkāppiyar nūṉmarapil.
meyyi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (tol. 15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē  (tol. 16)
eṉṟu kūṟiyaviṭattum poruntal vēṇṭum.
ekara okaram puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭutta liṉmi yāṉum, ekara... aṟṟē eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟku. ekara okaraṅ kaḷatu iyalpum avvāṟu puḷḷi peṟum iyalpiṟṟu eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum. ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ niliyum mey pōlap puḷḷi peṟum iyalpiṟṟu eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum uri kūṟuvatāṉum. kuṟṟiya... moḻipa eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟkum kuṟṟu karamum meypōlap puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatē uriyākak kōṭal poruttamām.
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukku meṉṟu uritta viṭattum, māttirik kuṟukkattāl aṅṅaṉam iṭaṅkoṭukkum eṉṉum karuttiṉarē aṉṟi meyyīṟāy iruttalāl iṭaṅkoṭukkum eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉarallar. kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vantu uyirēṟi muṭiyāmiyum tammuḷ vēṟṟumi (tol. 36, uri) eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, tolkāppiyar karutti nōkkiṉ, avar kuṟṟiyalu karatti meyyīṟeṉṟu koṇṭār eṉṟu koḷḷutaṟku eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli. avar,
kuṟṟiya lukara muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ
oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum  (tol. 67)
eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻimutal varumeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. nunti eṉṉum colliṉ mutalil (tolkāppiyar karuttiṉ paṭi) uḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟāyiṉ, ac col nnti eṉṟu eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa meḻutap paṭāmiyum, kuṟṟiyalukarattit tolkāppiyar meyyeṉṟu koṇ ṭirupparāyiṉ, atu,
paṉṉī ruyirum moḻimuta lākum  (tol. 59)
uyirmey allaṉa moḻimuta lākā  (tol. 60)
eṉṟu avar kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu muraṇutalum nōkkuka.
mēlum, orē yukaram ōriṭattuk kuṟṟukaramākavum ōriṭattu muṟ ṟukaramākavum olikkappaṭum. aṅṅaṉam olikkappaṭu pavaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟukara muṟṟukarap poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. cilavaṟṟiṟkilli.
muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
tappeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiya lāṉa  (tol. 68)
eṉṉum nūṟpāviyum, ataṉ uriyil,
kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟpōla, nunti yeṉṟu itaḻ kuvittu muṟṟak kūṟiyaviṭattum itaḻ kuviyāmaṟ kuṟiyak kūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē tantavāṟu kāṇka. nuntāyeṉpatō veṉiṉ, aḵtu itaḻ kuvittē kūṟavēṇṭu taliṟ kuṟṟukaramaṉṟu. iyaleṉṟataṉāṉ iṭamum paṟṟuk kōṭum iraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭiṉṟeṉṟu koḷka eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittiruppatiyum nōkkuka. itaṉāl, kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyīṟāyiṉ muṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟṟāy ukaram eṉṉum uyirē tamiḻukkilli yeṉṟu peṟappaṭutalum aṅṅaṉa miṉmiyum aṟika.
ceyyuḷiyalil, iruvaki yukaramu moṉṟākak koṇṭē.
iruvaki yukaramō ṭiyintavi variṉē
nērpum niripu māku meṉpa  (tol. cey. 4)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
itaṉ uriyil, iruvaki yukarameṉpaṉa, kuṟṟukara muṟṟu karaṅkaḷ, avaṟṟōṭu mēṟkūṟiya nēraciyum niriyaciyum oru col viḻukkāṭu paṭa iyintuvariṉ niṟutta muṟiyāṉē nēraciyō ṭoṉṟi vanta kuṟṟukaramum ataṉōṭu oṉṟivanta muṟṟukaramum nērpaci yeṉappaṭum; niripaciyō ṭoṉṟi vanta kuṟṟukaramum ataṉōṭoṉṟi vanta muṟṟu karamum niri paci eṉappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu...
muṉṉar nēraci nāṉkum niriyaci nāṉkumeṉa eṇ vaki yāṉ acikūṟi avaṟṟuppiṉ iruvaki yukaramum varumeṉavē, avi kuṟṟukarattōṭu eṭṭum muṟṟukarattōṭu eṭṭumākap patiṉāṟu utāraṇap pakutiyavāyc ceṉṟatēṉum, avaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟeḻuttup piṉvarum ukaram nērpaci yākā teṉpatu kuṟi liṇi yukara malvaḻi yāṉa (tol. cey. 4) eṉpuḻic collu tum: oḻintaṉa kuṟṟukara nērpaci mūṉṟum niripaci nāṉku māyiṉa. utāraṇam : vaṇṭu. nāku, kāmpu, varaku, kuraṅku, malāṭu, malāṭṭu; ivi kuṟṟukaram aṭuttu nērpum niripum vanta vāṟu eṉṟu urittār pērāciriyar. itaṉāl, kuṟṟukaramum muṟṟukaramum pōla alaku peṟumeṉṟum acik kuṟuppā meṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
coṟkaḷ ilakkaṇa muṟiyilum ilakkiya muṟiyilum pala vakiyākat tirintu, muṉpu muṟṟukara vīṟāyiruntavi allatu ukaramallāta īṟāyiruntavi piṉpu kuṟṟukara vīṟākiṉṟaṉa. av vakikaḷāvaṉa :
1. toḻiṟpeyar
	mutaṉili tirinta toḻil peyar :
	e-ṭu: paṭu - pāṭu, cuṭu - cūṭu.
	vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar :
	e-ṭu : paṭi + pu = paṭippu. muṭi + cu = muṭiccu.
2. vēṟṟumip peyar :
	e-ṭu : yāṉ+ku = eṉakku. avar + ku = avarkku.
3. kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu :
	e-ṭu : tāḷ + tu = tāṭṭu. kaṇ + tu = kaṭṭu. pāl + tu = pāṟṟu. aṉ + tu = aṟṟu.
4. piṟaviṉi :
	e-ṭu : paṭu + tu = paṭuttu. naṭa + tu = naṭattu. vāḻ + tu = vāḻttu. pāy + cu = pāyccu.
5. pōli :
	e-ṭu : aṭivu - aṭavu - aṭaku.
6. col tiripu :
e-ṭu : tirum - tirumpu. porun - poruntu, uriñ - uriñcu, uṭaṉ (uṭal) - uṭam - uṭampu. paṇ - pāṇ - pāṭu. kuḷ- koḷ - kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṭu. oḷi - oḷir - oḷiṟu. pō- pōtu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya pāṭu, paṭippu, eṉakku, tāṭṭu, paṭuttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ, kuṟṟukara vīṟṟi meyyīṟākak koḷḷiṉ pāṭṭ paṭipp eṉakk tāṭṭ paṭutt eṉṟa mutal vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉavāka vaṉṟō koḷḷal vēṇṭum! iḵtu ettuṇip pētamiyākum! mēlum, āṭṭu pāṭṭu kalakku vilakku mutaliya piṟaviṉikaḷum toḻiṟpeyarkaḷum mutaṉili yīṟṟu valiyiraṭṭiyum mutaṉili yiṭimeli valittum muṟiyē āṭu pāṭu kalaṅku viḷaṅku eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu tirintirukkavum, avaṟṟi āṭṭ pāṭṭ kalakk viḷakk eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu piṟantaṉavākak koḷḷal iḷañ ciṟārum eḷḷinakiyāṭat takka toṉṟaṉṟō? orucila oliyaṭic coṟkaḷoḻinta ellāc coṟkaḷum ōraciyāṉa vērccoṟ kaḷiṉiṉṟē piṟantavi yātaliṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟākak koḷvārellām coṟpiṟappiyali eṭ ṭuṇiyum tāmaṟiyātiruttali muṟṟuṟak kāṭṭu pavarēyāvar.
kuṟṟukaravīṟu meyyīṟāyiṉ, aṟuvakik kuṟṟiyalukarat toṭar kaḷum valliṉa meyyīṟṟiṉavātal vēṇṭum.
ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa eṉṉum
appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti  (tol. 78)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar pirinili yēkāraṅ koṭuttuk kūṟiṉa miyāṉum, kuṟṟukaravīṟu tamiḻccollil ōriṭattum mey yīṟāka eḻutappaṭāmiyāṉum, aḵtuṇmiyaṉmi veḷ ḷiṭimali.
kaṇ + yātu = kaṇṇiyātu eṉpatu pōṉṟē cukku + yātu cukkiyātu eṉṟu kuṟṟukaramum puṇarvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟākak koḷḷalāmē yeṉiṉ, katavu + yātu = kataviyātu eṉṟu muṟṟukaramum aṅṅaṉam puṇarvatāl atuvum meyyīṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭu ukaravuyirē tamiḻukkilli yeṉṟākum eṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka, tolkāppiyar moḻi marapiyalil.
ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē  (tol. 75)
uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa  (tol. 76)
eṉṟu variyaṟuttatu ucu mucu tapu eṉṉum mūṉṟu muṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷiyē yaṉṟik kuṟṟukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷi yaṉṟu. cu, pu eṉṉum iru muṟṟukara īṟukaḷik koṇṭa coṟkaḷ ettaṉi eṉṟu variyaṟuppatē mēṟkūṟiya nūṟpākka ḷiṉ nōkkam, ku, ṭu, tu, ṟu eṉṉum nāṉku muṟṟukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum aṟuvakik kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum aḷaviṟan taṉavātaliṉ, avaṟṟi variyaṟuttilar tolkāppiyar. iku, uku, ceku, taku, toku, naku, neku, paku puku miku vaku viku eṉak kukaravīṟum, aṭu iṭu uṭu eṭu oṭu kaṭu keṭu koṭu cuṭu taṭu toṭu naṭu neṭu noṭu paṭu piṭu maṭu vaṭu viṭu eṉa ṭukaravīṟum koṇṭa muṟṟukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ peruntoki yiṉavāyum eṇ varampu paṭātaṉavāyu miruttal kāṇka. piṟa muṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum iṅṅaṉamē, kuṟṟukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷō veṉiṉ, akarātikaḷālum variya ṟukkappaṭāta pallāyirak kaṇakkiṉa.
mēṟkūṟiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉ uriyil,
ukārattōṭu kūṭiya cakāram iraṇṭu moḻikkē īṟām eṉṟavāṟu
e-ṭu : ucu, iḵtu uḷuviṉ peyar. mucu, itu kuraṅkiṉuḷ oru cāti . pacu eṉpatōveṉiṉ. aḵtu āriyac citivu. kaccu kuccu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukaram ēṟiya cakaram iru moḻikku īṟāmeṉavē ēṉi uyirkaḷ ēṟiya cakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāmāyiṟṟu. (tol. 75, uri) eṉṟum,
ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram oru moḻikkallatu paṉmoḻikku īṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar
e-ṭu: tapu eṉa varum..... uppu kappu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram, ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram oṉṟeṉavē ēṉiyuyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiya pakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāyp pala poruḷ tarumeṉṟā rāyiṟṟu (tol. 76, uri) eṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruttali nōkkuka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram uyirīṟē yeṉṟum, tolkāppiyar ōriṭattum kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟākak koṇṭila reṉṟum iyaṟkiyum eḷimiyum taṉimiyum tāymiyum cāṉṟa tamiḻiyalpi yaṟiyāta āriyavaḻi yiyalārē tam moḻiyip pōṉṟē tamiḻum āriya vaḻitteṉṟu mayaṅki, tamiḻi vaṭamoḻi vaḻittākak kāṭṭak karuti ataṟku mutaṟ paṭiyākak kuṟṟukara vīṟṟi meyyīṟākak kūṟiyiṭarp paṭṭup piṉpu av viṭarppāṭṭiṉiṉṟu nīṅkap paṇṭit tamiḻc coṟkaḷ valliṉa meyyilum iṟṟaṉa veṉṟum, valiyiraṭṭal eṉa oru tirivu muṟiyum aravu eṉa oru toḻiṟpeyar vikuti illiyeṉṟum colliyal nūlukkum moḻinūlukkum muṟṟum māṟākat tattam uḷantirintavāṟē urippareṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.  (tolkāppiyam eḻuttu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri 1944)
kuṟaṅkaṟuttal 
oru kālvāyiṉiṉṟu kiḷik kālvāy veṭṭutalukkuk kuṟaṅkaṟuttal eṉṟu peyar. kuṟaṅku - toṭi. ōr āṟum ataṉiṉṟu piriyum kālvāyum irutoṭi pōṉṟaṉa.
kuṟaḷ 
makkaḷuḷ mikak kuḷḷamāṉavaṉukkuk kuṟaḷaṉ eṉṟum ciṟitu kuḷḷamāṉavaṉukkuc cintaṉ eṉṟum, aḷavāka vaḷarntavaṉukku aḷavaṉ eṉṟum, neṭṭaṭiyāṉavaṉukku neṭiyaṉ eṉṟum, mika neṭṭiyāṉavaṉukkuk kaḻineṭiyaṉ eṉṟum peyar.
im muṟiyi oṭṭic ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷuḷ : iru cīraṭikkuk kuṟaḷ eṉṟum, muccīraṭikkuc cintu eṉṟum, nāṟcīraṭikku aḷavu allatu nēr eṉṟum, aiñcīr aṭikku neṭil eṉṟum, aṟu cīrum mēlum koṇṭa aṭikkuk kaḻineṭil eṉṟum peya riṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (col. 10)
kuṟikkōḷ vaki
koḷki mata nampikki allatu cittāntam. (Doctrine)
kōṭpāṭu ciṟu koḷki (Tenet)
kuṟikkōḷ kuṟittuk koṇṭa poruḷ (Object)
ēṭal contak karuttu (Idea)
neṟimuṟi oruvaṉ tāṉē amittuk koḷḷum niyati. (Principle)
nōkkam oru poruḷiṉ mēluḷḷa nāṭṭam. (Aim)
ilakku aṭiya vēṇṭiya elli (Goal)
ilakkam eyyum kuṟi (Target)
viti oḻukkam allatu moḻivarampu (Rule)
caṭṭam araciyalār amitta viti (Law)
mēlvariccaṭṭam, oruvar tām kiyāḷuvataṟkut tāmē amittuk koṇṭa tiṭṭa vākkiyam (Motto)  (col : 61.)
kuṟiñci (kuñci) viḷiyāṭṭu
potuvāka aivarkkuk kuṟiyāta pala piḷḷikaḷ variciyāka vō vaṭṭamākavō kūṭṭamākavō niṉṟu koṇṭu, avaruḷ orutti ēṉi ovvoruttiyiyum varicippaṭi cuṭṭi, oṇṇari, ṭūvari, ṭikkari, āvaṉ, cāvaṉ, iṅkilīṣ, meṉ, piḷuṉ, pōṭiṅ, ciṭ, eṉṟu colluvāḷ. ciṭ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa piḷḷi nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, mīṇṭum mīṇṭum pala taṭavi ic coṟkaḷ collappaṭum. ovvoru taṭaviyum ciṭ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa piḷḷi nīṅkiviṭa vēṇ ṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu ceyyiṉ iṟutiyil oru piḷḷi eñciniṟkum. avaḷ maṟṟavari ōṭit toṭa vēṇṭum.
āṭṭan toṭaṅkumuṉ kālāl oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum. toṭa vēṇṭiyavaḷ piṟarellām ōṭip pōy oḷintu koḷḷumvari, taṉ kaṇṇi mūṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ellārum oḷintukoṇṭapiṉ varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, avar varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ, toṭa vēṇṭiyavaḷ. aṅkumiṅkum ōṭip pōyp palaviṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭip pārp pāḷ. oḷintiruntavar ōṭivantu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟu viṭiṉ, avarit toṭal kūṭātu. vaṭṭam nōkki ōṭivarum pōtu, āṭi aviḻntuviṭṭālum kīḻē viḻuntu viṭṭālum, aṉ ṟum avarit toṭalkūṭātu. cilar, oḷintirāmalē nīṇṭa nēram āṭṭamkāṭṭi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vantu niṉṟu koḷvar.
vaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē toṭappaṭṭavaḷ, aṭutta muṟi muṉ coṉṉavāṟu piṟarit toṭal vēṇṭum.
vaṭṭattiṟkup patilāka ōr ellipporuḷ kuṟikkap paṭuvatu muṇṭu. av vellip poruḷit toṭṭu viṭṭālum, ataṉpiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu.
kuṟippolikaḷum kuṟippuc coṟkaḷum
Onomatopoetics and Frequentatives
 (1) 	oṟṟikkiḷavi	e.ṭu: caṭār, veḷḷeṉa, caraṭṭeṉṟu, potukkeṉṟu.
 (2) 	iraṭṭik kiḷavi. 	e.ṭu: kalakala, curucuru, maṭamaṭa.
 (3) 	etukik kiḷavi. 	e.ṭu: caṭṭuppuṭṭu (caṭpuṭ), caṭucaṭu, ālē pūlē, kāmā cōmar.
 (4) 	aṭukkuk kiḷavi. 	e.ṭu: paṭṭuppaṭṭu (paṭpaṭ), katakkuk katakku, kaṇīr kaṇīr.
 (5) 	iraṭṭitta kiḷavi. 	e.ṭu: cekkaccevēr, kaṉṉaṅ kariya.

kuṟippu
(1) 	iṅkuk kūṟiya kiḷavikaḷellām eṉaveṉ ṉeccamum eṉṟeṉ ṉeccamumākum.
(2) 	veḷḷeṉa, curucuru mutaliya coṟkaḷ cuṭṭaṭit tōṉṟi yavi; caṭār kalakala mutaliyavi oliyaṭit tōṉṟiyavi.
(3) 	aṭi aṭiyeṉṟaṭittu, kuṟukuṟu naṭantu eṉpavi ikkālat til aṭukkut toṭarkaḷāyiruppatu pōla, miṉumiṉu, kalakala eṉpavum mutaṟkālattil aṭukkut toṭarkaḷā yiruntaṉa.
(4) 	veṭi, kuttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ cuṭṭum oliyumākiya īraṭip piṟappiṉa.  (mu.tā.)

kuṟiyetirppi 
kuṟiyetirppiyāvatu aḷavu kuṟittuk koṭuttu avvaḷavil tirumpap peṟuṅ kaṭaṉ. (ti.ma. 134)
kuṟiyoli
icivu veṟuppu mutaliya maṉanilikaḷi yuṇarttaṟku, vāyālum mūkkālum icikkum aṭiyāḷa volikaḷ kuṟiyolikaḷākum.
e-ṭu :
1. vāyoli
ccu eṉpatu pōṉṟa oliyi vāyālicippatu, piṟar kūṟṟi oppukkoḷvatiyum oppukkoḷḷāmal, ikaḻvatiyuṅ kuṟikkum. itu uccukkoṭṭal eṉappaṭum. maṟuppoli uṭaṉpāṭṭoliyiṉum valiyatāyum mukakkuṟippōṭu kūṭiyatāyumirukkum.
kiyāl vāyilaṭittuk koṇṭu ā veṉṟu āravārittal veṟṟikkuṟiyākum. itu āvalaṅ koṭṭal eṉappaṭum. (valam = veṟṟi.)
2. mūkkoli
vāy mūṭiyirukkum niliyil, ūm eṉpatu pōṉṟa oliyi mūkkālolippatu, uṭaṉpāṭṭik kuṟikkum. itu ummeṉal allatu ūṅkoṭṭal eṉappaṭum. itu taṉitta niliyil uṭaṉpāṭṭiyum iraṭṭiya niliyil maṟuppiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
iṉi, kural vēṟupāṭṭālum māttiri vēṟupāṭṭālum aciyaḻutta vēṟupāṭṭālum, ūṅkoṭṭal viyappu iḻippu veṟuppu vekuḷi mutaliya pala kuṟippukkaḷiyum uṇarttum. aṉṟu atataṟkuriya mukakkuṟippōṭu kūṭivarum.
iṉi, vilaṅkukaḷiyum paṟavikaḷiyum viḷikkap payaṉ paṭuttum olikaḷum orucār kuṟiyolikaḷē.
āṭṭi aḻikkum oli 	- 	pāy!
māṭṭi aḻikkum oli	- 	pā!
 nāyi aḻikkum oli	- 	cū cū! tō tō! tuvā tuvā!
 kōḻiyi aḻikkum oli	- 	pē pē!
iṉi, cariyāy olittukkāṭṭa muṭiyātaṉavum moḻikkup payaṉ paṭātaṉavu māṉa, ettuṇiyō kuṟiyolikaḷ uḷaveṉa aṟika.   (mu.tā.)
kuṟil neṭil 
uyireḻuttukkaḷil kuṟil neṭil eṉpaṉa kuṟuvāḻkki uyiriyum neṭu vāḻkki uyiriyum uṇarttum. (col. 34)

keṭavaral 
ōr iḷaṅkaṉṉikkuk kaḷiṟu puli mutaliya viḷaṅkukaḷālum āḻnīrālum nēravirunta kēṭṭi, taṟceyalāka avvaḻi vanta kāḷip paruvattāṉoruvaṉ nīkkiya ceytiyic ciṟār naṭittāṭuvatu keṭavaral.... (ta.vi.muka.)
kēṭṭal

kēṭutaruvaṉa 
vaṟumi, nōy, aṟaṅkaṭi (pāvam), paḻi, aṟiyāmi, paki mutaliyaṉa. (kuṟaḷ : 832)
kikkiḷi 
kikkiḷiyāvatu, āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya iruvaruḷḷum oruvarukkē kātal iruppatu; ...... oru talik kāmam eṉṟum collap peṟum. ki eṉpatu pakkam. kiḷi eṉpatu nēyam. ākavē kikkiḷi eṉpatu oru talik kātal.
kikkiḷikkum peruntiṇikkum, nila millātapaṭi aivaki nilaṅkaḷi yum, naṭunāyakamāṉa aṉpiṉ aintiṇikkē urimi yākki viṭṭataṉāl, makkaṭku nalam payavāta ēṉiyirumaṇa muṟikaḷum innilavulakattillātu nīṅkal vēṇṭum eṉpatu, muṉṉit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāriṉ karuttākum. (ta.ti. 15, 16)
kikkiḷi maṇam 
maṇamakkaḷiṉ icivik kēḷātu peṟṟōrē muṭittu vikkum tirumaṇaṅkaḷil, maṇamakaṉukku allatu maṇa makaḷukku maṭṭum kātal iruppiṉ, atu kikkiḷi maṇamām.
maṇamakkaḷ iruvaruḷ oruvarukku maṭṭum kātal irukkum pōtu, ēṉiyavarum etirppiṉṟi icintiruntāltāṉ, atu kikkiḷi maṇamākum.
kātaliṉṟiyum icintu varum peṇṇi āṭavaṉum āṭa vaṉip peṇṇum maṇappatēyaṉṟi, iciyāp peṇṇiyum āṭavaṉiyum valitil maṇappataṉṟu. (ta.ti. 16, 17.)

koṅkaṇam
koṅkaṇam - kōṅkāṇa

koṅkaṇavaṉ
yārēṉum oruvar pētit taṉmiyākap pēciṉāl avarip piṟar koṅkaṇavaṉ (koṅkaṇiyaṉ) eṉpatu vaḻakkam.
koṅkaṇavaṉ eṉṉum peyariyuṭiya muṉivar tam tava valimiyāl oru kokkik koṉṟu vīḻttiyapiṉ makkaḷi yum, aṅṅaṉamē cāvikkalām eṉṟu karuti, tamakku aiyamiṭak kālan tāḻttu vanta vācukiyammiyāri uṟuttu nōkka, avvammiyār kokkeṉṟu niṉittiyō koṅkaṇavā? eṉṟu kaṭintār. ataṉpiṉ ammuṉivar kaṟpuṭi maṅkiyariṉ perumiyi uṇarntu avvammiyāriṭam maṉṉippuk kēṭṭuc ceṉṟār eṉpatu varalāṟu.  (col. 30.)
koṅkunāṭṭu aracukaḷ
atikar (atikamāṉar), kaṅkar, kaṭṭiyar eṉṉum mummarapiṉa rum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattiliruntu 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari, pēraracarkku aṭaṅkiyum aṭaṅkātum, koṅkunāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷi āṇṭuvantaṉar.
cōḻar āṭci - 1004 - 1303.
iṭiyiṟ pāṇṭiyar mīyāṭci - 1365 - 1300.
cōḻarāṭcikkup piṉ, kēraḷarāṭciyum oycaḷarāṭciyum vica yanakara nāyakkarāṭciyum uṭiyārāṭciyum aitarali tippu culuttāṉāṭciyum, cicciṟitu kālam naṭipeṟṟaṉa.
āṅkilarāṭci - 1799 - 1947.
intiya viṭutaliyum oṉṟiyamum (Union) - 1947.
kēraḷanāṭṭuc ciṟṟariyaṅkaḷum intiya oṉṟiyattiṟ kalantaṉa.
koñcam 
koñcam - kiñcit
kuñcu = ciṟiyatu, paṟavik kuñcu. kuñcu - kuñci = ciṟiyatu, paṟavik kuñcu. kuñcippeṭṭi = ciṟupeṭṭi. kuñciyappaṉ = ciṟṟappaṉ. kuñcu - kuñcaṉ = ciṟiyavaṉ, kuṟaḷaṉ.
kuñcu - koñcu - koñcam - kiñcam = ciṟitu.
kiñcam - kiñcit (va.) (va.va : 131)
koṭi 
āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya irupālaruḷ peṇṭu mika melliyaḷ ātaliṉ koṭi eṉappaṭuvāḷ. itaṉālēyē kuṟiñci nilap peṇṭiṟkuk koṭicci eṉṟu peyar.
peṇṇik koṭiyeṉa uvami ākupeyarāṟ kuṟippatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, peṇkoṭi eṉa uruvaka vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟuva tum uṇṭu. (col. 2.)
melliyatum virintu vaḷarvatum oru koḷkompip paṟṟippaṭarvatum koṭiyē. peṇṇik koṭi eṉṟu coṉṉa aḷavilēyē avaḷ āṭavaṉilum melliyaḷ eṉṟum, avaṉilum virintu vaḷarpavaḷ eṉṟum, oru koḻu naṉit tuṇik koṇṭē vāḻpavaḷ eṉṟum mūṉṟu peṇ pāṟ kuṇaṅkaḷ kuṟippāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  (col. 2)
koṭikaṭṭip paṟattal 
oruvar tam conta muyaṟciyāl perum poruḷ īṭṭiyatip pārāṭṭum pōtu kōṭiyum tēṭik koṭimaramum naṭṭi eṉṟu kūṟuvatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakkam. itaṉāl paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil kōṭi tokuttavarellām koṭikaṭṭip paṟakkaviṭ ṭaṉar eṉpatu teriyavarukiṉṟatu.  (col. 25.)
koṭiyar 
koṭiyarāvār, uṇarātu koli ceyvār, ūril tī vippār, kuṭinīr niliyil nañciṭuvār, vaḻippaṟippār, koḷḷiyiṭuvār, kōyil cottik kaḷavu ceyvār, veḷippaṭiyākap piṟaṉil viḻivār, aracaṉukkum añcātār mutaliyōr.  (ti.ma. 316)
koṭi vaki 
ivar koṭi ēṟippaṭarvatu (Climber) paṭarkoṭi - nilattil paṭarvatu (Creeper).
ivar koṭi vaki.
valantiri valamākac cuṟṟi ēṟuvatu; iṭantiri iṭamākac cuṟṟi ēṟuvatu. (col. 70)
koṭi - ciṟiyatu.
patāki allatu paṭāki - periyatu. (col. 41)
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭiyum.
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭaṅ kuṭaṅkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
paṉṟi yaruvā vataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ
eṉṉum veṇpāviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉavākak kūṟuvar pala uriyāciriyar. iviyellām mutaṟkālattil centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭamiyiṉ, ivvuri poruntātu. in nāṭukaḷuḷ vēṇāṭu puṉaṉāṭu eṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkup patilāka, poṅkarnāṭu oḷināṭu eṉpavaṟṟik kuṟippar iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar mutaliyōr, poṅkar nāṭṭip potuṅkar nāṭeṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
tāyit taḷḷi eṉpa kuṭanāṭṭār. nāyi ñamali eṉpa pūḻināṭṭār eṉṟu ticiccoṟkuk kāṭṭuk kūṟiṉar iḷampūraṇar. iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa irunāṭukaḷum ippōtu maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷāka vuḷḷaṉa.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar piṉvarumāṟu ticiccoṟkuk kāṭṭuk kūṟiṉar :
teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā erumi eṉpaṉavaṟṟip peṟṟam eṉṟum; kuṭanāṭṭār tāyit taḷḷi eṉṟum, nāyi ñeḷḷi eṉṟum; kuṭṭanāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉ eṉṟum; kaṟkānāṭṭār vañcarik kiyar eṉṟum; cītanāṭṭār ēṭā veṉpataṉi eluvaṉ eṉṟum; tōḻiyi ikuḷi eṉṟum; tammāmiyeṉpataṉit tantuvi eṉṟum; pūḻi nāṭṭār nāyi ñamali eṉṟum; ciṟukuḷattip pāḻi eṉṟum; aruvānāṭṭār ceyyic ceṟu eṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattik kēṇi eṉṟum; aruvā vaṭataliyār kuṟuṇiyik kuṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.
iṉic ciṅkaḷam antōveṉpatu; karunaṭaṅ kariya cikka kuḷira eṉpaṉa; vaṭuku ceppeṉpatu; teluṅku eruttip pāṇṭi leṉpatu; tuḷu māmarattik kokkeṉpatu. oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum vantuḻik kāṇka.
itaṉāl teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavi orukālattil tamiḻiṉ ticivaḻakkukaḷākavē yiruntaṉa veṉṟum, piṉpu kiḷi vaḻakkukaḷāki, iṟutiyil vaṭacoṟ kalappāl vēṟu moḻi kaḷāyp pirintuviṭṭaṉa veṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
teyvacciliyārum ik karutti yoṭṭiyē.
centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta paṉṉiru nilattu muḷḷōr tattaṅ kuṟippiṉiyuṭiya ticic collākiya col eṉṟavāṟu.
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa : viyiyāṟṟiṉ... nāṭu, oḷināṭu, teṉ pāṇṭināṭu, karuṅkuṭṭanāṭu, kuṭanāṭu, paṉṟināṭu, kaṟkānāṭu, cītanāṭu, pūḻināṭu, malāṭu, aruvānāṭu, aruvā vaṭatali eṉpa. ivi centamiḻ nāṭṭakatta. centamiḻ (cērnta) nāṭeṉ ṟamiyāl, piṟanāṭākal vēṇṭumeṉpār eṭuttukkāṭṭu māṟu : kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭaṟ paḻantīpam kollam kūpakam ciṅkaḷa meṉṉum elliyiṉ puṟattavum, kaṉṉaṭam vaṭukam kaliṅkam teliṅkam koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟakam eṉpaṉa kuṭapāliru puṟacciyattuṭaṉuṟipu kūṟun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum, muṭiyuṭiyavariṭu nilavāṭciyiṉ aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭumikaḷ patiṉmarum uṭaṉiruppiru varuvākiya paṉṉiruvar aracarum paṭitta paṉṉiru tēyattiṉum, tamiḻc collātaṟku viruppuṭiyaṉa eṉṟamiyāṉum, tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu.... poruḷum nāṭi eṉa niṟuttup piṉṉuñ centamiḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉa ōtiyavataṉāṟ civaṇiya nilamāvatu elli kuṟitta nilattic cārnta nilameṉa vēṇṭutalāṉum, paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa :
kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kūpakamum kollamum kaṭalkoḷḷap paṭutaliṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyik kollameṉak kuṭiyēṟiṉār pōlum. pañcattirāviṭameṉavum vaṭanāṭṭār urippavāka lāṉ, aviyintum vēṅkaṭattiṉ teṟkātaluṅ kūṭāmi yuṇarka.
annilattu vaḻaṅkuñ collākic ceñcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattu varuvaṉa nīkkappaṭā eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṭāvaṭiyuḷiyam eṉṟavaḻi, kuṭāvaṭi eṉpatu kuṭakattār piḷḷikaṭku iṭṭa peyar. antō eṉpatu ciṅkaḷavar aiyō eṉpataṟkiṭṭa peyar. yāṉ taṟkariya varutu eṉṟa vaḻi karital eṉpatu karunāṭar viḷipporuḷuṇarak kūṟuvatu. ceppu eṉpatu vaṭukar collutaṟkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. pāṇṭil eṉpatu teluṅkar pacuviṟkum eruttiṟkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. kokku eṉpatu tuḷuvar māvukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa palavaṟṟiyum vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa mutaliyavi piṟkālattil piṟamoḻikaḷāyp pirintupōṉapiṉpu, avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉi niliyiyuṇarātu avi vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷiyum ayaṉmoḻi nāṭukaḷiyuñ cērttuk koṭuntamiḻnāṭākak kūṟiṉar pavaṇantiyār,
centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi
 eṉṟu. tamiḻoḻinta patiṉeṇ nilaṅkaḷi,
ciṅkaḷañ cōṉakañ cāvakañ cīṉan tuḷukkuṭakaṅ
koṅkaṇaṅ kaṉṉaṭaṅ kollan teluṅkaṅ kaliṅkamvaṅkaṅ
kaṅka makataṅ kaṭāraṅ kaṭavuṅ kaṭuṅkucalan
taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuvi tāmiviyē
 eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷāṉaṟika.
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavi tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉa piṉpē, poṅkarnāṭu oḷināṭu mutaliya paḻañcentamiḻ nāṭṭup pakutikaḷ koṭun tamiḻnāṭākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, muṉpu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭāyiruntavi pala piṉpu piṟa moḻi nāṭāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpatum, centamiḻ nāṭu varavarat teṟku nōkki oṭuṅkikkoṇṭē varukiṉṟa teṉpatum, itaṟkuk kāraṇam āriyak kalappum centamiḻt toṭar piṉmiyum eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
ceppu eṉpatu, collutal eṉṉum poruḷil maṭṭum ticic collākum. maṟṟappaṭi atu centamiḻc collē eṉpatu.
ceppum viṉāvum vaḻāal ōmpal  (kiḷavi. 13)
aḵtaṉ ṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē (cey. 78)
 eṉpavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
ceppu eṉpatu, teluṅkil collutaliyum, tamiḻil viṭi collu taliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. atōṭu tamiḻilakkaṇan tōṉṟiya toṉmutu kālattilēyē. ceppu eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭāyamintatiyum nōkkuka. (ceppal veṇpā vōci.)
ī tā koṭu eṉṉum mūṉṟu centamiḻc coṟkaḷil, ī eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam taṉ nuṇporuḷiyiḻantu teluṅkil vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatō, aṅṅaṉamē ceppu eṉpatum eṉka.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya ayaṉmoḻic col vaṭacol oṉṟē. ataṉālēyē atu taṉ peyarāl vaṭa col eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉpaṭi ippōtu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām. āṅkilac col, intustāṉic col eṉa avvam moḻippeyarālēyē kūṟal vēṇṭum. tirāviṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum ticic collākavē kūṟappaṭum.
tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷum, arukiya vaḻakkēyaṉṟip perukiya vaḻakkaṉṟu. avvarukiya vaḻakkum vēṇṭāmiyāyt tamiḻik keṭuttaṟkeṉṟē pukuttappaṭṭatākum.
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu, vaṭacol mēṉmēluṅ kalantu tamiḻt tūymiyik keṭukkātavāṟu oruvāṟu taṭi ceytatē yaṉṟi, ikkālattuc cilar eṇṇuvatu pōla vaṭacolliyum piṟacolliyum tārāḷamāyc cērttukkoḷḷumāṟu viṭitantataṉṟu.
koṭumpāvi 
nallavar poruṭṭu maḻi poḻintu nāṭu ceḻikkiṟateṉṟum, tīyavariyiṭṭu maḻi poyttup pañca muṇṭākiṟateṉṟum paṇṭiyōr karutiṉar. maḻi poytta kālattil ataṟkuk kāraṇam oru koṭumpāvi eṉṟu karuti ūrukkuḷ mikattīyavaṉ eṉṟu pēr peṟṟa oruvaṉik kaṭṭi ūrukku veḷiyē iḻuttuk koṇṭupōy uyirōṭu erittu viṭuvatu mutupaṇṭi vaḻakkam. itaṟkuk koṭumpāvi kaṭṭiyiḻuttal eṉṟu peyar.
nākarikam mikka ikkālattil koṭumpāvi kaṭṭi iḻuppa taṟkup patilākac cūntu kaṭṭit terut teruvāka iḻuttu ūrukku veḷiyē koṇṭu pōyk koḷutti viṭukiṉṟaṉar. (col. 26.)
 koṭi maṇam 
maṇamakaṉēṉum avaṉ peṟṟōrēṉum maṇamakaḷ peṟ ṟōri aṭuttuk kēṭka, avar koṭuppatu. atu (i) tāṉak koṭi (ii) vilik koṭi (iii) nilip pāṭṭuk koṭi eṉa mūvakittu.
tāṉakkoṭiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷit takka ēḻi maṇāḷaṉukkut tāmē ellāc celavum ēṟṟu maṇañceytu vippatu; vilik koṭiyāvatu, oruvaṉ taṉ makaḷukku īṭṭāka oru tokiyi oru celvaṉiṭam peṟṟukkoṇṭu avaḷi avaṉukku maṉiviyākak koṭuppatu; nilippāṭṭuk koṭiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷi oruvaṉukku ētēṉum nilippāṭu allatu akkuttup (Condition) paṟṟi maṉiviyākak koṭuppatu annilippāṭu, ittuṇip parican taral vēṇṭu meṉṟum, ittuṇikkālam peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōrkku uḻittal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, iṉṉa maṟac ceyalip purital vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṇamakaḷi iṉṉa kaliyil vellutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṇattiṉpiṉ peṇ vīṭṭilēyē vatital vēṇṭumeṉṟum, palvēṟu tiṟappaṭṭatāyirukkum.
maṟac ceyalkaḷ, kollēṟu kōṭal (ēṟu taḻuval), tiripaṉṟi yeytal, pulippāl kaṟattal, koṭu vilaṅku kōṟal, pakivari yaḻittal vil nāṇēṟṟal, peruṅkal tūkkal mutaliyaṉa. (ta.ti. 3,4)
koṭi vaki 
aiyam irappōrkkiṭuvatu; kinnīṭṭam vēlikkārarkku viḻā nāḷil koṭuppatu; īvu iṟivaṉ piṟappil oruvaṉuk kaḷittavaram; paricu allatu paricil tiṟami kaṇṭaḷippatu; koṭi uyarntōrkkum ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷukkum koṭuppatu; naṉkoṭi (māṉipam) viḷivi uṇṇumpaṭi koṭukkum nilam; kaṭṭaḷi kōyiṟkuṟippiṭṭa nikaḻccikku viṭum nipantam; uṟāvari allatu muṟṟūṭṭu muḻuvurimiyāka aḷikkum nilam; iṟiyili aṟattiṟku variyillātu viṭappaṭṭa nilam; tāṉam aṭiyārkkum pārppārkkum aḷippatu; paricam maṇappeṇṇukkaḷippatu; vaṇmi variyātaḷippatu; kurupūci aṭiyārkkup paṭikkum viruntu. (col. 46)
koṇṭāṭutal 
koṇṭāṭutal eṉpatu, oru poruḷik kiyiṟ paṟṟi allatu talimēṟ koṇṭu kūttāṭutal.
tanti taṉ kuḻantiyiyum, pattaṉ tāṉ vaḻipaṭum kaṭavuḷ uru viyum, kiyil ēntik koṇṭu allatu talimēṟ koṇṭu makiḻcciyāl kūttāṭutalē koṇṭāṭutal.
tiruviḻāk kālattil teyva uruvi allatu ataṉoṭu toṭar puḷḷa oru poruḷik kiyil ēnti allatu talimēṟ koṇṭu kūttāṭutal iṉṟum vaḻakkamāy iruntalāl viḻāc ceytalukkuk koṇṭāṭutal eṉṟu peyar. viḻāvukku maṟupeyar koṇṭāṭṭam. cila iṭaṅkaḷil maruḷāḷiyi allatu tēvarāḷaṉic cāmi koṇṭāṭi eṉpar.
teyvattiyum kuḻantiyiyum koṇṭāṭic ciṟappuc ceyta lum virumpiyaṉa koṭuttalum pōla, oruvarip pārāṭṭic ciṟappippatum koṇṭāṭutal eṉappaṭum.
kuḻantiyum teyvamum koṇṭāṭum iṭattilē eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyil koṇṭāṭum eṉṉum col, coṟporuḷum aṇipporuḷum ākiya iraṇṭiyum tāṅki niṟṟal kāṇka.  (col. 11)
kottamalli 
 kottamalli - kustumparu
 kottumalli - kottamalli.  (va.va: 131)
koppari 
 koppari - karppara
 koppari - kappari - karppara (va.)  (va.va: 131)
koḻuntuvaki 
tuḷir allatu taḷir nel pul mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ koḻuntu; muṟi allatu koḻuntu puḷi vēmpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ koḻuntu; kuruttucōḷam karumpu teṉṉi paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ koḻuntu.
koḻuntāṭi karumpiṉ nuṉip pakuti.  (col : 66)
koḷnaṉ 
koḷkompu koḷnaṉ eṉṟa irupeyarkaḷum koḷ eṉṉum colliyē mutaṉiliyākak koṇṭiruppatum, muṟiyē koḻu kompu, koḻunaṉ eṉa maruvit tirivatum kavaṉikkattakkaṉa. koḷ kompu eṉpatil koḷḷutal toḻil koṭiyiṉatākavum koḷnaṉ eṉpatil kaṇavaṉa tākavum koḷḷappaṭum.  (col. 3)
kō 
kō - kō (g), kus (g) - i. vē.
kō = ā. kōvaṉ = 1; māṭṭiṭiyaṉ, iṭiyaṉ (āyaṉ).
kōvaṉ niri mīṭṭiṉaṉ (cīvaka. 455).
2. aracaṉ. kōvaṉu makkaḷum (cīvaka. 1843).
kuṭikaḷ āṭukaḷum aracaṉ mēyppaṉum pōlak karutappeṟṟa tiṉāl, aracaṉ kōvaṉ eṉap peṟṟāṉ. aracaṉ ceṅkōl āyaṉ kikkōluk koppām. (va.va:)
kōcam - kōca = uruṇṭi.
kōḷam - kōcam = muṭṭi (piṅ.) ḷa-ca, pōli. o. nō. uḷu - ucu. iḷi - ici. (va.va: 132)
kōcar
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil, kōcar eṉṟoru vakuppār mūvēntarkkum paṭittalivarākavum, tamiḻakattil āṅkāṅku vevvēṟu ciṟṟaracarākavum iruntami, paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷā lum ceyyuṭkaḷālum aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. iv vakuppārip paṟṟi aṟiñariṭi palvēṟu karuttukaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.
kālañceṉṟa rā. irākaviyaṅkār, tam kōcar eṉṉum ārāyccic cuvaṭiyil, kōcarāvār kācumīra nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vēḷiriyaṭuttuk kōcāmpiyit talinakarākakkoṇṭa vatta (vatsa) nāṭṭu vaḻiyāyt tamiḻnāṭu pōntavar eṉṟum; vattam allatu vaccam (vatsam) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku iḷamip poruḷuṇmiyālum avar kōcam eṉṉum ōr ariya cūḷ muṟiyik kiyāṇṭatiṉālum, iḷaṅkōcar eṉṉap paṭṭirukkalāmeṉṟum kōcar eṉṉum peyarukkuk kōcam eṉṉum cūḷmuṟiyaṉṟi, kōcam (kōcāmpi) eṉṉum nakarp peyarum, kucar eṉṉum āṭṭup peyarum, kōcam (tiraviyam) eṉṉum celvappeyarum, kāraṇamāyirukkalā meṉṟum kōcar mutaṟkaṇ koṅkil vatintu piṉpu kuṭakiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya var eṉṟum; akuti, titiyaṉ, kuṟumpiyaṉ, ātaṉeḻiṉi, taḻumpaṉ mutaliya kuṟunila maṉṉar kōcar eṉṟum: itu pōtuḷḷa kikkōḷarum ceṅkuntarum kōcar marapiṉar eṉṟum; piṟavum; tam ārāycci muṭipākak kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭēyaṉṟi ellām uṇmiyalla.
orucār aṟiñar, kōcari vampa mōriyar paṭikku muṉṉaṇi yāka vanta vaṭukarākavuṅ koḷvar. ituvum uṇmi aṉṟu.
kōcar kācumīrattiṉiṉṟō vatta nāṭṭiṉiṉṟō vantavar eṉpataṟku oruvaki varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟumilli. avar toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntava reṉpatē, paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyattāl teriyavarukiṉṟatu. tamiḻukkum tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭiṟkum māṟāṉatoṉṟum avarippaṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli.

cērar koṅkuvi kāvūrna ṉāṭital
eṉṟu aruṇakirinātar pāṭiyiruppatālum, takaṭūrāṇṭa atikamāṉkuṭi varalāṟṟālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil, muttamiḻnāṭṭilum āṅkāṅkirunta kuṟunila maṉṉar tattam valimikka kālattu, tattam vēntar talimiyiṉiṉṟu tiṟampiyatoṭu avar nāṭṭiyum kippaṟṟiṉar eṉpataṟku.
vāymoḻi niliiya cēṇviḷaṅku nallici
vaḷaṅkeḻu kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭi nūṟi
nilaṅkoḷa veḵkiya polampūṭ kiḷḷi  (akam. 205)
tittaṉ uṟanti  (akam. 122)
eṉpaṉa cāṉṟām. im muṟiyē, koṅkum tuḷuvum kōcar vayiṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kuṭakak koṅkar eṉa varantaru kātiyiṟ kuṟittatu. orukāl, kuṭaku nāṭṭuk kōcari nōkkiyatāyirukkalām. tuḷuvum, kuṭakum oru kālattil kuṭakoṅkup pakutikaḷāka viruntaṉa. kuṭakak koṅkar eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum kūṟutal kāṇka.
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭak kalliṭikkuṟiccit tirumāl kōvilukkuk kōcarkuṭi perumāḷ kōvil eṉṟu peyar. atē māvaṭṭattuk kaḻukumaliyil oru teruviṟkuk kōcark kuṭit teru eṉṟu peyar.
kōcaruṭ peruntamiḻp pāvalarāṉa nallicip pulavarum iruntaṉar eṉpati. cellūrkkōcaṉār (akam. 66). karuvūrk kōcaṉār (naṟṟiṇi. 214) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ kāṭṭum.
iviyellām, kōcar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ koṅkil maṭṭumaṉṟi eṅkum vatintavar eṉṟum, avar tamiḻarē eṉṟum, teri vikkum.
vēta āciriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukumuṉ nāvalantēya muḻuvatum tamiḻarum avar iṉattārāṉa tiraviṭarumē paraviyiruntatiṉāl, akattiyar (kācumīrat) tuvarāvatiyiṉiṉṟu patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷirit teṉṉāṭu koṇṭuvantaṉa reṉpatum, kācumīra nāṭṭu varalāṟu kūṟum irācataraṅkaṇiyil an nāṭṭār kōcam eṉṉum oru cūḷmuṟiyik kiyāṇṭami kūṟappaṭṭirup patum, kōcanāṭu eṉap peyariya cuṉittaṭam kācumīra nāṭṭi luṇmiyum, utayaṇaṉ tāy civaṉaṭiyār aṟupattumūvaruḷ oruvarāṉa ēyarkōṉ kalikkāma nāyaṉāriṉ kulamāyiruntatum, kōcar vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavar eṉak kāṭṭum cāṉṟākā.
mūlappaṭi, kūlippaṭi, nāṭṭuppaṭi, kāṭṭuppaṭi, pakip paṭi, tuṇippaṭi, eṉa ivvirupālākap pakukkappaṭum aṟuvakip paṭiyuḷ; nāṭṭup paṭi eṉpatu paṭiyāṭci kikkōḷar ceṅkuntar paṭikaḷiyum, kāṭṭuppaṭi eṉpatu kaḷḷar maṟavar paṭikaḷiyum kuṟikkum. paṭittalivar iyal pākat tattam paṭimaṟavar pāṅkākavē vativarātaliṉ, vēntari aṭuttum avar talinakarilum eṉṟumirunta paṭittalivar nāṭṭuppaṭittalivarē,
vēḷāṇ māntark kuḻutūṇ alla
tilleṉa moḻipa piṟavaki nikaḻcci  (tol marapiyal. 80)
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṟ paṭiyuṅ kaṇṇiyum
vāyntaṉar eṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē  (tol marapiyal. 81)

villum vēlum kaḻalum kaṇṇiyum
tārum māliyum tērum vāḷum  (tol. marapiyal. 83)

koṭiyum piṟavik kuṇam
eṉṉum auviyār kūṟṟum.
aṉpilā rellān tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭiyār
eṉpu muriyar piṟarkku  (kuṟaḷ. 172)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar kūṟṟum, aṉpu piṟavikkuṇam eṉpatiyum, aḵtuṭiyār tammāl aṉpu ceyyappeṟṟōrkku uyiri yum utavuvar eṉpatiyum, terivikkum.
iṟivaṉaṭiyār tam tiruvaṭimit toṇṭil tam uyiriyum viṭa aṇiyamāy (tayārāy) iruppatupōṉṟē aracappaṟṟu mikka paṇiyāḷarum tam aracaṉporuṭṭu eṉṟum uyirviṭa iruppar. ivvulakattu nīṭuvāḻntu illaṟiṉpan tuykka virumpum potuppaṇiyāḷar maṉaniliyē iḵtāyiṉ, pōrkkaḷattu maṭitalip poṉṉulakam pukutalākak koṇṭu kūṟṟuvaṉiyum aṟikūvum, koṟṟavikkut tammittāmē paliyiṭṭut tam taliyit tam valakkiyilēntik kūttāṭavum valla taṟukaṇ maṟavar niliyic collavum vēṇṭumō!
paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻaraciṉmiyālum, kaḻi pala nūṟṟāṇṭākap piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyamuṟik kulap piriviṉiyālum, tamiḻar perumpālum maṟamiḻantirukkum iṟṟinili nōkki, paṇṭit tamiḻar maṟampaṟṟiyum cilar aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyalilum paṭipaṟṟiya tirukkuṟa ḷatikāraṅkaḷiyum, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyiyum nampāviṭiṉum, cila puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaḷiyum, karikālvaḷavaṉ, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ mutaliyōriṉ vaṭanāṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppiyum, mutalām irācarācaṉ, mutalām irācēntiraṉ mutaliyōriṉ meykkīrttiyiyum, kaliṅkattup paraṇiyiyum, nōkkiyēṉum tam karuttit tiruttik koḷvārāka.
paṇṭit tamiḻppaṭi maṟavaruḷ, pōriṟ puṟaṅkoṭukkak kūṭāteṉṟum aracaṉukkāka uyirit tuṟattal vēṇṭumeṉ ṟum cūḷiṭṭuk koṇṭa orucārār iruntaṉar. avar pūṭki maṟavar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉar. pūṭki, uṟuti pūṇutal, avar pōṉṟē, aracaṉukku avvappōtu vēṇṭum paṇikaḷit tappātu ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟum, tappiṉ uyir tuṟappateṉ ṟum, cūḷiṭṭukkoṇṭa orucār paṇiyāḷar iruntaṉar. avar vēḷikkārar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉar. vēḷi tavaṟātu paṇi ceypavar vēḷikkārar. avar talivaṉ vēḷikkāra nāyakam eṉṉappaṭṭāṉ.
ōṭāp pūṭki viṭali  (puṟam. 295)
pōreṉiṟ pukalum puṉikaḻaṉ maṟavar  (puṟam. 31)
uṭpaki yorutiṟam paṭṭeṉap puṭpakik
kēvā ṉākaliṟ cāvēm yāmeṉa
nīṅkā maṟavar vīṅkutōḷ puṭippat
taṇipaṟi yaṟiyu maṇikoḷ tērvaḻi  (puṟam. 68)
eṉṉum pakutikaḷ, paṇṭit tamiḻ maṟavariṉ mēṟkōḷiyum pōr viruppattiyum teḷivākap pulappaṭuttum.
pūṭki, mēṟkōḷ, kōḷ eṉpaṉa oruporuṭcoṟkaḷ, oṉṟi uṟutiyākak kaṭippiṭippateṉa mēṟkoḷvatu mēṟkōḷ. aṅṅaṉamē uḷḷattil allatu ceyaṉmuṟiyiṟ koḷvatu kōḷ.
māṭciyiṉ mācaṟṟār kōḷ  (kuṟaḷ. 646)
 eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
koḷvatum pūṇuvatum mēṟkoḷvatum naṉṟiṟkum tītiṟkum potuvēṉum, kōḷ pūṭki mēṟkōḷ eṉṉum muc col lum vaḻakkāṟṟil naṟkoḷkiyiyē kuṟikkum.
pūṭki maṟavarum avar talivarum uṟutiyāṉa kōḷuṭimi paṟṟik kōḷar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉar. ki eṉpatu paṭiyuṟuppāta liṉ, paṭiyuṟuppic cērnta kōḷar kikkōḷar eṉṉap paṭṭaṉar. ēṉeṉil, paṭiyuṟuppic cērātu taṟcārpāṉa kōḷarumiruntaṉar (free lance) :
oppaṉa paṭiyuṟup poḻukkam ciṟumi
karamum piṉpiṟan tāḷum kiyē
 eṉpatu piṅkalam.
perumpulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkār, iṭaṅki valaṅki eṉpaṉa paṭivakuppukaḷ eṉṟum, avaṟṟic cērnta kōḷar kikkōḷar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iṭaṅki valaṅki eṉpaṉa paṭi vakuppukaḷēyaṉṟi, aracaṉ amarn tirukkumpōtum cellumpōtum iṭattilum valattilum irukkumāṟum cellumāṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭa kulaṅkaḷiyē kuṟittalāṉum, iṭaṅkiyic cērntavar valakkiyiyum, valaṅkiyic cērntavar iṭaṅkiyiyum cērāmiyāṉum, atu poruntāteṉka. ki eṉpatu paṭivakuppātaliṉ, uṟutiyāṉa oḻukkattik kikoṇṭavar kikkōḷar eṉpatum poruntātu.
aṟamporuḷ iṉpam uyiraccam nāṉkiṉ
tiṟamterintu tēṟap paṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 501)
eṉṉum muṟipaṟṟiyō, vēṟuvakiyilō, maṟam uṇmi paṇivu naṉṟiyaṟivu, aṉpu, kaṭippiṭi, valimi mutaliya paṇpiṟ ciṟantavarāka vēntarāl terinteṭukkappeṟṟa kikkōḷar, terinta kikkōḷar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉar. parāntakaṉ terinta kikkōḷar, cuntaracōḻar teriñcakikkōḷar, pāṇṭiya kulācaṉi teriñca kikkōḷar eṉak kalveṭṭil varutal kāṇka.
aracariṉ muḻu nampikkikkuriyavarāyirunta kikkōḷar araṇmaṉiyilum uvaḷakattilum akap parivāramākavum amarttappeṟṟaṉar eṉpatu.
nampirāṭṭiyār nēriyaṉ mātēvi
yakapparivārattuk kikkōḷaṉ cōṟuṭiyā
ṉarukkaṉāṉa aṉpāra pāṇati rāyaṉ
(S.I.I.Vol.No.700) eṉavarum kalveṭṭup pakutiyāl aṟiyalām.
kōḷar kuṭiyirunta pala vūrkaḷ avar kuṭiyāṟ peyar peṟ ṟiruntaṉa. koṭuṅkuṉṟattiṟku arukil tirukkōḷakkuṭi eṉṟoru maliyaṭiyūr uḷateṉṟum kōḷar irukkumūr kōḷkaḷavu kaṟṟavūr eṉṟum kāḷamēkappulavar taṉippāṭ ṭil `kōḷar eṉpatu kuṭippeyar eṉṟum, perumpulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkār kūṟuvar.
pāṇaṉ eṉṉum kuṭippeyar pāṇ eṉṟu kuṟukuvatupōl, kōḷaṉ eṉṉum kuṭippeyarum kōḷ eṉṟu kuṟukum. ik kuṟukkam perumpālum pāṇcēri, kikkōṭ paṭi eṉap puṇarmoḻip peyarkaḷilēyē nikaḻum. ippaṭic cammatittu vilippiramāṇam paṇṇik koṭuttōm. āticaṇṭēccura tēvarkku, tirunelvēlik kikkōṭcēṉāpatikaḷōm. (S.I.I. Vol. V. No. 118) eṉavarum kalveṭṭup pakutiyik kāṇka.
cila coṟkaḷil ḷakaram cakaramākat tirikiṉṟatu. itaṟku uḷi-uci. tūḷi-tūci eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. immuṟiyil kōḷaṉ eṉpatum kōcaṉ eṉat tirintatākat terikiṉṟatu. piṉpu, tēcam - tēyam eṉpatupōl kōcaṉ - kōyaṉ eṉṟāyiruttal vēṇṭum. pāṇ, kōḷ eṉṉuṅ kuṟukkaṅkaṭkēṟpa, kōyaṉ eṉpatu kōy eṉṟu kuṟukutalum iyalpē.
kōyaṉ paḷḷi eṉpatu karuvūriyaṭuttuḷḷatu. itaṉāṟ kōcaṉ eṉṉum peyar kōyam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉṟum uṇaralām. kōyaṉ puttūr eṉpatum ippaṭippaṭṭatēyām. ik kōyarkuṭi kōy eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉpatu iḷaṅ kōykkuṭi eṉṟa peyarāṉ aṟiyalām. muḷḷi nāṭṭu iḷaṅ kōykkuṭi (ampācamuttirattiṉ peyar) eṉpatu cācaṉam (Top. List. Tinnevelly Dist. No.s. 28, 29 pārkka). iḷaṅkō paṭiyaracaṉ muṉiyataraṉ (S.I.I. VI. No. 538 of 1909) eṉat tañciyic cērnta kōvalūrc cācaṉattu varutalāṉ. ik kuṭivaḻakkuṇmi uṇarappaṭum. tirunel vēliyic cārnta kaṭiyam uḷḷa nāṭu kōynāṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum. eṉap perumpulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkār eḻutiyiruppatu poruttamē. (kōcar. pak. 54, 55) āyiṉ, kōcar eṉṉum collē nāḷaṭiviṟ kōḷar eṉa marīiyiṉateṉṟu niṉiyalām. (kōcar pak. 57) eṉṟu avar talikīḻākak kūṟiyiruppatu poruntātu. ḷakaram cakara mākat tiritalaṉṟic cakaram ḷakaramākat tiritalilli. tūḷi, tūci eṉa varutalum niṉika eṉa avar eṭuttukkāṭṭiyiruppatē ataṟkuc cāṉṟām.
ākavē, kōcar eṉṟu kaṭikkaḻakac ceyyuṭkaḷilum, kōḷar eṉṟu piṟkālac ceyyuḷilum, kikkōḷar eṉṟu kalveṭṭu kaḷilum, kuṟikkap peṟṟavar orēvakuppiṉar eṉṟu tuṇiyattakum. paṇṭic ceyyuṭkaḷil kōcar eṉṟa vaṭivamē kāṇappeṟu vatāl, atuvē muntiṉatākum eṉṟu orupālar karutalām. ulaka vaḻakku ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṉum muntiyateṉṟum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc collellām mūlavaṭivattik kāṭṭāveṉṟum, terintu koḷka. nī eṉṉum muṉṉili yorumippeyariṉ mūlavaṭivam nīṉ eṉpatē yāyiṉum, muṉṉatē ceyyuḷ vaḻakkākavum piṉṉatu ulaka vaḻakkākavum iruttal kāṇka.

iḻitta tikavāmic cāvāri yārē
piḻitta toṟukkiṟ pavar  (kuṟaḷ. 779)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷoṭu poruntiya, tamiḻp pūṭki maṟavariṉ potu viyalpik kuṟippataṉṟi, vēṟṟunāṭṭu maṟavariṉ ciṟap piyalpik kuṟippatākātu. ātalāl,
iḻiciṉa ṉākiya kācaṉ aracaṉuṭiya
aiya naṭukkam pōkka utirattāl naṉinta
tōlil iṉṟu kōcamuṟiyil āṇiyiṭṭaṉaṉ
 eṉap perumpulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkār. mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭum kācumīranāṭṭu varalāṟākiya irāca taraṅkaṇip pakuti, kōcar tamiḻarallar eṉṉuṅ koḷkikkuc cāṉṟākāmi kāṇka.
tamiḻakattiyaṭuttu vaṭakkiluḷḷa vaṭukar, mōriyariṉ teṉṉāṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppil avar paṭikku muṉṉaṇiyāka vantutaviṉar eṉpatu.
muraṇmiku vaṭukar muṉṉuṟa mōriyar

mākeḻu tāṉi vampa mōriyar  (akam. 251)
 eṉṉum pakutikaḷ terivikkum.
kōcar kācumīrattiṉiṉṟu vatta nāṭṭu vaḻiyāyt tamiḻnāṭu vanta vaṭavareṉiṉ, tamiḻariyaṭutta vaṭukarē vaṭanāṭṭarākiya mōriyarkku utaviyirukkumpōtu, avarum utavātirun tirupparō?
mēṟkāṭṭiya maturikkāñcip pakutiyuḷ vantuḷḷa nāṉ moḻik kōcar eṉṉum toṭarukkum, kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷuḷ vantuḷḷa nālūrk kōcar eṉṉum toṭarukkum, tamiḻ, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku ākiya nāṉmoḻiyum vaḻaṅkiyatāl nāṉmoḻināṭu eṉappeyarpeṟṟa nāmakkal vaṭṭattil vatinta kōcar eṉṟum, vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṟ collappaṭṭa kucar eṉpavariṉ nāṟputalvar amitta kucāmpi mutaliya nālūriṉiṉṟu paraviya kucar vaḻiyiṉar eṉṟum, perumpulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkār muṟiyē kāraṇaṅ kāṭvar. iṉi, avarē,
kaḻukumalic cācaṉaṅkaḷil nālūr, nālkūr eṉṟa peya ruṭiya ūr palliṭattum varutal kāṇalām.... kōcar oru tiraḷākac capatañ ceytukoḷpavareṉpatu ivvūriyaṭut tuḷḷa kuḻuvāṇi nallūr eṉṟa peyarāṉ ūkikkap paṭuvatu ik kuḻuvāṇi nallūrk kōcartām kuṟuntokiyil nallūrkkōcar eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉarō eṉa aiyuṟukiṉṟēṉ. aṉṟi mēlē kāṭṭiya kaḻukumalippuṟattu nālūr paṟṟi nālūrkkōcar eṉappaṭṭaṉareṉiṉum poruntum. (pak. 51, 52) eṉavum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār kūṟiya tolkāppiyar kāla moḻi niliyē kaṭikkaḻakak kālattum tamiḻakattiruntatiṉāl, ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ pukunta teluṅkum. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ tirinta kaṉṉaṭamum, 12 ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkuppiṉ tirinta maliyāḷamum, 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nāmakkal vaṭṭattil vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉpatu kālamalivu eṉṉum kuṟṟamām. ākavē nāṉmoḻik kōcar eṉṉum toṭarukku, oṉṟu moḻital, naṇparkku uṟṟuḻi yutaval, eḷiyārik kāttal, paḻikkup paḻi vāṅkutal ākiya nālvakip pūṭki moḻikaḷik koṇṭa kōcar eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatē uṇmikkum uttikkum poruttamām. moḻi iṅku uṟutimoḻi, nāṉmoḻik kōcar tōṉṟi yāṅku eṉṉum maturik kāñcit toṭa rukku, nāṉku vakiyākiya kōcar vañciṉa moḻiyālē viḷaṅ kiṉāṟ pōṉṟu eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittiruppati yum nōkkuka. kuṟun tokic ceyyuḷil varum nālūrk kōcar eṉṉum toṭarum nālurik kōcar eṉpataṉ vaḻūu vaṭivō eṉa aiyuṟa iṭantarukiṉṟatu.
koṅku maṇṭilattuṭ pala pākaṅkaḷil, vattavaṉ, vattarāyaṉ, vaccarāyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ikkālattu vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉa eṉṟu, perumpērāciriyar paṇṭārakar (Dr.) u.vē. cāminātiyar peruṅkati mukavuriyiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu. koṅkuvēḷ av vaṉappi allatu toṭarnilicceyyuḷi iyaṟṟiyapiṉ nērntuḷḷa nilimiyiyē kuṟippatākum. ikkālattum iraciyāvippaṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭa tamiḻaruḷ orucilar tam putalvarkkut tāliṉ (Stalin), leṉiṉ (Lenin) eṉap peyar iṭuvatiyum, atupaṟṟi avari iraciyar eṉakkoḷḷa iṭa miṉmiyum, nōkkuka.
iṉi, iḷaṅkōcar eṉṉum vaḻakkil uḷḷa iḷa eṉṉum aṭimoḻik kāraṇam paṟṟi, perumpulavar rā. irākavi yaṅkār.
vēṭṭoḻiva tallāl viṉiñar viḷivayaluḷ
tōṭṭa kaṭiñar cuṭunantu - mōṭṭāmi
vaṉpuṟattu mītuṭikkum vaccat tiḷaṅkōvi
iṉpuṟutta vallamō yām
 ... ... ... iv vaccat toḷḷāyirap pāṭṭil vaccattiḷaṅ kōvi eṉap pāṭiyataṉāl, innāṭṭuk kuṭiyēṟiyavaṉ vaccat tiḷaṅkō vēntaṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉ eṉṟu teḷiyalākum. ivaṉ kōcattiṉiṉṟu iṅku vantavaṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉātalāl ivaṉvaḻik kōcarellām iḷaṅkōcar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉareṉa uyttuṇaralām
iṉi ik kōcāmpiyi āṇṭa aracarē iḷaṉ eṉṉum tiṅkaṭkulattu vēntaṉ vaḻiyiṉa reṉṟum, atupaṟṟiyē avaṉ vaḻivēntar iḷaṅkō eṉap peyar ciṟappareṉṟum karutuvāruṇṭu
iṉi, ivar vatsa tēcattuk kōcam eṉṟa talinakariṉiṉṟu vantavarā talāṉ. iḷaṅkōcar eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉareṉiṉ, atuvum naṉku poruntum, vatsa moḻi āriyattil iḷamikkup peyarātalāṉ vatsakōcar - iḷaṅkōcar eṉa moḻiyerttut tamiḻarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉareṉṟuṅ koḷ ḷalām. kōcarik kūṟiya palaviṭattum cāṉṟō rellām palliḷaṅ kōcar, nalliḷaṅkōcar, koṅkiḷaṅ kōcar eṉa vaḻaṅkik kāṭṭalāṉ. iḵtu uṭaliḷamipaṟṟiya tākāteṉpatu orutaliyākat tuṇiyalām. vatsa tēcatti iḷanāṭeṉak koṇṭu annāṭṭu vēntaṉi iḷaṅkō veṉṟum, an nāṭṭuk kōcari iḷaṅkōcar eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉareṉpatu poruttiyatām. eṉap palavāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār (pak. 7, 8). piṉṉar, avarē, muṉṉukkuppiṉ muraṇāka, kōcar eppōtum iḷamiyōṭi yiruppareṉṟu tuṇivatu poruntātām. ūrmutukōcar (akam. 262) eṉa vaḻaṅkalāṉ. ivar ellā makkaḷum pōṉṟu yākki mūttuk kaḻitaluṇṭeṉka. itaṉāl, ivari iḷaṅkōcar eṉap palliṭattum vaḻaṅkuvatu ivar kuṭipaṟṟiyatu allatu nāṭupaṟṟiyateṉiṉ, naṉku poruntumeṉka. eṉavuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār (pak. 9).
pācili yamaṉṟa payaṟā pukkeṉa
vāymoḻit tantiyik kaṇkaḷin taruḷā
tūrmutu kōcar navitta ciṟumiyiṟ
kalattu muṇṇāḷ vālitu muṭāaḷ
ciṉattiṟ koṇṭa paṭivam māṟāḷ
maṟaṅkeḻu tāṉik koṟṟak kuṟumpiyaṉ
ceruviyal naṉmāṉ titiyaṟ kurittavar.
iṉṉuyir cekuppak kaṇṭuciṉa māṟiya
aṉṉi miñili pōla  (262)
eṉṟa akacceyyuḷiṉiṉṟu. vāymoḻit tanti eṉṟatu ūrmutu kōcarōṭu orē iṉattavaṉ āṉa tanti eṉpatu pulappaṭaniṉṟatu. mutukōcarāyiruntum iṉameṉṟu nōk kātu. taṇṭan takutiyaṉṟeṉpatum eṇṇātu, ellā vuṟuppiṉuñ ciṟanta kaṇṇi aruḷātu kaḷintaṉa reṉṟār..... kuṟumpiyaṉun titiyaṉum kōcariṉa mallarāyiṉ iṅṅaṉam eḷitil oṉṟumoḻikkōcarik koṉṟu muraṇpōkka lākā teṉka. muraṇpōkiya (akam. 196) eṉṟataṉāl ōriṉat tiṟkuḷ nērnta muraṇpāṭu iḵteṉat tuṇiyalām. ūrmutu kōcar piḻiyātalāl, ivvaḻakkil vēḷir iṭiyiṟpukkuk koṉṟu muraṇpōkkil iyalā teṉka. kōcar anniyarāṟ kollap paṭattakka eḷiyarallar eṉpatum niṉika, eṉat tammaṉam pōṉavā ṟellām uytturippar perum pulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkār.
kōcar maṭṭumaṉṟi uṇmi colvōrellām vāy moḻi vāyar eṉappaṭuvar. vāymoḻivāyar niṉpukaḻētta eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattuḷ varutal kāṇka (37.2). kōcar palaviṭattup piṟarāl vellappaṭṭami paṇṭi ilakkiyattiṉiṉ teriya varutalāl. anniyarāṟ kollappaṭat takka eḷiyarallar eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntātu. avarellārum attakiya valiya reṉiṉ, mūvēntar avarmuṉ eṅṅaṉam nāṭāṇṭirukka muṭiyum?
iḷiyarē porutaṟkuc ciṟantavarātalālum, iḷiyar, maḻavar mutaliya iḷami kuṟitta peyarkaḷ pōrmaṟavarkkup paḻañceyyuṭkaḷuḷ varuvatālum, iḷiñarāṉa kōcarē, iḷaṅkōcar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉareṉṟum, avaruḷ mūttup pōrt toḻiliṉiṉṟum nīṅki ikkālattu ōyvupeṟṟa porunar (ex-service men) pōl ūrkkuḷ vatintavarē ūrmutu kōcar eṉṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum, koḷvatē mikap porutta mām.
kōcar tamiḻarē eṉpatu itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl teritalāl, paḻiyaṉ, atikaṉ, ñimili, akuti, titiyaṉ, kuṟumpiyaṉ, āta ṉeḻiṉi, taḻumpaṉ mutaliya kuṟunilamaṉṉarum paṭittali varum porunarum tamiḻarē eṉṟu teḷika. avar peyarellām tūya tamiḻccollāyiruttaliyum, avar eṉṟēṉum vēṟṟumoḻi pēciyatāka eṅkēṉum collap paṭāmiyum, nōkkuka.
ettoḻiliyum iruvakuppār ceytuvariṉ, avarkkuḷ ikalum icalippum ēṟpaṭuvatu iyalpē. kōcarkkum vēḷirkkum pōrttoḻil ōraḷavu ottiruntamiyiṉ, avariṭic ciṟitu pakimi ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. itupaṟṟik kōcari vaṭanāṭṭāreṉak koḷvatu poruntātu. āriya varukikkumuṉ nāvalantēya muḻuvatum tamiḻarum avar vaḻiyiṉarāṉa tiraviṭarumē kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, vēḷirip pōṉṟē kōcarum paṉimali (imayam) vari paraviyirun tirukka lām. ātalāl, kōcam eṉṉum cūḷmuṟiyi vaṭanāṭṭār kiyāṇṭami, ataṉ ayaṉmiyik kāṭṭātu.
kōcarkkum vēḷirkkum pōrttoḻil ōraḷavu potu vāyirunta tēṉum, atu kōcarkkē ciṟantiruntami
irumpiṭam paṭutta vaṭuvuṭi mukattar

eṉa varupavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
itaṉāl, kōcar
uliviṭat tūṟañcā vaṉkaṇ toliviṭattut
tolpaṭik kallāl aritu  (762)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaṭku ilakkāṉa mūlappaṭiyic cērnta allatu tolvaravāṉa pūṭki maṟavarum, paṭittalivarumāvar. vēḷir uḻuvit tuṇṇum vēḷāṇ vakuppiccērnta kuṟunila maṉṉarum paṇṇiyār (mirācutār) eṉṉum perunilak kiḻārumāvār.
iṉi, kōcar, attikōcam (yāṉiyeṟikōcam), vīrakōcam (āḷeṟikōcam) eṉa iruvakiyarākavum collap peṟuvar. paṭikkuc ciṟantatum vilaṅkiṟ periyatum yāṉiyeṉṟu koṇṭu, ataṉiyē pōrkkaḷattil eṟintukolvōr yāṉiyeṟikōcattār. aḵtu oru vilaṅkeṉṟu iḻittu maṟavariyē kolvōr āḷeṟikōcattār.
kāṉa muyaleyta ampiṉil yāṉi
piḻittavēl ēntal iṉitu  (kuṟaḷ. 772)
kivēl kaḷiṟṟoṭu pōkki varupavaṉ
meyvēl paṟiyā nakum  (kuṟaḷ. 774)
vēntūr yāṉik kalla
tēntuvaṉ pōlāṉṟaṉ ilaṅkili vēlē  (puṟam. 301)
kaṟiyaṭi yāṉik kalla
tuṟikaḻip paṟiyā vēlōṉ ūrē  (puṟam. 323)
āṉi yāyiram amariṭi veṉṟa
māṉavaṉukku vakuppatu paraṇi  (ilak. vi. 839)
 eṉpaṉa yāṉiyeṟi kōcattāṉip paṟṟiyaṉa.
tāṉāl vilaṅkāl taṉittāl piṟaṉvarittāl
yāṉi yeṟital iḷivaravāl - yāṉi
oruki yuṭiya teṟivalō yāṉum
iruki cumantu vāḻvēṉ  (tol. puṟattiṇi. 5 uri)
eṉpatu āḷeṟi kōcattāṉip paṟṟiyatu.
icceyyuṭkaḷellām tamiḻ maṟavaruṭ ciṟantārik kuṟippa tallatu. oru vēṟṟunāṭṭu vakuppāri vitantō kiḷantō kuṟiyāmi kāṇka.
piṟkālac cōḻa pāṇṭiya koṅku nāṭukaḷil, paḻantamiḻk kōcariṉ vaḻiyiṉarāṉa kikkōḷap paṭimaṟavaruḷḷum talivaruḷḷum pōrkkaḷattiṟ paṭṭavariṉ kuṭumpattārkku, irattakkāṇikki yeṉṟum utirappaṭṭiyeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum mānilam aracarāl viṭappaṭṭami āṅkāṅkuḷḷa kalveṭṭu kaḷ terivikkum.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟṟuṭaṉṟu mēlvariṉum āṟṟaluṭaṉ porum taṟu kaṇāṇmip paṭimaṟavarāṉa kikkōḷar, 16ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkup piṉ (cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarāṉa) muttamiḻaraca marapum aṟṟuppōṉatiṉālum, pōrttoḻiṟ kiṭamiṉmi yālum, piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyamuṟik kulappirivi ṉālum tolvaravun tōlum keṭṭu maṟam eṉṉum tiṟamum aṟavē iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. avar iṉṟu necavut toḻili mēṟkoṇ ṭiruppiṉum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ teṉpākattil kikkōḷar eṉṟum vaṭapākattil ceṅkuntar eṉṟum iṉṟum avarkku vaḻaṅkivarum peyarkaḷ. avariṉ muṉṉōr nilimiyit teṟṟeṉak kāṭṭāniṟkum. muṉṉark kikkōḷar paṭi eṉpatu marapāka vaḻaṅkiyatu pōṉṟu, iṉṟu kikkōḷar taṟi eṉpatu marapāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kikkōḷar peyar eṉṉun talippil tivākaram kūṟum.
ceṅkuntap paṭiyar cēṉittalivar
tantuvāyar kārukar kikkōḷar
eṉṉum nūṟpā, kikkōḷariṉ paṇṭi nilimiyiyum iṟṟi nilimiyiyum oruṅkē uṇarttum, tantuvāyār, kārukar eṉpaṉa necavupaṟṟi vanta peyarkaḷ, kuntam īṭṭi, ceṅkuntam kurutiyāṟ civanta īṭṭi.
paṇṭi vēḷiruḷḷum kōcaruḷḷum iruntatupōṉṟē, muṟiyē avariṉ marapiṉarāṉa veḷḷāḷaruḷḷum kikkōḷa ruḷḷum paṭittalivar iruntamiyiṉ, iṉṟum avviru vakuppāriṉ vaḻiyiṉarum kulattārum. mutaliyār eṉṉum paṭṭattuṭaṉ aḻikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉar. mutaliyār eṉpatu cēṉi mutaliyār allatu paṭi mutaliyār eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. mutaliyār iṅkut talivar, vaṭakoṅkil vatiyum akampaṭiyar tamakku mutaliyār paṭṭam iṭṭukkoṇṭatu piṟkāla vaḻakku. tañci māvaṭṭattil camaṇarkku vaḻaṅkum mutaliyār paṭṭam paṭit toṭarpiṉṟip potuvākat talivar allatu periyōr eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatē.
kōcarum kikkōḷarum oru vakuppārē eṉṟu mutaṉmutaṟ kūṟiyavar perumpulavar rā. irākaviyaṅkārē. kōcar tamiḻarā vēṟṟu nāṭṭarā eṉpatupaṟṟi maṭṭum karuttuvēṟupāṭuḷḷatu. kaḻakak kālattuk kōcarum vēḷirum vaṭanāṭṭiṉ vevvēṟu pakutiyiṉiṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭup pukuntavar eṉpatu avar koḷki. teṉṉāṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kiyāḷappeṟ ṟamiyē. avar, aṅṅaṉam talikīḻāṉa muṭipiṟku varakkāraṇam. teṉṉāṭṭu māntar vaṭanāṭṭāri ṉum muṟpaṭṭavar eṉpati aṟintiruppiṉ, avar aṅṅaṉam koṇṭirār. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaṇṇapirāṉ nappiṉṉiyi ēṟutaḻuvi maṇantatum, kāḷik kōṭṭattil (Calcutta) kāḷikku erumik kaṭāvik kāvu koṭuttatum, teṉṉāṭṭu (tamiḻnāṭṭu) vaḻakkaṅkaḷē. aṅ ṅaṉamē kācumīrattār kōca muṟiyil cūḷiṭṭatum teṉ ṉāṭṭu vaḻakkamē. ātalāl, atupaṟṟik kōcari vaṭanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vantavar eṉṟu koḷvatu varalāṟṟu malivām.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil ulaka vaḻakkil kōḷar eṉṟum, ceyyuḷ allatu ilakkiya vaḻak kil kōcar eṉṟum, iṭikkālattilum ikkālattilum iruvaki vaḻakkilum kikkōḷar eṉṟum, ceṅkuntar eṉṟum, peyar vaḻaṅkappeṟṟavar oru vakuppārēyeṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭut tūya tamiḻp paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷēyeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
paṇṭikkālattil, kollēṟu taḻuvi maṇanta iṭiyarum kōḷarikkum añcāk kuṟavarum ākiya iru vakuppāriṉ vaḻi yiṉar āriyattāl iṉṟu tam muṉṉōriṉ taṟukaṇāṇmit tamiḻa maṟatti muṟṟum iḻantiruppatu pōṉṟē, kūṟṟuvaṉic cīṟum kōcariṉ vaḻiyiṉarāṉa kikkōḷar ceṅkuntarum iṉṟuḷḷaṉar eṉka.
piṉ kuṟippukaḷ :
 1. 	kōyaṉputtūr eṉpatu kōvaṉputtūr eṉpataṉ tiri pākavum irukkalām. kalveṭṭukaḷil uḷḷatu kōvaṉ puttūr eṉṉum vaṭivamē. vakaram yakaramākat tirital iyalpu.
 	e-ṭu : kōvil - kōyil.
 	iṉi, yarakam vakaramākat tiritalumuṇṭu.
 	e-ṭu : cēyaṭi - cēvaṭi.
 	itu. kōyaṉ - kōvaṉ eṉṉum tiripiṟkuc cārpākalām.
 2. 	muttamiḻ nāṭṭilum kōcar iruntaṉar. avaruṭ cilar tuḷu nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiṉar. ataṉāl kōcarnāṭu tuḷu nāṭeṉap paṭṭatu. itu, cērar, koṅku eṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
 3. 	aṉṉi miñiliyiṉ tanti kaṇṇi kōcar orucilar piṭuṅkiya koṭumi paṟṟik kōcar ellāriyum ayalār eṉṟu koḷḷamuṭiyātu. vāykkālil viḻunta māṅ kaṉiyit tiṉṟa ciṟumiyik koṉṟa naṉṉaṉum tamiḻaṉē.
 4. 	ceṅkuntar kuṭippiṟanta oṭṭakkūttar pōṉṟa kaḻakakkālak kōcaruḷḷum tamiḻppulavar orucilar iruntaṉar.
 5.	iṟṟik kikkōḷarum ceṅkuntarum kaṭukaḷavēṉum ayaṉmi yuṭiyavarallar; aṅṅaṉamē kaḻakakkālak kōcarum.
 6. 	mōriyap paṭiyeṭuppiṟku muṉpē kōcar tamiḻakattirun taṉar. ātalāl, avar mōriyaroṭu vantavarallar. (teṉ moḻi, poṅkal malar 1960.)
kōṭṭam
ul - ulam = uruṭci. ulamvarutal - ulamarutal = cuḻalutal. ul- ula - ulavu - ulāvu. ulavutal = vaḷital, cūḻtal, cuṟṟutal, tirital.
ul - kul - kula - kulavu.
kulavutal = 1. vaḷital kulavuc ciṉip pūkkoytu (puṟam. 11:4). 2. ulāvutal emataṉṉiyi niṉittē kulaviṉaṉō (civaraka. vicayi. 14).
kulavu = vaḷivu. kulavuk koṭuñcili (pu.ve. 1: 10)
kulavu - kulāvu. kulāvutal = vaḷital. vaḷittal. kulāvuñ ciliyar (pu.ve. 4:3)
kulu - kuluttam = vaḷinta kāyiluḷḷa payaṟṟu vaki. (tivā.)
kul - kol - kōl = 1. (uruṇṭa) tiraṭci, kōṉiṟavaḷi yiṉārkku (cīvaka. 209). kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu (cilap. 26:121). 2. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa taṭi.
kōl - kōlam = pantupōṟ curuḷum muḷḷeli.
kōl - kōli = kuṇṭu vaṭivāṉa viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvi. ma. kōli, te. kōli (g), tu. kōḷi (g), marā. kōlī (g).
kul - kuṉ - kuṉi, kuṉital = 1. vaḷital, kuṉivaḷar cili (cīvaka. 486). 2. vaṇaṅkutal. (cūṭā).
kuṉ - kuṉuku = kuṉukutal = cirittu uṭal vaḷital.
kuṉ - kūṉ = 1. vaḷivu, kūṉirumpiṉiṟ kuṟittu (niṭata. nāṭṭup 10.) 2. uṭaṟkūṉal. (tivā). kūṉuṅ kuṟaḷum (puṟam. 28). 3. kūṉaṉ. (kūṉi). ciṟukuṟuṅ kūṉum (cilap. 27: 214).
kūṉ - kūṉi = vaḷinta ciṟṟiṟāl. kūṉikotti....... kok kirukkum paṇṇi (kuṟṟā. kuṟa. 94).
kūṉutal = 1. vaḷital. 2. mutuku vaḷintupōtal.
kūṉ - kūṉi - mūliyiṟ kūṉuḷḷa nīrccāl.
kulavu - kuravu - kuravi = ciṟappu nāṭkaḷiṟ kuṟiñci allatu mulli nila makaḷir eḻuvar allatu paṉṉiruvar vaṭṭamāka niṉṟu. pāṭiyāṭic cuṟṟivaruṅ kūttu... āycciyar kuravi. kuṉṟak kuravi. (cilap.)
kuravu - o.nō: L. curvo, E. curve, to bend.
kuravi - o.nō: Gk. choros, a dance in a ring.
E. “chorus, n. (L. chorus, from Gk. koros, a dance in a ring, a chorus). 1. In the Greek drama, (a) originally a company of dancers dancing in a ring accompanied by their own singing or that of others; a band of singers and dancers.”
E. “choir, n. (Written also quire, from O.Fr. choeur L. chorus, Gk. choros, a dance in a ring, a band) 1. a band of dancers. .... 2. a collection of singers, especially in divine service, in a church.....”  (The Imperial Dictionary of the English Language.)
kuravu - kuraku - kuraṅku, kuraṅkutal = vaḷital. pāṭiṉāḷ ... ilippoḻil kuraṅkiṉa (cīvaka. 657).
kuraṅku - 1. vaḷivu. kuraṅkami yuṭutta marampayi laṭukkattu (cilap. 10 : 157), 2. kokki.
kuraṅku - o.nō : E. crank.
kulu - kuru - kuruku = 1. vaḷiyal, kikuvi piṭittuk kurukaṇi ceṟitta (kallā. 42 : 22). 2. vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷa nīrp paṟavi nāri. vāṉpaṟik kuruki ṉeṭuvari poṟpa (patiṟṟup. 83:2). 3. nāri vaṭivotta kollulit turutti. ūtulik kuruki ṉuyirttaṉar (cilap. 4:59) 4. ōtimam (aṉṉam). nīroḻiyap pāluṇ kurukiṟṟerintu (nālaṭi. 135). 5. aṉṟil, kuruku peyark kuṉṟam (maṇi. 5:13).
kuruku - o.nō: E. crane, OE. cran, OS., OHG. krano F. grue.
kuru - kuruḷ - kuruḷutal = curuḷutal. kuruṇṭa vārkuḻal (tiruvici. tiruvāli. 1:3)

4. vilaṅkiṉ viti. 5. veṭikuṇṭu. 6. uruṇṭi māmpaḻa vaki. 7. uruṇṭi kala vaki. 8. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa āṇ kutiri allatu āṇ kaḻuti. te. kuṇṭa. marā. kuṇṭa (g).

kōḷ - kōṭu = kuli (piṅ.) koṭu - kōṭakam = pala teruk kūṭumiṭam (piṅ.).
kōṭṭi ka. oruvarōṭu kūṭiyirukki. taṉ tuṇivi kōṭṭi liṉīṅki.) (cīvaka. 1025) 2. kūṭṭam (piṅ.). (ti.ma. 238)
kōṣippatu eṉṉum poruḷiṉum kūṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷē kūṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. coṟku ēṟṟatā yiruttal kāṇka. āravārippatu kallār tiraḷum kalakak kūṭṭamēyaṉṟi, āṉṟavin taṭaṅkiya cāṉṟō raviyamākātu. (ti.ma. 238)
kōṭṭi 
kōṭṭi - kōṭṭa
kōṭutal = viḷital. kōṭu - kōṭṭi = 1. nilāvic cūḻnta vaṭṭakkōṭu.
cantiraṉōr kōṭṭi. kaṭṭi (koṇṭal viṭu. 72). 2. vaṭṭa māṉa neṟkūṭu.
ulavākkōṭṭi (tiruvālavā. 50:13). 3. neṟkutir. 4. oru mukattalaḷavi.
itu mutalil oru neṟkutiraḷavāyiruntu, iṉṟu 21 marakkāl koṇṭa aḷavik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. 5. araṇmaṉiyi allatu nakaric cūḻnta matilaraṇ. k., f., te. kōṭṭa. mā.vi. akaramutali kōṭṭa eṉṉuñ coṟku, vaḷivu, kuṭici, matilaraṇ eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, kuṭ (vaḷi) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭum. kōṭu eṉṉuñcol vaṭamoḻiyiliṉmiyāl iṅṅaṉaṅ kāṭṭa nērntatu. kuṭ eṉpatu kuṭa eṉṉum teṉcoṟṟiripu. (va.va: 133).
kōṭakam - kōṭṭaka (gh)
kōṭutal = vaḷital, tirumputal.
kōṭu - kōṭakam = vaṭṭamāyōṭuṅ kutiri.
o.nō : maṇṭilam = kutiri (piṅ.). kōṭakam - kōṭam -kōṇam = kutiri.
paccik kōṭakak kāṟṟi  (kallā. 17:48).
kōṭu - kōṭaram = kutiri  (piṅ.)
kōṭaram - kōram = kutiri, cōḻaṉ kutiri.
kōrattiṟ koppō kaṉavaṭṭam  (taṉippāṭal).
vaṭavar etirppu, kāppu, tirumpal ākiya poruḷkaḷik kuṟik kum kuṭ (gh) eṉṉum colli mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. (va.va:134)
kōṭari 
kōṭari - kuṭṭāra (th)
kōṭu = marakkiḷi. arital = aṟuttal, veṭṭutal, kōṭari = marakkiḷiyi veṭṭum allatu piḷakkuṅ karuvi.
kōṭari - kōṭāri - kōṭāli.
ka. kōṭāli, te. koṭṭali (g), ma. kōṭāli (va.va: 133.)
kōṭi 
 kōṭi - kōṭi (t)
kōṭi = elli, kaṭici, nuṉi, nilamuṉi, muṭimāli, ucci yeṇṇākiya nūṟilakkam.
aṭukkiya kōṭi peṟiṉum  (kuṟaḷ. 954).
te. kōṭi, marā. kōṭī, i. kuṭōṭ, ā. krōr (crore)
mutaṟkālattiṟ kōṭiyē kaṭiciyeṇṇāka viruntatu. piṉpu kuvaḷi, tāmari, kumutam, veḷḷam, āmpal, neytal mutaliya aṭukkiya kōṭā kōṭip pēreṇkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
aiam paleṉa varūum iṟuti
 alpeyar eṇṇiṉum āyiyal niliyum.  (tol. 393).
kōṭu = 1. vaḷinta kompu. (va.va.)
2. vilaṅkiṉ uccantaliyiluḷḷa kompupōṉṟa maliyuccik kuvaṭu.
poṟkōṭ ṭimayamum  (puṟam. 2. 24).

kuraṟkūntaṟ kōṭu (kalit. 72 : 20).
kōṭu - kōṭaram = tēriṉ moṭṭu (piṅ.)
kōṭu - kōṭakam = muṭi (makuṭam).

kōṭi eṉṉum coṟku ellipporuḷ, kōṭu eṉṉum colliṉ nīrkkarip poruḷiṉiṉṟu vantiruttal vēṇṭum.
