kōpanilikaḷ 
kōpam ciṟitu poḻutu niṟpatu; ciṉam nīṭittu niṟkum kōpam; cīṟṟam cīṟiyeḻuṅ kōpam; vekuḷi allatu kāyvu allatu uruttiram nerupput taṉmiyuḷḷa kaṭuṅkōpam; kotippu kaṇ pōṉṟa uṟaviṉarkkuc ceyyappaṭṭa koṭumipaṟṟip poṅki yeḻuṅ kōpam; ericcal maṉatti uṟuttuṅ kōpam; kaṭuppu poṟāmiyōṭu kūṭiya kōpam; kaṟam allatu vaṉmam paḻivāṅkuṅ kōpam; kaṟuvu taṇiyāk kōpam; kaṟuppu karuppaṉ mukam kaṟuttut tōṉṟum kōpam; cikappu allatu ceyir; civappiṉ mukam civantu tōṉṟuṅkōpam;
viḷam naccut taṉmiyāṉa kōpam; veṟi aṟiviḻanta kōpam; muṉivu allatu muṉivu peṟuppōṭu kūṭiya kōpam; katam eṉṟum iyalpāṉa kōpam; kaṉivu mukañcuḷikkum kōpam; ceṟṟam allatu ceṟal pakivaṉi aḻikkum kōpam; ūṭal maṉivi kaṇava ṉoṭu kōpittuk koṇṭu uriyāṭāta meṉkōpam; pulavi ūṭaliṉ vaḷarnta nili; tuṉi ūṭaliṉ mutirnta nili; caṭivu uṟaviṉar kuṟikūṟum amitiyāṉa kōpam. (col: 53)
kōpuram 
 kōpuram = kōpura (g)
puriyuyar pākum  (tol. (785).
puram = uyaramāṉa kaṭṭiṭam, mēṉmāṭam. kō = aracaṉ, talimi.
kōpuram = aracaṉirukkum mēṉmāṭam, talimiyāṉa kāvaṟkottaḷam, atupōṉṟa kōyiṟ kōpuram.
mā.vi. akaramutali, kōpura eṉṉum colli āvikkuṟikkum kō eṉṉum colliṉkīḻk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. mutaṉmutaṟ kōpuram kaṭṭappeṟṟatu teṉṉāṭṭilēyē. (va.va: 135)
kōyil 
kōvil allatu kōyil eṉpatu mutalāvatu aracaṉatu araṇ maṉiyiyē kuṟittatu. kō - aracaṉ. il - vīṭu. araca vaṇakkat tiṉpiṉ teyva vaṇakkam tōṉṟiyataṉāl aracamaṉip peyar teyvamaṉip peyarāyiṟṟu. (col. 24)
kōlam 
kōlam - kōla (g) = uruṇṭi.
kōḷ - kōḷ - kōḷam = uruṇṭi, kōḷakkaṭṭi (gland) (va.va: 135)
kōlakam 
kōlakam - kōlaka (g) = uruṇṭi.
kōḷakam = uruṇṭa miḷaku, maṇṭala viriyaṉ.
kōḷaki = 1. vaṭṭa vaṭivam.
 aṇṭakōḷakip puṟattatāy (kamparā. akalikip. 60). 2. yāṉikkimpuri. 3. maṇṭalippāmpu. (va.va: 135)
kōli viḷiyāṭṭu
pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kallāluṅ kaṇṇāṭiyālum iyaṉṟa ciṟṟuruṇṭi kaḷit teṟittum uruṭṭiyum āṭum āṭṭu, kōli eṉappaṭum. (kōli = uruṇṭi).
āṭuvār toki : ciṟuvaruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum, perum pāṉmi iruvarum ciṟupāṉmi mēṟpaṭṭavarum iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu ēṟattāḻa nālaṭit tolivil, akalaḷavāṉa vāyum ōraṅkula āḻamumuḷḷaṉavāka, variciyāy nilattiṟ killappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kuḻikaḷum, āṭakaṉ ovvoru vaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
kōliyāṭṭiṉ ēṉi muṟikaṭkuriya kuḻiyum, iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa aḷaviṉatē.
āṭiṭam : akaṉṟa muṟṟamum puṟakkaṭiyum poṭṭalum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar, kuḻivaricikku nērāṉa iru ticikaḷuḷ oṉṟil, kaṭicikkuḻikku mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku kacattoli viṟ kīṟappaṭṭa kōṭṭil niṉṟu koṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉtaṉ kōliyi akkuḻi nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttiyavar muṉṉum, vīḻttātavar piṉṉum, āṭal vēṇṭum. oru varum kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttāviṭiṉ, kuḻikku neruṅka uruṭṭiyavar muṉṉum, ataṟkaṭutta aṇmikku uruṭṭiyavar piṉṉum āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭuvār palarāyiṉ, iṅṅaṉamē aṇmi cēymi muṟippaṭi muṉṉum piṉṉum āṭal vēṇṭum.
mutalil āṭuvāṉ kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttātavaṉāyiṉ, taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuḷ teṟittu vīḻttiyapiṉ, ataṟkaṭutta naṭukkuḻikkuḷḷum, ataṉpiṉ ataṟkaṭutta etirppakka iṟutik kuḻikkuḷḷum, piṉpu toṭarntu muṉṉum piṉṉumāka aṭuttaṭutta kuḻikkuḷḷum, pattām eṇvari muṉpōṉṟē vīḻttavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vīḻttuvataṟku nāṉkumuṟi muṉṉum piṉṉumākat tici tirumpa nērum. pattām vīḻttu naṭukkuḻikkuḷ nikaḻum. ataṉpiṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyit taṉ kōliyāl teṟittu aṭittuviṭiṉ kelippākum. pala etiri kaḷāyiṉ av aṉivar kōliyiyum aṭittal vēṇṭum.
mutalil āṭuvāṉ ētēṉum oru kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttat tavaṟiṉ, etiri āṭal vēṇṭum. etiriyum, tavaṟiṉ mutalāvāṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tavaṟuntoṟum āṭakaṉ māṟuvāṉ.
oruvaṉ āṭumpōtu etiriyiṉ kōli arukiliruppiṉ, atu aṭuttamuṟi kuḻikkuḷ vīḻvatit taṭukkumāṟum, ataṉ aṭiyiṉiṉṟu tappumāṟum, ataṉi aṭittut toliviṟ pōkkiviṭuvatu vaḻakkam. āṭṭiṉ iṭiyilāyiṉum iṟutiyi lāyiṉum etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭikkat tavaṟiṉ, etiri āṭal vēṇṭum. etiriyiṉ kōli tolivilirukkumpōtu ati aṭikkum āṟṟal allatu uṟutiyillāviṭiṉ, taṉ kōliyic caṟṟē muṉtaḷḷi aṭuttamuṟi etiriyiṉ aṭikkut tappumāṟu ceyvatumuṇṭu.
oruvaṉ tavaṟi maṟṟoruvaṉ āṭumpōtu, āṭṭiṉ toṭakkat til āṭiya muṟippaṭiyē āṭal vēṇṭum.
āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum tōṟpavaṉ oruvaṉē. palarāyiṉ, iṟutiyil tōṟpavaṉoḻinta ēṉiyarellārum kelikkumvari āṭṭut toṭarum.
āṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, tōṟṟavaṉ kelittavariṭam muṭṭuvāṅkal vēṇṭum. tōṟṟavaṉ taṉ muṭṭik kiyi, iru kaṇuviṟkum iṭippaṭṭa pakuti kelittavarkku etirākat tōṉṟumāṟu, nilattil ūṉṟi vittukkoṇṭirukka, kelittavar tam kōliyāl avaṉ muṭṭiyil aṭippar. itu muṭṭuppōṭutal eṉappaṭum. potuvāka mūṉṟu muṭṭu aṭippatu vaḻakkam. muṭṭup pōṭuvatuṭaṉ ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum. aṭutta āṭṭi āṭuvatu āṭakariṉ viruppattip poṟuttatu. oru viḷi yāṭṭi oru muṟi āṭi muṭippatu ōr āṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, arukiluḷḷa paḷḷattilirukkum kāṭṭuppaṟaviyik kiyil villillāviṭattu viralkoṇṭu kallāl teṟikkum vēṭṭi viṉiyiṉiṉṟu, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : viral narampu uraṅkoḷvatum, kuṟi tappāmal teṟittaṭikkap payilvatum, iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu muṟi
cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ potuvākak kōliyik kuṇṭu allatu kōlikkuṇṭu eṉṟum, kōliyāṭṭattik kuṇ ṭāṭṭam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. av āṭṭam, pēntā, añcala kuñcam, irukuḻiyāṭṭam, mukkuḻiyāṭṭam mutaliya pala vakippaṭum.
	I. pēntā
 (i) caturap pēntā
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēntā eṉṉum nīḷcaturak kōṭṭiṟkuḷ uruṭṭiyāṭuṅ kōliyāṭṭu pēntā eṉappaṭum. iti vaṭṭap pēntāvuṭaṉ oppu nōkkic caturap pēntā eṉpar. itu koṅku nāṭṭil ciṟappāy viḷiyāṭappaṭuvatu paṟṟi, tirucci vaṭṭārattil oru cilar iti īrōṭṭup pēntā eṉpatu muṇṭu.
āṭuvār toki : ciṟāruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi : ēṟattāḻa ōraṭi nīḷamum mukkālaṭi akalamum nīṭṭup pōkkil naṭukkōṭum uḷḷa oru nīḷ caturakkōṭum, āṭuvāṉ ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. peruṅkōliyum kallumāyiṉ, teṟittaṭikkap paṭāmal uruṭṭiyaṭikkappaṭum. pēntāviṟku ēṟattāḻap pattaṭittolivil ōraṭi nīḷam oru kuṟuṅkōṭu kīṟappeṟum. atu utti yeṉappaṭum.
āṭiṭam : āṭiṭam pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk kōliyāṭṭiṟkup pōṉṟē itaṟkum.
āṭu muṟi : āṭakar iruvarum, uttiyeṉṉum kōṭṭil mēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyk kōliyip pēntā nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. pēntāviṟku uḷḷē allatu arukē yār kōli niṉṟatō, avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭu āṭal vēṇṭum. pēntāviṟku veḷiyē allatu mika veḷiyē yār kōli niṉṟatō, avaṉ taṉ kōliyip pēntāviṉ naṭuk kōṭṭu naṭuvil vittal vēṇṭum.
āṭum valiyuṭiyavaṉ mīṇṭum uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu taṉ kōliyip pēntā nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. kōli pēntāviṟkuḷḷēṉum pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭikkuḷ ḷēṉum pōy niṟpiṉ tavaṟām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟip pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭik kappāṟpōy niṟpiṉ, pēntāviṟ kuḷḷirukkuṅ kōliyi aṭittal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aṭitta piṉ, aṭikkappaṭṭa kōliyaṉṟi aṭitta kōli pēntāviṟkuḷḷum pēntāvi ṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭikkuḷḷum niṟṟal kūṭātu; niṟpiṉ tavaṟām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa maṉṟip pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟkiṭik kappāṟ pōy niṟpiṉ, aṭittavaṉ mīṇṭum uttikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉmēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭittal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam aṭikkumuṉ, etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟku veḷi yil niṟpiṉ, pēntā cettu āṟu mācam eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇ ṭum; allākkāl tavaṟām. tavaṟiya pōtellām āṭakaṉ māṟal vēṇṭum.
aṭippavaṉ mūṉṟām muṟi uttiyil niṉṟu aṭikkumpōtu, etiriyiṉ kōliyi mūṉṟu taram toṭarntu aṭittal vēṇ ṭum. ataṉāl etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟkut toliviṟ pōkkappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
mūṉṟām muṟi uttiyil niṉṟu aṭikkumpōtu, etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟkuḷ illākkāl, aṭikkuṅ kōli pēntā viṟkuṭ ceṉṟu niṟpiṉum kuṟṟamilli.
oruvaṉ pēntāviṉ arukiṟpōy niṉṟa taṉ kōliyāl etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭikkumpōtu, pēntāmēṟ kipaṭum eṉṟu aṭippavaṉ muntic colliṉum, pēntāmēṟ kipaṭak kūṭātu eṉṟu aṭikkappaṭum kōlikkāraṉ muntic colli ṉum, avvāṟē niṟivēṟṟappaṭal vēṇṭum.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli oru paḷḷattiliruppiṉ, aṭippavaṉ nō eṉṟu colvāṉ. uṭaṉē, aṭikkappaṭum kōliyi etiri eṭuttu aṭippavaṉukku etirākap paḷḷanērukkuc camanilat til vittal vēṇṭum.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli ōr iṭukkiliruppiṉ aṭippavaṉ cāṇcūṭi caṭāpuṭā eṉṟu colvāṉ. uṭaṉē etiri ati eṭuttu aṭippavaṉukkut teriyum paṭi vittal vēṇṭum. cāṇcūṭi caṭāpuṭā illi eṉṟu etiri muntic colli ṭiṉ, ati eṭuttu vikka vēṇṭiyatilli.
oruvaṉ taṉ kōliyāl aṭikkumpōtu, ati vaḻic celvōr yārēṉum taṭuttuviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ taṭuttāl tūr eṉṟu colvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, avvaḻic celvōr taṉ kālāl atit taḷḷumpaṭi vēṇṭappaṭuvar.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli uttikkuk kiṭṭē irukkum pōtu ataṟkuriyavaṉ avviṭattiṉiṉṟu uttikkut tāṇṭiviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ taṉ kōliyit taṉ kaṇṇil vittāvatu uyarttiya kiyil vittāvatu etiriyiṉ kōliyiṉ mēliṭ ṭaṭittal vēṇṭum. av aṭi tavaṟiṉ, mīṇṭum nilamaṭṭattiṟ kiyāl teṟittaṭikkalām. av vaṭiyum tavaṟiṉ tōlviyām.
mūṉṟāmmuṟi uttiyil niṉṟu aṭippavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyit toṭarntu mūṉṟu taram aṭittu viṭiṉ etiri taṉ kōliyit taṉ muṭṭikkiyāl oru taṭavi allatu pala taṭavi taḷḷip pēntāviṟkuḷ koṇṭu vantu niṟuttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam niṟuttiviṭiṉ, avaṉē kelittavaṉāvaṉ; allākkāl tōṟṟavaṉāvaṉ. muṭṭikkiyāl kōliyit taḷḷuvati muṭṭitaḷḷal eṉpar.
 (ii) vaṭṭappēntā
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēntā eṉappaṭum vaṭṭakkōṭṭiṟkuḷ uruṭṭiyāṭuṅ kōliyāṭṭu vaki vaṭṭappēntāvām. itu caturap pēntāvuṭaṉ oppunōkki vaṭṭap pēntā eṉappaṭ ṭatu.
āṭuvār toki : mūvar mutal ettuṇip pērāyiṉum iti āṭalām. āyiṉum, aivarkkuk kuṟiyātum patiṉmarkkuk kūṭātum iruppiṉ ciṟappām.
āṭukaruvi : āṭakaruḷ ovvoruvaṉukkum pala kōlikaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟōṭu tel eṉṉum oru peruṅ kōli ovvoru vaṉiṭattumāvatu ellārkkum potuvāka vāvatu iruttal vēṇṭum.
ēṟattāḻa mukkaca viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭakkōṭu kīṟappaṭum. ataṉ naṭuvil oru kuḻi killappaṭum. vaṭṭattiṉ aṭiyil kāl vaṭṭam amiyumāṟu oru kuṟukkuk kōṭum kīṟappaṭum.
kāl vaṭṭamuḷḷa pakkattil, vaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu ēṟattāḻa mukkacat tolivil, utti kīṟappaṭum. ataṟku mēlum iru pakkattum kaviyumāṟu oru talikīḻāṉa pakarak kōṭu iṭappaṭum. atu mūṭi eṉappaṭum. ati iṉṟu ṭāppu (Top) eṉṉum āṅkilac collāṟ kuṟippar.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟamum puṟakkaṭiyum poṭṭalum veḷi nilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakarellārum mutaṉ mutal uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu , ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyi vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuḻiyi nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. yār kōli kuḻikku aṇṇaṇittāy (atavātu mika neruṅki) niṟkiṉṟatō, avaṉ muṉṉum; ataṟku aṭuttu niṟkuṅ kōlikkāraṉ piṉṉum; ataṟkaṭuttu niṟkuṅ kōlikkāraṉ ataṉ piṉṉumāka; iṅṅaṉam kuḻiyaṇmi varicippaṭi ellārum āṭal vēṇṭum. kōliyi uruṭṭumpōtu oruvaṉ kōli iṉ ṉoruvaṉati aṭittuviṭṭāl, ellār kōliyum mīṇṭum ovvoṉṟāy uruṭṭappaṭum. kōlikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu aṭipaṭuvatu caṭapuṭā eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār varici yoḻuṅku ivvāṟu tuṇiyappaṭṭa piṉ, muntiyāṭukiṟavaṉiṭam ēṉiyōrellārum āḷukkoru kōli koṭuttu viṭal vēṇṭum. avaṉ avaṟṟuṭaṉ taṉ kōliyiyuñ cērttu, ellāvaṟṟiyum mottamāy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ uruṭṭu vāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, tellāl (atāvatu kuṇṭāl) mukkāl vaṭṭattiluḷḷa kōlikaḷuḷ etiyēṉum evaṟṟiyēṉum aṭippāṉ. kāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷavaṟṟi aṭittal kūṭātu; aṭippiṉ, paccāvām. ataṟku oru kōli pōṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, aṭuttavaṉ āṭuvāṉ.
uruṭṭuvāṉ yārāyiṉum, mūṭiyi mitikkavāvatu tāṇṭavā vatu kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyiṉ tavaṟiyavaṉāvaṉ. ataṉāl aṭuttavaṉ āṭa nērum. mūṭi potuvāka mutaliṟ pōṭappaṭuva tilli. palamuṟi colliyuṅkēḷātu, oruvaṉ uttikku muṉṉāṟ kāl vittu muṉpuṟamākac cāyntu aṭippiṉ, mūṭi pōṭappaṭum. atu pōṭappaṭṭa piṉ ati mitippiṉ allatu tāṇṭiṉ paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṟku oru kāy pōṭṭu viṭavēṇṭum.
uruṭṭappaṭṭa kōlikaḷuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟu nērē kuḻiyil viḻuntuviṭiṉ, veṟṟiyām. avaṉ kāykaḷi aṭikkat tēvi yilli. uruṭṭiṉavaṉ tirumpavum āṭalām.
mukkāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷa kōliyi allatu kōlikaḷi aṭippiṉ, aṭipaṭṭa kōliyaṉittum vaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyēṟiviṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam veḷiyēṟiviṭiṉ atu oru veṟṟiyām. kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭālum kāyaṭittālum, ēṉiyōrellārum kelittavaṉukku ovvoru kāy koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻiyuḷ pōṭṭuk kāyum aṭittu viṭiṉ iraṭṭik kelippātaliṉ, ēṉiyōr ivvirukāy koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. mukkāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷa kāy kaḷi aṭikkumpōtu, kuḻik kāyiyum aṭikkalām; veḷik kāyiyum aṭikkalām. veṉṟavaṉ eṉṟum tirumpiyāṭuvāṉ.
kuḻiyilum pōṭāmal mukkāl vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ uḷḷa kāyi yum aṭikkāmal pōṉavaṉ, taṉ āṭṭatti iḻantu viṭuvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu oruvarum kāy pōṭuvatilli.
aṭitta kāyāvatu aṭikkappaṭṭa kāyāvatu vaṭṭattiviṭṭu veḷiyē pōkāviṭiṉ, paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṟku oru kōli aṭuttavaṉiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, āṭṭatti viṭṭu viṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭaviṭattum aṭitta kāy allatu aṭikkappaṭṭa kāy veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉ, iraṭṭip paccāvām. aṉṟu, aṭittavaṉ iru kāy koṭuttuviṭṭu āṭṭatti viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavaṉ avaṟṟiyuñ cērttu uruṭṭuvāṉ. avaṉ kelittuviṭiṉ, ap paccāk kāykaḷiyum vaḻakkappaṭi koṭukkiṟavaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk koḷḷalām; kelikkāviṭiṉ paccāk kāykaḷ paḻaṅkāy kaḷuṭaṉ cērttu uruṭṭappeṟum. evaṉ eṅṅaṉam kelip piṉum, paḻaṅkāykaḷi orupōtum kelippuk kāyākap peṟal muṭiyātu. āṭṭam muṭiyum vari, avi oru mutalpōl iruntukoṇṭēyirukkum. āṭṭam muṭintapiṉ, avaṉavaṉ mutaṟkāy avaṉavaṉic cērum.
oruvaṉ kelittaviṭattu iṉṉoruvaṉukkup pōṭak kāyillā viṭṭāl, avaṉ kaṭaṉākavāvatu vilikkāvatu piṟaṉiṭam vāṅkikkoḷḷalām; allākkāl, avaṉ mutalil iṭṭa kāyi iḻantuviṭuvāṉ.
ellārum āṭi muṭintapiṉ, oruvariṭamāvatu palariṭamāvatu kāy mikuti yāyc cērntirukkum. avaṟṟuḷ avaravar contak kāykku mēṟpaṭṭavellām kelippuk kāyākum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ: kōliyāṭṭiṉ potuppayaṉāka muṟkūṟap paṭṭavaṟṟoṭu, peruntokiyāṉa kōlikaḷi eḷitāy īṭṭikkoḷvatum, ciṟu mutalīyiṭṭup pērūtiyam peṟum karuttuṟavum, iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
 II. añcala kuñcam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aintām eṇṇic collum pōtu añcala kuñcam eṉṟu collappaṭum kōliyāṭṭu vaki, at toṭar moḻiyiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu. itaṟku orē kuḻiyuḷḷamiyāl, itu oṟṟik kuḻiyāṭṭam eṉavumpaṭum.
āṭuvār toki : ciṟuvaruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : pēntāviṟkuriyatē itaṟkum.
āṭukaruvi : pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk kōliyāṭṭiṟkuk kūṟiya aḷavuḷḷa oṟṟik kuḻiyum, ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar, kuḻikku 8 aṭi allatu 10 aṭit toliviluḷḷa uttik kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟu koṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyi uruṭṭal vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉatu kōli mutalilēyē kuḻikkuḷ viḻuntu viṭṭāl oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇām. ataṉ piṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭittu viṭṭāl pattām. atu paḻamākum. atōṭu ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum.
mutaliṟ kuḻikkuḷ viḻāviṭṭāl, kuḻikkuk kiṭṭa irukkiṟa kōlikkāraṉ muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. avaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭikkalām; allatu kuḻikkuṭ pōṭalām. etiri yiṉ kōliyit tavaṟātu aṭittuviṭiṉ aintām; aṅṅaṉa miṉṟik kuḻikkuṭ pōṭṭu viṭiṉ nāṉkām.
nāṉkām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉum aintām eṇṇi liruntu toṭaṅkiṉum, pattām eṇvari toṭartal vēṇ ṭum. cila eṇkaṭkuk kuḻiyum cila eṇkaṭku aṭiyum ākum. kuḻi eṉpatu kuḻikku aṭittal. nāṉkām eṇṇi liruntu toṭaṅkiṉ, 5, 8, 9, 10 eṉpaṉa aṭiyām; 6, 7 eṉpaṉa kuḻiyām; aintām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉ 6, 7 eṉpaṉa kuḻiyām; 8, 9, 10 eṉpaṉa aṭiyām. pattāvatu, kōliyi aṭittaṟkup patilāka kuḻikkuḷ aṭippiṉ, mīṇṭum nāṉkām.
 kuḻikkuḷ aṭikkum pōtum kāyi (atāvatu etiriyiṉ kōli yi) aṭikkumpōtum tavaṟiviṭiṉ, aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭumpōtu, aintām eṇ mutal pattām eṇ vari ovvōr eṇṇiṟkum ovvoru maraput toṭarmoḻi kūṟikkoḷvatuṇṭu. avi,
 añcala kuñcam,
 aṟuvakka tāṭi,
 eḻuvakka miṉā,
eṭṭakka kōṭṭi, (eṭṭōṭaṭṭak kōṭṭi)
tompaṉip pēṭṭi, (tomparap pēṭṭi, tompalap pāṭi).
tocukkaṭṭurācā, (tēciṅkurācā, campā viḻuntatu) eṉpaṉavām.
pattām aṭi tavaṟātu muntiyaṭittavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi mūṉṟu taṭavi aṭittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṭittut taḷḷap paṭṭa kōlikkāraṉ muṭṭi taḷḷa vēṇṭum; atāvatu taṉ kōliyit taṉ muṭṭik kiyāl kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavi taḷḷik koṇṭu vantu kuḻikkuḷ vīḻtta vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vīḻtti viṭiṉ kelippām; illāviṭiṉ tōṟpām.
pattām aṭi tavaṟātu aṭittavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭittut taḷḷumpōtu, etiri taṉ kōlineṭun toliviṟkup pōy viṭātavāṟu taṉ muṭṭikkiyāl atit taṭukkalām; kālāl taṭuttal kūṭātu; kālāl taṭuppiṉ 7 taṭavi muṭṭi taḷḷal vēṇṭum.
muṭṭi taḷḷukiṟavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavi taḷḷiyum taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuṭ koṇṭu vantu niṟutta muṭiyāviṭiṉ, aṭittavaṉ ati mīṇṭum aṭittup pōkkuvāṉ. muṭṭi taḷḷiṉavaṉ mīṇṭum muṭṭi taḷḷuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam nāṉku muṟi aṭippum taḷḷum nikaḻalām. ovvoru muṟikkum aṭippiṟkum taḷḷiṟkum kaṇakkuṇṭu.
mutaṉmuṟi 	3 aṭi 	3 taḷḷu
2 ām muṟi 	2 aṭi 	2 taḷḷu
3 ām muṟi 	1 aṭi 	1 taḷḷu
4 ām muṟi 	1 aṭi 	3 taḷḷu
muṭṭi taḷḷukiṟavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavi taḷḷiyum taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuṭ koṇṭu vantu niṟutta muṭiyāviṭiṉ, kīḻvarumāṟu oru valakkārattik kiyāḷvatuṇṭu. avaṉatu kōli aṭikka muṭiyāta tolivilirukkumpōtu, aṭikkiṟa vaṉ taṉakku vacatiyuṇṭākumāṟu mēluñ caṟṟut taḷḷac colvāṉ. appōtu taḷḷukiṟavaṉ, aṭikkiṟa vaṉukkut teriyātavāṟu taṉ kōliyi oru kikkuḷiṭṭu maṟittu iru kikaḷiyum muṭṭiyāka vittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟic caṟṟu muṉṉāka eṭuttu vittup pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ pōtātu eṉṟu colliṉ, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ iṉṉoru muṭṭik kiyi muntiṉatiṉum caṟṟu muṉpāka eṭuttuvittup pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ mīṇṭum pōtātu eṉṟu colliṉ, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ mīṇṭum piṉṉāka irukkum kiyi muṉṉāka eṭuttu vippāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ kōlik ki etuveṉṟum veṟuṅki etuveṉṟum teriyāmal, pōtātu,; pōtātu eṉṟu mēlum mēlum collac collat, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ taṉ iru kikaḷiyum māṟi māṟi muṉṉāka eṭuttu vittu, iṟutiyil kōlikkiyik kuḻimēl vittukkoṇṭu, pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ kuḻik kiyi veṟuṅki eṉṟu karutikkoṇṭu, pōtum eṉpāṉ; uṭaṉē taḷḷukiṟavaṉ taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuḷ iṭṭuviṭṭut taṉ kiyi eṭuttuviṭuvāṉ; aṭittavaṉ ēmāṟip pōvāṉ; taḷḷiṉavaṉ kelittu viṭuvāṉ. itaṟku mīṉ piṭittal eṉṟu peyar.
taḷḷukiṟavaṉ mīṉ piṭikkalāmā piṭikkak kūṭātā eṉpatu, muṭṭi taḷḷu muṉṉarē muṭivu ceyyappeṟum.
 III. irukuḻiyāṭṭam (iṣṭam)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cuvaraṭi araṅkiṟkuḷ iru kuḻi vittāṭum kōliyāṭṭu vaki irukuḻiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum. itaṟku iṭṭam (iṣṭam) allatu kicēpi eṉṟum peyar.
iv āṭṭu perumpālum tirucci vaṭṭārattil āṭap peṟuvatākum.
āṭuvār toki : ciṟāruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum iruvar allatu ( iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa) palar iti āṭuvar. ōr āṭṭiyil iruvarē iti āṭa muṭiyumātalāl, palar āṭuvatāyiṉ ivviruvarākavē āṭak kūṭum.
āṭukaruvi : cuvaraṭi (allatu mēṭṭaṭi) nilattil, ēṟattāḻa 3 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamumuḷḷa oru nīḷ catura araṅkamiyu māṟu ōr akaṉṟa pakarak kōṭu kīṟik koḷḷal vēṇṭum. av varaṅkiṉ neṭumpakkaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu cuvaraṭiyāyirukkum vaṇṇam, pakarattiṉ iru pakkak kōṭṭu muṉikaḷum cuvarit toṭutal vēṇṭum. avvaraṅkiṟkuḷ iṭavalamāka iru kuḻikaḷ killal vēṇṭum.
araṅkiṟku ēṟattāḻa mukkacat tolivil ōraṭi nīḷam utti kīṟappaṭum. ataṟku ṭāppu (Top) eṉṉum mūṭi iṭappaṭum.
oruvaṉ āṭumpōtu iṉṉoruvaṉukkuk kōli tēvi yāyirāmiyiṉ, āṭakar ellārkkum potuvāka mukkōli yiruntāṟ pōtum. avaṟṟuḷ, iraṇṭu uruṭṭuvaṉa; oṉṟu avaṟṟi aṭippatu. aṭikkuṅkōli caṟṟup peritāka virukkum. ataṟkut tiruccip pāṅkaril kuṭṭu allatu tel eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam: cuvaraṭi nilamum mēṭṭaṭi nilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : tōṟṟavaṉ kelittavaṉukku ivvaḷavu kācu koṭut tal vēṇṭumeṉṟu muṉṉatāka muṭivu ceytukoṇṭu, uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉpaṭiyō yārēṉum oruvaṉ muntiyāṭuvaṉ. āṭuvār ellārkkum kōli potuvā yiṉ, kōlikkuc contakkāraṉ muntiyāṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
āṭukiṟavaṉ uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, mutalāvatu iru ciṟu kōlikaḷiyum araṅkiṟkuḷ uruṭṭi, avaṟṟuḷ eti aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu etiriyik kēṭpāṉ. av iraṇṭiṉuḷ āṭukiṟavaṉukkuk kiṭṭa iruppati aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉ kīḻ eṉṟum, caṟṟu eṭṭa (atāvatu tolivil) iruppati aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉ mēl eṉṟum, etiri colvāṉ. kīḻ eṉpatiṟkup patilāka etir eṉṟum colvatuṇṭu. āṭukiṟavaṉ etiri coṉṉa kōliyi aṭittu viṭṭāṟ kelittavaṉāvaṉ. avaṉukkup pēciṉapaṭi etiri kācik koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. mutalil eṟiyappaṭṭa iru kōlikaḷum iruviralukku mēl iṭiyiṭṭiruppiṉ, etiri coṉṉatit tāṉ aṭittal vēṇṭum; iruviraṟku uṭpaṭ ṭiruppiṉ, etiyum aṭikkalām. iṅṅaṉam viruppa māṉati aṭikkuṅ kāraṇam paṟṟiyō, iv āṭṭiṟku iṭṭam allatu kicēpi eṉṟu peyar.
iṭiyīṭu iruviraṟkuṭpaṭṭa niliyil etiyum aṭikkum pōtu iraṇṭilum paṭṭuviṭṭāl, paccara eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. atōṭu, aṭitta kōliyāyiṉum aṭikkappaṭṭa kōli yāyiṉum kuḻikkuḷ vīḻntuviṭiṉ, iraṭṭippaccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. kuṟṟamellām tōlvittāṉamē.
mutalil eṟinta iru kōlikaḷum araṅkiṟku uḷvīḻiṉum veḷivīḻiṉum, mūṉṟāṅ kōli nērē kuḻikkuḷ viḻuntuviṭiṉ, eṟintavaṉukku etiri pēciṉa tokiyi iraṭṭippāyk kaṭṭiviṭil vēṇṭum.
mutalil eṟinta oru kōlikaḷuḷ oṉṟu araṅkiṟku veḷiyē niṟpiṉ, etiri ati eṭuttu araṅkuk kōṭṭiṉ mēl vippāṉ; allatu muṭṭikkiyāl araṅkiṟkuḷ taḷḷi viṭuvāṉ. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉataṟku mūṭṭutal eṉṟu peyar.
aṭikkuṅ kōli mutal vīḻvilēyē araṅkiṟkuḷ viḻuntu viṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi ataṟku veḷiyē vīḻiṉ, atu veḷimaṭṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamātaliṉ āṭiṉavaṉ tōṟṟa vaṉāvaṉ.
mūṉṟāṅ kōli (atāvatu aṭikkuṅ kōli) araṅkiṟkuḷ kāyi aṭikkātu kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpiṉ, kōṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ati liṉ (line) allatu lāk (lock) eṉṉum āṅkilac collāṟ kuṟippar.
uttiyil niṉṟu aṭittavaṉ ativiṭṭu nīṅkum pōtu, mūṭiyi mitikkātu ōreṭṭup piṉvittu iṭamākavāvatu valamāka vāvatu cuṟṟi muṉvaral vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi mūṭiyi mitittuviṭiṉ tavaṟiṉavaṉāvaṉ.
āṭukiṟavaṉ aṭitta kōliyum iṉṉoṉṟum araṅkiṟkuḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭukkoṇṭu niṟpiṉ, avaṉ avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi iṉṉoṉṟu alukkātavāṟu eṭuttal vēṇṭum. alukkiviṭiṉ tōṟṟavaṉāvaṉ. aluṅkāmal eṭuttaṟkāka iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil ciṟitu maṇṇit tūvuvatu vaḻakkam. āṭṭiṟ kelittavaṉ maṟu āṭṭiyil muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. āṭakar palarāyiṉ, tōṟṟavaṉ nīṅki vēṟoruvaṉ etiriyāvaṉ.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kuṟi tappāmal uruṭṭiyaṭikkap payilvatum, oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭuniṟkum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟip piṟitu allatu piṟa alukkātavāṟu eṭukkap paḻakuvatum, ivvāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
IV. mukkuḻiyāṭṭam
(i) cēlam vaṭṭāra muṟi.

āṭṭiṉ peyar : cuvaraṭiyaṅkiṟkuḷ irukuḻikkup patilāka mukkuḻi vittāṭum kōliyāṭṭa vakiyē mukkuḻiyāṭṭam.
āṭu muṟi : mēṟkūṟiya iru kuḻiyāṭṭamum iṅkuk kuṟitta mukkuḻiyāṭṭamum oṉṟē. āyiṉ, iṭa vēṟupāṭu kāraṇa mākap piṉvarumāṟu mūvaki vēṟṟumiyuṇṭu.

kōvaṉ - kōṉ = 1. iṭiyaṉ. 2. aracaṉ.
kōṉ - kōṉār = iṭiyar. kōṉ - kō = aracaṉ.
kōvaṉ eṉpatik kōpa eṉṟu tirittum piṉpu atik kō + pa eṉṟu pirittum, kōvip pātukāppavaṉ eṉṟu vaṭavar poruḷ kūṟuvatu poruntātu. kōvaṉ eṉpatu āyaṉ (ā +aṉ) eṉpati yottatē.
āṅkilattil (tiyūttāṉiyattil) kō (OS), kū (OE) ku (E) eṉṟu eṭuppoliyiṉṟiyē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. vēta āriyat til tāṉ go eṉṟu eṭuttum gous eṉṟu tirintum olittiruk kiṉṟatu. (va.va: 131 - 132.)
kōvaṉ
āyaṉ ākkaḷik kāppatu pōla aracaṉ makkaḷik kātta lāl, aracaṉukkuk kōvaṉ eṉṟu peyar. kōvaṉ eṉpatu muṟiyē kōṉ, kō eṉat tiriyum. kō - pacu. kōkkaḷi mēyppatu paṟṟi āyaṉukkuk kōṉ, kōṉār eṉṉum orumip peyarum uyarvup paṉmip peyarum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. āyaṉik kuṟikkum kōṉ eṉṉum collum aracaṉik kuṟikkum kōṉ eṉṉum collum oṉṟē. aracaṉukkum kuṭikaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu āyaṉukkum ānirikkum uḷḷa toṭarpu pōṉṟatu eṉpatē, kōṉ eṉṉum collāl kuṟikkappaṭum karuttām. aracaṉ kiyiluḷḷa kōlum āyaṉ kikkōl pōṉṟatē. kōl eṉṉum col ākupeyarp poruḷil aracāṭciyik kuṟikkum. nērmiyāṉa āṭci ceṅkōl eṉṟum koṭumi yāṉa āṭci koṭuṅkōl eṉṟum collappaṭum.
(kiṟittavak kurumāri ik karuttup paṟṟiyē āṅkilattil Shepherd eṉṟum Pastor eṉṟum aḻippar. kiṟittava capiyār āṭukaḷum, accapik kurumār mēypparum pōlvar. nāṉē nalla mēyppaṉ eṉṟār ēcu perumāṉār.)
iṉi, civaperumāṉum uyirkaḷi yellām kāppatil āyaṉi allatu aracaṉi oppavarātaliṉ kōvaṉ eṉappaṭuvar. uyirkaḷi allatu āṉmākkaḷip potuppaṭap pacu eṉpatum, civaperumāṉip pacupati eṉpatum vaṭamoḻi vaḻakku. (col. 4-5.)
ṅammutal
ulaka vaḻakkiluḷḷa ovvoru moḻiyilum coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum nāḷaṭivil māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. am māṟṟam tamiḻil marūu, tiripu, citivu, pōli eṉap palpeyarāṟ kūṟappaṭum. moḻikaḷiṉ vaṭivu māṟavē avaṟṟiṉ mutaliṭi kaṭi yeḻuttukaḷum māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu collāmē pōtarum.
piṟamoḻikaḷil moḻimutaliṭikaṭi yeḻuttukaṭku varam pilli. tamiḻilō av varampuṇṭu. oru taṉimoḻiyiṉ allatu taṉittu vaḻaṅkak kūṭiya moḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu maṟṟōreḻut tāyt tiriyiṉ, attiripeḻuttē mutaleḻuttāyk koḷḷap paṭum. ilakkaṇam iṭantariṉ.
e-ṭu:		naṇṭu - ñaṇṭu.
ariñāṇ (arināṇ), piññīli (pinnīli), cēyñalūr (cēynallūr) mutaliya toṭarmoḻikaḷiluḷḷa varumoḻikaḷ ñāṇ, ñīli, ñallūr eṉat tam tirinta vaṭivilum taṉittu vaḻaṅkun takuti ciṟituṭimiyiṉ avaṟṟiṉ mutaliluḷḷa tiripeḻuttukaḷum moḻimutaleḻuttukaḷākak koḷḷap paṭal kūṭum. āṉāl, oru toṭarmoḻiyiluḷḷa varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu moḻi mutalallā eḻuttākat tiriyiṉ, av vitimutal iyalpumutalākak koḷḷappaṭātu. īṇṭu iyalpu mutal vitimutal eṉpavaṟṟi iyalpīṟu vitiyīṟu eṉpavaṟṟi yeṇṇi yuṇarka.
e-ṭu: 		al + tālam = aṟṟālam.
			viṇ + nāṭu = viṇṇāṭu.
ivaṟṟil ṟā, ṇā eṉṉum vitimutalkaḷ moḻi mutaleḻut tākāmi yuṇarka.
tolkāppiyar ciṟanta ārāycciyuṭiya rātaliṉ, ṅakaram varu moḻiyiṉ vitimutalāy maṭṭum varuvatu kaṇṭu ati moḻi mutaleḻuttākak kūṟiyilar. naṉṉūlārō av vārāycci yiṉmiyiṉ, vitimutalāy vanta ṅakaratti iyalpu mutaleṉa mayaṅkik kūṟiṉar.
ṅakaram aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam, eṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam eṉak cuṭṭu viṉāmutaṟ ṟoṭarmoḻikaḷil varumoḻi vitimutalā yirukkumēyaṉṟi, yāṇṭum taṉimoḻiyil iyalpu mutalāy varuvataṉṟu. aṅṅaṉam. iṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉa avvitam ivvitam eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvaṉavākum. avvaṇṇam. av vaki, avvitam, appaṭi eṉṉum toṭar moḻi kaḷiluḷḷa vaṇṇam. vaki, vitam, paṭi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ taṉittu vaḻaṅku vatu pōla, ṅaṉam eṉṉum col taṉittu vaḻaṅkuvataṉṟu. avaṉ ceyta vitam, avaṉ ceyta ṅaṉam eṉṉun toṭar kaḷik kūṟik kāṇka.
aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliya toṭarmoḻikaḷil mellōci ciṟantiruttaliṉ, melittal (Nasalization) ōci mikutiyum payil kiṉṟa maliyāḷa vaḻakkil orukāl ṅaṉam eṉṉum col taṉittu vaḻaṅkalāmeṉṟu karutip palaviṭattumuḷḷa maliyāḷarik kēṭṭatil avarkaḷum ṅaṉam eṉṉumcol tamiḻiṟpōlavē maliyāḷattilum toṭarmoḻip pakutiyāy vaḻaṅkuvataṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkuvataṉṟu eṉa orupaṭittāyp pakarntuviṭṭaṉar.
aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam eṉṉum coṟṟoṭarkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷami caṅka nūlkaḷālaṟiyappaṭum. ikkālattilum cilar aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam eṉṟē kūṟuvati ulakavaḻakkiṟ kāṇalām.

kaṇ eṉṉum iṭappeyar cuṭṭu viṉāvoṭu puṇarntu. ak kaṇ, ikkaṇ, ekkaṇ eṉa niṟkum. piṉpu melittaṉ muṟiyāl aṅkaṇ, iṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉat tiriyum.

aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliya peyarkaḷum coṟpaṭi mutalil iṭattik kuṟittaṉavēṉum, piṉpu iṭam - vaḻi - vaki eṉṉum iyipupaṟṟi ākupeyart taṉmiyil vaki, vitam eṉṉum poruḷ taruvavāyiṉa.

cakkaram
cakkaram - cakra (c) - i.vē.
cuḻital = vaḷital, tirukutal.
cuḻi - caḻi. caḻital = oru pakkañ carital.

cakaṭi
cakaṭi - cakaṭī (l, va@)
cakaṭiki - cakaṭikā (l, va@)  (va.va.137)
cakōram
cakōram - cakōra (c)
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam cak (c) eṉpatākum. itu kuṟikkum poruḷ kaḷ pontiki (tirupti), etirppu, oḷirvu eṉpaṉavē.  (va.va.137)
caṅkam
caṅkam - caṅka (ḷaÝ, mā) - a.vē.
vaṭamoḻiyil iccoṟku mūlamumilli; caṅku eṉṉum mūla vaṭivumilli.  (va.va.137.)

vēṅkaṭamaki	caturtaṇṭi pirakācikā	ki.pi. 1637
talikkaḻaka iṭikkaḻakat tamiḻ ici nāṭaka nūlkaḷ aṉittum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa.
nāratar iyaṟṟiya pañca pāratīyam, yāmaḷēntirar iyaṟṟiya intirakāḷiyam, cikaṇṭiyār iyaṟṟiya ici nuṇukkam ākiya tamiḻ ici nūlkaḷ pāratak kālattiyaṭuttu, ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum iyaṟṟap peṟṟaviyākum.
ki.pi. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari pāṇaruṅ kūttarumē tamiḻakattil iṉṉiciyum nāṭakamum payiṟṟiyum vaḷarttum vantārēṉum, pirāmaṇar kaḻakak kālattilēyē muṉṉōkkoṭu avviru kalikaḷiyum payiṉṟum avi paṟṟiya nūlkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peyarttum vittiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
āriyar tamiḻ nūlkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyil moḻipeyarkkum pōtu, peyarpput tōṉṟāvāṟu cila vaḻikaḷik kiyāṇṭi rukkiṉṟaṉar. avi, maṟu pākupāṭu (Re-classification), putup peyarīṭu (Change of nomenclature), māṟṟam (Alteration), cērkki (Addition) eṉpaṉa. itu icinūliṟ piṟaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻp pāṇar, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum vīṇi vāyilāka ici nuṭpaṅkaḷiyellām tam eḵkuc cevi yālum kūrmatiyāluṅ kaṇṭaṟintu; paṇṇum paṇṇiyalum tiṟamum tiṟattiṟamumāka nālvakip paṇkaḷ vakuttu, nāṟperum paṇkaḷiṉiṉṟu nūṟṟumūṉṟum paṉṉīrāyiramumāka (11991) narappaṭivāṟ paṇperukki; āyappāli vaṭṭappāli caturappāli tirikōṇappāli eṉṉum paṇtirivu muṟi kaḷāl pēricikaḷ ariyuṅ kālum arikkālumāṉaciṟṟici kaḷāka nuṇukum vaki nuṇittaṟintu; peṇṇiṟkup piṉ paṇ eṉṉu māṟu elliyaṟṟa iṉpam payakkum muṟiyil vaḷart tirunta icittamiḻ eṉṉum tamiḻiciyik karunāṭaka caṅkītam eṉap peyar māṟṟi, pāṇarit tīṇṭātavareṉa vilakki, paṉṉīricik kōviyip patiṉāṟicik kōvi yeṉak kāṭṭi, ākaṇavuḻi (cutta mattimam) antaravuḻi (piratimat timam) eṉṉum ici vēṟupaṭṭāl eḻupattiru tāyp paṇṇeṉa vakuttu; āmpal, kuṟiñci, curuṭṭi, cevvaḻi, takkam, nāṭṭi, nērici, puṟanīrmi, mulli, viḷari eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷi māṟṟik karakarap piriyā, caṅkarā paraṇam, tanyāci, nīlāmpari, piravi, mattiyamāvati, vaṉajāṭci eṉa āriya iṭukuṟip peyar kaḷiyiṭṭu, vaṭa moḻiyilum vaṭamoḻi kalanta tiraviṭattilum pāṭṭukkaḷi iyaṟṟippāṭi, āriyak kaliyākak kāṭṭiyiruppatu ettuṇi yiraṇṭakac ceyal!
iṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ mutaṉūlkaḷiyoṭṭiya kaṇitam, maruttuvam mutaliya pala nūlkaḷum. kāma cūttiram, kokkōkam mutaliyaṉa vum piṟavum, vevvēṟu kālattu vaṭamoḻiyil iyaṟṟap paṭṭaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kalikaḷiyellām 64 āka eṇṇiṉar. nālvētam, āṟucātiram, patiṉeṇpurāṇam, aṟupattu nāṉku kaliñāṉam eṉṟu colvatu marapu. kalikaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭ cila, kalikaḷalla.
vaṭamoḻi pēccumoḻi yaṉṟāyiṉum, pirāmaṇar tam kūṭṭaraviyal mēmpāṭṭip pōṟṟik kākka, iṉṟum atil nūliyaṟṟiyum kaṭṭuri varintum tirumukam viṭuttum varavēṟ pitaḻ paṭittuk koṭuttum varuvatu, avaratu oppuyarvaṟṟa moḻiveṟiyi viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
vaṭanūlkaḷ tōṉṟat tōṉṟat teṉṉūlkaḷ maṟintu koṇṭē vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉ moḻit toṉṉūlellām iṟantupaṭṭapiṉ, vaṭamoḻip peyarppu nūlum putunūlum, mutunūlum mūla nūlumpōṟ kāṭciyaḷik kiṉṟaṉa. itu, katiravaṉ karantapiṉ tiṅkaḷ tikaḻva tottatē.
vaṭamoḻi tamiḻakattil tamiḻālēyē vaḷarntateṉpati, vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷum viyākaraṇaṅkaḷum icinaṭa kaṇiya maruttuva nūlkaḷum, civa tirumāl neṟikaḷum maṭṭumaṉṟi, ciṟanta vaṭa nūlāciriyar palar tamiḻ nāṭṭārāyiruntatum, 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vētaṅkaṭku viḷakkavuri varinta cāyaṉāc cāriyār teṉṉāṭṭārāyiruntatum, iṉṟum taliciṟanta vaṭamoḻip paṇṭitar tamiḻ nāṭṭārāyiruppatum, teḷḷat teḷiyak kāṭṭum.
vaṭamoḻip paṭṭāṅku nūlkaḷil, peruntoṭariyam (mahā vākya) eṉap paṟicāṟṟappeṟum tat tvam asi eṉṉum coṟṟoṭarē, muṟṟun tamiḻttiripāyiruntu vaṭamoḻi yāḷarit talikaviḻac ceykiṉṟatu.
tāṉ - tat, nūm - num - tum - tvam, iru - i (tiyūt) - e (ilat, ki.) - a (va.). ti (muṉṉiliyorumiyīṟu) - si.
tāṉ eṉpatu mutaṟkālattiṟ paṭarkkiyorumic cuṭṭup peyarāyiruntu, piṉpu taṟcuṭṭup peyarāyiṟṟu.
caccari
caccari - jarjarā (jh)
itu teṉṉāṭṭup paṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
kokkariyiṉ caccariyiṉ pāṇiyāṉi  (tēvā. 722:1)
ippeyar olikkuṟippaṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟiyatu.  (va.va. 137.)
caṭam - jaṭa
vaṭavar kuḷirmi eṉpati mūlakkaruttāka vittu, kuḷir, cilleṉal, viṟippu, marappu. aciviṉmi, uṇarviṉmi, uṇarcciyiṉmi, mayakkam, timir, maṭami, mantam, uyiriṉmi, aṟiviṉmi eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷ vaḷarppar. aṟiñar itaṉ poruntāmiyi aṟintukoḷka.
kuḷirmip poruḷil jaṭa eṉpatu caḷi eṉṉum teṉcol tiripāka irukkalām.
caṭam - caṭalam - uṭampu. itu ulaka vaḻakku.
x.neh.: paṭam - paṭalam.
caṭalam eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
caṭalam - cataram = uṭampu (nelli vaḻakku).
cataram - catiram (koccit tiripu)  (va.va. 140-141.)
caṭu kuṭu viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: āṭuvāruḷ ovvoruvaṉum etirkkaṭciyiṉ ellikkuṭ ceṉṟu, caṭukuṭu eṉṟu colliyāṭum āṭṭu, caṭukuṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. itu, pāṇṭi nāṭṭil kuṭaṭṭi eṉṟum, vaṭacōḻa nāṭṭil paliccappiḷāṉ allatu palīṉ caṭukuṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum. caṭukuṭu eṉpatu teṉcōḻanāṭṭilum koṅku nāṭṭilum itaṟku vaḻaṅkum peyarām.
āṭuvār toki : nālvarkkuk kuṟiyāta iraṭṭip paṭiyāṉa tokiyiṉar, iti āṭalām.
āṭiṭam : veḷinilattilum, poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvārellārum, uttikaṭṭic camat tokiyāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar. iru kaṭciyār iṭattiṟkum iṭiyil oru kuṟukkuk kōṭu kīṟappeṟum, cōḻa nāṭṭār iti uppukkōṭu eṉpar.
mutalāvatu, entak kaṭciyār pāṭivaruvatu eṉṟu ētāvatoru vakiyil tīrmāṉikkappeṟum. aṅṅaṉam tīrmāṉikkappeṟṟa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ uppukkōṭṭit tāṇṭi etirkkaṭci yellikkuṭ pukuntu, caṭukuṭu caṭukuṭu eṉṟō, caṭukuṭu kuṭu kuṭu kuṭu eṉṟō, palīṉ caṭukuṭu kuṭukuṭu eṉṟō, iṭi viṭātu pāṭikkoṇṭu etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉiyō, palariyō toṭṭu viṭṭu avaṉiṭamēṉum avariṭamēṉum piṭikoṭātu taṉ kaṭci ellikkuḷ vantuviṭa vēṇṭum. aṅ ṅaṉam varumvari pāṭuvati niṟuttakkūṭātu. avaṉāl toṭap paṭṭava rellām tolintuviṭuvar. avar āṭṭiṟkalavātu oru puṟamāyp pōyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṉ, toṭap paṭṭa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ iṅṅaṉamē etirkkaṭci yellik kuṭ pukuntu āṭic cella vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ etirkkaṭci ellikkuḷ āṭumpōtu pāṭuvatit tāṉē niṟuttiviṭṭālum, etirkkaṭciyāriṭam piṭipaṭṭu ati niṟuttiviṭṭālum, avaṉ tolintavaṉāvaṉ. toli talip paṭṭup pōtal eṉpar. oru kaṭciyār tam ellikkuḷ āṭum etiriyit toṭṭup piṭikka muyaṉṟu muṭiyāmaṟpōyiṉ, avaṉit toṭṭavarellām toṭappaṭṭarākavē karutap paṭṭut tolintu viṭuvar. oruvaṉ etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oru vaṉiyō palariyō toṭṭuviṭṭu varumpōtu, taṉ kaṭci yellikkuṭ kālvikkumuṉ pāṭuvati niṟuttiviṭiṉ avaṉāl toṭappaṭṭavar paṭṭuppōkār.
taṉippaṭṭavarkku veṟṟiyō tōlviyō illāmal iru kaṭciyārum camamākavuḷḷavari, kaṭcik koruvaṉāka māṟi māṟip pāṭivaral vēṇṭum. oru kaṭciyār oruvaṉi iḻantu viṭiṉ (aḵtāvatu avaruḷ oruvaṉ paṭṭuppōṉāl) avarē aṭutta muṟiyum pāṭic cella vēṇṭum.
orucila āṭakar caṭukuṭu eṉṟu collāmal pāṭṭup pōl mōṉi yetuki aminta neṭumoḻit toṭarkaḷiyum colvatuṇṭu. (oruvaṉ taṉ veṟṟi vīram valimi mutaliya vaṟṟi etiriyiṭam mikuttuk kūṟuvatu neṭumoḻiyām).
caṭukuṭu mutaliya toṭarkaḷi mūccuviṭāmaṟ collavēṇṭum eṉpatu marapu. āyiṉum cilar mūccu viṭṭuk koṇṭē iṭiviṭātu pāṭikkoḷvar.
entak kaṭciyil aṉivarum muṉpu paṭukiṉṟaṉarō, antak kaṭci tōṟṟatākum. tōṟṟavarkkut tōlviyāl uṇṭākum ciṟṟavamāṉamēyaṉṟi vēṟoru taṇṭaṉiyumilli.
āṭṭuttōṟṟa viḷakkam: paṇṭit tamiḻaracar ceytu vanta pōrvakikaḷuḷ veṭci eṉpatu oṉṟu. atu niri kavartal allatu niri mīṭṭal. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉatu karanti yeṉappaṭum.
kuṟumparacar koḷḷit talivar ceyalākavāvatu, vēntar ēvalālāvatu nikaḻvatu niri kavartal. niri kavarappaṭṭa nāṭṭaracaṉ taṉ paṭikaḷik koṇṭu niri mīṭṭal karanti.
niri kavarumpōtum niri mīṭkumpōtum pōr nikaḻum. ap pōril veṟṟi tarumāṟu koṟṟavi eṉṉum kāḷikkut tuṭi koṭṭip paliyiṭutal marapu. itu koṟṟavi nili yeṉap paṭum.
maṟaṅkaṭik kūṭṭiya tuṭinili ciṟanta
koṟṟavi niliyum attiṇip puṟaṉē  (puṟat. 4)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
caṭukuṭu eṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭu, veṭcip pōriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya tākat terikiṉṟatu. iru kaṭciyārum veṭci maṟavarum karanti maṟavarum pōlvar. iṭikkōṭu, avvirucārār nāṭṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ellippuṟam pōṉṟatu. palicaṭukuṭu eṉṟu pāṭuvatu, kāḷikkup paliyiṭuvatiyum tuṭi koṭṭuvatiyum kuṟippatu (tuṭippaṟiyiṟ ciṟiyavaki caṭukuṭukki allatu kuṭuku ṭuppi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.) irukaṭciyārum tattam etirk kaṭciyārip piṭittu niṟuttuvatu, niri kavar taliyum niri mīṭṭaliyum nikarppatu. paṭṭuppōtal eṉṉum vaḻakku veṭcippōril vīḻntiṟattalik kuṟippatu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : inta āṭṭiṉāl, nīṇṭa nēram mūccaṭakkavum, tiruṭaṉip piṭikkavum, etiriyiṉiṉṟu tappik koḷḷavum, payiṟci peṟalām.
koṅkunāṭṭu vēṟupāṭu : pāṇṭināṭṭār caṭukuṭuviṟku iṭikkō ṭoṉṟē kīṟikkoḷvar. koṅku nāṭṭārō, irukaṭciyārkkum iru vēṟu caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷi amittuk koḷvar. āṭumpōtu ovvoruvarum tattam kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷḷēyē niṟṟal vēṇṭum. veḷiyēṟiṉ vilakiviṭal vēṇṭum.

caṭiccampā, caṭiccāmi, caṭippayaṟu, caṭipparutti, caṭiyavari eṉpaṉa, kaṟṟiyāṉa allatu kottāṉa poruḷ kaḷi uṇarttum.

cataram - caturacra (u,ḷaÝ)

caṭṭam = 1. palaki ōli mutaliya taṭṭiyāṉa poruḷ. 
2. kottaṉ caṭṭap palaki, 3. caṭṭap palaki pōṉṟa nērmi, 4. variyiḻukkuñ caṭṭam (Flat ruler) 5. nērvari, 6. nērvari pōṉṟa oḻukkaneṟi, caṭṭa tiṭṭam.
7. nāṟpuṟamun titta caṭṭam (Frame), nāṟpuṟac caṭṭamāṉa caturam;
8. uyirukku allatu ātaṉukku (āṉmāviṟku)c caṭṭam pōṉṟirukkum uṭampu.
(āṅkilattilum caṭṭattik kuṟikkum (kacayaana) eṉṉuñ col uṭampik kuṟittal kāṇka.);
9. kaṭṭil mutaliyaṉa piṉṉutaṟkum vīṭu kaṭṭutaṟkum paṭam varitaṟkum amittuk koḷḷum caṭṭa amippu, 
10. muṉṉēṟpāṭāṉa tiṭṭam (Plan), 11. aṇiyam (āyattam);
12. kaṭṭuri mutaliyaṉa varitaṟkuk kuṟittuk koḷḷum kuṟippu;
13. pārtteḻututaṟku vittuk koḷḷum mēlvariyeḻuttu, 14. etaṟkum aḷaviyāṉa mēlvaric caṭṭam, 15. ceppam allatu cīrmi.

cataram (2) - carīra (i.vē.).
ta-ra. pōli. x.neh.: viti-viri.
vaṭavaruḷ oru cārār cri eṉṉum collik kāṭṭit tāṅku vatu eṉṟum, maṟṟoru cārār crū eṉṉuñ collikkāṭṭi eḷitāy aḻikkappaṭuvatu eṉṟum, poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar.
cataram - caturam = nāṉ mūlic caṭṭampōṉṟa nāṟkōṇam. 
vaṭṭamuñ caturamum  (peruṅ. uñcik 42: 29).
caturakkal, caturakkaḷḷi, caturat tūṇ, caturap palaki, caturap pāli, catura māṭam, catura mukam, catura varam, catura vōṭu eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakku.
vaṭṭam eṉpatu pōṉṟē caturam eṉpatum tūya teṉcol, āyiṉ, vaṭa nūlkaḷil āḷappeṟṟiruppatu paṟṟi, caturam eṉpatip pōṉṟē vaṭṭam eṉpatiyum vaṭacolleṉa valippar vaṭavar. vaṭṭam (ta.) - vaṭṭa (pirā.) - vrutta (va.) eṉṉum uṇmiyāṉa muṟiyit talikīḻāyk kāṭṭuvar avar.
caturam eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illi.  (va.va. 141).
caturam - catur acra = nāṟkōṇam.
mēliyāriyattil nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟē caturattik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
L. quatuor = four; exquadra - OF esquarre - E square.
vaṭamoḻiyil atu piṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟuk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. catur eṉṉum peyar iyalpāka niṉṟu nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇup peyariyē uṇarttukiṉṟatu. caturikā, catuṣka, catuṣṭaya eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷum piṟkālattilēyē catura vaṭiviyum uṇarttiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
acra = kōṇam, mūli.
caturam eṉṉum vaṭivak karuttu nāṉmūlic caṭṭattoṭu maṭṭumaṉṟi, kaṭṭuṭampoṭum toṭarpuṭiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
kaṭṭuṭampiṉ amippic caturkkaṭṭu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. āṅkilattilum square - built, a man of square frame eṉṟu vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka;
caṭalam, cataram eṉṉum col vaṭivaṅkaḷ kumarikkaṇṭattil caṭṭak karuttum uṇarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
catura eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu, tōṉṟiya caturi eṉṉum viṉi vaṭamoḻiyililli.
caturittal = nāṟkōṇamākkutal.
kuḻittal (varukkamākkutal). cariyāṉa caturattik kuṟikkum caccatu ram eṉṉum iraṭṭitta vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyililli.  (va.va.142.)
cantu
cantu - santi (i.vē.)
um - am - antu - cantu = poruttu, kūṭṭu. vaṭamoḻiyil sam + tā (dha#) eṉṟu pirittu, uṭaṉvi, poruttu eṉṟu poruḷurippar. sam = kūṭa. tā = vi. iṭu.  (va.va. 142).
cappaṭṭi
cappaṭṭi - carppaṭṭa (c)
cappi - cappaṭṭi  (va.va. 142)
cappāṇi
cappāṇi - cappēṭṭa (c)
cappu - cappāṇi = viritta kikaḷic cērttut taṭṭutal.
cappāṇi koṭṭiyaruḷē  (mīṉāṭ. piḷḷit. cappāṇi, 1)
te. cappaṭlu.  (va.va. 142).
campaḷam
paḻaṅkālattil campaḷam kūlamum uppumākak koṭukkappaṭ ṭatu. kūlam - tāṉiyam. (kūlamākak koṭukkappaṭuvatu kūli) kūlattil ciṟantatu nellātaliṉ, nel vakiyil ciṟanta campāviṉ peyarālum, uppiṉ peyarālum campaḷam eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
campum aḷamum cērntatu campaḷam. campu eṉpatu ciṟanta nel vakikkum ciṟanta kōri vakikkum potuppeyar. ōṅki vaḷarnta campā neṟpayirukkum campaṅkōrikkum otta tōṟṟa muṭimiyāy iruttal kāṇka. nellik kuṟikkum campu eṉṉum peyar iṉṟu campā eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. 
ukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ ākāra īṟu peṟuvatu iyalpē. e-ṭu: kumpu - kumpā, tūmpu - tūmpā, kuṇṭu - kuṇṭā. kumputal tiraḷutal.
campaḷam
aḷam eṉpatu uppu. uppu viḷippōr aḷavar eṉappaṭutal kāṇka. aḷam eṉṉum col taṉ poruḷ iḻantu īṟāka māṟiya piṉ uppik kuṟikka uppaḷam eṉṟoru col vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. uppic campaḷap pakutiyākak koṭukkum vaḻakkam niṉṟu viṭavē umpaḷam eṉṉum collum apporuḷil vaḻakku vīḻntu māṉiyamākak koṭukkum nilattiṟkup peyarāka āḷappaṭṭatu. umpu - uppu.
uppukku uḻittal uppit tiṉṉutal eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷiyum uppiṭṭavari uḷḷavum niṉi eṉṟum paḻamoḻi yiyum nōkkuka.  (col. 22, 23.)
(Salary (Soldier’s pay. which was given partly in Salt - wages English word book p.90)]  (col. 22).
camam
camam - sama = oppu.
um - am - cam - camam = oppu. camam - camaṉ = oppu, naṭunili.  (va.va.142.)
camayam
matam, camayam eṉṉum iru collum oru poruḷaṉavāyi ṉum, tammuḷ nuṇ poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṭiyaṉa. oru teyvatti allatu iṟivaṉi matittu vaḻipaṭuvatu matam; iṟivaṉi immiyilēṉum maṟumiyilēṉum aṭivataṟkuc camivatu camayam. ākavē, matattiṉum camayattiṟku oḻukkamum nōṉpum mika vēṇṭuvaṉavām.
uttal = poruntutal, utti (u+ti) = poruttam, nūṟkup poruntum muṟi, viḷiyāṭṭiṟ kaṉṉi (kaṭci) pirikka iruvar iṇital.
uttam = poruttam. uttam - va. yukta.
utti - va. yukti.
u - o. ottal = poruntutal, pōlutal.
u - um. ummutal = poruntutal, kūṭutal.
eṇṇummi yiṭiccollāṉatu. ituvē,
um - am = poruntum nīr, nīroṭu nīrum nilattoṭu nīrum ākap poruntutal iruvakittu.
am - amar. amartal = poruntutal, aṉpāl uḷḷam oṉṟutal.
am - ami. amital = poruntutal, aṭaṅkiyiruttal, kūṭutal, neruṅkutal, nukarccik kēṟṟatātal, takkatāka vāyttal.
ami - avi = kūṭṭam.
ami - amiyam = poruntum nēram, ēṟṟa vēḷi, takka cevvi, viṉi nikaḻum ciṟupoḻutu.
ami - cami. camital = ākkappaṭum arici pōlum pūp paṭiyum kaṉṉi pōlum nukarccik kēṟṟatātal, pataṉaṭital, iṉitākac ceyporuḷ viṉi muṭital, aṇiyamātal.
camiyal = cōṟu kuḻampākkum viṉi.
cakkiliyap peṇṇum cāmik katirum camintāl teriyum  (paḻamoḻi)
cami - camiyam - pataṉaṭinta allatu nukarccik kēṟṟa nili, viṉikkut takka vēḷi.
camiyam - camayam - ātaṉ (āṉmā) tāṉ iṟivaṉi aṭi taṟku allatu pēriṉpatti nukartaṟkut taṉṉit takutippaṭut tum mukkaraṇa voḻukkam.
poruḷi vēṟupaṭuttaṟku, colliṭi makara aikāṉ akara māyiṟṟeṉa aṟika.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, camayam eṉṉum teṉcolli samaya eṉṟu tirittum, sam + aya eṉṟu pirittum, uṭaṉvarutal (to come together) allatu oṉṟu cērtal eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. aṅṅaṉaṅ kāṭṭiya piṉpum,
ūḻiṟ peruvali yāvuḷa maṟṟoṉṟu
cūḻiṉun tāṉmun tuṟum.  (kuṟaḷ. 380)
eṉṉun tiruvaḷḷuvar tiruvāymoḻik kēṟpa, avvaṭacol teṉcoṟ ṟiripēyātal kāṇka.
 sam = uṭaṉ, kūṭa. aya = celki, varuki.
aya eṉṉum coṟku, ay eṉpati aṭiyākavum i eṉpati vērākavum kāṭṭuvar. i - ay - ayaṉa = celavu.
tamiḻil, kūṭutalik kuṟikkum aṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ kum eṉpatu oṉṟu. kummutal = kūṭutal, kuvital.
kum - kummal. kum - kumpu - kumpal.
kum - kumi - kumiyal. kumi - kuvi - kuviyal.
kummal - L. cumulus = heap; cumulare = to heap up. E. cumulate, accumulate.
kum - kumma (nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam - Infinitive Mood) - kūṭa. kūṭutal = oṉṟu cērtal, mikutal.
kūṭa (to gather) eṉṉum (ni.kā.) viṉiyeccam, uṭaṉ (together, with) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi uṭaṉikaḻcci yurupākavum, muṉṉoṭ ṭākavum (prefix) āḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu: 	tantikūṭa vantāṉ = tantiyuṭaṉ vantāṉ.
		kūṭappiṟantāṉ = uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ.
kūṭutalik kuṟikkum kum eṉṉum viṉiccol, u-a tiripu muṟippaṭi kam eṉṟu tiriyavuñ ceyyum.
kamam niṟit tiyalum  (tol. uri. 57)
niṟital - niramputal, mikutal, kūṭutal.
Cum eṉṉum ilattīṉa aṭic collum, muṉṉoṭṭākum pōtu com eṉṟu tirintu, varuñcoṉ mutaleḻuttiṟkēṟpa con, col, cor eṉṟu īṟumāṟum. cila eḻuttukkaṭku muṉ co eṉṟu īṟu keṭavuñ ceyyum. iṅṅaṉamē āṅkilattilum.
iṅṅaṉam ilattīṉattil kum-kom eṉṟum, āṅkilattiṟ kam eṉṟum tiriyum muṉṉoṭṭu, kirēkkattil sum (sum) eṉṟum sim (sym) eṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil sam (sam) eṉṟum, tiriyum. kirēkka muṉṉoṭṭum, ilattīṉa muṉṉoṭṭup pōṟ piṉvarum eḻuttiṟkēṟpa, ciṉ (syn), cil (syl) eṉṟum, tiriyum. camaṟkiruta muṉṉoṭṭu īṟukeṭṭu sa (sa) eṉṟum niṟkum.
ivaṟṟiṟ kellām, kūṭa (uṭaṉ) eṉpatē poruḷ.
kūṭa eṉpataṟku nērāṉa kumma eṉṉum teṉcollē, muṉṉoṭṭākik kum eṉṟu kuṟukiyum, kum - kom - kam, kum - sum, sum - sam eṉṟu tirintum, āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum eṉa aṟika. ataṉ mutaṉmey, airōppāviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pakutiyilum mēṟkup pakutiyilum iyalpākavum, ataṉ teṉ kiḻakkuppakutiyilum intiyāvilum sakaramākat tirintum, vaḻaṅkuvati ūṉṟi nōkki varalāṟṟuṇmi teḷika.
iṉi, intiya āriyattil (vētamoḻiyilum camaṟkirutattilum) cellutalik kuṟikkum i eṉṉum vērum ay eṉṉum aṭiyumākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷum, uy eṉṉum teṉcolli ṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē.
ū allatu u, itaḻkuvintolittu muṉṉiṭatti allatu muṉṉiṟpatic cuṭṭum muṉmic cuṭṭu.
ū-ūṉ = nī (muṉṉili yorumip peyar).
ū - u - untu. untutal = muṉ taḷḷutal.
u - uy. uytal = muṉ cellutal, cellutal.
uyttal = muṉceluttutal, celuttutal.
uy - iy - iyavu = ka. celavu.
iṭineṟik kiṭanta iyavukkoṇ maruṅkil  (cilap. 11: 168) 2 vaḻi.
iyavu - iyavuḷ - ellā vuyirkaḷiyum vaḻinaṭattum iṟivaṉ.
iy - i - iyal - celavu. puḷḷiyaṟ kalimā  (tol. po. 196).
iyalutal - cellutal, naṭattal.
ariviyoṭu meṉmela viyali  (aiṅ. 175)
iya - iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam. iyaṅkutal - cellutal, naṭattal, acital.
iy - va. ay. iṉi, uy eṉpatum ay eṉṟu tirintirukkalām.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku muntiyatu eṉṉum varalāṟṟuṇmiyiyum, mēṟkāṭṭiya col varalāṟukaḷiyum, naṭuniliyāka nōkku vārkku, matam camayam eṉṉum irucollum teṉ collē yeṉpatu, teṟṟeṉat teriyāmaṟ pōkātu.  (ta.ma.)
camayamum koḷkiyum
immi maṟumi nalaṅkaṭkum vīṭṭiṉpattiṟkum iṟivaṉaruḷ iṉṟiyamiyātateṉṉuṅ koḷki paṟṟi, paṇṭit tamiḻaracar tamakkum tamkuṭikaṭkum nalam varuvittaṟ poruṭṭut, tamakkicintatoru camayattik kaṭippiṭittu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.
tamiḻaracaruṅ kuṭikaḷum mutalāvatu aintiṇit teyvaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. kuṟiñcikkuc cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉum, mullikku māyōṉ eṉṉun tirumālum, marutattiṟku vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉum, neytaṟku varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) eṉṉum kaṭalōṉum, pālikku māyōḷ eṉṉuṅ kāḷi (koṟṟavi) yum, teyvamāyiruntaṉar.
varuṇa vaṇakkam nāḷaṭivil niṉṟuviṭṭatu. intira vaṇakkam kaṭiccaṅkat tiṟutivariyiruntatu. maṟṟa mutteyva vaṇakkamum neṭukalun toṭarntatu.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattil, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattilum maturi yilum vañciyilum paṟpala teyvaṅkaṭkuk kōyil iruntaṉa. pukārilirunta kōyilkaḷ, kaṟpakak kōyil, veḷyāṉik kōyil, palatēvar kōyil, katiravaṉ kōyil, kayilikkōyil, murukaṉ kōyil, vaccirap paṭikkōyil, cāttaṉ kōyil, arukaṉkōyil, nilāk kōyil, civaṉkōyil, tirumāl kōyil mutaliyaṉa. murukaṉ civaṉ tirumāl palatēvaṉ ākiya, nālvarkkum ellāt talinakarkaḷilum kōyil iruntaṉa.
teyvaṅkaḷ perunteyvam eṉṟum ciṟu teyvam eṉṟum iruvakippaṭum. camayat teyvaṅkaḷ perun teyvamum maṟṟavi ciṟuteyvamumākum. civam viṇavam ārukatam puttam piramam ulakāyatam eṉṉum āṟum, kaṭiccaṅka kālattu aṟucamayam eṉṉalām. akam akappuṟam puṟam puṟappuṟam eṉa nālāṟu camayamākap pakuttuk kūṟiyatu piṟkālatta tākum. civavaḻipāṭum mālvaḻipāṭum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuriya vātaliṉ, avviraṇṭum paṟṟiya civamum viṇavamumē tamiḻaracarāl perumpālum kaṭippiṭikkap paṭṭaṉa. avviraṇṭaṉuḷḷum civaneṟiyē aracariṭic ciṟantiruntatu. iṟivaṉ eṉṟum piṟavāyākkip periyōṉ eṉṟum civaṉukku vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷē, civaneṟit talimiyi uṇarttiviṭum.
ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷ, kāṟṟum neruppum mutaliya iyaṟkit teyvamum, cūraramakaḷum pūtamum mutaliya pēyt teyvamum, katiravaṉun tiṅkaḷum mutaliya kōḷ teyvamum, paṭṭamaṟava ṉākiya naṭukalteyvamum, iṟanta kaṟpiyākiya pattiṉit teyvamum, nālvaki varuṇattiṟkuriya varuṇatteyvamum, āṟum, maliyum nakarum mutaliya iṭattiṟkuriya iṭatteyvamum, kalviyum kātalum pōrum mutaliya toḻiṟkuriya toḻiṟ ṟeyvamum, kāppumaṭṭum aḷippatākak karutappaṭum kāpput teyvamum eṉap palavakiyām.
kaṭiccaṅkak kālamvari, aracarum pulavarum potu makkaḷu mākiya muttiṟattāriṭiyum, camaviyaṟ koḷkiyum piṟar matap poṟiyum iruntuvantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ camayap pōr tamiḻnāṭ ṭil tali tūkkiṟṟu. mutalāvatu, vitika camayam eṉappaṭum civa viṇavaṅkaṭkum avitika camayam eṉappaṭum camaṇa puttaṅkaṭkum iṭiyē pōr eḻuntatu. piṉṉarc camaṇa puttaṅkaḷ oṭukkappaṭṭapiṉ, civa viṇavaṅkaṭ kiṭiyē pōr mūṇṭatu.
caṅkakālattaracarum mutalām irācarācaṉ irācēntiraṉ mutaliya piṟkālattaracarum, tām kaṭippiṭitta camayak kōyil kaṭku maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟa matak kōyil kaṭkum māṉiyam viṭṭa ṉar. āyiṉ 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum camaṇaṉumāṉa makēntira varmap pallavaṉ civa camaya kuravaruḷ oruvarāṉa tirunāvuk karacarip palavakiyil vatittuk kolla muyaṉṟāṉ. atē nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum civaṉaṭiyāruḷ oruvaṉumāṉa neṭumāṟaṉ eṇṇāyiram camaṇarik kaḻuvēṟṟiṉāṉ. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṉaṉum civaṉu māṉa 2 ām kulōttuṅkaṉ, kūrattāḻvāṉ periyanampi eṉṉum iru viṇava kuravari muṟiyē kaṇ paṟittuc cākaṭittāṉ; atōṭu tilli maṉṟilirunta tirumā luruvattiyum peyarttuk  kaṭalil eṟintuviṭṭāṉ. iṅṅaṉam oru cila aracar neṟi tiṟampi naṭantaṉar. tirumaṅkiyāḻvār nākiyilirunta poṟputta vuruvik koḷḷiyaṭittut, tiruvaraṅkak kōyilip putup pittāreṉṟu, oru paravi vaḻakkuc ceyti vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ma.)

camarttu
camarttu - samartta (th)
vaṭavar sam + artta eṉṟu pakuttu, otta allatu takunta nōk kaṅ koḷḷutal, takunta nilimi peṟutal, valimiyaṭital eṉṟu poruḷ poruttuvar.
sam = sama. art = aṭi, peṟu.  (va.va.143).
camaram
camaram - samara = pōr iruvar allatu iru paṭikaḷ ceyyum pōtu oṉṟu kalattalāl, kalattal allatu oṉṟātaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu pōrk karuttup piṟantatu.
e-ṭu: kala - kalām, kalakam. kikalattal = caṇṭiyiṭutal. poru tal - ottal, pōr ceytal. poru - pōr = poruttu, camar.
iṅṅaṉamē oppik kuṟikkum camam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum pōrik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
camam = pōr. oḷiṟuvā ḷaruñcama murukki (puṟam. 382).
camam - camar = pōr (tivā.) o.nō: kaḷam - kaḷar.
camarttal = porutal. camarkka vallāyēl (vināyakapu. 74:249)
pōrukku valimi vēṇṭumātaliṉ, pōreṉṉum col valimi allatu tiṟamiyiyum uṇarttum.
pōr = valimi. camarttu = tiṟami.
camar - camaram = pōr. ilaṅkiyi leḻunta camaramum  (cilap. 26: 238).
camar - amar = pōr. āramar ōṭṭalum  (tol. 1006).
camaram - amaram = pōr. te. amaramu.
amarttal = māṟupaṭutal. pētik kamarttaṉa kaṇ  (kuṟaḷ. 1084).
vaṭamoḻiyil camara eṉṉum vaṭivē uḷḷatu. camam, camar, amar, amaram eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ illi.
vaṭavar samara eṉṉum colli sam + ru eṉṟu pirittu, oṉṟu kūṭu, māṟupaṭu, pōrcey eṉṟu poruḷ kāṭṭuvar.
sam = kūṭa. ru = cēr.  (va.va.142-143).
camaṟkiruta ākkam
vēta āriyar eṉṟu collappaṭum intiya āriyar intiyāviṟ kuṭ pukuntavuṭaṉ tam (kirēkkatti yotta) moḻiyi maṟantu viṭṭaṉar. itaṟku avar ciṟutokiyiṉarā yiruntatum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu pōṉatumē karaṇiyam.
āriyap pūcāriyarum avarip piṉpaṟṟiya vicuvāmittiraṉ pōṉṟa orucila nāṭṭumakkaḷum, pāṭiya mantirat tokuti eṉṉum pāṭaṟṟiraṭṭēirukku vētamām. ataṉ moḻi, maṟantupōṉa kīḻiyāriyamum vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭamākiya pirākirutamuṅ kalanta moḻiyām. akkalappiṉāl, intiya āriya moḻi ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷiyum iḻantatu.
āriyap pūcārikaḷāṉa pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ, vētamoḻiyuṭaṉ ērāḷamāṉa tiraviṭac coṟkaḷum tamiḻccoṟkaḷum avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa putuccoṟkaḷum iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷum kalanta, kalavi moḻiyil itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷum taruma cāttiraṅkaḷum nāṭakaṅkaḷum piṟavum iyaṟṟappaṭṭa piṉṉarē, ki.mu. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭupōl camaṟkirutam eṉṉum aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭimoḻi niṟivāka uruvāyiṟṟu. pirākirutam (pirākruta) eṉpatu muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟum, camaṟkirutam (samkruta) eṉpatu kalantu ceyyap paṭṭatu eṉṟum, poruḷpaṭuvaṉa vākum.
intirōppiyam, vētiyam, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum mūvēṟu nilikaḷil, āriyam tamiḻccoṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷatu. vētiyam kaṭaṉkoṇṭatu vaṭanāṭṭup pirākiruta vāyilāka veṉṟaṟika. vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum peyar vētamoḻikkum camaṟkirutattiṟkum potuvākum. picāci, cūracēṉi, mākati eṉṟu vaṭanāṭṭil mūṉṟum, tirāviṭi eṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil oṉṟumāka, pirākirutam nāṉkeṉa vakuttaṉar.
vētamoḻi pōṉṟē camaṟkirutamum nūṉmoḻiyākum. atu piṟantatumilli; iṟantatumilli. oru moḻikku uyir ulaka vaḻakkē. cila pala paṇṭitar pallāṇṭu varuntikkaṟṟuc camaṟ kirutattiṟ pēcuvataṉāl, atu uyirmoḻiyākiviṭātu. atu uyirmeyyumaṉṟu; cavamumaṉṟu; vallōṉ puṉinta pāvi pōṉṟatē.
ulaka vaḻakku moḻikaḷāyiruntu iṟantu pōṉavaṟṟi maṭṭumaṉṟi, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu mēli moḻiyaṟiñar puṉinta ecupperāntō (Esperanto), nōviyal (Novial) mutaliya ceyaṟki moḻikaḷik kūṭak kaṟṟup pēcakkūṭiyavar ulakiluḷḷaṉar.
maṟiyiyalāka maṭṭumuḷḷa vētamoḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuttatu aintiram eṉṟum, maṟiyiyalum (vitīkamum) ulakiyalum (lukīkamum) kalanta camaṟkiruta moḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuttatu pāṇiṉīyam eṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
pāṇiṉīyam
camaṟkirutattiṉ taliciṟanta ilakkaṇa nūlākiya pāṇiṉīyam pāṇiṉiyāl ki.mu.4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. ilak kaṇanūli viyākaraṇam eṉpar vaṭanūlār. atu kūṟupaṭuppu (Analysis) eṉṉum poruḷatu. nan nāṉku iyalkaḷ (pātaṅkaḷ) uḷḷa eṇṇatikāraṅkaḷ (attiyāyaṅkaḷ) koṇṭatu pāṇiṉi viyākaraṇam. ataṉāl atu aṭṭāttiyāyī (aṣṭāt yāyī) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ataṉ nūṟpākkaḷ (cūttiraṅkaḷ) ēṟattāḻa 3980. annūṟku muṉ eṇṇilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟap paṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu vēta kālattateṉappaṭum aintiram.
tolkāppiyam pāṇiṉīyattiṟku munnūṟṟāṇṭu muntiyatu. ataṉālēyē, aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟu maṭṭuṅ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyariṉ uṭaṉmāṇavarāṉa paṉampāraṉār. camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷellām pavaṇanti naṉṉūlpōl eḻuttuñ collumē kūṟuvaṉa veṉṟum, tamiḻiṟpōl oru vaḻakku moḻiyi viḷakkātu vētamum iti kāca purāṇaṅkaḷumākiya ilakkiyattiluḷḷa aric ceyaṟki yāṉa naṭimoḻiyiyē (Semi-artificial literary dialect) viḷakkuvaṉa veṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. 
tolkāppiyam eḻuttuñ collum poruḷumākiya mūvatikāraṅ koṇṭatu; poruḷatikārattil, tamiḻukkē ciṟap pāṉa poruḷilakkaṇattuṭaṉ ceyyuḷiyaliyum aṇiyiyalāṉa uvamaviyaliyum uṭkoṇṭatu. ātalāl, pāṇiṉīyatti ṉiṉṟu tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyatākac cilar kūṟuvatu, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟa katiyum kaḷācceṭiyiṟ palāppaḻam paṟitta katiyumē yākum.
ghÂÅ, “mïc©”, “UY¡”, “VX§.”, “I xs¢.”, “Aatu£.”, “y©.”, ñamaṅaṇa nam. mutaliya 14 kuṟuṅkaṇakku nūṟpākkaḷi, civaperumāṉiṉ uṭukki yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉa olikaḷākak kūṟi, avaṟṟiṟku makēcuvara cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉappeyariṭṭatu, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi vāṇari ēmāṟṟat tuṇiccaluṭaṉ ceyta paṭumōcac cūḻcciyākum.
puṇṇiya caravaṇam poruntuvi rāyiṉ
viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻunū leytuvir
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷi (11:98-9) nōkkiṉ, pāṇiṉiyiṉ ēmāṟṟi makkaḷ nampi avarilakkaṇattiyē pōṟṟip payilumāṟu, tamiḻt toṭarpu kāṭṭum aintira viyā karaṇap paṭikaḷi yellām tokuttu, aḻakar maliyaṭuttatum makkaḷ vaḻaṅkātatum āḻam mikkatumāṉa oru poykikkuḷ eṟintu viṭṭatākak karuta iṭamuṇṭākiṟatu. tamiḻakattut tōṉṟiya aintiram tamiḻakattilēyē aḻiyuṇṭatu pōlum!
	camaṟkirutavākkam - eḻuttu
tamiḻeḻuttu
eḻuttu, oliyum variyum eṉa iruvaṭivuṭiyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ uṇmiyāṉatu oliyē. oliyē eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉap paṭṭatu. uḷḷattileḻum karuttukaḷi nēraṭiyāy aṟivikkum kuṟi oli. celippulaṉāṉa oliyik kaṭpulaṉākkum kuṟiyē vari.
muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattil, ki.mu. aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭu kaṭku muṉpē tamiḻ tāṉē tōṉṟi.
1. aci nili + (monosyllabic stage)
2. kūṭṭu nili + (compounding stage)
3. paku coṉṉili - (Inflexional stage)
4. koḷuvu nili - (Agglutinative stage)
eṉṉum nālvaki moḻi niliyuṅ kaṭantu muḻu vaḷarcci yaṭintu. ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē eḻuttum ilakkiyamum ilakkaṇamum nirampappeṟṟu. mutaṉ mutalākat teṉmaturit talikkaḻakattil ārāyap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻolikaḷ perumpālum iyaṟkiyāṉavi; eḷiyavi; ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉavi. potuvāka, eḻut tolikaḷ teṟkē cellac cellat tokiyilum vaṉmi yilum kuṟintum. vaṭakkē cellac cella avaṟṟil mikuntum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻ valliṉa volikaḷ vaṭamoḻi valliṉa voli kaḷi nōkka melliyavi. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉavi iraṭṭittā laṉṟip piṉṉavaṟṟi vaṉmiyil ovvā. innilimiyi iṟṟit teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭuppuṟap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ nāviltāṉ cevviyākak kāṇamuṭiyum.
1. paṭaveḻuttu (pictogram or Hieroglyph)
2. karutteḻuttu (Ideogram)
3. aciyeḻuttu (Syllabary)
4. oliyeḻuttu (Phonemic character)
eṉṉum nālvaki variniliyum muṟiyē aṭinta tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku. ki.pi. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iṟṟi nili yilēyē yirunta teṉpatu.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttum āṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi  (98)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvālum; ki.mu. 6 ām allatu 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, uyirum uyir meyyumākiya ekara okaraṅkaḷum kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum puḷḷi peṟutalum, uṭpuḷḷi peṟṟa pakara vaṭivē makara vaṭivātalum, tavira, maṟṟiyavellām iṟṟiyavē yeṉpatu tolkāppiyattālum, aṟiyakkiṭak kiṉṟaṉa.
eḻuttu eḻutappaṭum karuvikkēṟpa vēṟupaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭu mātalāl, eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu ēṭṭil eḻututaṟku oru variveḻuttu muṟiyum. uḷi koṇṭu cempilum kallilum cetukkaṟku oru veṭṭeḻuttu muṟiyum, toṉṟu toṭṭuk kiyāḷappaṭṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. tamiḻiṉ toṉmi yiyum talimiyiyum āyntaṟiyum matukiyum naṭu nilimi yum illā ayalār, variveḻuttum veṭṭe ḻuttākiya vaṭ ṭeḻuttum oṉṟē yeṉap piṟaḻavuṇarntu viṭṭaṉar.
āriyavolittiripu
tamiḻolikaḷ, mutalāvatu, teṉ tiraviṭamākiya teluṅkil vaṉmipeṟṟu vaṭa tiraviṭattil vaḷarcciyaṭintu. vaṭa mēli yāriya (airōppiya) moḻikaḷil iyaṟkiyāriya vaṭivuṟṟu. iṟutiyil kīḻiyāriyamākiya camaṟkirutattil perumpālum ceyaṟki vaṭivu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. iti,
centiṟatta tamiḻōci vaṭacol lāki
eṉṉum tirumaṅkiyāḻvār tiruneṭuntāṇṭakat toṭar iraṭṭuṟal muṟiyiṟ kuṟippatākak koḷḷalām.
eṉṟum eṅkum piñcu muṟṟik kāyākumēyaṉṟik kāy iḷantu piñcākātu. atu pōl, iyaṟkiyāṉa moḻi vaḷarcciyil, melloli valittu valloliyākumē yaṉṟi valloli melintu melloliyākātu. kuḻanti vāyil valloli piṟavātatu pōl, muntiyal māntaṉ vāyilum valloli piṟappatilli. paḻantamiḻaṉ cūṭu eṉṟu coṉṉa colli iṟṟit tamiḻaṉ kocci vaḻakkil jūṭu eṉṟu colvati yum, triplus eṉṟa ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu treble eṉṉum āṅkilaccol tirintiruppatiyum nōkkuka. kna eṉṟa tiyūttāṉiyac col gno eṉṟu ilattīṉil tirintatum atuvē.
iṟṟi nilippaṭi, valliṉa eḻuttolikaḷiyellām.
(1) kaṭuppoli (voiceless unaspirate)
(2) urappoli (voiceless aspirate)
(3) eṭuppoli (voiced unaspirate)
(4) kaṉippoli (voiced aspirate)
eṉa nālvakiyāka vakukkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻil iruppavi yellām meṉkaṭuppolikaḷum mellolikaḷumē. ivaṟṟuḷ, piṉṉavi meṉkaṭuppolikaḷ colliṭi kaṭi taṉittum melliṉattiṉ piṉpum vantālaṉṟi nikaḻā. ilattīṉil, mūccoli cērnta puṇarmeykaḷē illi; k - yum s - um cērnta x(ks) eṉṉum puṇarmeytāṉ uṇṭu. kirēkkattil, th, ph, kh eṉṉum mūccolip puṇarmeykaḷ mūṉṟum. ks, ps eṉṟa mūccoliyillāp puṇarmeykaḷ iraṇṭum. uḷḷaṉa. ivviru moḻikkum potuvāka b,d,g,z eṉṉum nāleṭuppolikaḷ uḷa. eḻuttup puṇarcciyāl ēṟpaṭum jakara voliyum (J) kirēkkattiṟkuṇṭu. camaṟkirutattilō valliṉa volikaḷ aintiṟkum, kaṭuppoli eṭuppoliyākiya iruvaṭivilum, mūccoliyic cērttum, ceyaṟki muṟiyil irupatu meykaḷip piṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟuḷ, mūccoli cērnta pattum uṇmiyil puṇar meykaḷē (consonantal diphthongs) kṣa (k+ṣa), jña mutaliya puṇar meykaḷum kūṭṭu meykaḷum ērāḷam.
vēta āriyark keḻuttiṉmi
vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntapōtu, avarkku eḻuttu milli; ilakkiyamumilli. vētattil mēṉāṭṭup poruḷ kaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippiruppiṉum. aḵtu eḻutappaṭṭatu vaṭa intiyāvilēyē mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilellām ekara okarak kuṟilum neṭilum uḷḷaṉa. kīḻiyāriyattilō vaṭa tiraviṭattiṟ pōṉṟē ekara okarak kuṟililli. āriyavētam mutalil oṉṟāyiruntu. piṉpu mūṉṟāki, iṟutiyil nāṉkā yiṟṟu. camaṟkirutam tōṉṟum variyum kīḻiyāriyattiṟku eḻuttilli. āriya vētam nīṇṭakālamāka āciriyaṉāl vāyppāṭa māka ōtappeṟṟu māṇavarāl cevi vāyilākavē kaṟkappaṭṭu vantataṉāl, ataṟku āriyattil curuti yeṉṟum, tamiḻil eḻutākkiḷavi eṉṟum peyar eḻuntaṉa.
cru = kēḷ, cruti = kēḷvi, cru eṉpatu ceviyuṟu eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripēyeṉa vaṟika. iṅṅaṉam pala vaṭa coṟkaḷ tūya āriyamāyiṉum, tamiḻ vērkaḷiṉiṉṟē tirintaviyē.
tamiḻ muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi yeḻunta āriya neṭuṅkaṇakku
vēta āriyar ki.mu. 1500 pōl teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē camaṟkirutam tōṉṟiṟṟu. aṉṟu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷum teṉ coṟkaḷum kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatuṭaṉ. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkip piṉpaṟṟi āriya neṭuṅkaṇakkum amikkappaṭṭatu.
	camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakku tamiḻ muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
(1) muṟi
mēliyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟiṟkēṉum neṭuṅkaṇakkil muṟiyilli. uyirum meyyum vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalantu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻilō, uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum, uyiruḷ kuṟil muṉṉum neṭil piṉṉum, kuṟiluḷ akaram muṉṉum ikaram iṭiyum ukaram piṉṉum, ataṉpiṉ ekaramum iṟutiyil okaramum, ēkārattiyaṭuttu aikāramum ōkārattiyaṭuttu aukāramum;
meyyuḷ iṉavaliyum meliyumāka ivviraṇṭāy valliṉam muṉṉum melliṉam piṉṉum, iṭiyiṉam avaṟṟiṉ piṉ ṉum, pintiyeḻunta nāleḻuttum iṟutiyilum; valliṉam ka ca ṭa ta pa eṉṟa muṟiyilum, iṭiyiṉam ya ra la va eṉṟa muṟi yilum.
kakarattiṟkiṉamāṉa nuṇṇoli (āytam) kakarattiyaṭuttu muṉṉum, aṭittoṇṭi mutal itaḻ varippaṭṭa eḻuttup piṟappiyal muṟi paṟṟi oḻuṅkāka vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulakil mutaṉ mutal im muṟiyil eḻuttukaḷ vikkappaṭṭa neṭuṅ kaṇakkuṭiya moḻi tamiḻē, tamiḻil eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉi raṇṭaṉuḷ muṟi eṉpatum oṉṟu.
2. puṇarcci
eḻuttup puṇarcciyum mēliyāriya moḻikaṭkilli. itaṉāl, uyir meyyeḻuttumilli.
3. oppu
tamiḻāytatti orupuṭi yotta vaṭamoḻi vicarkkam, uyirukkum meykkum iṭiyil vikkappaṭṭiruppatu ūṉṟi nōkkattakkatu.
4. toṉmi
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarik kaṇṭattil tōṉṟiyatu, vēta āriyar intiyāviṟku vanta kālam ki.mu. 2000-1500.
5. olinūṟpulami
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil taṉiyeḻut tolikaḷē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakkil. acikaḷum puṇar meykaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; uyir meykaḷ uyirkaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ru lu mutaliya eḻuttukaḷ kuṟṟiyalukaram cērnta rakara lakara meykaḷ; am, a : eṉpavi akarattoṭu kūṭiya mūkkoli vicarkkaṅkaḷ; mūkkoli cērnta valliṉam puṇarmeykaḷ.
6. varuṭolikaḷ (Linguals or cerebrals)
ṭa,ṇa eṉṉum varuṭolikaḷ āriyattiṟkuriyaviyalla. tamiḻuk kum tiraviṭattiṟkum tiraviṭa aṭippaṭiyik koṇṭa vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaṭkumē yuriyavi.
camaṟkiruta vari vaṭivu vaki
camaṟkiruta vari vaṭivu. kirantam, tēvanākari eṉa iru vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muntiyatu kirantam, vēta āriyar tamiḻoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉ, tamiḻ varivaṭivip piṉpaṟṟic camaṟkirutattiṟku amittuk koṇṭatē kirantam. kirantam ēṟpaṭṭa piṉpum, vētam nīṇṭa kālam eḻutappaṭāmalē yiruntu vantatu. iti,
āṟṟa laḻiyumeṉ ṟantaṇarkaḷ nāṉmaṟiyip
pōṟṟiyurit tēṭṭiṉ puṟatteḻutār - ēṭṭeḻuti
vallunarum vallārum vaḷḷuvaṉār muppālic
colliṭiṉum āṟṟalcōr viṉṟu.
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvamālic ceyyuḷ uṇarttum.
teṉṉāṭṭuk kirantattiṟkuppiṉ vaṭanāṭṭileḻunta vaṭa moḻi varivaki tēvanākari nakarattil allatu nākarikamākat tōṉṟiyateṉpatu paṟṟiyum, tēva moḻikkuriya teṉpatu paṟṟi yum, tēvanākari eṉappaṭṭatu. atippaṟṟi māṉiyar ulliyamcu eṉpavar tam camaṟkiruta akara mutaliyiṉ muṉṉuriyil 28ām pakka aṭik kuṟippil piṉ varumāṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
camaṟkirutak kalveṭṭukaḷuḷ mikap paḻamiyāṉatāka aṟiyap paṭṭatu kattiyavāril cuṉākaril oru pāṟi mēluḷatu. aḵtu uruttira tāmaṉ kalveṭṭeṉṉum peyar koṇṭatu. atu ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatu. atu nākariyil illi; paḻiya kalveṭṭeḻuttukaḷil uḷḷatu. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 400 ām āṇṭiṉatāṉa pavar kiyeḻuttuppaṭi nākari nōkkiya peru muṉṉēṟṟattik kāṭṭa, ki.pi. cumār 750 ām āṇṭiṉatāṉa tantiturukkaṉ kalveṭṭu iṟṟi vaḻakkiluḷḷa nākariyi mikavum otta oru niṟivāṉa kuṟittokutiyik kāṭṭuvatā yuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, uṇmiyāṉa taṟkāla nākariyil uḷḷa mutaṟ kiyeḻuttuppaṭi, ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭataṉ ṟeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
teṉṉāṭṭuk kirantamum vaṭanāṭṭut tēvanākariyum mutaṟkaṇ ulakiyaṟ kali nūlkaṭkē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. iṉṟu maṟi nūlkaḷum avaṟṟil eḻutappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
āḻa ūṉṟi nōkkum naṭunili āyvāḷarkku, kirantamum tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṉaveṉṉum uṇmi, muṟiyē, veḷippaṭiyākavum kuṟippākavum tōṉṟāmaṟ pōkātu.
	camaṟkirutavākkam - coṟkaḷ
vēta āriyar nāvalantēyam allatu nāvalampoḻil eṉṉum intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu, iṅkirunta ilakkiyam tamiḻilak kiyamē. avar intiya nākarikam tamateṉak kāṭṭit tammik kumukāya (camutāya) niliyil ucca nilikku uyarttuvāṉ vēṇṭi, tamiḻnūlkaḷi moḻi peyarttu ōr ilakkiyatti amittaṟkup pōtiya colvaḷam tam muṉṉōr moḻik kiṉmiyāl āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam kaṭaṉ koṇṭa minta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkik kalavi moḻiyē camaṟkirutam. itaṟku, ēṟkeṉavē iruvēṟu nilikaḷil nērallā vaḻiyiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭirunta tamiḻccoṟkaḷum tuṇi ceytaṉa. itaṉāl, kīḻiyāriyam mūvēṟu kālattil tamiḻc coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭami aṟiyappaṭum.
avaṟṟuḷ :
mutaṟkālam mēliyāriyak kālamākum. ak kālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavi mutaṟ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the First Period) eṉat takum.
iraṇṭāṅ kālam vēta āriyak kālamākum. akkālattil kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavi iraṇṭāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Second Period) eṉat takum.
mūṉṟāṅ kālam camaṟkirutak kālamākum. akkālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavi mūṉṟāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Third Period) eṉattakum.
1. mēliyāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
e-ṭu:
tiyūttāṉiyam
amma - amma. āṉ (= aṅku) yon. iru are. irum(pu) - iron. īṉ - yean, ean; uraṟu - roar, kari - cry, kāṇ - con, cun, can; kuraṅku (= vaḷi) - crank, kuruḷ - curl koṭukku crook, cappu - sup, sip; turuva through, nāku - nag paṭṭu (aṭi) - beat, pullam - bull, metu - smooth, muṉṉu - mun (think) vēṇṭu - want.
ilattīṉam
amar - amo (love), amor; uruḷi - uruṇṭa - rota (wheel), uri - or, kummal - cumulus. camam - similis. cēr - scr. tā - do. nāvāy - navis. paṭaku - barca. puri - par (equal), maṉ - man (remin) mutir - maturus. mel - mellow (soft), val - val (strong), vari (vaḷi) - vers (turn).
kirēkkam
aracaṉ - arthos, arkhon, kuru (niṟam) - chroma, kōṇam - gona. cāyi (niḻal) - skia. taccaṉ - tekton, tiruppu - trepos, turappaṇam - trapanon, toli - telos. narampu - neuron. nīram - neros (wet). pala - polus, pari - pcri (round), paṟaḻ - proles, pāḻi (nakaram) - polis, puri - poros, mattiki - mastix, mastigos, māl - meals (black) viḻi - philos.
potu
pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēliyāriyattil pala moḻikaṭku allatu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaṭkup potuvākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
a. (cēymic cuṭṭu) - E. a (that), Du. a (dat), Ger. a (das).
i. (aṇmic cuṭṭu) - E.i (this) OE. (hit) Goth (hita), L. (hic).
ī. (aṇmic cuṭṭu) - E. (these).
itōḷ (ivviṭam) - E. hither. OE. hider, L. citra.
(iy) - iyal - L. i,e; Gk. i ei; Goth. i. Slav. i, Lith. ei.
iyalutal = iyaṅkutal, naṭattal, cellutal. uy(iy) - iya - iyaṅku. iya - iyavu = celavu. vaḻi, iya - iyavuḷ = vaḻi. uytal = cellutal. uyttal = celuttutal.
ēr (kalappi) nu nayaca., ṭu. yaca.
oṉ - oṉṉu, oṉṟu E. one. Du. een, Ger. ein, L. unus, Gk. oinos.
kotti = pūṉi (kaṉṉaṭac col). pakaliṟ kuruṭu pōliruppatu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. L. catta; E.cat.
cāli - Ger. saal, It. sala, salone, F. salon, E. saloon, E. hall Du. and Dan. hal.
pati - patam - pātam. nilattiṟ pativatu patam allatu pātam. pātam - pāti = vaḻi. patam = vaḻi. aṭittaṭam toṭarntu pativataṉāl oru vaḻi amiyum. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippākam atu. kāl, pātam, kīḻiṭam mutaliya pala poruḷ kaḷi yuṇarttum. pātam eṉpatu nilattiṟ patiyakkūṭiya paranta aṭippakuti. pātam vitta viḷakku eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. aṭimaram paruttatu. marattaṭiyil uṭkārntāṉ. mēṭik kaṭiyiṟ pār. aṭināḷiliruntu collivarukiṉṟēṉ, eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiṉ pātam eṉṉum col poruntāmi kāṇka.
patam, pātam - Gk. podos. L. pedis. OE. fot (E. foot), Ger. fuss.
pāti - E. path, OE. peth, Dut. pad, Ger. pfad.
bghW - OE., OHG. ber., Goth. bir, E. bear, L. fer, Gk. pher.
pōṭu - E.put, pot. pyt; Dan. putte. L. pon.
pōkaviṭu - pōkku - pōṭu.
iṅkuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷuḷ, kīḻiyāriyattil illātaṉavu muḷa. avi mēliyāriyattiṉiṉṟu pirintu varumpōtu kīḻiyāriyattāl iḻakkap paṭṭaṉavākum.
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēliyāriyattiṉum kīḻiyāriyattil tirintiruppatu. piṉṉataṉ piṉmiyik kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
ammā - O.G. amma - Skt. amba.
kāṇ - OE. con - cna (metathesis) - E.know, OHG. cna, L. and Gk. gno - Skt. Jna (Ph.)
muḻuku - L. merg, Skt. majj.
irum(pu) - O.E. iren. O.G. Mod Ger. eise, Goth. eisarn. L. aer. aes; Skt. ayas.
val - L. val Skt. bal. 
cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ kīḻiyāriyattiṉum mēliyāriyattil tirintiruppatu. vaṭatiraviṭam vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu paṭippaṭi yāyt tirintu airōppāvil muḻuvāriyamāy māṟiyatiyum. kīḻiyāriyam intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉ vaṭintiya vaṭivattic cila coṟkaḷil mēṟkoṇṭatiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:
umpar - va.ti. ūppar. Teut. upper, over; L. super, Gk. huper. Skt. upari.
āyiram. ayir - (ayiram) - aciram). North Indian. hazar, Persian. hazar Gk. kesloi; kilioi; Skt. sahasra (sa+hasra).
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ tiyūttāṉiyattiṉum ilattīṉil tirintiruppatu. vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa tiraviṭam airōppāviṉ vaṭamēl mūliyi muṭṭit tirumpiyatiyum, tirumpukālil kiḻakku nōkkit tirintatiyum kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
turuva - Teut. through, L. trans.
tuḷ - turu - turuvu - turuva (vi.e.).
turuttu - Teut. thrust, L. trud (o) (intrude, protrude, obtrude etc).
2. vētavāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
vētavāriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu (ki.mu.2000 - 1500), aṅku vāḻntavaruṭ perumpālār tiraviṭarē. peluc cittāṉattilum vaṅkāḷattilum iṉṟum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiyum, kāḷik kōṭṭam (Calcutta) eṉṉum peyar tūya tamiḻc collāyiruppatiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
āriya vētam vaṭatiraviṭam vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭil iyaṟṟap peṟṟamiyāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ atiṟ pukuntuviṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: akavu. avi - cavi, ilakku, uttaram, uru - uruvu - uruvam, ulaku, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, kaṭukam, kālam, karuḷ (karumi), kumaraṉ - kumari, takkaṇam, taṇṭam, tūtu. naṭam, pakkam, maṇṭalam, mantiram, muttu - muttam, vaṭṭam.
virivañci ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkē iṅku mūlam viḷakkappeṟum. 
amalutal = neruṅkutal. amartal = neruṅkutal. amital = neruṅkutal. niṟital. poruntutal,cērtal, kūṭutal, tiraḷutal, ami = tiraṇṭa keṭṭi mūṅkil.
ami - avi = kūṭṭam. ma.va. pōli.
o.nō. ammi - avvi. kumi - kuvi.
avi - cavi. uyir mutaṟ coṟkaḷ collākkattil moḻi mutal meykaḷ ēṟappeṟum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cakaram.
e-ṭu: uruḷ - curuḷ, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, ēṇ - cēṇ, ēmam - cēmam, capā (Sabha) eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ porutta māṉa vērilli.
uttaram = uyaram. uyaramāṉa vaṭatici. takkaṇam = tāḻvu. tāḻvāṉa teṉtici. toṇṭi = tāḻnta kural. kuṭamaliyāl uyarntiruppatu mēṟṟiciyeṉṟum. kuṇakaṭalāl tāḻntiruppatu kīḻttici yeṉṟam vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal. uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam. vaṭivuṭip poruḷ. uru - uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumi vaṭivam. atik kāṭṭum iṭiccol. x.neh.: aḷavu - aḷapu. uruvu - uruvam. tamiḻilakkaṇam āriya varukikku eṇṇāyiramāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatu.
ulavutal = vaḷital, cuṟṟutal. ulā = nakaric cuṟṟi varum pavaṉi. ulakki = uruṇṭa perumpiṭi. ulamvā - ulamā. ulamarutal = cuḻalutal. ulavu - ulaku = uruṇṭiyāyiruppatu allatu cuṟṟi varuvatu. x.neh.: koṭkutal = cuṟṟi varutal. koḷ - kōḷ. ulaku - ulakam. lōk eṉṉam vaṭacol. pārttatu allatu pārkkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu nōkku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripām.
iṉi, ulakam vaṭṭamāyirukkum ñālam (pūmi) eṉṟumām. x.neh.: kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam (kuṟun. 300).
ukaram uyarattik kuṟikkum vērccol. e-ṭu: ukkam = tali. uvvi = tali. utti = oruvakit taliyaṇi ucci, umpar, uvaṇ, uyar.
uvaṇ = mēliṭam. uvaṇi = tēvarulakam. uvaṇam = uyarcci, paruntu, kaluḻaṉ, kaḻuku. paruntu uyarap paṟakkum paṟavikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. uyaravuyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. uvaṇam - cuvaṇam = paruntu, kaluḻaṉ. kaḻuku. uvaṇam (cuvaṇam) mulli nilattup paṟaviyātalāl, an nilatteyvamākiya māyōṉukku ūrtiyākak kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
cuvaṇam eṉṉum colli vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cuparṇa eṉṟu tirittup piṉpu cu + parṇa eṉṟu pirittu, nalla ili, aḻakiya iliyuḷḷatu. aḻakiya ili pōṉṟa ciṟakiyuṭiyatu. eṉṟu poruntāp puḷukalākak karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
kummutal = tiraḷutal. kum - kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, iḷiñaṉ kumari = tiraṇṭavaḷ. iḷiñi. o.nō. L virginem, E. virgin from virgo to swell viṭittal = paruttal. vilaṅkiṉat tilum paṟaviyiṉattilum tiraṇṭu iḷimiyā yiruppavaṟṟi viṭiyeṉpatu marapu. āṭu, māṭu, kutiri, māṉ, pūṉi, kōḻi, pāmpu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, tiraṇṭu iḷamiyāyiruppavaṟṟi viṭiyeṉpatu iruvaki vaḻakkilumuṇmi kaṇṭukoḷka. viṭi - viṭali = iḷaṅkāḷi, maṟavaṉ (vīraṉ), iḷiñaṉ, 16 mutal 30 āṇṭu varippaṭṭavaṉ. tiṇṇiyōṉ. iḷanīri viṭaliyeṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku. uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum āṇ paruttiruppatāl, viṭiyeṉpatu iḷa vāṇiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. kumar eṉṉum col uyar tiṇiyil irupāṟ potuvām. kumari eṉpatu, iḷami paṟṟic cila marappeyarkkum, putumi (mutaṉmi) kuṟittuc cila toḻiṟ peyarkkum, aḻiyāmi kuṟittuc cila iṭappeyar poruṭ peyarkkum aṭimoḻiyām.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷi, vaṭavar kumāraṉ kumāri eṉa nīṭṭi, muṟiyē tamiḻp poruḷoṭu makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷilum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉar.
muḷ - muṇṭu - muṇṭi = muṭṭi. muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭi.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kāli maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam. vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaḷittal.
maṇṭalam eṉpatu irukkuvētap perumpiriviṉ peyar. tamiḻc ceyyuḷil ellāvaṭikaḷum aḷavottuvarum yāppu maṇṭilam eṉappaṭum.
ottā ḻiciyum maṇṭila yāppum
kuṭṭamum nēraṭik koṭṭiṉa eṉpa  (cey. 114)
maṇṭilam kuṭṭam eṉṟivi yiraṇṭum
centūk kiyala eṉmaṉār pulavar  (cey. 116)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ. eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṉum vaḻinūṉ marapurikaḷ. maṇṭilam eṉṉuñ col mutaṉūṟ kuṟiyīṭeṉpatit terivikkum.
maṇṭalam, maṇṭilam eṉṉum irucollum vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷkaḷiyum, tōṟṟattiyum celaviyum kālattiyum iṭattiyum toṉṟutoṭṭu iruvaki vaḻakkilum kuṟittu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṟpatu allatu nāṟpattintu nāṭ koṇ ṭatu maruttuva nūl kūṟum kāla maṇṭalam. toṇṭi maṇṭa lam. koṅku maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa paḻimiyāṉa iṭa maṇṭa lam. maṇṭilat tarumiyum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (977).
taḷ - tāḷ = marañ ceṭi koṭi payir pulliṉ taṭitta aṭi; ili pūviṉ aṭi tāḷi oṭṭiya payir pullili; tāḷ pōṉṟa kāl, viḷakkuttaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa.
taḷ - taṇṭu = ceṭikoṭikaḷiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi (e-ṭu: kīrit taṇṭu).

taḷ - taṭṭu, taṭṭi = cōḷap payir pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi = parumaṉ mikuti. taṭi, vīṇikkōṭu, taṭitta mūṅkil.
tāṅkum pallakku.  taṇṭi - taṇṭiyam = tāṅkum kaṭṭi. taṇṭi - taṇṭiki = pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiyal = taṇṭiki.

taṭittal = paruttal, taṭiyaṉ = paruttavaṉ, parutta pūcaṇikkāy.
taṭi - taṭippu - taṭippam. taṭi - taṭimpu - taṭimpal.
taṇṭu - taṇṭam = taṭi; paṭi taṇṭattalivaṉ = paṭit talivaṉ.
taṇṭi + am = taṇṭam (oṟuppu). taṇṭi + aṉam = taṇṭaṉam (oṟuppu), taṇṭi + aṉi = taṇṭaṉi (oṟuppu).

muṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal.
muṉṉam = maṉam (tivā.). muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam.
muṉ - maṉ, maṉ + tiram = (maṉṟiram) - mantiram.
x.neh.: maṉ - maṉṟu - mantu - manti.
muṉṟu - muntu, piṉṟu - pintu.
mantiram = iṉṉatu ākaveṉṟu uṟutiyāy eṇṇutal. aṅṅaṉam eṇṇic collum uri.
niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa  (1434)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpāviyum.
eṇṇiya veṇṇiyāṅ keytupa veṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ  (kuṟaḷ 666)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiyum, tirumūlar tirumantirattiyum mantiram collic cavittuviṭṭāṉ. mantirattiṉāl vāyik kaṭṭiviṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiyum; mantiri, mantiram (cūḻviṉi) eṉṉum araciyaṟ coṟkaḷiyum, kūrntu nōkkuka.
vāymoḻi eṉpatu mantirattiṉ maṟupeyar, nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalil nammāḻvār aruḷicceyta mutalāyiram tiruvāy moḻiyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
ākavē, mantiram eṉṉum col tamiḻil araciyal, mataviyal, ilakkaṇam ākiya muttuṟikkuriyatākum. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya varacukaḷ varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāt toṉmi vāyntavi. paṇṭi nāḷiṟ kiḻakkattiya nāṭukaṭkut tamiḻnāṭṭoṭu araciyal toṭarpum vaṇikat toṭarpum iruntatiṉāl. mantiri eṉṉum col malēyā cīṉam mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum paraviyiruk kiṉṟatu. Mandarin eṉṉum āṅkilac col pōrttukīciya vāyilāyp piṟkālattiṟ pukuntatākum. ataṟkumuṉ mēli yāriyattil ic col illi.
camaṟkiruta iru paṭikaḷ
1. aṭippaṭi - vēta āriyam
2. mēṟpaṭi 	1. vaṭa tiraviṭam (pirākirutam)
			2. teṉ tiraviṭam 
			   (tamiḻoḻinta pañca tiraviṭam)
			3. tamiḻ.
pirākirutam eṉpatu. vētak kālattil vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅ kiya vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷ. pirākirutam = muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu. camaṟkirutam eṉpatu, vētamoḻi yilakkaṇa vamitiyip perumpālum taḻuvi, am moḻiyoṭu pirākirutattiyum tiraviṭattiyum (teṉ tiraviṭam) tamiḻiyum kalantu ceyta aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭimoḻiyākum. camaṟ kirutam = naṉṟāyc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
pirākirutam eṉṉum col pākatam eṉṟum, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum col caṅkatam, caṉukkirakam allatu cāṉukkirakam eṉṟum, tiriyum.
pirākirutam camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyateṉpatu ataṉ peyarālum moḻiyālum teḷivāyt teriyiṉum. camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu pirākirutam tirintatākap pētimi mikka paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiyum ārāycciyillāta ayalāriyum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mayakki varu kiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam). camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ tirintum tiriyāmalum pākatattil vaḻaṅkuvatē.
e-ṭu:tiriyātavi 	tirintavi
	caṣṭi (sashti)		caṭṭi (satihi)
	tarma (dharma)		tamma (dhamma)
tirintu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭāntam (truṣ ṭānta), cōttam (tōtra) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ pōṉ ṟaviyē. avi yāvum aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷalla. pirākirutat tiṟkum aṭippaṭi tamiḻē āyiṉum. atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟāta vāṟu attuṇi vaṭacoṟkaḷ atiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa. vaṭacoṟ kalappu tamiḻil iruppativiṭat tiraviṭattil mikutiyākavum, tiraviṭattil iruppativiṭap pākatattil mikutiyākavum, irukkum. vaṭakkē cellac cella vaṭacoṟ kalappu mikum eṉa aṟika. vēta āriya moḻi vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉataṉāl, atu paḻaṅ kuṭi moḻikaḷiṟ kalantu pulluruviyum uṇṇiyum pēṉum pōl mēluṇṇiyāy (Parasite) iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āriyac col vērūṉṟiyavuṭaṉ ataṟku nērāṉa teṉcol vaḻak kaṟṟup pōm. vaḻakkaṟṟa col ilakkiyattil iṭam peṟā viṭiṉ maṟintu mīḷā niliyaṭiyum.
pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatiraviṭattiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ perumpālum īṟu tirintē vaḻaṅkum.

paḻañcol eṉpaṉa vēta āriyam. iṭukuṟikaḷ āṉanta piravi kaliyāṇi, caṅkarāparaṇam, taṉṉiyāci, nāta nāmak kiriyi, mutaliya paṇṇup peyarkaḷ pōṉṟavi.

(1) muḻuccol : e-ṭu: : akavu, tūtu.
(2) vērccol : e-ṭu: paṇ - pāṇi (ki), maṉ - manu.

pārppāṉ = nūlkaḷip			pirāmaṇaṉ = piramatti

kuṟippu : pārppaṉaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivil aṉaṉ (aṉ + aṉ) eṉpatu īṟu. viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkku mūlamāṉa viṉimuṟṟu āṉ īṟu pōṉṟē aṉaṉ īṟu koḷḷum.

	i.kā.			ni.kā.				e-ṭu:

(5) talikīḻt tirippu
	tamiḻ 		>	pirākirutam   >	camaṟkirutam
	vaṭṭam (vaḷ)		vaṭṭa		vrutta

ivaṟṟit talikīḻākat tirippatu vaṭanūlār iyalpu.
(6) valitturittal
irumoḻikaḷ kuṟukiya kālattil aṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉum, oṉṟi ṉiṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟu oru cila coṟkaḷiyēṉum koḷḷā tirātu. vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu mūvāyiram āṇṭiṟku mēlāyiṉum, atiṟ kalantuḷḷa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷum oṉṟiyēṉum teṉ colleṉa oppuk koḷḷātu vaṭa collēyeṉa valippatu. iṉṟum vaṭa moḻiyāḷar iyalpāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu. vaṭa moḻiyi luḷḷa teṉ coṟkaḷ aintiliraṇṭu pakutiyākum.
(7) tēvamoḻiyeṉal
vēta āriyar, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ pētimiyiyum matappittiyum aḷaviṟantu payaṉpaṭutti, tammi nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum, tam muṉṉōr moḻiyāṉa vēta āriyattiyum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa camaṟkirutattiyum tēva moḻiyeṉṟum collit tamiḻar ati muṟṟum nampumāṟu ceytu viṭṭataṉāl, tamiḻil aḷaviṟanta vaṭacoṟkaḷ vēṇ ṭātu kalakkavum. ataṉāl atu taṉ vaḷaṅkuṉṟit tūymi yiḻakkavum, vaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takātateṉṟu taḷḷappaṭavum, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum tamiḻar tammit tāḻttik koḷḷa vum, nērntateṉa aṟika.  (teṉmoḻi ceppiṭempar 1963.)
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ
(1) cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ
tiraviṭattiṟ pōṉṟē āriyattilum, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ a, i, u eṉṉum muttamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiyē mūlamākak koṇṭaṉa.
tamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyaviyeṉṟum, āriyattiṉ mūla moḻiyoṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉaveṉṟum, kālṭuvelār tēṟṟamākak kūṟiyiruttalik kāṇka.
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ, tiricoṟkaḷātalāl, ōreḻuttuc collā yirātu pala veḻuttuc collākavē yirukkum.
e-ṭu:	ata = atu, appaṭi, aṅkē.
		iha = iṅkē, ivviṭattil, ivvulakil.
		cila cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ iṭamāṟic cuṭṭum.
e-ṭu:	atra - iṅkē, atuṉā = ippoḻutu.
		cila cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ cēymi yaṇmi yiraṇṭiyum cuṭṭum.
e-ṭu:	atta (atas) = ataṉāl, itaṉāl.
		ikarac cuṭṭu ēkāra mākavum tiriyum.
e-ṭu:	ētat (etad) = itu, iṅkuḷḷatu, iṅku.
		ēvam = ippaṭi.
