ic coṟkaṭ kellām vērum mūlamum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉpati nāṭṭaṟkut tā eṉṉum col oṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām. tā-ca. tā, tat (dad), ilat. tō, kirēk. titō.
ic col tamiḻil toṉṟu toṭṭup peruvaḻakkāyiruntu varuvatuṭaṉ,
ītā koṭuveṉak kiḷakkum mūṉṟum
iraviṉ kiḷavi ākiṭa ṉuṭiya.  (tol. ec. 48)
avaṟṟuḷ,
īyeṉ kiḷavi iḻintōṉ kūṟṟē.  (mēṟpaṭi. 49)
tāeṉ kiḷavi oppōṉ kūṟṟē.  (mēṟpaṭi. 50)
koṭuveṉ kiḷavi uyarntōṉ kūṟṟē  (mēṟpaṭi. 51)
tarucol varucol āyiru kiḷaviyum
taṉmi muṉṉili āyī riṭatta.  (mēṟpaṭi. kiḷavi. 29)
eṉṉum varampukaṭ kuṭpaṭṭa ciṟappup poruḷatāyum, tā eṉṉum tanti peyarāyum, uḷḷatu. kaṉṉaṭattilum mali yāḷattilum pōṉṟē āriyattilum potup poruḷil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, avvāriya moḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu ic collik kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu, teritaru tēṟṟamām.
orē teṉcol mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirinta tiricoṟkaḷ, camaṟkirutattil vevvēṟu mutaleḻuttup peṟṟu vevvēṟu mūlattiṉa pōl tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: cāy (mutaṉili). cāytal = kōṇutal, vaḷital, viḻutal, paṭuttal, iṟattal.
cāyuṅ kālam = poḻutu cāyum eṟpāṭu. poḻutu cāya vantāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
cāyuṅ kālam - cāyaṅ kālam - cāyṅ kālam (koccit tiripu.)
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cāyaṅ kālam eṉpatic cāyam + kālam eṉat tavaṟākap pirittu, cāyam eṉpati eṟpāṭṭiṉ peyarākak koṇṭu, ati vaṭamoḻiyil sāyam eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
cāy - cāyi = cāyum niḻal. niḻal cāykiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku. cāyi - ca. cāya (cha#ya#). niḻal vaṭivattiyum niṟattiyum kuṟikku mātalāl, cāyal, cāyam mutaliya coṟkaḷum tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa.
cāy - cāyi = paṭuttavaṉ, paḷḷi koṇṭāṉ. cēṣa cāyi = aravaṇit tuyiṉṟōṉ. tali cāyttal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. cāy - cayaṉam = paṭukki.
cāy - cā. viḻutalum paṭuttalum iṟattaliyuṅ kuṟikkum. cā - cāvu - cavam. āḷ cāyntu viṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu, otta poruṭ karaṇiyamuḷḷa vēṟoru colliyum paṭit tirukkiṉṟaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
e-ṭu: cuḷ - cur - curam = cuṭum pāli nilam.
curam - jvara = cuṭum kāyccal nōy.
cila teṉcol mutaṉilikaḷiṉiṉṟu, ērāḷamāṉa putuc coṟ kaḷit tōṟṟuvittuc camaṟkirutatti vaḷampaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar.
e-ṭu: pū (mutaṉili). pūttal = tōṉṟutal, uṇṭātal, iruttal.
pūttaliṟ pūvāmi naṉṟu  (nītineṟi. 6)
pūttiḻi matamali  (kamparā. kumpakar. 315)
puku - poku - pokil - arumpu.
pokil - pōkil = arumpu.
puku - pō - pōttu = putitāy veṭikkum ciṟu kiḷi.
pū - pūtu - pūtam = tōṉṟiya aimpūtaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  (any of the five elements)
pū - bhu#. itiṉiṉṟu tāṉ puvaṉam, puvi, pūti, pūmi, pavam, pavaṉam, pavitram, paviṣyam, pāvam, pāvakam, pāvi, pāvaṉi, pāvikam, apāvam, aṉupavam, aṉupavi, aṉupūti, utpavam, campu, tatpavam, prapu, prapāvam, paripavam, sampavam, sampāvitam, sampāvaṉi, cuyampū mutaliya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ piṟakkum. tamiḻarkku eḷitāy viḷaṅkumāṟu ivi iṅku tamiḻeḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷ, tirintum muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟum urumāṟi vaṭacoṟkaḷāki yuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: āyiram - aram - ara - sakara.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷ mēli yāriya vaḻiyākat tirintu, nampa muṭiyā aḷavu camaṟkirutattil muṟṟum vaṭivu māṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:	kāṇ - Teut. kun, ken, con, cun, can, know, L. gno, ḻumu. panṭi, ḷumava. tan!yaja. (jñā). kāṭci (aṟivu) - jñāṉa.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷic camaṟkirutattil moḻi peyarttu amittuḷḷaṉar. e-ṭu: vilaṅku - triyac, aṅkuṟṟi - tatrapavat.
camaṟkirutam ilakkiya moḻiyum ceyaṟki moḻiyumātalāl, pala poruḷkaṭkum poruṭ pākupāṭukaṭkum viruppam pōl iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷip paṭittuḷḷaṉar.
e-ṭu: eḻu mukil (sapta mēkam) : camvarttam, āvarttam, puṭkalā varttam, caṅkārittam, turōṇam, kāḷamuki, nīlavarṇam eṉpaṉa.
caṅkarāparaṇam, nāta nāmak kiriyi mutaliya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa paṇṇup peyarkaḷum iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷē.
kallāla mara nīḻaliṟ civaperumāṉiṭam pāṭaṅ kēṭṭa āriya muṉivar peyarākac collappaṭum caṉakar, caṉantaṉar, caṉātarar, caṉaṟ kumārar eṉpaṉavum ittakiyaṉavē.
iṅṅaṉam, camaṟkirutattil aintiliru pakuti muḻut tamiḻc col; aintiliru pakuti tamiḻ vēriṉiṉṟu tirinta tiri col; aintiloru pakuti iṭukuṟic col.
perum pērāciriyar u.vē. cāminātiyar, tāmvarinta kuṟuntoki yuriyuḷ, nūlārāycci eṉṉun talippiṉ kīḻ, 44 coṟkaḷi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭi, mutaliyaṉa eṉṉuñ collāl muṭittu, ivaṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟit tamiḻeṉavē koḷvārum uḷar. eṉṟu eḻuti yirukkiṉṟār.
avaṟṟuḷ, akil, amayam, amiḻtam, aracaṉ, avi, āriyar, ulakam, ēmam, kaṭiki, kalāvam, kāmam, kālam, kuṇaṉ, kuvaḷi, cakaṭam, cūli, cēmam, cēri, taṇṭu, tātu, tūtu, teyvam, nakar, nīlam, pakkam, paṇilam, paruvam, pavaḻam, maṇṭilam, maṇi, matam, mati, māli, mukam, muttu, muracu, yāmam eṉṉum muppattēḻum teṉcollām.
avi - aviḻ = cōṟṟup parukki, cōṟu. aviḻ - aviḻtu - amiḻtu - amitu - amutu = cōṟu, uṇavu. parup pamutu, kaṟiyamutu, tayiramutu, iliyamutu eṉpaṉa uṇṇuṅ kaṟivaki kaḷik kuṟikkum kalveṭṭuc coṟkaḷ.
amutu ceytal = uṇṇutal. amutu paṭittal = uṇavu parimāṟutal. tiruvamutu = teyvap paṭimikkup paṭikkappaṭum uṇavu.
amiḻtu - amiḻtam. amutu - amutam.
kaṭṭip poruḷāyiṉum, nīrp poruḷāyiṉum, uṇṇap paṭuvatellām amutamām. uṇaviṟkuc cuviyūṭṭum uppum amutam eṉappaṭṭatu.
amutu = 1. cōṟu. 2. uṇavu. vāṭā malarum nallamutum (ñāṉavā. pirakalā. 8) 3. pāl. (aka.ni.). 4. nīr. (piṅ.).
amutam = 1. cōṟu (piṅ.) 2. pāl. (piṅ.). 3. nīr. tulaṅkiya vamutam  (kallā. 5).
uṇṭavaṉ vāḻvāṉ; uṇṇātavaṉ cāvāṉ. cōṟum nīrum cāvit tavirppatāl iru marunteṉappaṭum.
tayir amutam eṉappaṭuvatāl veṇṇeyum amutamām.
tēvar makkaḷiṉum uyarntava rātalāl, avaruṇavu ciṟantatākak karutappaṭṭatu.
ēṉatu cuvippiṉum nīki toṭṭatu
vāṉō ramutam puriyumāl emakkeṉa  (tol. kaṟ. 5).
amutam eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil amruta eṉṟu tiriyum. ati a+mrut eṉṟu pirittuc cāvit tavirppatu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu, ataṟ kēṟpat tēvarum acurarum kūṭit tiruppāṟ kaṭal kaṭintu veṇṇe yeṭuttatākak kati kaṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. akkatiyiṟ pakuttaṟiviṟ kovvāta pala ceyti kaḷiruttal kāṇka.
mantiram mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiyatu tamiḻilēyē.
mantirap poruḷvayiṉ āakunavum  (tol. ecca. 53)
muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻu muṉivar tam tūya vuḷḷattiṉ eṇṇa valimi yāl āyntu kūṟum meym moḻi, niṟimoḻi māntariṉ niṟivēṟuṅ kūṟṟu.
mantiram vāymi paṟṟiyum vāyppatu paṟṟiyum vāymoḻi yeṉavum paṭum.
civaṉiyac cārpāṉa tirumantiramum māliyac cārpāṉa tiruvāy moḻiyum tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa.
tattuva maci
ōkanilikkuriya tattuva maci eṉṉum coṟṟoṭari, peruṅ kūṟṟiyam (makā vākkiyam) eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paṟi yaṟivar. atu muṟṟum teṉ coṟṟiri peṉpati avar aṟiyār.
tat + tvam + asi = tattvamasi.
tāṉ, tām eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutaṟ kālattil orumiyum paṉmiyuṅ kuṟitta paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷāka viruntu, akara vaṭi koṇṭa aimpāṟ paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṟkālattil, taṟcuṭṭup peyarkaḷāka (Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiṉa.
tāṉ (avaṉ, avaḷ, atu.) - ca. tat = avaṉ, avaḷ, atu.
x.neh.: tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ - ca. srīmat.
OE. thæt, E. that.
vētap peyarkaḷ
vēta moḻiyilum camaṟkirutattilum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkum āyirak kaṇakkumāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ iruppatuṭaṉ, vētattiṉ peyarkaḷum teṉcol tiripākavē uḷḷaṉa.
viḻittal = kaṇ tiṟattal, pārttal, aṟital, viḻi = aṟivu, ōti (ñāṉam).
viḻak kaṇṭun tēṟār viḻiyilā māntar (tiruman.) viḻi L. vide, OS. wit, Goth, wit, ON. vit, Skt. - vit - vēta = aṟivu, aṟivu nūl, maṟi.
ceviyuṟutal = kēṭṭal. ceviyuṟu - cru - cruti = kēḷvi, eḻu tāk kiḷaviyāyk kēṭṭaṟiyap paṭṭa āriya maṟi.
vētap piriviṉ peyarāṉa maṇṭalam eṉpatum teṉcollē.
maṇṭutal = vaḷital. maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭi yiṭutal = kāli vaḷittu niṟṟal allatu iruttal. maṇṭu - maṇṭalam - vaṭṭam, nāṭṭu vaṭṭam, kāla vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ, nūṟ pirivu. maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam. eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu pārkka.
mūṉṟaṉ pakutiyum maṇṭilat tarumiyum  (tol. akat. 41)
toṇṭi maṇṭalam, koṅku maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa toṉmi yāṉa nāṭṭup peyarkaḷ.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaṭṭamiṭutal, pāṭṭiṉ ellā aṭikaḷum aḷavot tiruttal.
maṇṭilam kuṭṭam eṉṟivi yiraṇṭum  (tol. cey. 116)
vaṭṭamiṭum allatu vaṭṭamāyirukkum, pāmpu, naccup pūcci mutaliya uyirikaḷum, narappicik karuvikaḷum, maṇṭalam allatu maṇṭali eṉap peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
ūṉ, ūm eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ, nūṉ, nūm eṉṟu nakara meym mutal peṟṟu, piṉṉar nīṉ, nīm eṉat tirintaṉa.
nūṉ, nūm eṉpaṉa, vaṭatiraviṭattuḷ oru vakiyāṉa cūracēṉip pirākirutattil tūṉ, tūm eṉṟu tirintu, iṉṟa ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa intiyil tū, tum eṉṟa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
tū (nī) eṉṉum inti vaṭivamē, iyalpākavum kuṟukiyum mēli āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
OE. OS. thu#, OHG. du#, ON. thu, Goth. thu, L. tu, E. thou.
ēṉ, ēm eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum nāṉ nām eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum iṭiyē, yāṉ yām eṉpavi iruppatu pōṉṟē, ūṉ ūm eṉpavaṟ ṟiṟkum nūṉ nūm eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum iṭiyē, yūṉ yūm eṉpaṉa vum iruntaṉa.
iv yakara aṭiyiṉiṉṟē āṅkila cākacaṉīyam yī (ye) allatu yū (you) eṉṉum muṉṉilip paṉmip peyarit tōṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
matippuṟavu paṟṟit tum eṉṉum paṉmip peyari intiyum, yū eṉṉum paṉmip peyari āṅkilamum, orumikku vaḻaṅku vatu pōl, vēta āriyamum, tum eṉṉum paṉmip peyarit tvam eṉṟu tirittu orumikkum, yuvām yūyam eṉṉum yakara vaṭip peyarkaḷi muṟiyē irumi paṉmikkum, vaḻaṅ kiṟṟup pōlum.
ākavē, nūm eṉṉum muṉṉilip paṉmip peyariṉ tiripē, intiya āriyattiluḷḷa tvam eṉṉum muṉṉili yorumip peyar eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
a
camaṟkiruta muṉṉili yorumi nikaḻkāla viṉiyīṟu si. a eṉṉum viṉi itaṉoṭu puṇarum pōtu īṟu keṭṭu asī eṉa niṟkum. ākavē, tat, tvam, asi eṉṉum muccol lum cērntu, nī atuvāyirukkiṉṟāy eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum tattvamasi eṉṉum kūṟṟiyam amiyum.
tat eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyariṉiṉṟē tatva (tattuvam) eṉṉum collum piṟakkum.
tatva = atāyirukkum taṉmi, uṇmiyāṉa taṉmi, uṇmi, meyp poruḷ.
vaṭacol eṉpataṟkum camaṟkirutac col eṉpataṟkum oru vēṟṟumi yuṇṭu. toṉṟu toṭṭu vaṭanāṭṭil taṉic ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkuñ collellām vaṭa collē. camaṟkirutac col eṉpatu, vēta āriyar vantapiṉ putitāka amikkappaṭṭa āriyac col. āriyar varumuṉpē, vaṭa tiraviṭar (pirākirutar) pala putuc coṟkaḷip puṉintiruntaṉar. aviyum vaṭacol. ākavē, vaṭacol eṉṉum peyar, āriyamallāta vaṭanāṭṭuc ciṟappuc coṟkum, camaṟkirutac coṟkum, potuvām. ellāc camaṟkirutac collum vaṭa collākum; āyiṉ, ellā vaṭacollum camaṟkiruta mākā.
āti eṉṉum col vaṭacollē; camaṟkirutamaṉṟu. atu kaḻaka ilakkiya vaḻakkil illi. āyiṉum, uṇṭātal eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa ātal viṉiyiṉiṉṟu atit tirippar tamiḻaṉpar cilar.
ākkutal = paṭittal. aṉittulakum ākkuvāy kāppāy aḻippāy (tiruvāca. 1: 42). ivvāṭci avarkkut tuṇi ceyyalām.
camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam
tamiḻilakkaṇattip piṉpaṟṟic camaṟkirutattil mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya ilakkaṇam aintiram. ataṉ kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 8-ām allatu 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ataṉpiṉ, ki.mu.4 ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu aṣṭāt yāyī eṉṉum pāṇi ṉīyam. iraṇṭum eḻuttum collum ākiya iru kūṟukaḷiyē koṇṭaṉa.
eḻuttiṉ muṟi, piṟappu, uyirmeyt taṉi vaṭivam, māttiri, kiḷavi (patam), puṇarcci ākiyavaṟṟil, camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam tamiḻiyē taḻuviyuḷḷatu. eḻuttiṉ vari vaṭivum, kirantam nākari eṉṉum iru vakiyilum perumpālum tamiḻi ottiruppatu, kūrntu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākum.
peyar viṉi iṭi eṉṉum tamiḻp pākupāṭṭiyē, camaṟkirutac colli lakkaṇamum taḻuviyuḷḷatu. vēṟṟumikaḷ ilattīṉil aintu; kirēkkattil āṟu; camaṟkirutattil tamiḻiṟ pōl eṭṭu. itaṉāl, vēṟṟumi yamippil, camaṟkirutam tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam
patiṉeṇ toṉmaṅkaḷum (purāṇaṅkaḷum) irāmāyaṇam pāratam ākiya maṟa vaṉappukkaḷum (itikācaṅkaḷum) tavira, ici, nāṭakam, kaṇiyam, maruttuvam mutaliya piṟa kali yaṟiviyal ilakkiya mellām tamiḻ mutaṉūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi nūlkaḷām. camaṟkirutattil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭapiṉ, tamiḻ mutaṉūlka ḷellām aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa.
camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiya intiya āriya ilakkiyam vētam oṉṟē. vētattip peṇṭirum cūttirarum ōtak kūṭāteṉpar. āyiṉ, peṇṭirum cūttirarum cilar vēta mantiraṅkaḷiyē iyaṟṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar.
vētam iyaṟṟiya peṇṭir
pulōmaṉi 	makaḷ 		ṣaci
kāmi 		makaḷ 		ciratti
cakti 		makaḷ 		kōravi
ap pirīṉā 	makaḷ 		vākku
akattiyar 	maṉivi	lōpā muttiri
vētam iyaṟṟiya paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ
aracar (cattiriyar) mantāttiri, ṣivi, vacumaṉāṉ, piratarttaṉaṉ, vivāmittiraṉ, matu cantaṉaṉ, riṣapaṉ, rēṇu, ampariṣaṉ, parataṉ, mētāniti, nāpakaṉ, irakukaṇaṉ, vakṣap piriyaṉ, purūravaṉ, vēṉaṉ, cutācaṉ, kiruta camataṉ, tēvāpi, cantāṉu mutaliyōr.
toḻilāḷar (cūttirar) : kavaṣaṉ, ailuṣaṉ mutaliyōr.
kalappuk kulattār : kakṣīvatu (aṅka nāṭṭu aracaṉiṉ puḻukkacci makaṉ) pōṉṟa palar.
ippeyarkaḷ varuṇa cintāmaṇiyil uḷḷavāṟē eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
āriyar karuttuppaṭi, vaṭa nāṭṭu makkaḷē pirāmaṇar, cattiriyar, viciyar, cūttirar eṉṉum nālvakip pākupāṭṭiṉar; teṉ ṉāṭṭut tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum ākiya antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar aṉivarum cūttirarē. āyiṉ, āṭci ceyyum aracaric cattiriyar eṉṟum, poruḷ koṭukkum vaṇikari viciyar eṉṟum, pukaḻvar. itu atikāramum poruḷum ceyyum vēli. vēḷvi ceyvitta paṇṭi mūvēntariyum, cattiriyar eṉṟu pukaḻntē vēḷvi ceyvikkat tūṇṭiṉar.
vētattiṉ piṉ vēḷvi muṟikaḷi viḷakkum pirāmaṇaṅkaḷum, āriyar tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ upaniṭataṅkaḷum kulavoḻukka nūlkaḷākiya taruma cāttiraṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
vētam ceyyuḷ vaṭivāṉa mantiraṅkaḷik koṇṭatu. mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiyatu irukku (ruk) oṉṟē. vēḷvi ceytaṟ kuriya mantiraṅkaḷi atiṉiṉṟu pirittut tokuttatu ecur (yajur). vēḷviyiṟ pāṭak kūṭiya mantiraṅkaḷip pirittut tokuttatu cāmam (sāma). iṅṅaṉam oru vētam mūvētam (vētatrayī) āyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ āṟiloru pakuti irukku vēta mantiraṅ kaḷik koṇṭatum, perumpālum cāvippu mantiraṅkaḷā lāṉatum, urinaṭi kalantatumāṉa, atarvaṉam (atarvaṉ) tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉṉarē nāṉ maṟi (catur vēta) eṉṉum vaḻakkeḻuntatu.
vētak kālattiṉ piṉ, vēta cāttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷiyum payaṉiyum ciṟappiyum perumpālum urinaṭiyil viḷak kum uriyākat tōṉṟiyavi pirāmaṇam. kāṭuṟivu (vāṉap piratta) niliyiṟ pirāmaṇaṉukkup payaṉpaṭum koṇ muṭipu nūlkaḷ āraṇiyakam (āraṇyaka). tuṟavu (canniyāsa) niliyiṟ pirāmaṇaṉukkup payaṉpaṭum meypporuḷ nūlkaḷ upaniṭatam (upa niṣat).
upa niṣat = uṭaṉ kīḻirukki. upa = uṭaṉ, ni - kīḻ, sat (Sad) = iruttal. āciriyaṉ aṭi yarukiruntu avaṉ nuvaṟciyik kēṭṭal eṉpatē viḷakkap poruḷ. paramporuḷaṟivāl aṟiyāmiyi ōyn tirukkac ceyki eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. naṉku aḻittaṟku sātaṉam eṉpar par. (P.S.) cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār.
vētat teyviyam palteyviyamē (Polytheism). palloru teyviyam (Honotheism) eṉṟu mākcu mullarum, oru teyviyam (Monotheism) eṉṟu mākaṭāṉelum (Mac Donell) kūṟiyatu tavaṟām. āriyar paramporuḷaṟivit tamiḻariṭamē peṟṟaṉar. palciṟu teyva vēḷvi matattiṟkum param poruṭ camayat tiṟkum maṭuviṟkum mali muṭikkum uḷḷa tolivē.
sat eṉṉum collum teṉ col tiripē.
kuttu - kuntu, L. Sid, Sed, AS. Sitt, E. Sit, Goth. Sit. ON, Siti, OHG. Sizz, Skt. Sad.
ki.pi. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ, kōvilamippu, paṭimi yamippu, vaḻipaṭu muṟi, pūcakaṉ takuti, koṇ muṭipu mutaliya vaṟṟik kūṟum toḻu maṟikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, civa ṉiyattiṟkuriyavi tōṉṟiyam (ākamam) eṉṟum, māliyattiṟ kuriyavi tokuppiyam (samhiti) eṉṟum, kāḷiyattiṟku (cāk tattiṟku) uriyavi pāvakam (tantiram) eṉṟum peyar peṟum.

tēva moḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷ
toṉmi, muṉmi, iyaṉmi, viyaṉmi, tāymi, tūymi, eṇmi, oṇmi, iḷami, vaḷami, ammi, cemmi, iṉimi, taṉimi, maṟimi, iṟimi eṉṉum patiṉāṟum tēva moḻikku irukka vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇpu kaḷām. ippatiṉāṟum uṭiyatu tamiḻ oṉṟē. vaṭa moḻikku ōriru paṇpukaḷē uḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻip pōṟ piṟa moḻi kalavātu pēcat takka moḻi ulakil vēṟoṉṟu milli. āriya moḻikaḷ piṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷi vilakkiṉum, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷi vilakkavē muṭiyātu. ātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ uyir vāḻviṟkut tamiḻt tuṇi iṉṟiyamiyāta tām. ākavē, tamiḻē tēva moḻi eṉṟu teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
camaṉ
camaṉ: ammutal = poruntutal, ottal am - cam - camam = oppu. camam - camaṉ = tulikkōl. niṟukkumuṉ irupuṟamum ottu niṟkum nili. camam - sama (va.).  (ti.ma.: 742)



caram
caram - sā
nīrpporuḷ oru tuḷiyiṉiṉṟu nērāka virintu oḻukutali, car eṉṟu pāykiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
oḻukal nīṭcik karutti yuṇarttum.
vārtal pōkal oḻukal mūṉṟum
nērpum neṭumiyum ceyyum poruḷa  (tol. 800)
car - caram = nīṇṭa allatu nērāṉa maṇikkōvi allatu malarttoṭi.
carañcaramāyt toṅkukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
carakkoṉṟi = nēr malart toṭiyākap pūkkum koṉṟi.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum ru eṉṉum coṟkum oḻuku, ōṭu eṉpaṉavē poruḷ.  (va.va. 144-145).
carukkam
carukkam - sarka (g)
carukkam - nūṟpirivu.
vaṭavar ruj eṉṉum colloṭu poruttik kāṭṭuvar. ataṟku viṭu, eṟi, vīcu, veḷiviṭu eṉṟa poruḷkaḷē uṇṭu.  (va.va. 144.)
carukkari
carukkari - carkkarā
vaṭamoḻiyil caraḷ, ciṟukal, kūḻāṅkal, kaṟkaṇṭu eṉṟu poruḷ toṭarpu kāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.144).
caruvu
caruvu - caruku. carukutal = cāytal.
caruku - carukku. carukkutal = cāytal, vaḷital, caṟukkutal.
carukku - carukkam = vaṭṭam, nūṟpirivu L. circum = vaṭṭam.
carukku - carukkaram - cakkaram = vaṭṭam, uruḷi, kuyavaṉ cakkaram, cakkarappaṭi, vaṭṭakkācu, cakkarappuḷ, mānilam, cekku.
cakkarap pāṭit teruvu  (periyapu. kaliyaṉā 5).
Gk kuklos, E cycle = cakkaram.
carukkaram - carukkari - carkkari - cakkari = vaṭṭamāka vārkkap peṟṟa vellakkaṭṭi.
o.nō. vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa karuppukkaṭṭi.
carukkaram - (carukkāram) - cakkāram (mū.a.) = iṉiyatēmā. iṉṟum cakkarikkuṭṭi eṉṟu oru māmpaḻap peyar vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
cakkāram - akkāram = carukkari.
akkāramaṉṉār  (nālaṭi. 374)
akkāraṭali, akkāravaṭicil eṉpaṉa carukkarip poṅkal vakikaḷ.
patanīri (teḷivi) akkāranīr eṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
akkāram eṉṟu māṅkaṉikkum peyar. akkārakkaṉi naccumaṉār eṉṉum pulavarpeyar akkaṉiyāl vantatupōlum!
cakkaram - (cakku) - cekku = eṇṇeyāṭṭum vaṭṭamāṉa ural.
x.neh.: paru-peru, cattāṉ - cettāṉ.
cakku - cakkaṭam - cakkaṭā = kaṭṭi vaṇṭi.
te. cekkaṭā paṇṭi.
cakkaṭam - cakaṭam = cakkaram, vaṇṭi, tēr, vaṭṭil, muracu.
cakaṭam - cakaṭi = vaṇṭi. cakaṭiki = kivaṇṭi.
cakaṭu = vaṇṭi. cakaṭu - cākāṭu = vaṇṭi. pīlipey cākāṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 476).
cakaṭu - cāṭu = vaṇṭi. kuṟuñcāṭ ṭuruḷi (perumpāṇ. 188).
cāṭu - cāṭukam = vaṇṭi.
cakaṭu - cakaṭi = vaṇṭi, muracu, amaṅkalac ciṟupaṟi.
cakaṭi - cakaṇṭi = muracu, paṟippotu.
cakaṭam - cakōṭam = cakkarappuḷ, ataṉ vaṭivāṉa yāḻ.
cakōṭam = cakōram = cakkarappuḷ.
cakku - caṅku =- uḷ vaḷintuḷḷa nantukkūṭu, valampuri, iṭampuri. purital - vaḷital.
x.neh.: kōṭu = caṅku. curimukam = caṅku. vaḷi = caṅku. vāraṇam = caṅku.
caṅku - caṅkam = 1. caṅku. aṭutiric caṅka mārppa (cīvaka. 701).
1. kivaḷi. caṅkaṅ kaḻala (iṟi. 39 uri).
cakkaram mutal caṅku vari pala coṟkaḷ vaḷivuk karuttaṭip paṭiyil orē toṭarpuṭiyaṉavātaliṉ, oruṅku kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
cakkara eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭiviṟku vaṭamoḻiyāḷarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa mūlam kru(cey) eṉpatē. mā.vi. akaramutali car (car) eṉṉum colli viṉākkuṟiyuṭaṉ kuṟittuḷḷatu. car = iyaṅku ivviraṇṭum, muṉṉatiṉ poruntāmiyiyum piṉṉatiṉ vaṉpuṇarpput taṉmiyiyum, aṟiñar kaṇṭu koḷka. cakaṭam - cakaṭa (ḷaÝ,va@).
mā. vi. a. “of doubtful derivation” eṉṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 135-137.)

cittam
cittam - citta (c)
vaṭavar cittaṉ eṉṉum colli, sāt (dh) eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa sit (dh) eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, sitta (siddha) eṉṟum, cittiyi sitti (siddhi) eṉṟum, kāṭṭuvar.
sit = muṭi, kikūṭu. sitti = muṭivu. muṭipu, kikūṭal, pēṟu. sitta = arumpēṟu peṟṟavaṉ.
cittaṉ āṟṟalit tamiḻiṟ cittu eṉpatē marapu. cittu viḷiyāṭal eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. kalampaka vuṟuppum cittu eṉṟē peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 150).

citamparam
citamparam - citampara (c)
ciṟṟampalam - (cittampalam) - (cittamparam) - citamparam.
tilliyiṟ ciṟṟampalam pērampalam eṉa īrampalaṅkaḷuṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟṟampalamē iṟivaṉ tirukkōyil.
ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟampalattum  (tirukkō.).
vaṭavar tiripu vaṭivi iyalpu vaṭivākak koṇṭu aṟivuveḷi (ñāṉākācam) eṉṟu poruḷ puṇarkka muyalvar. ampalam (maṉṟam) vēṟu; amparam (vāṉam) vēṟu. mēlum, cit. ampara eṉṉum iru vaṭacoṟkaḷum teṉcoṟṟiripē eṉpati aṟika.
citi
citi - cit (chid) - i.vē.
citittal = pirittal, kulittal, piyttal, keṭuttal, veṭṭutal, aḻittal.
citarttal = pirittal, kulittal, veṭṭutal.  (va.va. 152).
cintāmaṇiyiṉ cevviya vaṉappiyal
ceyyuḷ, oṉṟāyt taṉittiruppatu paṟṟit taṉinili eṉṟum, palavāyt toṭarntiruppatu paṟṟit toṭarnili eṉṟum iru vakippaṭum toṭar niliyum, coṟṟoṭarnili. poruṭ ṭoṭar nili eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum.
ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷiyellām piṟkālat tilakkaṇiyar vaṭanūṟ pākupāṭṭit taḻuvic ciṟu kāppiyam, peruṅkāppiyam eṉa iru vakiyākap pakuppar. tolkāppiyar uḷḷiṭṭa tollā ciriyarō, avaṟṟi
ammi aḻaku toṉmi tōlē
viruntē iyipē pulaṉē iḻipeṉa
eṇ vaki vaṉappākak pakuppar. ākavē, vaṉappu eṉpatē avar ceyyuḷ nūṟku iṭṭa peyarām. taṉinilic ceyyuḷum ōrāyiram aṭiyum īrāyiram aṭiyum koṇṭu oru kāviya mākalā mātalāṉum. toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ kāviyamāy maṭṭumaṉṟi. ilakkaṇa nūlum maruttuva nūlum aṟa nūlum piṟa nūlumāyirukkalā mātalāṉum. toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ eṉpatu kāviyam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku nērāṉa teṉcollaṉṟu.
itupōtu tamiḻiluḷḷa vaṉappukaḷuḷ, cintāmaṇiyum cilap patikāramum taliciṟantaṉa eṉpati, naṭuniliyaṟiñar evarum maṟār.
oru vaṉappu coṟcuvi, poruṭcuvi, yāppuccuvi, aṇic cuvi eṉpavaṟṟiṟ ciṟantiruttal maṭṭum pōtātu. mikunta col vaḷamum ilakkaṇac cemmiyu muṭiyatāyum. palakaliyiyalpi viḷakkuvatāyum, varuṅ kālattil variyappaṭum varalāṟṟu nūṟkuc cāṉṟākap pala ceytikaḷik koṇṭatāyum, ciṟanta vāḻkki varampukaḷi vakuttu uyarnta kuṟikkōḷi valiyuṟuppatāyum, collārāyccikkut tuṇi ceyvatāyum iruttal vēṇṭum.
iv vaḷavippaṭi, cintāmaṇiyiṉ vaṉappiyaṟ cemmiyi ārāyvām.
(1)  coṟṟiṟam
1. coṉṉayam
1. iṉcoṟpuṇarppu
tēvar icittamiḻil muṟṟat tuṟipōki iṉṉici yiṉpatti nuṇṇitiṉukarum eḵkuc cevi paṭittavarātaliṉ iṉṉōcic coṟkaḷit tērntāḷuvatō ṭamiyātu. cevikkiṉpañ ciṟakkumāṟu iṉṉōcic cāriyi koṇṭum coṟkaḷip puṇarttuk koḷkiṉṟār.
e-ṭu:	aṇṇalaṅkumaraṉ, āḻiyaṅkaḻaṉi, mayilañcāyal, mulliyaṅ kōti.
2. ilakkaṇac cemmi
mataviyal paṟṟic cintāmaṇiyil pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ pukuttappaṭṭi ruppiṉum, avaṟṟi yāṇṭum tamiḻiyalpoṭu poruntat taṟpavamākavē vaṭittāḷukiṉṟār tēvar. kamparāmāyaṇattil lōpēṉ eṉṉum lakara mutaṟcol vantuḷḷatu pōla. aṉmutaleḻutti moḻi mutaṟkoṇṭa oru vaṭacollum cintāmaṇiyil vantilli.
3. coṟṟūymi
cintāmaṇic coṟṟokuti, an nūṟkālattōṭu porunta nōkkiṉ, ettuṇiyō tūymiyāṉatākum. perumpāṉmi yāṉa ceyyuṭkaḷ taṉittamiḻilēyē iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. vaṭacoṟkaḷ vantuḷḷa cila ceyyuṭkaḷilum, avi nūṟṟukku ari mutal muppatu variyē vantuḷḷaṉavātaliṉ. cintāmaṇiyiṉ cen tamiḻttiṟattip peritum citikkavilli.
cāmeṉil cātal nōtal taṉṉavaṉ taṇanta kālip
pūmaṉum puṉita liṉṟip poṟpuṭaṉ pulampa vikik
kāmaṉi yeṉṟum collār kaṇavaṟki toḻutu vāḻvār
tēmalart tiruvō ṭoppār cērntavaṉ cellal tīrppār  (1605)
eṉṉumicceyyuḷ taṉittamiḻil iyaṉṟatu. kāmaṉ, tiru eṉpaṉa teṉcollē.
pavaḻavāyc ceṟuvu taṉṉuḷ nittilam payila vittik 
kuḻavinā ṟeḻuntu kāḷik koḻuṅkati rīṉṟu piṉṉāk
kiḻavutāṉ viḷikkum piṅkūḻ kēṭṭirēṟ piṇicey paṉmā
uḻavirkāḷ! mēyum; cīla vēliyuyt tiṭumiṉ, eṉṟāṉ.  (379)
itil, cīlam eṉṉum oru collē vaṭacollām. atuvum cīr eṉṉum teṉcoṟku iṉamātaliṉ. ati iru moḻip potuccol eṉak kōṭalē cālacciṟantatām.
kuralkura lākap paṇṇik kōtitāḻ kuñci yāṉṟaṉ
viralkavarn teṭutta kītam miṭaṟeṉat terital tēṟṟār
curaroṭu makkaḷ vīḻntār; cōrntaṉa puḷḷu māvum;
urukiṉa maramuṅ kallum; ōrtteḻīip pāṭukiṉṟāṉ.  (723)
itil, kītam eṉṉum oṉṟē teḷivāṉa vaṭacol. curar eṉpatu irumoḻip potu.
kurōtaṉē māṉaṉ māyaṉ kūrppuṭi yulōpa ṉeṉpār
virōtittu viraliṟ cuṭṭi veruvarat tākka vīraṉ
nirōtaṉi yampiṟ koṉṟāṉ nittinīḷ pacalip pērōr
virākeṉum vēliṉ vīḻa vekuṇṭaṉaṉ; avarum vīḻntār.  (3080)
itil, kurōtaṉ, ulōpaṉ, virōti, vīraṉ, nirōtaṉ, nitti (nittiri), pacali (piracali), virāku (virākam), eṉṉum eṭṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. māṉaṉ, māyi eṉpaṉa aruka cittāntac coṟkaḷāyiṉum muṟiyē māṉi māy eṉṉum teṉcol laṭiyāyp piṟantavi. ic ceyyuḷil eṇ vaṭacoṟkaḷirup piṉum, centamiḻōci citiyā tiruttal kāṇka. tīntamiḻc cilappatikārattilum, maturit tamiḻ maṇimēkaliyilumē vaṭa coṟkaḷ payiṉṟiruttaliṉ, cintāmaṇikku avaṟṟāl ciṟap piḻappilli eṉka. tēvar, pala iṭaṅkaḷil, aruka cittāntak kuṟiyīṭukaḷāṉa vaṭacoṟ kaḷiyum tūya tamiḻil moḻi peyarttē celkiṉṟār. 3081 ām ceyyuḷil, ñāṉam, taricaṉam. antarāyam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ, muṟiyē, uṇarvu, kāṭci, pēṟu eṉa moḻi peyarkkappaṭ ṭiruttal kāṇka.
	



2. col vaḷam
1. arumporuṭ peyarkaḷ
e-ṭu: accuṟu koḻuntoṭar - marakkaṭṭikaḷil irumpit tittu yāṉiyiṉ vēkatti aṭakkumāṟu ataṉ kaḻuttil māli pōla iṭuvatoru karuvi.
aṭiyūci - aṭiyoṭṭi (tappiyōṭuvār aṅṅaṉam ōṭātavāṟu nilattil naṭṭu vikkum muḷ).
aṇikam - ūrti.
iḷavuṭiyāṉ - iḷavaracaṉ.
kōṭṭakam - payiruḷḷa nīr nili.
paṇṇuri - puṉinturi (upacāra vārtti)
pāmpuri - akaḻiyiṉ kīḻc cūḻa amitta oru matiluṟuppu
mukaviyar - ēṟṟuk koḷpavar.
valañcuḻi - nantiyāvaṭṭam.
vaṭakam - ōr uṭi vaki.
2. aruntoṭar moḻikaḷ
tēvar collāṭcit tiṟaṉil mika viñciyavarātaliṉ, vēṇṭiya vāṟellām viḻumiya toṭarmoḻikaḷip puṉintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
e-ṭu: kaṭṭaḻalevvam - iṇareri tōyvaṉṉa iṉṉā.
poṉ vaṇṇappuḻukkal - paruppuccōṟu.
nāṇ meyk koṇṭīṭṭappaṭṭār - nāṇamē vaṭivāya peṇṭir.
	(2) poruṭṭiṟam
1. kalikaḷ
nīrniṉ ṟiḷakiṟ ṟituvēṇṭā; nīriṉ vanta titupōka;
vārniṉ ṟiḷaku muliyiṉāy! vāṭpuṇ ṇuṟṟa titunaṭakka;
oru murumē ṟituvuṇṭa toḻika; oṇpo ṉukukoṭiyē!
cīrcāl kaṇiki ciṟuvaṉpōṟ ciṟappiṉ ṟamma vitu.  (718)
eṉṟāṉ.
itu ici paṟṟiyatu. iṅṅaṉamē. yāḻ marattiṉ ēṉik kuṟṟaṅ kaḷum, narappuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷum, yāḻuṟuppukaḷum, yāḻvakikaḷum, pāṭum muṟiyum kāntaruvatattiyā rilampakattil kalimuṟip paṭi kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tōṟpoli muḻavum yāḻum tuḷipayil kuḻalu mēṅkak
kāṟkoci kompu pōlap pōntukit talaṅkaḷ kāṭṭi
mēṟpaṭa veruvi nōkkit tāṉiyi viṭṭiṭ ṭolkit
tōṟṟiṉāḷ mukañcey kōlam tuḷakkiṉāḷ maṉatti yellām  (675)
itu kūttuppaṟṟiyatu.
moḻintunañ cukutta laccam iriperu vekuḷi pōkam
kaḻintumī tāṭal kālam piḻippeṉa veṭṭi ṉākum;
piḻintuyi ruṇṇun taṭṭam ataṭṭamām piḷiṟṟi ṉumpar
oḻinteyi ṟūṉañ ceyyuṅ kōḷeṉa maṟṟuñ coṉṉāṉ.  (1286)
itu (naccu) maruttuvam paṟṟiyatu. itaṟkaṭutta iru ceyyuṭkaḷum ituvē.
oḷḷilic cūlan teṇṇī rulāmukiṟ kiḻikku māṭa  (2527)
cūla neṟṟiya kōpurat tōṟṟamum  (303)
paṉmalark kiṭaṅkucūḻ pacumpoṟ pāmpurik
kaṉṉimū teyil  (1250)
ivi ciṟpam paṟṟiyaṉa. cūlam - iṭi tāṅki.
2. toḻiṉmuṟi
nāmaka ḷilampakattil, uḻavuttoḻil mika virivāka variyap peṟṟuḷatu. kōvintiyā rilampakam, kāntaruvatattiyā rilampakam. maṇmaka ḷilampakam ākiya mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum, pōrt toḻil akkāla muṟippaṭi aḻakākak kūṟuppaṭṭuḷḷatu.
poṟkammiyam paṟṟiya cila ceytikaḷ āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: 
kaṟanta pāliṉuṭ kācil tirumaṇi
niṟaṅki ḷarntutaṉ nīrmikeṭ ṭāṅku  (1325)
kantukkaṭaṉ eṉṉum āṭciṟappup peyar, orukāl, kantu vaṭṭikkuk kaṭaṉ koṭukkum vaṇikat toḻili uṇartta lām.
kāntaruvatattiyā rilampakattil kaṭal vāṇikam kūṟap paṭṭuḷatu.
3. viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ
kōṭṭiḷanta karkaḷuṅ koymmalara tōṉṟipōṟ 
cūṭṭuṭiya cēvalun tōṇikkōḻi yātiyā
vēṭṭavaṟṟi ṉūṟuḷār veruḷimāntar pōrkkoḷīik
kāṭṭiyārkkuṅ keḷaviyuṅ kaṭiyuṅkeḷavi keḷaviyē  (73)
itu, takarp pōr, cēvaṟ pōr, kāṭip pōr mutaliya āṭavar viḷiyāṭṭik kūṟukiṉṟatu. itil, avaṟṟiṉ ūṟuḷār eṉṟu tēvar kuṟippatu, avaratu aruṭpolivi uṇarttum.
vitta pante ṭuttalum māli yuṭka rattalum
kitta latti ṉōṭṭalum kaṇṇi neṟṟi tīṭṭalum
patti yiṟpu ṭittalum piya ravvi ṉāṭalum
itti ṟatta pantiṉō ṭiṉpa melli yilliyē.  (151)
itu makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭākiya pantāṭṭu.
kuṇamāliyā rilampakattil, irupālārkkum potuvāṉa iḷavēṉil nīr viḷiyāṭṭu virivāka varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
4. paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ
aṟupattunāḷkaliyuṅ kaṟṟal, iḻanta araci ettiṟattum kippaṟṟutal, vēṇṭiyavari uyartti vēṇṭātavari aṭiyōṭaḻittal. pala makaḷiri maṇattal, tiripaṉṟi eytal mutaliya aruñceyalkaḷi yāṟṟi maṟak kikkiḷi maṇam purital. pārppaṉarkkup perumpoṟkoṭi aḷittal, kuṟṟavāḷikaṭku naṭi viḷakkerittal mutaliya kaṭuntaṇṭam vitittal ivi mutaliyaṉa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.
5. aracar mutaliyavar vaḻakkaṅkaḷ
vaḷamaṉikaḷil vāḻtal. kollāppaṇṭi, civiki mutaliyaṉa vūrtal, aimpula viṉpamum āci tīra nukartal ivi mutaliyaṉa aracar vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.
naṟuñcuṇṇam pūcutal, kiḷi pūvi mutaliyaṉa vaḷarttal, mullik koṭiyi illattil vaḷarttu ataṉ mutaṟpū malarcci yaṉṟu viḻavayartal ivi mutaliyaṉa makaḷir vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.
āṇuṭi yaṇintu uvaḷakak kāval pūṇpatu maṟamakaḷir vaḻakkam.
puḷḷuṅ kuṟiyum (nimittam) kaṇṭu pōrkkup puṟappaṭal, pūciyum paliyumiṭṭut tēric celuttutal, poṟṟakaṭuvāyuḷiṭṭup pōr ceytal ivi mutaliyaṉa maṟavar vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.
kārttiki mātak kārttiki nāḷil viḷakkēṟṟi viḻāvayartal. kaṇiyam pārttal. poṉ toṭṭuc cūḷiṭutal. aracarit teyvamākak karututal, celvariyē matittal, māliyamippu vāyilākak kātalar ceyti yaṟivittuk koḷḷutal ivi mutaliyaṉa irupāṉmakkaḷ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.
6. piḷḷi vaḷarppu muṟi
kāṭi yāṭṭit tarāyccāṟuṅ kaṉṉaṉ maṇiyu naṟuneyyuṅ
kūṭac cempoṉ koḷattēyttuk koṇṭu nāḷum vāyuṟīip
pāṭaṟ kiṉiya pakuvāyuṅ kaṇṇum peruka vukiruṟuttit 
tēṭit tīntēṉ tippilitēyt taṇṇā vuriñci mūkkuyarttār.  (2703)
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ mika iṉpuṟattakkatu.
7. vāḻkki muṟi
tēvar, tuṟavaṟamē ciṟantateṉak koḷḷum aruka matattiṉarā yiṉum, ulakiyaluṇmiyiyum illaṟattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyā miyiyum uṇarnta varātaliṉ, irupālārum maṇavāmalē tuṟakka iyalāviṭattu, mutaṟkaṇ ilvāḻkkiyi naṭātti illaṟa iṉpatti iyaṉṟa aḷavu nukarnta piṉṉarē tuṟavi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ karutti, cīvakaṉum avaṉ tāyum avaṉ tēvimārum vāḻnta vāḻkki muṟiyil vit tum; immiyil tuṟavu kūṭāviṭiṉum ummiyil kavaṉittuk koḷḷalām eṉṉuṅ karuttip piṟar vāḻkki muṟiyil vittum; oru kaṇavaṉukkup pala maṉiviyarirup piṉum. oru maṉivikkup pala kaṇavariruttal ukantataṉṟeṉpati,
tirumaka ṉaruḷap peṟṟut tirunilat tuṟiyu māntar
oruvaṉuk korutti pōla uḷamakiḻn toḷiyiṉ vikip
paruvaru pakiyu nōyum paciyuṅkeṭ ṭoḻiya  (2377)
eṉṉum aṭikaḷil vittum ulakiṟkuṇarttukiṉṟār. oruvaṉ, kaṟpukkālattil ciṟcila kāraṇampaṟṟit taṉ maṉiviyi allatu maṉivi yariviṭṭup piriya nēriṉum. avarik kiviṭak kūṭāteṉpatum, avaroṭu mīṇṭum kūṭi vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉ patum cīvakaṉ vāḻkkiyāl uṇarttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
8. kuṟikkōḷ
maruntit taṉiyāka vuṇṭalum uṇṭiyiṟ kalantuṇpittalum payaṉaḷavil oṉṟēyātal pōla. aṟavitikaḷi aṟanūl vāyilāka aṟivittalum vaṉappunūl vāyilāka aṟivittalum kuṟikkōḷaḷavil oṉṟē. ciṟanta aṟavaṉappu nūlkaḷ kuṟik koḷvatellām aṟam poruḷiṉpa vīṭṭup pēṟē. cintā maṇi oru ciṟanta vaṉappu nūlātaliṉ, ataṉ kaṇṇum nāṉ māṇ poruḷkaḷē naṉmuṟiyil valiyuṟuttap peṟṟuḷa.
kuṭittuṇṇuṅ kūṟṟaṅ kuṭilpiriyā muṉṉē
koṭuttuṇmiṉ kaṇṭīr kuṇampurimiṉ kaṇṭīr  (2620)
nāṇmeykkoṇ ṭīṭṭap paṭṭār
naṭukkuṟu naviyi nīkkal
āṇmakkaḷ kaṭaṉ  (1119)
uṟuvarp pēṇal uvarppiṉmi
uliyā viṉpan taliniṟṟal
aṟivar ciṟappiṟ ketirvirumpal
aḻintōr niṟuttal aṟampakartal
ciṟiyā riṉattuc cērviṉmi
ciṉaṅki viṭutal cerukkavittal
iṟiva ṉaṟattu vārkkellām
iṉiya rātal ituteḷivē.  (2816)
ivi aṟam.
tuṉpattiṟ kalaṅkāmi, naṭpiṟ kulavēṟṟumi kāṭṭāmi mutaliya cila paṇpāṭṭiyalpukaḷum, tēvarāl āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ceykaporuḷ yāruñceṟu vāricceṟu kiṟkum
eḵkupiṟi tilliyirun tēyuyiru muṇṇum
aiyamili yiṉpamaṟa ṉōṭeviyu mākkum
poyyilporu ḷēporuḷmaṟ ṟallapiṟa poruḷē.  (492)
itu poruḷ.
tēvar paṇṇiya tīntoṭi yiṉcuvi
mēvar teṉtamiḻ meypporu ḷātaliṉ 
kōvat taṉṉameṉ cīṟaṭik kompaṉāḷ
pūvar cōli pukuval. eṉ ṟeṇṇiṉāḷ  (1328)
kātalāṟ kāma pūmik katiroḷi yavaru mottār
mātaruṅ kaḷiṟa ṉāṉu mācuṇa makiḻcci maṉṟal
ātaram peruku kiṉṟa vaṉpiṉā laṉṉa mottum
tītilār tiḷippi ṉāmāṉ celvamē peritu mottār  (189)
ivi iṉpam
meyvaki terital ñāṉam; viḷaṅkiya poruḷka ṭammip
poyvaki yiṉṟit tēṟal kāṭci; aim poṟiyum vāṭṭi
uyvaki yuyirit tēyā toḻukuta loḻukkam; mūṉṟum
ivvaki niṟinta pōḻtē iruviṉi kaḻiyum, eṉṟāṉ.  (1436)
itu vīṭu. muttiyilampakattil vīṭupēṟṟippaṟṟi ārukata muṟiyil mika virivākak kūṟukiṉṟār tēvar.
aṟavaḻiyiṟ poruḷīṭṭi ataṉikkoṇṭu aṟavaḻiyil iṉpantuykka vēṇṭu meṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvattēvar koḷ kiyē. tiruttakka tēvar koḷkiyumām. āyiṉ, illaṟat tālum vīṭupēṟuṇ ṭeṉpatu piṉṉavarkku uṭampāṭaṉṟu. aḵtu avar maṉappāṉmiyum matamum paṟṟiyatu.
cintāmaṇi, cilappatikāram pōla uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatum illatum puṇarkkātu. illōṉ talivaṉāka illatum uḷḷatum puṇarttaliṉ, ataṉkaṇ varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇakkūṭa villi. āyiṉum, tēvar kālattut tamiḻnāṭṭu nākarikatti aṟivataṟkut tuṇiyāṉa pala ceytikaḷ, nūl neṭumiyuṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
(3) yāpputtiṟam
veñciṉa vekuḷiyiṟ kuñcara muḻaṅkaliṉ
mañcutam vayiṟaḻin tañcinī rukuttavē  (570)
itu vañcittuṟi. itaṉik kuṟaḷaṭi nāṉkāy vanta koccaka vorupōku eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
maṭamā mayilē kuyilē maḻali
naṭimā ṇaṉamē nalamār kiḷiyē
uṭaṉā ṭumeṉi yaṉiyeṉ ṟurukāt
toṭiyāḻ maḻali moḻicōrn taṉaḷē.  (1526)
itu kali viruttam.
mācit tiṅkaḷ māciṉa ciṉṉat tuṇimuḷḷiṉ
ūcit tuṉṉa mūciya vāṭi yuṭiyākap
pēcip pāvāy picci yeṉakki yakalēntik
kūcik kūci niṟpar koṭuttuṇ ṭaṟiyātār.  (2929)
itu kalittuṟi
karuṅkoṭip puruva mēṟā kayalneṭuṅ kaṇṇum āṭā
aruṅkaṭi miṭaṟum vimmā taṇimaṇi yeyiṟum tōṉṟā
iruṅkaṭaṟ pavaḷac cevvāy tiṟantivaḷ pāṭi ṉāḷō
narampoṭu vīṇi nāviṉ naviṉṟatō eṉṟu nintār  (668)
iḵtu aṟucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam.
maṇkaṉinta poṉmuḻava maḻiyiṉ vimma 
māmaṇiyāḻ tīṅkuḻalka ḷiraṅkap pāṇṭil
paṇkaṉiyap pāvimār pimpoṟ ṟōṭuṅ
kuṇṭalamun tāmpatippa viruntu pāṭa
viṇkaṉiyak kiṇkiṇiyuñ cilampu mārppa
muripuruva vēṉeṭuṅkaṇ viruntu ceyyak
kaṇkaṉiya nāṭakaṅkaṇ ṭamarar kāmak
koḻuntīṉṟu tantavantā makiḻntā raṉṟē.  (3138)

cintinali kiṉṟatiru nīrkkumari yāṭa
... ... ... ... ...
muntinali kiṉṟamutu mūppoḻiyu meṉṟāṉ.  (2020)
iḵtu iraṭṭuṟal. itiyē villiputtūrar tam pāratattil āḷkiṉṟār.
kāḻaka mūṭṭap paṭṭa kāriruṭ ṭuṇiyu moppāṉ
āḻaḷi yuṭumpu paṟṟip paṟittumār poṭuṅki yuḷḷāṉ
vāḻmayirk karaṭi yoppāṉ vāykkili yaṟita lillāṉ
mēḻakak kurali ṉāṉōr vēṭṭuvaṉ ṟalippaṭ ṭāṉē.  (1230)
iḵtu oru collōviyam.
kamparāmāyaṇattiṟ pōla varampiṟanta uyarvu naviṟcikaḷ cintā maṇiyililli. pakuttaṟiviṟkup poruntumāṟu aṟiñari iṉ puṟuttum ariya aḻakiya aṇikaḷiyē tēvar āḷukiṉṟār.
	(5) collārāyccit tuṇi
cintāmaṇic collārāycciyāl aṟiyappaṭum colliyal uṇmi kaḷāvaṉa :
1. coṟkaḷiṉ muntu vaṭivam
aravu (arā), naṟavu (naṟā), kōṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ muntu vaṭivam, muṟiyē, ara, naṟa, kōvaṉ eṉapaṉa.
ivviruntāṉ (ivviṭattiruntāṉ) eṉṉum vaḻakku. cuṭṭiṭic coṟkaḷ ātiyil peyarum īreccamumākap payaṉpaṭṭati uṇarttum.
kātaṟ poruḷi uṇarttum mātar eṉṉum colliṉ pakuti mātu eṉpatu, mātiyāḻ eṉa varutal kāṇka.
2. vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷ
ilakkittu (eḻuti) eṉṉuñcol. ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ pakutiyāṉa ilakku eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭu ilakki eṉa oru viṉi paṇṭikkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉamiyi uṇarttum.
poṟṟa (nalla), poṟṟatu (polivu peṟṟatu) eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ. pol eṉṉum vaḻakkaṟṟa viṉiyaṭiyāyp piṟanta peyareccamum. viṉimuṟṟumākum. kal, vil eṉṉum viṉi yaṭiyāka, kaṟṟa kaṟṟatu viṟṟa viṟṟatu eṉṉum peyarecca viṉimuṟṟukaḷ piṟattalik kāṇka.
pollutal = polital. aḻakāyiruttal. pol - poṉ. pol - polam. pol - poli - polivu. pol + pu = poṟpu. polam - poṉam.
poṟpu eṉṉum uriccol. poṟṟa eṉat tirintateṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu poruntātu. uriccol eṉpatu ceyyuṭ collē yaṉṟi ilakkaṇa vakic col aṉṟu. poṟpu eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar ceyyuḷiṟ ciṟappākap payiṉṟu uriccollāyiṟṟu.
3. pulavar coṟṟirippu
pulavar coṟkaḷit tam ceyyuṭkaḷil mōṉiyetukik kēṟpat tirittuk koḷvar eṉpatu, kampalam (kampali), aḷamaru (alamaru). iṟuvari (iruvari), piḷiṟṟal (piliṟṟal), mattam (mattu). poṉam (polam) mutaliya coṟkaḷāṟ peṟappaṭum.
4. tolkāppiya vaḻu
num eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu nīyir eṉṉum muṉṉilip paṉmip peyar tirintatākat tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatu paṟṟi. nīm eṉṉum cintāmaṇic collit tēvar paṭippākac cila pulavar karutukiṉṟaṉar.
nīm eṉṉuñ collē muntiyatām. nīṉ, nīm nīṅkaḷ (nīm + kaḷ) eṉpaṉa muṉṉili yorumi paṉmip peyarkaḷ. nīṉ, nīm eṉṉum coṟkaḷ iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭil nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷiṭi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kaṉṉaṭattil. nīṉ eṉpatu tiri yātum. nīm eṉpatu nīvu (nīm-nīmu-nīvu) eṉat tirintum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa.
num eṉpatu (iṟantupaṭṭa) nūm eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumi yaṭi yākum.
taṉmi muṉṉilip peyarkaḷum paṭarkkip patiṟpeyar kaḷum, eḻuvāy vaṭivil neṭiṉmutalavē aṉṟik kuṟiṉ mutal alla.
5. vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkum teṉcoṟkaḷ
kōṭṭi = oruvaroṭu kūṭa iruttal. koḷḷutal = poruntutal. kūṭutal. koḷuttutal = poruttutal. koḷ - kōḷ = kuli (kāyiṉ kūṭṭam). kōḷ + ti = kōṭṭi (kūṭṭam, avi).
ilakki eṉṉuñcol muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. ituvē vaṭamoḻiyil lēk eṉṟum, intiyil lik eṉṟum tiriyum.
6. nūlaḷavi
oru tuṟiyil pala nūlkaḷiruppiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭē aḷavi nūlākum. tirukkuṟaṭ karuttuka ḷaṉittiyun taḻuvic col māṟṟi oruvar oru putunūl iyaṟṟiviṭiṉ. atu mutaliyaṉmi peṟṟu viṭātu. tēvar kālattil, pāviṉa yāppil cintāmaṇikku accukkaṭṭāka allatu vaḻikāṭṭi yāka oru vaṉappu nūlum iruntatākat teriyavilli. tolkāppiyar kāla vaṉappukkaḷellām kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē iṟantu paṭṭaṉa. tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷō, vaḻuttu nūṟkaḷ. tirukkōvi taliciṟanta pāviṉa nūlāyiṉum. yāppiṉum poruḷiṉum oru tuṟip paṭṭatu. ākavē, tēvar, tam matiyit tolkāppiyat tāl tulakkit tirukkuṟaḷāl tīṭṭip pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭut toki, patiṉeṇkīḻk kaṇakkāl pārittiruppiṉum, vaṉappuk kaliyip poṟuttamaṭṭil, cintāmaṇi yāppiṉ perum pakuti avaratu tiṟappāṭṭiṉ viḷivē eṉpatu tiṇṇam. kamparāmāyaṇam uṭpaṭṭa piṟkāla vaṉappukkellām. cintāmaṇi ciṟanta vaḻikāṭṭiyā yiruntateṉpati evarum maṟukka muṭiyātu.
cintāmaṇiyip piṉpaṟṟiya piṉṉaruṅkūṭak kalittiṟattil vaṟumip paṭṭum, aṇittiṟattil aḻakiḻantum, tīrppiyalil naṭuttiṟampiyum piṟkāla nūlkaḷ polivaṟṟiruppatu, cintā maṇiyi mēṉmēluñ ciṟappikkum.
cutañcaṇaṉ ceytiyum kaluḻavēkaṉ ceytiyum tēviyaṟ paṭṭa vāyiṉum, matattuṟi cārntavātalāṉum, cilappatikāra maṇi mēkaliyiṉuḷḷum attakiya ceyti kāṇappaṭutalāṉum, illōṉ talivaṉākavuḷḷa puṉiyiyal vaṉappiṟku ētum iḻukkilli eṉka. (cintāmaṇic coṟpoḻivu niṉivu
malar 1952.)
cintu
icit tamiḻp pāṭṭu vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu aṭikkuṟumi paṟṟic cintu eṉappeyar peṟum. ac cintu vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu neṭiyatum kuṟiyatumāka īraṭi koṇṭuḷḷamiyālum noṇṭi noṇṭic celvatu pōṉṟa ōciyuṭimi yālum noṇṭic cintu eṉappaṭum.  (col 10).
cintu eṉṉum peyar
āriyar varumuṉṉar tamiḻarē vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟi yiruntami yālum, kumarimali muḻuki arapikkaṭal tōṉṟiyapiṉ, cintuveḷi vaḻiyākavē tamiḻar allatu tiraviṭar mēliyā ciyāviṟkum ataṉpiṉ airōppāviṟkum ceṉṟiruppa rātalālum, cintu eṉṉum āṟṟuppeyar tamiḻar iṭṭa tamiḻppeya rākavē yirukkalām.
cintutal citaṟutal. citaṟutal nīrpporuḷiyum kaṭṭip poruḷiyum ciṟuciṟu pakutiyāka vīḻttutal, maḻi tuḷi tuḷiyākap peytaliṉ, maḻi peytal tuḷi citaṟutal eṉṟu collappaṭum.
tāḻiruḷ tumiya miṉṉit taṇṇeṉa
vīḻuṟi yiṉiya citaṟi yūḻiṟ
kaṭippiku muraciṉ muḻaṅki yiṭittiṭittup
peytiṉi vāḻiyō peruvāṉ  (kuṟun. 120)
eṉpatu mukil tuḷi citaṟutaliyum,
aruvi yaṉṉa paruvuṟi citaṟi
yāṟuniṟi pakaru nāṭaṉi  (kuṟun. 121)
eṉpatu āṟu tuḷi citaṟutaliyum, kuṟittaṉa.
mukil tuḷi cintiyē makkaṭkuk kīḻnīrum mēlnīrum āṟṟunīrum kiṭittalāl, cintu eṉṉuñ coṟku nīr (piṅ.), āṟu (piṅ.) eṉṉum iruporuḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, cintutal eṉṉum viṉiccoṟkut teḷittal eṉpata ṉoṭu oḻukutal eṉṉum poruḷu miruttalāl, cintu eṉṉuñ col ōr āṟṟiṉ peyarāvataṟku muṟṟum porutta māṉatē. vaṭamoḻiyil iccoṟku mūlamilli. māṉiyar uvilliyamcu tam camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutaliyil, oru kāl cit (Sidh) eṉpatu mūlamā yirukkalām eṉṟu aiyuṟavākak kuṟippar. accoṟkuc cellutal (to go) eṉṉum poruḷē uḷḷatu. mēlum, irukku vētattil cintāṟṟiṟkuc caracuvati yeṉṉum peyarē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭiruppatākat terikiṉṟatu. piṟkālattil āriyar piramavarttam ceṉṟa piṉṉarē, annilap pakutikku mēlelliyākavum caṭṭilecu eṉṉum kiḷi yāṟṟiṟkut tuṇik kiḷiyākavum irunta, ciṟṟāṟṟiṟku appeyari iṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. atu iṉṟu curcūti (Sursooty) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
paḻampāracīkariṉ corāttiriya (Zoroastrian) vētamākiya centu avettāvil (Zend-Avesta), hapta hintava eṉpatu akuramattāviṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭa patiṉāṟu teyvaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭiruppataṉālum, harakviti (Haraquaiti) eṉpatu ōr āpukkāṉiya āṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppataṉālum, pala teyvaṅkaḷ, ciṟappāka akkiṉi, corāttiriyarkkum  irukku vētiyarkkum potuvāyiruppataṉālum, avviru vakuppārum orukālattiṟ cintuveḷiyil ōriṉattārum orē matattārumāy vāḻntiruntu, piṉṉar muṉṉavar mēṟkum piṉṉavar kiḻakkumākap pirintu pōyiṉar eṉpatu, uyt tuṇarap paṭum.
cakaram allatu sakaram pāracīkar vāyil hakaramākat tiriyum.
e-ṭu: cintu (sintu) - hintu
		saptaṉ (ēḻu) - haptaṉ
		acuraṉ (asūra) - ahūra
immuṟiyilēyē, saptasintu eṉpatu haptahintu eṉṟum, saravati eṉpatu harakviti eṉṟum, tirintuḷḷaṉa.
itaṉāl, cintāṟu orukālattil eḻukiḷikaḷ koṇṭirunta tākat terikiṉṟatu. cintāṟṟut tēvikku ēḻ uṭaṉ piṟantāṇ mār (sisters) eṉṟa irukkuvētiyar koḷkiyiṉālum itu valiyuṟum.
iṉi, cila teyva vēṟupāṭṭiṉālum koḷki māṟupāṭṭi ṉālum, corāttiriyariṉ muṉṉōr pakimi koṇṭē pirintu pōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. centu avettāviṟ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷa ahūra matā irukku vētattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭavilli. corāttiriyar acurari nallavarākavum tēvarit tīyavarākavuṅ koṇṭaṉar. intiya āriyar karuttu itaṟku muraṇāṉatu.
cintūram
cintūram - sintūra
centūḷ = cempoṭi, cennīṟu.
centūḷ - centūḷam - centūram - cintūram = 1. cennīṟu.
cintūrat tātu koṭuttilarēl  (upatēcakā. uruttirā. 67)
2. cempoṭi. 
matakariyic cintūra mappiyapōl  (kamparā. mitilik. 151)
3. civappu (piṅ.). 4. ceṅkuṭi. (piṅ.). 5. cempuḷḷiyuḷḷa yāṉi mukam 6. yāṉi. (piṅ.). 7. cemmalar pūkkum veṭci maram. 8. cēṅ koṭṭi. 9. cevvīyam.
cintūram - cinturam = 1. civappu 
cinturac cēvaṭiyāṉ  (tiruvāca. 18 : 5).
2. cempoṭi.
cinturamilaṅkat taṉ tiruneṟṟimēl  (tiv. periyāḻ. 3:4. 6).
3. neṟṟic cempoṭṭu. 
cintura vātitta vittāramuṭiyār  (kantaran. 5).
4. pukarmuka yāṉi  5. puḷiyamaram.
pukarmukam (cempuḷḷiyuḷḷa mukam) eṉpatu ciṉiyāku peyarāy yāṉiyik kuṟippatu pōṉṟē, cintūram, cinturam eṉṉum coṟkaḷum yāṉiyik kuṟikkum.
cintūrappoṭṭu = kuṅkumappoṭṭu.
cintam, cintakam, cinturam, cintūram eṉpaṉa puḷiyiṉ cenniṟam paṟṟip puḷiyamarattiyuṅ kuṟikkum.  (va.va. 152-153).
cintūram - cintiṭī (c), tintiṭa = puḷiyamaram
cintūrittal = tātukkaḷi (ulōkaṅkaḷi)c cintūramākkutal. 
curukkuk koṭuttatic cintūrittu  (paṇavīṭu 230)
cintūri - cinturi.
ivviṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyililli.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam yant (allatu yat) = iyaṅku, oḻuku, pāy, ōṭu.
ceṉṉip pa.ka.ka.ta.a. cintam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkuc ciñccā (cinca# = puḷiyamaram) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭum.  (va.va. 153).
cillāṅ kuccu viḷiyāṭṭu
pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cillāṅkuccu eṉṉum oru ciṟu kucci oru kōlāl taṭṭi āṭum āṭṭu cillāṅkuccu eṉappaṭum. ippeyar ciṟupāṉmi cīyāṅkuccu eṉavum maruvi vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki : ivviḷiyāṭṭi āṭak kuṟintatu iruvar vēṇṭum. palarāyiṉ uttikaṭṭic camatokiyāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintu koḷvar.
āṭu karuvi : iruviral mutal aṟuviral vari nīḷamum, iruviral mutal nālviral vari cuṟṟaḷavum, oru nuṉiyil kūrmiyum, uḷḷa ōr uruṇṭi kuccum; oru muḻam mutal irumuḻam vari (avaravar kikkēṟṟavāṟu) nīḷamum, iruviral mutal nālviral vari cuṟṟaḷavum, oru taliyiṟ ciṟitu kūrmiyum, uḷḷa ōr uruṇṭa kōlum; iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. perum pālum kuccum kōlum orē cuṟṟaḷaviṉavāka irukkum. kōlik kampu eṉpar.
āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum, ovvōr iṇi yarkkum ov voru kōluṅ kuccum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. cila camiyaṅkaḷil ovvoruvaṉum tāṉ tāṉ payiṉṟa allatu taṉ taṉ kikkēṟṟa karuvikaḷit taṉit taṉi vittukkoḷvatu muṇṭu.
kucci mēṉōkkiya cāyvāka vittu ataṉ kūr nuṉiyiṟ kōlāl taṭṭi yeḻuppumāṟu, kucciṟ pātiyaḷavu nīḷamum ari viral mutal oruviral vari āḻamum uḷḷa oru ciṟu paḷḷam nilattiṟ killappaṭum. kuccip paḷḷattil vit tirukkum pōtu, ataṉ aṭippakkam (allatu moṭṭip pakkam) paḷḷattilum, ataṉ nuṉip pakkam (allatu kūriya pakkam) veḷiyilum irukkum.
āṭiṭam : arippaṭiccāl (½ furlong) catura allatu nīḷcatura nilappakutiyilāvatu, paranta veḷi nilattilāvatu iv āṭṭu āṭappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum, āṭumuṟi yoṉṟē. iruvarāyiṉ, iruvarum orē kuḻiyil muṉpiṉṉāka vāvatu, vevvēṟu kuḻiyil uṭaṉikaḻvākavāvatu, tam kucci vittu nuṉiyiṟ kōlāl taṭṭiyeḻuppi, atu nilattil viḻu muṉ aṭittu iyaṉṟa tolivu pōkkuvar. yār kuccu mikat toliviṟpōy viḻuntatō avaṉ muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. eḻumpiya kuccu nilattil viḻumuṉ ati aṭikkat tavaṟiṉum, kuṟinta tolivu kuccip pōkkiṉum, pintiyāṭal vēṇṭum.
viḷiyāṭṭit toṭaṅkupavaṉ, muṉ coṉṉavāṟu kucci yeḻuppiyaṭittu iyaṉṟa tolivu pōkkuvaṉ. eḻumpiya kuccu nilattil viḻumuṉ aṭikkat tavaṟiṉ aṭuttavaṉ aṭikka vēṇṭum. yār aṭippiṉum kucciyaṭittup pōkkiyapiṉ, eṭuttaṭikkum niliyil kōlik kuḻiyarukē kiṭatti niṟpiṉ.
iṉṉoruvaṉ (etiri), tolivil, kuccuppōkum ticiyilum atu viḻakkūṭiya iṭattilum niṉṟukoṇṭiruntu, atu nilattil viḻumuṉ atippiṭikka muyalvāṉ. piṭittuviṭiṉ, avaṉ kuccaṭippavaṉākavum, muṉpu aṭittavaṉ ati eṭuppava ṉākavum, māṟavēṇṭum. piṭikka muṭiyāviṭiṉ, kuccu nilattil viḻuntavuṭaṉ atiyeṭuttu, aṭittavaṉ kuḻiyarukē kiṭatti yirukkum kōliṟpaṭumpaṭi yeṟiyavēṇṭum. kōliṟ paṭṭu viṭiṉ, aṉṟum iruvar nilimiyum māṟum. paṭāviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ virivāyk kucci yeṭuttu ati muṉ pōl aṭittup pōkkuvāṉ. avaṉ ati aṭikkumuṉ kucceṭuppa vaṉ vēkamāy ōṭivantu avaṉit toṭṭuviṭiṉ, aṉṟum, iruvar nilimiyum māṟum. toṭāviṭiṉ, kucceṭuttavaṉ muṉpōṟ kucceṭukka vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam, iruvarum virumpiya vari toṭarntu āṭuvar. aṭitta kucci eṭut teṟi talukku eṭuttūṟṟutal eṉṟu peyar.
kuccaṭikkiṟavaṉ evvakiyilum tavaṟātum piṭikoṭātum aṭippiṉ, viḷiyāṭṭi niṟuttumvari ettaṉi muṟiyum toṭarntu aṭikkalām.

pala iṇiyar cērntu āṭiṉ, toṭaṅkuṅ kaṭciyi tīrmāṉik kum āṭṭattil kucci mikat toliviṟ pōkkiya kaṭciyār muntiyāṭuvar; itaṟku, oru kaṭciyār aṉivarum mikat toliviṟ pōkka vēṇṭum eṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. avaruḷ oruvar pōkkiṉum pōtum, muntiyāṭuṅ kaṭciyār aṭippārum pintiyāṭuṅ kaṭciyār eṭuppārumāy, āṭṭan toṭaṅkum.
aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyāruḷ ovvoruvaṉum aṭikkuṅ kucci, av vavaṉuṭaṉ uttikaṭṭiya etirkkaṭci iṇiñaṉē eṭuppāṉ. iṇiñaṉukku uttiyāḷ eṉṟu peyar. aṭitta kucci uttiyāḷ uṭaṉē vantu eṭukkāviṭiṉ, aṭittavaṉ atit toṭarntu aṭittu, mikat toliviṟ pōkkuvatumuṇṭu. eṭukkuṅ kaṭciyār ellāruṅ kuccukkaḷi yeṟinta piṉputāṉ, aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyār orē camiyattil mīṇṭum aṭippar.
tavaṟum vaciyum tavaṟātu āṭum vakiyum, iruvar āṭiṉum iru kaṭciyār āṭiṉum oṉṟē. ovvoruvaṉākavō oruṅkēyō aṭikkum kaṭciyār aṉivarum toliyum vari (atāvatu tōṟkum vari), etirk kaṭci nilimi māṟātu āyiṉ, aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyār toliyat toliya, ava ruṭiya iṇiñarāṉa etirk kaṭciyār niṉṟukoṇṭē varuvar. aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyiṉ iṟutiyāḷun tolinta piṉ, iru kaṭciyum viṉimāṟum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : iv viḷiyāṭṭu vēṭṭi viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. kuṟiñci nilattiluḷḷa kuṟavarā yiṉum, kuṟiñci nilattilum pāli nilattilumuḷḷa vēṭarāyiṉum, piṟarāyiṉum, kāṭṭiluḷḷa kōḻi muyal mutaliya orucār uyir kaḷik kuṟuntaṭi koṇṭē yeṟintu kolluvatu vaḻakkam. ivvaḻakkattiṉiṉṟē kōḻiyaṭikkak kuṟuntaṭi vēṇṭumā? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum eḻuntuḷatu.
oru kuṟavaṉum avaṉ kiyāḷāṉa ciṅkaṉ eṉṉuṅ kuṟavaṉum kuṟuntaṭikoṇṭu vēṭṭiyāṭiṉ, kuṟavaṉ putar putarāyk kuṟuntaṭiyāl taṭṭip pārppāṉ. oru putariṉiṉṟu tiṭumeṉṟu oru kāṭṭuk kōḻi paṟakkum; allatu oru muyal kutikkum. an noṭiyē, kuṟavaṉ atik kuṟuntaṭiyāl aṭippāṉ; allatu eṟivāṉ. kōḻi allatu muyal aṭipaṭṭuc caṟṟut toliviṟ pōy viḻum. kuṟavaṉ ōṭippōy ati eṭuttu varuvāṉ. vēṟu nilattiṉiṉṟu iruvar vēṭṭiyāṭac celliṉum ivvakiyē nērum.
ittaki vēṭṭi viṉiyiyē cillāṅ kuccuk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. kucci yaṭippavaṉ atit taṭṭi yeḻuppuvatu, kuṟavaṉ putarit taṭṭik kōḻi muyali yeḻuppuvatu pōṉṟatu. kucci mīṇṭum aṭippatu, avvuyirikaḷik kuṇilāl aṭippatum eṟivatum pōṉṟatu. kuccu toliviṟpōy viḻuvatu, aṭipaṭṭa vuyirikaḷ toliviṟpōy viḻuvatu pōṉṟatu. kucci eṭuppavaṉ ati eṭutteṟivatu, aṭipaṭṭu viḻunta vuyirikaḷik kuṟavaṉ eṭut teṟivatu allatu eṭuttu varuvatu pōṉṟatu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : mēleḻum oru poruḷi virintu kuṟitappātu vaṉmiyāy aṭippatum; vāṉiṉṟu viḻum poruḷi atu takāta iṭattil viḻu muṉṉum, tāḻappaṟakkum paṟaviyi atu taṉṉiviṭṭuk kaṭakkumuṉṉum piṭippatum; toliviluḷḷa poruḷik kuṟitappātu oru karuviyāl aṭippatum; oru poruḷit toliviluḷḷa kuṟitta iṭattiṟku eṟivatum; oru kuṟitta iṭattiṟku virintu ōṭuvatum; ākiya viṉip payiṟciyē iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.



(2) cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu muṟi
I. killittāṇṭu
pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc cillāṅkuccum cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭuk killittāṇṭum oṉṟē. āyiṉum, iṭavēṟu pāṭu kāraṇamāka, īriṭa āṭṭiṟkum piṉvarumāṟu cila vēṟṟumikaḷ uḷa.
1. peyar : cillāṅkuccu eṉpatu killi eṉṟum, kampu eṉpatu tāṇṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭil, kucceṭuppavaṉ kuccu nilattil viḻumuṉ ati antarattiṟ piṭittuk koḷvataṟku oru ciṟappup peyarum vaḻaṅkavilli. cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷil atu uttam allatu puṭṭam eṉap peyarpeṟum, itu kīḻvarum kiṭṭip puḷḷiṟkum okkum.
eṭuttu ūṟṟutal eṉṉum pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk kuṟiyīṭṭiṟku nērāṉa cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭuk kuṟiyīṭu kañci vārttal eṉpatākum.
2. karuvi : cillāṅkuccu oru kaṭiyil maṭṭuṅ kūrāyirukkum. āyiṉ, killi irukaṭiyuṅ, kūrāyirukkum. itu irumuṉiyum aṭittaṟku vacatiyām.
3. muṟi : iru kaṭciyār āṭuvatāyiṉ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭil aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyār aṉivarum orē camiyattil aṭippar. avaṉavaṉ aṭikkuṅ kucci avaṉavaṉ uttiyāḷē eṭuppaṉ. cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭilō, aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉē 
oru camiyattil aṭippaṉ. avaṉ aṭitta kucci etirk kaṭciyār ellārum āṭukaḷattil niṉṟu avaruḷ yārēṉum piṭikkalām allatu eṭukkalām. aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ tolinta piṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ āṭuvaṉ. iṅṅaṉam ovvoruvaṉāka ellārun tolintapiṉ ōr āṭṭi oruvāṟu muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉam āṭuvatu kirikkaṭṭu (cricket) eṉṉum āṅkila viḷiyāṭṭi orupuṭi yottiruppatāl, iv āṭṭi intiyak kirikkaṭṭu eṉa nakic cuviyākak kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
pāṇṭināṭṭil, aṭikkappaṭṭa kuccup pōyviḻuntolivi aḷantu atukoṇṭu veṟṟiyik kaṇippatilli. aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyar aṉivarun tolintapiṉ, etirkkaṭciyār āṭal vēṇṭum. cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭilō, tāṇṭi aḷavu kōlāka vittuk koṇṭu, ovvoru muṟiyum aṭikkap paṭṭa kuccup pōyviḻun tolivik karuvikoṇṭō matippākavō aḷaviṭṭu, kuṟitta toki vantavuṭaṉ etirk kaṭciyārik kattik kāvaṭi, yeṭukkac ceyvar. kuṟitta toki varuvataṟku ettuṇippēr aṭittaṉarō, attuṇippērum kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu kucci mummūṉṟu taṭavi toṭarntaṭittup pōkkiya toliviliruntu, etirkkaṭciyār kattik kāvaṭi kavāṉak kāvaṭi eṉṟu iṭiviṭāmal maṭakki maṭakkic collikkoṇṭu kuḻivari varutal vēṇṭum. ituvē kattik kāvaṭi yeṭuttal eṉpatu. kattik kāvaṭiyeṭukkum pōtu iṭiyiṟ colli niṟuttiviṭṭāl, muṉpu pōkkiya toliviṉiṉṟu mīṇṭum kucci muṉpōl aṭittuppōkki, atu viḻunta iṭattiliruntu maṟupaṭiyum kattik kāvaṭi yeṭukkac ceyvar. atiyeṭuttu muṭintapiṉ, muṉpu aṭitta varē tirumpavum āṭuvar. ākavē, kattik kāvaṭi yeṭuttal tōṟṟavarkku vitikkum oruvakit taṇṭaṉi yeṉappaṭum.
mutalil aṭitta kaṭciyār aṉivarum aṭittapiṉpum kuṟitta toki varāviṭiṉ, avar tōṟṟavarāvar. āyiṉ, avar kattik kāvaṭiyeṭukka vēṇṭiyatilli. aṭutta āṭṭiyil etirk kaṭciyār aṭittal vēṇṭum. ituvē attōlviyiṉ viḷi vām. ākavē, kattik kāvaṭit taṇṭaṉi eṭukkuṅ kaṭciyārk kaṉṟi aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārkkilli yeṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭil eṭukkuṅ kaṭciyārkku av eṭuttalēyaṉṟi vēṟōru taṇṭaṉiyumilli.
II. kiṭṭippuḷ
kiṭṭippuḷ eṉpatu killittāṇṭiṉ maṟṟoru vakiyē. iraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭāvaṉa :
peyar : killi eṉpatu puḷ eṉṟum, tāṇṭu eṉpatu kiṭṭi eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.
muṟi : killittāṇṭil, kuccu kuḻiyiṉ nīṭṭup pōkkil vittuk kōlāl taṭṭiyeḻuppi yaṭikkap peṟum, kiṭṭip puḷḷilō, kuccuk kuḻiyiṉ kuṟukkē vittu ataṟku naṭṭuk kuṟukkāka oru kōlip piṭittu atu kiyiṉāl taṭṭi yeṭukkappeṟum. killittāṇṭil, aṭikkappaṭṭa kucci eṭuppavaṉ eṭutteṟintapiṉ, ati aṭippavaṉ ennili yilum aṭikkalām. āyiṉ, kiṭṭippuḷḷil, atu iyakkattil (acivil) irukkum pōtē ati aṭittal vēṇṭum. acivu niṉṟu kiṭiyāyk kiṭantapiṉ, ati aṭittal kūṭātu. aṭippiṉ, aṭittavaṉ tavaṟiyavaṉāvaṉ. ataṉāl, eṭutteṟintavaṉ aṭikkavēṇṭiyirukkum iyaṅkuṅ kuccu naṭṭukku niṟkumpōtu aṭikkappaṭiṉ, atu killikkuttu eṉappaṭum.
kuccup pōm tolivi aḷappatum kattikkāvaṭi eṭuppatum kiṭṭippuḷḷil illi. piṟavakiyil itu perumpālum cillāṅkucci ottirukkum.
perumpālum ciṟuvarē, avaruḷḷum iruvarē, ivviḷi yāṭṭi āṭuvar. kōlāl kuccit taṭṭi yeḻuppi aṭikka muṭiyāta iḷañciṟārkkeṉa iv āṭṭu eḻuntatākak kūṟuvar. cēranāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭil koṭṭip puḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum āṭṭu, cillāṅkuccic cērntatē.
cilli
cilli - jilli (jh) = ciḷvaṇṭu
cilliṭutal = olittal, cilleṉal = uccamākak kūrntolittal. 
cilli cilleṉ ṟollaṟāta  (tiv. periyati. 1:7:9).
cil = ciḷ. cil - cilli - cilliki.  cil - cilli. (piṅ.).
cilluṟu - cillūṟu = ciḷvaṇṭu.
cilliki - jillikā (jh) = ciḷvaṇṭu  (va.va. 153).
cila araciyaṟ karuttukkaḷ
paḻantamiḻaracarum pulavarum potumakkaḷum, piṉ varumāṟu cila araciyaṟ karuttakkaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉar.
1.	aracaṉ ulakattiṟku uyir.
2.	teyvam, tāṉ, taṉ viṉiñar, kaḷvar, pakivar, vilaṅku ākiya aṟuvaḻiyil vanta tīṅkukaḷi aracaṉ nīkkit taṉ kuṭikaḷik kāttal vēṇṭum.
3.	aracaṉ toḻilkaḷ, ōtal vēṭṭal ītal kāttal taṇṭittal eṉa aintu.
4.	aracaveṟṟikku aṟaneṟiyāṭci kāraṇam.
5.	aracaveṟṟikku uḻavut toḻil kāraṇam.
6.	ceṅkōlaracaṉ nāṭṭil maḻivaḷam poḻiyum; makkaḷ oḻukkan tappār; vilaṅku ūṟu ceyyātu; kalviyum aṟamun taḻikkum. koṭuṅkōl nāṭṭil ivi nikaḻā.
7.	koṭuṅkōl aracaṉ āḷum nāṭṭiṉum, kaṭumpuli vāḻuṅ kāṭu naṉṟu.
8.	kōṉnili tiriyiṉ kōḷnili tiriyum.
9.	aracaṉ iṟappik kōḷnili muṉṉaṟivikkum.,
10.	mātavar nōṉpum maṭavār kaṟpum, kāvalaṉ kāval iṉṟeṉil illi.
11.	makkaḷi naṉṉeṟip paṭuttuvatu aracaṉ kaṭami.
12.	maḻi peyyāmikkum viḷivuk kuṟiviṟkum iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa nikaḻccikkum, aracaṉ kāraṇam.
13.	allaṟpaṭṭaḻuta kuṭikaḷiṉ kaṇṇīr aracaṉi aḻittu viṭum.
14.	aṟam piḻitta aracaṉukku aṟamē kūṟṟamākum.
15.	kuṭi paḻitūṟṟutal aracaṉukku mika iḻivu.
16.	aracar teyvaṅkaḷi ikaḻntāl avar nāṭṭiṟku aḻivu varum.
17.	aracarāl avamatikkappaṭṭa muṉivarum pulavarum capikkiṉ aracum nakarum aḻiyum.
18.	teyvaṅkaṭkup paṭikkiṉum paliyiṭiṉum maḻi peytu nāṭu ceḻikkum.
19.	araca vāḻkki tuṉpam niṟintatu (ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ karuttu).
20.	nāṭukaḷiṟ ciṟantatu tamiḻnāṭu (tamiḻaracar karuttu).
21.	muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pāṇṭi nāṭu (pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ karuttu).
22.	araciṉmiyum vēṟṟaracum nāṭṭuk kuṟṟamākum.
23.	aracaṉ eppaṭi, kuṭikaḷ appaṭi.
24.	aracaṉ kuṟṟam kuṭikaḷiyum kuṭikaḷ kuṟṟam aracaṉiyum cārum.
25.	aracaṉ piṟanāṭukaḷi velvatu kuṇamēyaṉṟik kuṟṟamaṉṟu.
26.	oruvaṉ ceyta allatu ceyvitta kolikku avaṉiṭattu maṭṭumiṉṟi avaṉ kulattāriṭattum paḻi vāṅkalām. atu oru teyvattiṉ kōpattit taṇippatākak karutappaṭiṉ.
27.	koḷḷic cottum paṟimutalum kōyiṟkuc cērkkap peṟiṉ tītilli.
28.	pōril ceyta tīviṉikaṭkuk kōyiṟṟirup paṇikaḷ kaḻuvāy.
29.	cittarum, muṉivarum pōṉṟa periyārit tuṇikkōṭal aracarkkaraṇ ceyyum; avarip piḻittal avarukku aḻivu tarum.  (pa.ta.nā.pa.)
cilanti
uṭampil talikkuk kīḻ eṅkēṉum puṟappaṭum koppuḷam cilantip pūcciyiṉ uṭalpōl tiraṇṭu iruttalāl cilanti eṉappeyar peṟṟatu.
(oruvakik kaṭṭiyi āṅkilattil Cancer eṉṟum Canker eṉṟum aḻippar. atu naṇṭu pōl iruttaliṉ ippeyar peṟṟatu.
L. Cancer, Crab, Cancer. Tumour named from Swollen veins like crab’s limbs - C.O.D.]  (col. 9).
cilampu kaḻi nōṉpu
vatuvikku muṉ maṇamakaḷ kālil avaḷ peṟṟōrāl aṇiyappaṭṭirunta cilampi nīkkutaṟku oru caṭaṅku ceyyap paṭum. atu cilampu kaḻi nōṉpu eṉappaṭum.  (ta.ti.7).
cili
uruvam, cili, paṭimi eṉṉum oruporuḷ muccoṟkaḷuḷ mutalatum īṟṟatum aiyuṟavaṟṟa tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ.
uruvam
uru eṉṉum vaṭivum uruvattik kuṟikkum.
urukkal = kōvil kaṭṭiyavaruṭiya kaṟcili. itu kaṭṭaṭak kali vaḻakku.
uruvam = cili (piratimi).
uruvāram = cili (piratimi) itu ulaka vaḻakku.
uruvam eṉpatu rūpa eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉpar. itu ēmāṟṟu.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal.
uru = tōṟṟam. vaṭivam, vaṭivuṭip poruḷ, paṭimi.
uru - uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumi vaṭivam; atik kuṟikkum eḻuttu allatu aci allatu col.
uruvam eṉṟa teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil rūpa eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
paṭimi
paṭi = oppu, vaki, uruvam (cīvaka. 1156) appaṭi = atupōl. avvaki, orupaṭiyāy (vakiyāy) varukiṟatu. oru vaṭiyāy eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
paṭiyōli = mūla motta ōli.
paṭi = prati (va.) itiyum prati > paṭi eṉat talikīḻāyk kāṭṭi ēmāṟṟuvar vaṭavar.
paṭi - paṭimi - pratimā (va.) - patumi (taṟpavam).
paṭimi - paṭimam = nōṉpuk kōlam, tavavaṭivam.
(paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam; paṭivu - vaṭivu; paṭivam - vaṭivam)
cili
cili eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyil kal allatu pāṟi eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. Silex eṉṉum ilattīṉ collum. ataṉiṉṟu tirinta silica eṉṉum āṅkilac collum vaṉkalli (second stone) yaṉṟi uruvac ciliyik kuṟiyā. nāmtāṉ atik karuvi yākupeyarāy uruvam eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
cilā cācaṉam = kallil eḻutappaṭṭa kaṭṭaḷi. kaṟpaṭṭayam. vikkirakam, mūrttam eṉpaṉavē uruvattik kuṟikkum vaṭa coṟkaḷ. mūrttam uṭiyavaṉ mūrtti. pratimā eṉpatu teṉcoṟṟiripu.
nām kaṟcili eṉpatupōl vaṭavar cilācilā eṉṉa muṭiyātu iruppuccili, veḷḷiccili, ceppuccili, poṟcili, veṇkalac cili eṉṟu uruvapporuḷiltāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. kaṟporuḷil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatilli teyva uruvam teyvaccili eṉappaṭum.
vaṭamoḻiyil kal eṉṉum poruṭkum vēr allatu mutaṉili illi. māṉiyar villiyamcu tam akaramutaliyil orukāl ci mūlamāyirukkalām eṉpar. ci eṉpataṟku nalku. pontiki (tirupti)p paṭuttu. kūrākku eṉṉum poruḷkaḷtām uṇṭu. avi poruntā. tamiḻilum tiṭṭamākavō, tēṟṟamākavō mūlaṅkāṭṭutaṟ killi. cili eṉṉuñ coṟkuc cil eṉpatu tāṉ mūlamāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cil = tuṇṭu. vaṭṭam. vaṭṭapporuḷ.
cilleṉal = kuḷirtal, olittal.
cil - cili = vaḷinta vil, olikkum etiroli.
cil - cilampu = olikkum kaḻal (paral iṭappaṭṭa kālaṇi).
etiroliyiṭum mali, olikkum cilampak kaḻi (cilampu - cilampam).
cilittal = olittal, muḻaṅkutal, āravārittal - malipaṭu kaṭām. ikkaruttatu. pāṭumpōtu etirolittalic cili yōṭutal eṉpar nelli nāṭṭār.
cil eṉpatu vaṭṭam eṉṉum karuttil cul eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
culavutal = vaḷital. tuṇṭu eṉṉum karuttutāṉ cil eṉpatic ciliyōṭu iṇikka ōraḷavu utavum.
cil - cilukku = veṭṭiṉa tuṇṭu. cilukku - citukku - cetukku.
(la - ta pōli) o.nō.: calaṅki - cataṅki; kalampam - katampam.
mel - metu.
cil - cili = veṭṭiṉa allatu cetukkiṉa uruvam. ātalāl. kal eṉṉum poruḷ tamiḻil illi.
iṉi, cili eṉpatu cilampu eṉpatu pōl mali eṉṟum tamiḻil poruḷ koṇṭiruppatāl. mali eṉṉum poruḷiṉiṉṟē kal (malippiñcu) eṉṉum mutalākupeyarp poruḷum teṉmoḻiyilum, vaṭamoḻiyilum tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
cili = mali
vaṇaṅkāc cili aḷitta tōḷāṉ  (pu.ve.2:12)
cili = kal (piṅka).
ākavē, cili eṉṉum uruvapporuṭ coṟkut tamiḻ mūlam caṟṟu valintu koḷvatākavē iruppiṉum, piṉvarum ētukkaḷāl atit teṉcol eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
1.	vaṭamoḻiyil cili eṉṉum coṟku mūlamilli.
2.	am moḻiyil ac coṟkuk kaṟporuḷēyaṉṟi uruvap poruḷilli. tamiḻil iraṇṭumuṇṭu.
3.	āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉpē tamiḻakattil uruvak kali iruntatu.
4.	vaṭamoḻiyil kuṟintapakkam aintiliru pakuti teṉcol.
5.	uruvam paṭimi eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum vaṭacollākak kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi pōṉṟē cili eṉpatum.
6.	vēta āriyarkku vēḷvi vaḻipāṭēyaṉṟi uruva vaṇakka milli. teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉṉarē tamiḻariṭamiruntu mēṟ koṇṭaṉar.
7.	cilā eṉṉum vaṭacollil irumoḻip potuveḻuttē yaṉṟi vaṭamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttilli.
8.	tamiḻ aikāravīṟu vaṭamoḻiyil ākāra īṟāyt tirivatu iyalpē. e-ṭu: māli - mālā, vaṭi - vaṭā.
9.	cilā eṉṉuñ col irukku vētattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa vaṭanūl kaḷilēyē āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
10.	cilampu, cili eṉṉum iru (malipporuṭ) coṟkaḷum, tamiḻil cil eṉṉum orē mūlattiyum olittal eṉṉum orē vērpporuṭ karaṇiyattiyum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
11.	tamiḻ mēli āriyattiṟkum aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷi utaviya peruvaḷamoḻi.  (nākit tamiḻccaṅka maṟimali yaṭikaḷ niṉivu malar 1969.)
civaṉ 
civaṉ - civa
civattal = cenniṟamāyiruttal. civa - civam - civaṉ = civantavaṉ.
civaṉ kuṟiñcinilat teyvamāṉa cēyōṉ kūṟātalāl. civaṉ eṉap peṟṟāṉ. antivaṇṇaṉ, aḻalvaṇṇaṉ, cennī vaṇṇaṉ, māṇikkak kūttaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
vaṭavar civaṉ aḻipput toḻil tirumēṉi eṉṟu koṇṭu, civaṉ eṉṉum coṟkuc cī (paṭu) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭi, ellāp poruḷkaḷum oṭuṅkum iṭamāṉavaṉ eṉṟu poruntap poyttalākap poruḷ kūṟuvar.
cilar, maṅkalamāṉavaṉ, naṉmi ceypavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar.
civaṉ tamiḻt teyvamātalāṉum, neruppiṉ kūṟākak koḷḷap paṭutalāṉum, muttoḻil mutalvaṉ eṉpatē tamiḻk koḷki yātalāṉum, kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē civa vaṇakkam toṭaṅki viṭṭamiyāṉum, āriyavaḻip poruṭkāraṇam oru ciṟitum poruntā.
civaṉ - civi (malimakaḷ).  (va.va. 153-154)
civiki (1)
civiki : civital = curuṅkutal, ciṟuttal. civi - civiki = ciṟiya mūṭu pallakku. civiki - civikā, cipikā (va.).  (ti.ma. 742).
civiki (2)
civiki - civikā, cipikā
civital = curuṅkutal. civintapaḻam = muṟṟātu curuṅkippōṉa paḻam. civiṅki = ciṟuttip puli.
civiki = iruvar tūkkum ciṟiya mūṭu pallakku.  (va.va. 154).
civiki vaki
civiki - mūṭu pallakku
pallakku - tiṟanta pallakku  (col. 44)
ciṟpam
ciṟpam - valpa
ciṟ - ciṟpu - ciṟpam = mikak koñcam.
ciṟpaṅkoḷ pakaleṉa  (kamparā. caṭāyukāṇ 8)  (va.va. 154)
ciṟanta viruntu
uṟaviṉariyum naṇpariyum pēṇutalum avarkku viruntaḷit talum ilvāḻvāṉ kaṭamiyeṉiṉum, putumiyāy vantōrkku viruntaḷittalē ciṟappām. viruntu eṉṉum col mutalāvatu virumpic ceyyum vēḷāṇmiyik kuṟikkum. piṉpu aḵtu āku peyarāyp putumiyāy varum viruntāḷiyum avarkkiṭum uṇaviyum kuṟittatu. viruntuṇpōril ciṟantavar putitāy vantavar eṉpatu tamiḻar karuttu. itaṉāl tamiḻariṉ viruntōm paṟ ciṟappu aṟiyappaṭum.  (col. 109).
ciṟu kūlaṅkaḷ
varaku
kāṭikkaṇṇi
kutirivāli  (col 74).
ciṟuteyva vaṇakkam
1. pēy vakikaḷ
tiṭumeṉṟu koliyuṇṭavar avarkkuk kuṟitta vāḻ nāḷelli variyum, varampiṟanta koṭiyōrum tūya periyōrāṟ cāvikkappaṭṭavarum neṭuṅkālamum, iṟantapiṉ pēyāyt tiriva reṉpatu, potuvāṉa karuttu.
alaki, aḷḷi, iruḷ, kaṭi, karuppu, kāṟṟu, kuṇaṅku, kūḷi, maṇṇi, mayal (maruḷ) eṉpaṉa pēyiṉ potuppeyarkaḷ. pēykaḷuḷ nallaṉavum uḷa; tīyaṉavum uḷa.
kuṟaḷi (karuṅkuṭṭi), pēy, kaḻutu, pūtam, muṉi (caṭi muṉi), aramakaḷ, aṇaṅku eṉap pēyiṉam pala tiṟattatākac collap paṭum. kuṟaḷiyik kuṭṭic cāttaṉ eṉpar. pēykaḷuṭ kāṭṭēṟi tūrttēṟi mutaliya palavakika ḷiruppa tākak kūṟuvar. pūtaṅkaḷ kuṟumpūtam perumpūtam eṉa iru vakiya.
kuṇṭik kuṟaṭpūtam (tēvā. 944:1). aramakaḷit tēvamakaḷ eṉṟu, arit teyvattaṉmi koṇṭa oru taṉiyiṉamākak kūṟuvatu ilakkiya vaḻakku. aramakaḷir (nymphs), maliyara makaḷir nīrara makaḷir eṉa iruvakiyar. iruvakiyarum aṭutta māntari accuṟuttik kolvatāl, cūrara makaḷir eṉappaṭuvar. cūr eṉpatu appeyark kuṟukkam.
ekkar naṇṇiya emmūr viyaṉṟuṟi 
nēriṟi muṉki paṟṟic
cūrara makaḷirō ṭuṟṟa cūḷē.  (kuṟun. 53).
paṉmiyiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭataṉāl, avar kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka vāḻvatākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar pōlum! 
aṇaṅku eṉpatu, taṉ aḻaku mikutiyāl āṭavari mayakkik kollum peṇpēy. tākkaṇaṅku eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum (kuṟaḷ. 1082).
kollimali appeyar peṟṟatu oru tākkaṇaṅkiṉālēyē. kolvatu kolli.

tēṉuṭi neṭuvarit teyva meḻutiya
viṉimāṇ pāvi  (naṟ. 185)
itaṉuriyil, kollippāvi - ammaliyiluḷḷa tēvariyum muṉivariyun tuṉpuṟutta varukiṉṟa avuṇarum arakkarum, appāviyiṉ nakiyik kaṇṭu mayaṅki uyirviṭum paṭi, tēva tacca ṉākkivitta peṇvaṭiviṉatu. avuṇarum arakkarum pōtaru kāli, avar vāṭi paṭṭavuṭaṉ tāṉē nakiceyyu māṟu poṟiyuḷ vikkappaṭṭatu. atu nakittuk kollu meṉpataṉi,
